# Caitanya-caritāmṛtaṁ mahā-kāvyam \[BS\]

## Caitanya-caritāmṛtaṁ mahā-kāvyam (Bhanu Svami)

**Translated by Bhanu Svami**

## Chapter One

### Text 1

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya-candro jayati \|*  
*yaḥ śrī-vṛndāvana-bhuvi purā sac-cid-ānanda-sāndro*  
*gaurāṅgībhiḥ sadṛśa-rucibhiḥ śyāma-dhāmā nanarta \|*  
*tāsāṁ śaśvad dṛḍhatara-parīrambha-sambhedataḥ kiṁ*  
*gaurāṅgaḥ san jayati sa navadvīpam ālambamānaḥ \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Glory to the moon of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya!**  
**Kṛṣṇa, full of eternity, knowledge and bliss, of dark complexion, danced in Vṛndāvana with golden complexion gopīs of similar beauty. All glories to Gauraṅga, appearing in Navadvīpa, who is the same Kṛṣṇa but with a complexion golden because of constantly embracing the gopīs tightly.**

### Text 2

*yasyāṅgaṁ śrī-madhurima-parīllṇāha-pīyūṣa-sekair*  
*bhāsvac-cāmīkara-jala-mayaiḥ śānta-niḥśeṣa-tāpaiḥ \|*  
*yasya śrīmat-padajalaruhān mākaranda-pravāhaiḥ*  
*sākṣāt prakṣālitam iva jagac-chaśvad ānamyatāṁ saḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to Gaurāṅga, whose lotus feet constantly shower the universe with streams of nectar and whose limbs sprinkle the universe with expansive golden showers of sweetness that destroys unlimited suffering.**

### Text 3

*jānu-prāptaṁ prasṛmara-bhujā-daṇḍam uccaṇḍa-caṇḍa-*  
*dyota-śreṇī-paṭutara-maho-maṇḍalī-maṇḍitāṅgam \|*  
*ākarṇāntaḥ-skhalita-lalitāpāṅgam atyanta-rajyad-*  
*gaṇḍābhogaṁ mṛgapati-śatākrīḍamānaṁ bhajāmaḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship Gauraṅga whose arms reach his knees, whose limbs shine like the fierce sun, whose eyes extend to his ears, whose cheeks are radiant and who plays like a hundred lions.**

### Text 4

*yasya śrīman-nakhamaṇi-sudhā-raśmi-ramya-prakāśais*  
*trailokyāntar jaṭita-jaḍima-kṣālanāyonmiṣadbhiḥ \|*  
*svīya-premāmbudhi-lahari-kāpūra-pūreṇa bhūyo*  
*jāḍyaṁ cakre tam iha tad aho sevatāṁ jīva-lokaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May all the jīvas of the universe serve Gauraṅga, who stuns the three worlds by the pleasing rays of sweet light from his toenails and further stuns everyone with a great flood of waves from the ocean of his prema!**

### Text 5

*svīyair līlā-vilasita-rasaiḥ pāda-sevā-vilāsair*  
*lāsyollāsair yad ayam akarot pūrṇa-pūrṇāṁ trilokīm \|*  
*manye bhūyas tad iha karuṇā saiva nityaṁ navīnā*  
*bhūyo bhūyaḥ praṇamatu tarāṁ tām imāṁ jīva-lokaḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I consider that this is fresh mercy to the world-- that he filled the three worlds with the rasa of his pastimes, with pastimes of serving his lotus feet and with the joy of dancing. May all the jīvas offer respects to that mercy.**

### Text 6

*yatra śrīman-madhurimamayī kāntir eṣā jagāma*  
*vyāhārāntaṁ guru-karuṇatā pūrṇatām āgatāsīt \|*  
*vaidagdhīyaṁ nikhila-subhagā hanta nirvāham āptā*  
*gaurāṅgasya praṇama tad idaṁ pāda-pāthoja-yugmam \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects the lotus feet of Gaurāṅga, whose sweetness cannot be described by speech, whose great mercy attained the highest level, and in which all beauty attained perfection.**

### Text 7

*citraṁ tāvad-guṇa-jala-nidhes tasya lāvaṇya-dhāmnor*  
*vaidagdhyāder lavam api sudhīr bhāṣituṁ kaḥ samarthaḥ \|*  
*svīyāṁ śaktiṁ dviguṇa-guṇitāṁ ced vidhāyaiṣa vaktuṁ*  
*śaktaḥ śaktaḥ svayam api nahi śrīla-gauracandraḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What poet can describe even a particle of Gauracandra’s skill, the ocean of his qualities or the storehouse of his beauty? Even with twice the ability one cannot describe Śrī Gauracandra.**

### Text 8

*asya śrīmad-vraja-vadhū-prāṇanāthasya līlā-*  
*lāvaṇyāḍhyaṁ taruṇima-sudhā-sambhṛtaṁ taṁ vilāsam \|*  
*ye tat padāmbuja-madhukarā vaktrato hanta teṣāṁ*  
*śrutvā ko’pi pracala-hṛdayaś cāpalād eṣa vakti \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing from the mouths of bees at the lotus feet of Gauraṅga his pastimes endowed with youthful sweetness and intense beauty of Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes, this insignificant person, with trembling heart, out of insolence, now describes his pastimes.**

### Text 9

*kvāsau tat-tad-vibudha-nagarī-cakra-cūḍāmaṇīnāṁ*  
*brahmādīnāṁ mukuṭa-padavī-ratna-nīrājitāṅghriḥ \|*  
*cāpalyaika-pravaṇa-hṛdayaḥ kvāham atyanta-mugdhas*  
*tat kāruṇyaṁ mahad iti kadāpy eṣa sadbhir na heyaḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What I am, a great fool, with fickle mind, compared to the Lord whose feet are worshipped by the crown jewels of the best of the devatās? But still, I will not be rejected by the devotees who are full of great mercy.**

### Text 10

*yad yad dṛṣṭaṁ śrutam api ca yat tasya līlā-vilāsais*  
*tat tat prāṇair atiśaya-mahā-mūḍha-cittāya yan me \|*  
*bhūyo bhūyaḥ kathitam iti yat yad dhṛtaṁ tatra tatra*  
*kṣudro’yaṁ tat kathayati kiyat tat-kṛpāyā vaśaḥ san \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am most fallen and foolish. But by his mercy, I will describe a little of what was heard and seen, what was remembered and told repeatedly to me by his dear devotees.**

### Text 11

*sampūrṇo’yaṁ bhavati yadi vā nodyamas tena kiṁ me*  
*yāvat tāvat prabhu-vilasitotkīrtane bhūri-bhāgyam \|*  
*yad vā śakteḥ samam anuvadan naiva hāsyāya so’yaṁ*  
*yasmān naitat caritam akhilaṁ brahmaṇo’pi prameyam \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Even if this attempt cannot be completed, what loss is there for me? There is still great auspiciousness in glorifying Gauraṅga’s pastimes. Since I try my best to describe things properly, I will not be mocked. Even Brahmā cannot measure all the pastimes of the Lord.**

### Text 12

*yady etasminn ahaha bhavitā dūṣaṇaṁ na pramādāt*  
*kiñcit tasmin na khalu sudhiyām āgraho jātu bhāvī \|*  
*yat te śrīmac-caraṇa-kamala-dvandva-gāthānumattās*  
*tasmād eṣu kṣaṇam api na me vartate kāpy apekṣā \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If there is fault in what I write, it is not because of inattention on my part. The wise will not regard the faults seriously, since they are completely absorbed in singing at the lotus feet of the Lord. Therefore, why should I worry about them at all?**

### Text 13

*śrīmad-vṛndāvana-vara-vadhū-prāṇa-nāthaḥ samastaṁ*  
*viśvaṁ premāmṛta-laharibhir nirbharaṁ plāvayitvā \|*  
*tat-tal-līlāmṛtam api muhuḥ svādayitvā viśeṣaṁ*  
*bhūyas tāsāṁ nikaṭam agamat tad-viyogākṣamo’sau \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After flooding the whole universe with sweet waves of prema, Kṛṣṇa, the life of the gopīs, not able to tolerate separation from them, again came to them, making them taste constantly the sweetness of his pastimes.**

### Text 14

*itthaṁ tat-tad-vilasita-sudhā-pūram āsvādya bhūyaḥ*  
*śikṣā-vyājāṁ prathita-karuṇe hanta hāntardadhāne \|*  
*etat-prāṇāḥ iha katham api iha jīvanaiḥ saṁvisṛṣṭāḥ*  
*kecid bhūmau karuṇa-karuṇāḥ santi kecit prayātāḥ \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Tasting in this way the sweetness of the pastimes and spreading great mercy, he then disappeared on the pretext of teaching others. Some of his devotees somehow remained on earth for the remainder of their lives and some fell on the ground in misery, wanting to leave their bodies.**

### Text 15

*hā gaurāṅga priyatama hahā hā prabho dīna-bandho*  
*hā hā kaṣṭaṁ nija-dhana-jana-prāṇa-jāti-svarūpa \|*  
*itthaṁ bhūyaḥ karuṇa-karuṇaḥ krandatāṁ vāk-prabandhaś*  
*cittaṁ bhittīr api ca śatadhā hanta sadyaḥ karoti \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**"O dear Gauraṅga! O master! O friend of the fallen! You are our wealth, life air, caste and soul." The words of the crying devotees in great misery broke the hearts of the listeners into hundreds of pieces.**

### Text 16

*kecit kecid bahu vikalitās tad viyogāgni-tāpair*  
*dṛṣṭvā viśvaṁ pralaya-samaya-prāya-śūnyātiśūnyam \|*  
*antar bāṣpa-vraṇa-śata-kṛtāṁ vedanāṁ tair vilāpair*  
*dūrīkartuṁ rurudur asakṛd dhā hety ucca-nādaiḥ \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some in a most disturbed state, being burned by the fire of separation, saw the universe to be empty, as at the time of final devastation. They cried loudly many times in lamentation to remove the pain vaporizing their internal organs.**

### Text 17

*hā hā līnā bhavati satataṁ kṣobha-śokāgni-pūre*  
*hā hā prāṇa-priyatama bhavad-viprayoge dharitrī \|*  
*pūrvaṁ yāsau tava caraṇayoḥ snigdha-mugdhair vihāraiḥ*  
*snigdhair āsīt sukṛta-sukṛtā dhanya-dhanyātipuṇyā \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O dear Lord! The earth in separation from you has withered in the intense fire of lamentation and agitation. Previously she was pleased by the joyful play of your feet and was regarded as most fortunate, pious and pure from the touch of those feet.**

### Text 18

*kiṁ kiṁ tasmād ahaha sukṛtaṁ dīrgha-dīrghaṁ samantāc*  
*cakre pṛthvī tava pada-rasair yat prakṛṣṭā rasāsīt \|*  
*hā hā sampraty api virahitā hanta sarvaṁsaheti*  
*svīyaṁ nāma prakaraṇa-vaśād anvitārthaṁ cakāra \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Previously the earth was joyful with the taste of your feet. What great good deeds did she perform in the past to attain this position? But now, in separation, in this situation, she lives up to the name "all-enduring."**

### Text 19

*hā hā nātha priyatama mano-nātha kāruṇya-sindho*  
*niḥsīmāgaḥ śamana-dayita preṣṭha hā hā hatāḥ smaḥ \|*  
*sarvo lokas tava caraṇayor viprayoge’tidurge*  
*līno dīnaḥ śvasati paramair duṣkṛtānāṁ samūhaiḥ \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O master! O dear Lord! Lord of our minds! O ocean of mercy! Unlimited Lord! Merciful destroyer of offenses! Dearest Lord! Everyone is suffering. All people, miserable and wasting away because of intolerable separation from your feet, breathe heavily like sinful persons.”**

### Text 20

*ye ye snigdhāḥ parama-suhṛdas te ta eva prayātās*  
*te te dhanyāḥ prabhu-caraṇayoḥ prema-mātraika-sādhyāḥ \|*  
*hā dhik kaṣṭaṁ prabhum api ca taṁ taṁ ca saṅgaṁ sametya*  
*prāṇāntas tad-viraha-vikalāḥ santi hā dhik kaṭhorāḥ \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those great, affectionate friends who attained prema at his lotus feet and gave up their bodies are most fortunate. They attained his association. But the hard-hearted are unfortunate, being most agitated by separation.**

### Text 21

*ye tat śrīmat-pada-kamalayoḥ saurabhīṁ mādhurīṁ vā*  
*tām āsādya kṣaṇam api na yat sarvam eva tyajanti \|*  
*te vā kaṣṭaṁ kim uta paśavaḥ kiṁ nu vṛkṣā vimūḍhāḥ*  
*kiṁ grāvāṇaḥ śiva śiva na vā cetanābhir vihīnāḥ \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The animals, trees and rocks, tasting the sweet fragrance of his lotus feet, could not give it up. They now experienced the greatest confusion and were like lifeless stones.**

### Text 22

*yat pādāmbhoruha-yuga-rasāsvādanenaiva tṛptās*  
*tyaktaiḥ kāntaṁ dhana-jana-gṛhaṁ prema-mātraika-sādhyāḥ \|*  
*dīnāḥ santaḥ parama-kṛtino hanta santaḥ samantāt*  
*kāntārāntargiriṣu vipineṣv evam evaṁ caranti \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who satisfied with tasting the honey of his two lotus feet, penniless by giving up house, relatives and wealth, attaining prema for the Lord alone, are the most successful. They simply wander on the forest paths, in the caves and in the groves.**

### Text 23

*śrīmat-pādāmbuja-yuga-rasaṁ cakṣuṣāpīya gandhaṁ*  
*tasyāghrāya praṇaya-madhuraṁ prema-sīdhuṁ ca pītvā \|*  
*āsvādyaitad-vacana-madhuraṁ hanta ko jīva-lokas*  
*tad-vicchedaṁ śiva śiva hā hā kathaṁ hanta soḍhā \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having drunk the honey of his lotus feet with their eyes, having smelled their fragrance (with their noses), having drunk the nectar of prema and the sweetness of affection (with their minds), and having relished the sweetness of his words (with their ears), what jīva could tolerate separation from him?**

### Text 24

*adyāpy etac-caraṇa-kamala-dvandva-gandhena sarve*  
*tyaktāsaṅgā niravadhi-galat-sarva-bandhāḥ samantāt \|*  
*svairaṁ svairaṁ naṭana-rabhasaiḥ kīrtanaiḥ sañcaranto*  
*vartante tad-viraha-dahanaṁ kaḥ sahetāsya tasya \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the fragrance of his feet experienced even now, they give up all other association, destroy unlimited bondage completely and wander about independently, performing kīrtana while dancing wildly. Who can tolerate the fire of their separation from Gauraṅga?**

### Text 25

*kathaṁ vā dṛṣṭau tau parama-karuṇau hanta caraṇau*  
*kathaṁ vā dambholi-prakaraṇa-kaṭhino’yaṁ bata janaḥ \|*  
*kathaṁ vā tat-premṇaḥ padam ayam aho tiṣṭhati ca vā*  
*kathaṁ vā tad-vicchede śiva śiva vidher vaiśasam idam \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**How can I see his lotus feet, full of the highest mercy? My heart is as hard as a thunderbolt. How can I be the object of his prema? Without these, my life is not worth living.**

### Text 26

*jagac chūnyaṁ manye kṣitir api ca duḥkhāgni-nivahe*  
*vilīnā līyante sakala-manujās tatra vikalāḥ \|*  
*tathāpy ete prāṇāḥ śiva śiva na gacchanti vidhurā*  
*aho citraṁ śiva śiva vidhir vāma-caritaḥ \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The universe has become empty. The earth is inundated in a great fire of suffering. All humans perish because of torment. But my callous life airs do not depart. The uncooperative nature of the creator is astonishing.**

### Text 27

*aho adyāpy asya priya-guṇa-gaṇānāṁ lavam api*  
*kṣaṇaṁ saṁśṛṇvantaḥ kati kati na deha-tyaja iha \|*  
*sadā śrutvā dṛṣṭvā satatam anubhūyāpi ca sukhaṁ*  
*vinā taṁ jīvāmaḥ śiva śiva mahad duṣkṛtam idam \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who, even today, hear a drop of Gauraṅga’s dear qualities for a moment, have no death. Though I constantly heard about his qualities, saw him, and experienced happiness with him, I continue to live without his presence. Is this because of some great sin I have committed?**

### Text 28

*aho dhanyaiveyaṁ kṣitir atitarāṁ śrī-caraṇayo*  
*rasaiḥ pūrṇā nāmnā guṇa-gaṇa-mahimnā ca mahatā \|*  
*tad etad-vicchedānala-vidaliteyaṁ dalati no*  
*na jānīmaḥ sīmāṁ vidhi-vilasitasya kṣaṇam api \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The auspicious earth was full of the sweetness of his feet and the great glory of his qualities. Tormented by the fire of separation from him, the earth has not perished. I do not know at any moment the limits of the creator’s play.**

### Text 29

*itīhoṣṇa-dīrghaṁ śvasitam idam uccaiḥ pralapitaṁ*  
*vapuḥ kṣīṇaṁ kṣīṇaṁ nayana-jalam atyanta-bahulam \|*  
*vahanto’mī smṛtvā priya-guṇa-gaṇaṁ bhuvi karuṇaṁ*  
*rudanto viśrāntaṁ bata mumuhur āścaryam iti tat \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Remembering Gauraṅga’s dear qualities and his mercy to the earth, everyone exhales with hot sighs, utters loud words of lamentation, becomes thinner and thinner, weeps tears in excess, becomes stunned, cries and faints. This is most astonishing.**

## Chapter Two

### Text 1

*iyaṁ mahī bhāgyavatī mahīyasī*  
*divo’pi divyād api nirmalair guṇaiḥ \|*  
*mahānti ratnāni yadā dadhāty ato*  
*dadhau navadvīpam atīva durlabham \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The auspicious earth shone with more pure qualities than Svarga and displayed great jewels when she supported most rare Navadvīpa.**

### Text 2

*anekadhā sañcita-bhāgya-sañcayaṁ*  
*samastam ekatra vidhāya sarvataḥ \|*  
*mahīruhair utpulakeyam utsukā*  
*dadhau navadvīpa iti prathām kim u \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Taking auspicious qualities from various places and assembling them in one place, the new town became known as Navadvīpa. The earth became ecstatic with hairs standing on end through the trees.**

### Text 3

*prabhuḥ kadā vāvatariṣyatīty ado*  
*vicintayantyā manasi praphullayā \|*  
*manorathākrānti-vaśād anekaśaḥ*  
*satāṁ padābjānugatir yayā dadhe \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The earth became joyful, thinking "When will the Lord appear?" out of overwhelming desire for him. She arranged for the devotees to tread with their lotus feet at that place.**

### Text 4

*iyaṁ navadvīpa-miṣeṇa medinī*  
*dadhāra bhūyo mathurām ivāparām \|*  
*vaded amuṣyāḥ sukṛtāni ko nu vā*  
*prabhoḥ pada-sparśa-rasākulātmanaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**People will glorify the auspiciousness of the place, which is anxious for the touch of the Lord’s feet, saying that the earth in the form of Navadvīpa is supporting spiritual Mathurā.**

### Text 5

*āplāvya yā dhūrjaṭi-saj-jaṭā-taṭīṁ*  
*kapāla-mālā-cchaṭayā samanvitāṁ \|*  
*śaśāṅka-lekhā pratibimba-rūpiṇīm*  
*alabdha-pūrvā śapharīṁ samāsadat \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gaṅgā, who previously took shelter of Śiva’s matted locks, who has the crescent moon on her crest, and who is full of śaphari fish, has given up Śiva.**

### Text 6

*prabhoḥ padāmbhoja-yugasya pāvanī*  
*dhārā manojñā madhuno mahīyasaḥ \|*  
*cakāra yatrāspadam utsukā satī*  
*samantato’sau vimalāmbu-vāhinī \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The pure, attractive river now glorifies the sweet lotus feet of the Lord. Anxious for his appearance, the pure river flows everywhere.**

### Text 7

*drava-svarūpāpi bhavābdhi-śoṣiṇī*  
*śubhrāpi yāsīd dhṛta-kṛṣṇa-vigrahā \|*  
*kṣity-āśritāpi dyunadīti viśrutā*  
*bhramāpahāpi bhrami-vibhramāvahā \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though she is a river, she dries up the ocean of suffering. Though she is white, she embraces the form of Kṛṣṇa. Though she takes shelter of the earth, she is known as the river of Svarga. Though she removes all illusion, she is full of whirlpools.**

### Text 8

*seyaṁ navadvīpa-bhuvo mahīyasāṁ*  
*śobhām ivādhāya tad-anta-vāsinī \|*  
*prabhoḥ padāmbhoja-yugasya saurabha-*  
*prāptyai babhūvotkalikākulī-kṛtā \|\|8\|\|*  
*(caturbhiḥ kalāpam)*

**Перевод:**

**Glorifying the great land of Navadvīpa, she lives there with great longing to attain the fragrance of the Lord’s lotus feet.**

### Text 9

*vasanti yatra kṣiti-deva-sattamāḥ*  
*sadā sadācāra-parāḥ parāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*nirantaraṁ veda-vidhāna-karmasu*  
*śruti-smṛtīnāṁ vidhayaḥ śarīriṇaḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In Navadvīpa live excellent brāhmaṇas and other human beings, dedicated to proper conduct at all times, following the śruti and smṛti, always engaged in acts prescribed by the Vedas.**

### Text 10

*prabhāva-bhājāṁ bhiṣajāṁ mahattamāḥ*  
*svadharma-niṣṭhāś ca viśāṁ varāḥ pare \|*  
*pratiṣṭhayā niḥsaha-śubhrayā sadā*  
*samanvitā yatra vasanti mānavāḥ \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The greatest of doctors live there, as well as the best vaiśyas, fixed in their occupational duties. Men endowed with irresistibly pure qualities reside there.**

### Text 11

*yam etam advaita-mahāśayaḥ svayaṁ*  
*satāṁ mahimnā mahito mahīyasā \|*  
*alañcakāraitad yadīya-bhāvataḥ*  
*prabhur dharaṇyāṁ manujair vilokitaḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Advaita, honored with the greatest qualities of devotees ornamented the place. He took birth there because of its great power.**

### Text 12

*uvāsa yatrāniśam atyudāra-dhīr*  
*adhīta-sarvāgama-veda-kovidaḥ \|*  
*satāṁ variṣṭhaḥ paramo mahāśayaḥ*  
*śrīvāsa-nāmā dvija-vaṁśa-candramāḥ \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In that place, intelligent, most excellent Śrīvāsa, the best of the brāhmaṇas, expert in understanding the meaning of all the Vedas, the best of devotees, resided.**

### Text 13

*babhau mahā-vaṁśa-samudbhavaḥ sudhīr*  
*aneka-vidyāmbudhi-pāra-paṇḍitaḥ \|*  
*divjāti-vaṁśaka-vataṁsavad yataḥ*  
*śrīmān jagannātha itīha viśrutaḥ \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There, the intelligent Jagannātha Miśra, learned in vast areas of knowledge, an ornament in the brāhmaṇa lineage, appeared in a great family.**

### Text 14

*guṇaiḥ samastair ayam eva śuddha-dhīr*  
*adhīta-vedo varaṇya eva hi \|*  
*itīha nīlāmbara-cakravartinā*  
*varāya yasmai sudhiyā sutārpitā \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He was of pure intellect, endowed with all good qualities and had surpassed all others in the study of the Vedas. Intelligent Nīlambara Cakravartī offered his daughter to him in marriage.**

### Text 15

*śacīti nāmnātiśucer acīkḷpad*  
*guṇena sauśīlya-rasena te’nayā \|*  
*pratiṣṭhayā śuddhatamāṁ gariṣṭhatāṁ*  
*śacī hi yāṁ nāpa purandara-priyā \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The daughter was called Śacī because of her purity. Śacī was endowed with all gentle qualities. Because of the greatness of her purity, even the wife of Indra could not be compared.**

### Text 16

*upetya taṁ miśra-purandarāhvayaṁ*  
*nisarga-yogyaṁ padavīm upāśritam \|*  
*babhau śacī candrakaleva nityaśaḥ*  
*śacī samāsādya purandaraṁ yathā \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Similar to Śacī attaining Indra, she accepted Jagannātha Miśra, who was surrendered to the proper path and capable of producing offspring. She shone like the waxing moon.**

### Text 17

*tayor gṛhe saṁvasatoḥ satoḥ sadā*  
*gṛhastha-dharmaḥ sad-udāra sāsadat \|*  
*krameṇa cāṣṭau tanujāḥ puro’bhavan*  
*tathaiva pañcatvam upāyayuś ca tāḥ \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The couple lived in their house, constantly following the rules of household life. Eight daughters were born successively to them and each one died.**

### Text 18

*tataś ca tau santatam eva dampatī*  
*babhūvatur duḥkhitamau mahattamau \|*  
*prayatnam ādhāya sutārtham īyatuḥ*  
*prabhoḥ padābjaṁ śaraṇaṁ kṛpāmayam \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The couple fell into constant lamentation. With concentration, they surrendered to the lotus feet of the merciful Lord in order to attain a son.**

### Text 19

*tato’tibhāgyena tayor abhūt sutaḥ*  
*sa viśvarūpaḥ śubha-rūpa-śobhitaḥ \|*  
*mudaṁ yayau sā sumukhī pitāpy asau*  
*vyaḍambayac cādhanam ātta-sad-vasum \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By good fortune, they obtained a son named Viśvarūpa, endowed with great beauty. They were overjoyed, like a pauper on attaining a great treasure.**

### Text 20

*sa viśvarūpaḥ śubha-rūpa-garvitāṁ*  
*tanuṁ vahaṁś candra iva prakāśavān \|*  
*nipaṭhya kālena laghīyasāpy asau*  
*samasta-vidyāmbudhi-pāram āyayau \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśvarūpa had a beautiful body and shone like the moon. In a short time he learned all the scriptures.**

### Text 21

*śiśuḥ sa āsīd vayasyā laghīyasā*  
*sudhīr adhītāgama-veda-sañcayaḥ \|*  
*sarasvatīyaṁ rasanāgra-nartakī*  
*babhūva vaśyeva sadāsya nirbharam \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The intelligent child at an early age understood the meaning of the Vedas. He controlled Sarasvatī completely. She danced on the tip of his tongue.**

### Text 22

*tataś ca kālena śubhena sundarī*  
*śacī viśeṣaṁ śuśubhe śubhekṣaṇā \|*  
*bhaviṣyad indūdaya-śaṁsinī puraḥ*  
*purandarāśāṁ sadṛśī cakāra sā \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After some time beautiful Śacī by good fortune became pregnant again. She was like the eastern direction before the rising of the moon.**

### Text 23

*śacī satī bhāgyamahī mahīyasī*  
*sukukṣi-pīyūṣa-payonidhau mudā \|*  
*manoramāṁ dohada-lakṣaṇa-śriyaṁ*  
*kṣapākarasyeva navāṁ kalāṁ dadhau \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the moon keeps its first phases in the ocean of nectar, she held the beautiful child in her womb.**

### Text 24

*krameṇa māsā daśa te trayodhikāḥ*  
*samīyur āsannatarā samāptatām \|*  
*tapasyamāsaś caramaḥ sumaṅgalo*  
*babhūva teṣāṁ jagataḥ sukhaika-bhūḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thirteen months passed and then the auspicious Phalguna month, the last month of the year, which gives joy to the whole world, arrived.**

### Text 25

*asāv ṛtūnāṁ patir agratas tadā*  
*prabhoḥ prakāśo bhaviteti harṣitaḥ \|*  
*svakālam ullaṅghya nijaṁ padaṁ dadhāv*  
*ārtis tathā tad-viṣaye hi śobhate \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The king of seasons, spring, became joyful thinking that the Lord would appear before his time, made that season come before its scheduled time. Spring became most beautiful, longing for the Lord.**

### Text 26

*upetu-kāmā sahakāra-nāyakaṁ*  
*nava-prarohām avalambya vīrudham \|*  
*kvaṇantu mad-bhṛṅga-samūha-nūpuraṁ*  
*vasanta-lakṣmīr vipine padaṁ dadau \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lakṣmī of spring, desiring to approach her husband, spring, to assist him, produced new buds on the creepers and wore on her feet ankle bells in the form of humming bees.**

### Text 27

*sva-veśa-vinyāsam ivākarod iyaṁ*  
*prabhoḥ prakāśo bhaviteti santatam \|*  
*vasanta-lakṣmīḥ satatotsukā satī*  
*satīva kāntāgamane śuci-smitā \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As a wife arranges her clothing and brightly smiles when her husband comes, the Lakṣmī of spring arranged her ingredients of beauty, understanding the spring would manifest soon.**

### Text 28

*svabhāva-mādyat-kala-kaṇṭha-kākalī-*

*navaṁ samudyan-madhu-puṣpa-mādhurī-*  
*dhurīṇam īṣad dhasitaṁ ca komalam \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lakṣmī of spring took on soft, sweet voice in the form of cuckoos madly warbling and a sweet smile in the form of fresh flowers bearing honey.**

### Text 29

*tamāla-mālā-dala-mādhurī-mayā*  
*babandha dhammilla-bharaṁ manoharam \|*  
*madhuvratālī-maya-cilli-vallarīṁ*  
*pranartayām āsa sukhaṁ madālasyam \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She bound up her hair nicely in the form of bunches of tamāla leaves and made her eyebrows move in the form of swarms of bees.**

### Text 30

*unmīlayāmāsa ca vāma-locanaṁ*

*dadhāra vāso nava-mālikā-mayam \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Casting a glance with her left eye, she wore earrings in the form of new, beautiful buds, wore a necklace of clove flowers and a cloth of new jasmine blossoms.**

### Text 31

*sad-aṅga-rāgaṁ vidadhe’tiharṣitā \|*

*manohare keśara-kuṭmala-stane \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On her breasts in the form of kecāra buds, she placed kuṁkuma in the form of āśoka flowers and sandalwood paste in the form of pollen from sweet flowers.**

### Text 32

*prasedur āśā daśa nirmalaṁ babhau*  
*nabho vavuḥ puṇyatamāś ca mārutāḥ \|*

*prasannatāṁ svaccham abhūn nadī-jalam \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ten directions became pure and joyful. The sky was filled with pure breezes. All men’s minds became pleased. The river water became clear.**

### Text 33

*tadā śaśāṅkaḥ paripūrṇa-maṇḍalaḥ*

*cumban muhuḥ pūrva-dig-aṅganā-mukham \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The moon joyfully embraced its fullest form and, on kissing his bride in the form of the eastern horizon, attracted the whole world.**

### Text 34

*asāv ṛtūnāṁ patir agrato’bhavat*

**Перевод:**

### Text 35

*vana-priyās tat samaye madhūnmadās*

**Перевод:**

**The cuckoos drunk with honey uttered shouts of victory. The bees danced and the south wind blew.**

### Text 36

*sa nirbhara-stamba-bhareṇa mantharo*

*vavau maruc-candana-śaila-nandanaḥ \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The fresh wind from Malaya Mountain, bearing clusters of flowers, indolently embracing each creeper and carried lotus honey and water of the summer season.**

### Text 37

*jagat-prasādaḥ prababhūva nirbharam \|*

**At the moment of the most auspicious appearance of the Lord, the whole world became pleased. By his great effulgence he made all darkness disappear.**

### Text 38

*tataḥ prabhur bhūmi-gato mahaujasā*  
*rarāja sarvāḥ kakubhaḥ prakāśayan \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the Lord appeared on earth with great effulgence. He illuminated all directions. It was like moon rays combined with lightning falling on earth.**

### Text 39

*tadoparāgaḥ samabhūt tathā muhur*

*prakāśa-mātreṇa bhavet prakāśitam \|\|39\|\|*

**An eclipse occurred at that time, with the thought, "All men should chant the name of the Lord loudly. With his appearance nothing should be done except chant his name."**

### Text 40

*sudhā-nidhiṁ tat-samaye vidhuntudas*  
*tutoda sānandam aruntado bhṛśam \|*

*samudgato’nyo’sti bhuvīti bhāvayan \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rāhu at that time gleefully afflicted the moon. He said, "What use are you now? Another moon has arisen.**

### Text 41

*prabhur bubhūṣur nija-nāma-kīrtane*  
*nirantara-prema-vilāsa-lālasaḥ \|*

*jagat sva-nāmāmṛta-pūra-pūritam \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

### Text 42

*athāvalokya śriya eka vibhrama-*

**Перевод:**

**His mother and father seeing him as the sprout of a tree manifesting bewildering beauty, drowned in an ocean of bliss and cleansed his body.**

### Text 43

*dvijoccayebhyaḥ samadāt tadaiva hi \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The father, considering his son to be the result of accumulated pious acts, became joyful and gave away his earned wealth without limit to the brāhmaṇas.**

### Text 44

*prakāśa-mātreṇa sudakṣiṇā grahā*

**Перевод:**

**When the child was born, the benefic planets were strong. The Moon was in Pūrva-phāgunī in Siṁha sign.**

### Text 45

*manoramaṁ vastu jagad virāji yat*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever was attractive in the universe became his possession. Who else on earth is a worthy recipient of these things?**

**Перевод:**

**The moon along with spring doubled its attractiveness and became the servant of Gauraṅga’s moon-like face, since desires pursue what is most difficult to attain.**

**Fresh sesame flowers served his nose with their elevated beauty. The bewildering reflection in mirrors served his two cheeks.**

**Перевод:**

**The autumn moon took shelter of his attractive smile. The beauty of pearls stood there, desirous of the treasure of his teeth.**

### Text 51

*siṣeva āpīnam ura-sthalaṁ guru \|\|53\|\|*

### Text 56

### Text 57

### Text 59

*kramād athotthāna-vidhāna-maṅgalaṁ*

### Text 60

*tataḥ sa kālena sujānu-maṇḍala-*

**Перевод:**

**In time, the child began to touch the earth frequently with his two knees when he crawled. The earth became relieved of her distress in separation by the touch of all his limbs.**

### Text 61

*babhau pitur mānasa-haṁsa utsukaḥ \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

### Text 62

*acīkḷpannāma manoramāśayaḥ \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

### Text 63

*pratapta-kārtasvara-śaila-bhāsura-*

*rarāja rājan-marud-aṁśuko’sakau \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His body shining like molten gold, the beauty of his moon-like smiling face, his forehead decorated with fickle locks of hair, and his golden cloth shone.**

### Text 64

*mahā-mahā-ratnam ivātiharṣadam \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord in his infant years increased in beauty like the fresh moon, like a gigantic gem, stealing the beauty of an ocean of nectar, giving great joy to all.**

### Text 65

*jhanaj-jhanatkāra-manojña-kaṅkaṇaiḥ*  
*pravāla-muktā-maṇihāra-vibhramaiḥ \|*  
*nitamba-bimbaika-vilambi-kiṅkiṇī-*  
*raveṇa śaśvat kutukī nanarta saḥ \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Eagerly he danced at all times to the sound of the bells on his waist, the jingling of his bracelets and the swaying of his coral and pearl necklace.**

### Text 66

*athaiṣa kālena śanaiḥ śanaiḥ kṣitau*  
*padāravindaṁ madhu mādhurīm ayam \|*  
*nyasyann amuṣyāś cira-viprayogajaṁ*  
*jahāra tāpaṁ karuṇā-payonidhiḥ \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In time the Lord, the ocean of mercy, placed his sweet lotus feet upon the ground gently and relieved the earth of long separation.**

### Text 67

*khelā-vilāsena vayasya-bālakair*  
*vihartu-kāmaḥ kamanīya-vigrahaḥ \|*  
*navair navaiḥ pallava-sañcayair amūn*  
*jaghāna tais tair muditaiḥ sa cāhataḥ \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In his pastime of playing, the beautiful Lord desired to play with children of his age. He beat them and he was beaten with new shoots by the joyful children.**

### Text 68

*tam ekadā taiḥ śiśubhir nirantaraṁ*  
*khelantam enaṁ jananī vilokya sā \|*  
*abhūd vidhartuṁ kṛta-kaitavaṁ ruṣā*  
*samudyatā taṁ kṣaṇam atyudāra-dhīḥ \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing her children play in this way with the other children, his mother became angry and tried to catch him for his naughty acts.**

### Text 69

*vilokya tām ittham asau ruṣānvito*  
*babhañja bhāṇḍāni bahūni santatam \|*  
*tam īdṛśaṁ tatra vilokya sā śacī*  
*babandha bhītā svayam apy atisphuṭam \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The intelligent boy, seeing her in this angry mood, began breaking many pots. Seeing him do this, she became frightened and tied him up firmly.**

### Text 70

*upary upary āhita-bhāṇḍa-saṁhatau*  
*sugarhitocchiṣṭa-visarjana-sthale \|*  
*jagāma mātuḥ purato mahāprabhuḥ*  
*prakāśayan jñāna-parām sa vijñatām \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then, at a place piled with broken pots and unclean with food remnants, Mahāprabhu displayed to his mother his great learning.**

### Text 71

*vilokya tatrātya-śuci-sthale gataṁ*  
*sutaṁ śacī prāha bhayākula-kramam \|*  
*jahīhi tātāśuci-deśa-saṁsthitiṁ*  
*mamāṅkam āgaccha vidhāya śuddhatām \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing her son in the impure place, Śacī spoke with fear. “O son! Leave that unclean place. Purifying yourself, come to my lap.”**

### Text 72

*niśamya mātur vacanaṁ mahāprabhur*  
*nyarūpayat sac-cid-acit-svarūpatām \|*  
*avehi mātar vacanaṁ mamedṛśaṁ*  
*jahi bhramaṁ cetasi vibhramākule \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the words of his mother, Mahāprabhu began defining the eternal nature of everything conscious and unconscious. “O mother! Understand my words. Give up the false world in your heart which is overcome with illusion.**

### Text 73

*idaṁ hi viśvaṁ sacarācaraṁ tu yad*  
*vilokyate tad bhrama eva kevalam \|*  
*pavitratā vāpy apavitratāpi vā*  
*kathaṁ bhaved amba vicitram eva tat \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“This world of moving and immobile entities that you see is all simply illusion. O mother! How can it be pure or impure? This is astonishing.**

### Text 74

*yato hy anānātva ihaitad ātmano*  
*ghaṭeta naivedam ahaṁ mamety api \|*  
*sa eka ātmaiva sadāvaśiṣyate*  
*tad anyad etat sakalaṁ hi vibhramaḥ \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The ātmā has no variety. There is no distinctions of I and mine. The ātmā alone remains at all times. Everything else is illusion.**

### Text 75

*idaṁ hi yad vā sura-martya-rakṣasāṁ*  
*tanūṣu sarvāsu vasanti pañca te \|*  
*kṣitir jalaṁ vyoma maho marut tatas*  
*tad-ātmakaṁ sarvam abhinnam eva hi \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The five elements earth, water, air, fire and ether exist in all the bodies of devatās, humans and Rākṣasas. Everything is made of these elements and is non-different from ātmā.**

### Text 76

*ataḥ pavitraṁ sakalaṁ hi vastuto*  
*na cāpavitraṁ kiyad apy ado bhuvi \|*  
*itthaṁ vadantaṁ tam udāradhīḥ śacī*  
*dadhāra sā pāṇi-yugena satvarā \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“All objects are thus pure. Nothing impure exists on the earth.” Hearing him speak this, intelligent Śacī quickly took his hand.**

### Text 77

*tataḥ samānīya surāpagā-jalaṁ*  
*sutaṁ parisnāpya mudaṁ parāṁ yayau \|*  
*tataś ca kālena tathaiva taṁ śacī*  
*vilokya tatraiva tatarja bhāṣitaiḥ \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Taking some Gaṅgā water she bathed him and became happy. After some time, she looked at him and scolded him.**

### Text 78

*punaḥ punar mandamate’śuci-sthale*  
*prayāsi kiṁ kiṁ nu viruddham īhase \|*  
*iti krudhā lohita-lola-locanaś*  
*cukopa mātur vacanāntare prabhuḥ \|\|78\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She spoke in anger. “O fool! Repeatedly you go to impure places. Why do you not prefer the opposite?” With red, roving eyes he became angry at his mother’s words.**

### Text 79

*muhuḥ puroktaṁ kim apīha vartate*  
*na cāpavitraṁ sakalaṁ hi cinmayam \|*  
*tathāpi garhāṁ kuruṣe sadaiva mām*  
*itīha loṣṭreṇa jaghāna mātaram \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I have repeatedly told you that nothing here is impure. Everything is spiritual. Yet you scold me all the time.” He hit her with a stone.**

### Text 80

*tadā tad-āghāta-kṛta-vyathārditā*  
*papāta bhūmau mṛdulā svabhāvataḥ \|*  
*tataḥ sa hā mātar iti tvarānvito*  
*vadaṁs tad-aṅkeṣv aviśad dravan-manāḥ \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pained by the blow, his delicate mother fell to the ground. He quickly said “O mother!” and with an affectionate heart sat on her lap.**

### Text 81

*striyaḥ samāgatya suśītalair jalais*  
*tatas tad-āsyaṁ siṣicuḥ kṛta-tvarāḥ \|*  
*mumoda sāpi pratiruddhayā dhiyā*  
*tad-aṅga-saṅgāmṛta-pūra-secanaiḥ \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Women quickly came and sprinkled her face with cool water. Even half conscious, she experienced bliss from a sprinkling of nectar from the association with his limbs.**

### Text 82

*jagāda kācit jagad-eka-vallabhaṁ*  
*dravan-manā narma-parā mahāprabhum \|*  
*dadāsi mātre yadi nārikelakaṁ*  
*tadaiva sadyaḥ samupaiti susthatām \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One affectionate woman as a joke spoke to Mahāprabhu, dear to the universe. “If you just give a coconut to your mother, she will immediately recover.”**

### Text 83

*itīdam asyā vacanaṁ niśamya sa*  
*tvarāyutas tan-nikaṭād bahirgataḥ \|*  
*dadau tadā tat-kṣaṇa-pātanena tat*  
*sahārdra-vṛntaṁ sahasā phala-dvayam \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing her words, he immediately went out and obtained in an instant two coconuts with a part of their stems intact.**

### Text 84

*vilokya tās tat-phala-lambhanaṁ śiśor*  
*durāpam anyair api tat nisargataḥ \|*

*kutas tvayā labdham idaṁ phala-dvayam \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing him bring two coconuts hard to obtain by others so easily, the brāhmaṇa women were astonished. They asked, “Where did you obtain these two coconuts?”**

### Text 85

*sahuṅkṛtais tāḥ sahasātikopato*  
*nivārayāmāsa na kiñcid ūcivān \|*

*na hi prajeśo’pi bhavo’pi vetti yat \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With anger and grunts, he refused to speak to them. “His actions are astonishing. Even Brahmā cannot understand him.”**

### Text 86

*kadācid eṣā nija-mandire śacī*  
*sutena sārdhaṁ śayitā niśāntare \|*

*janair ivālakṣya sutaṁ jagāda tam \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One day, Śacī, lying down with her son in the evening, saw that the place was filled with many people. She spoke to him.**

*dhvanis tulākoṭi-bhavo vyavardhata \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O son! Go to your father’s room quickly.” When he went some distance, there was a pleasing sound of ankle bells from his beautiful lotus feet.**

*padābjayoḥ kevalayor manoramam \|*

**Перевод:**

**His mother and father heard the sound of ankle bells, which sounded like bees buzzing on lotuses blooming out of season.**

*parasparaṁ tau sabhayaṁ samūcatuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**They spoke to each other in fear. “Where did this great sound of ankle bells come from?” Overjoyed by the sound of the bells, Jagannātha Miśra embraced his son who had approached him.**

*athāgrajodvyaṣṭa-samāsam āśritaḥ*  
*sa viśvarūpaḥ samupetya sadvayaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**His older brother Viśvarūpa reached the age of sixteen years. He had attained an ocean of good qualities and considered the universe to be the same as his self.**

### Text 91

*babhūva sarvajñatayā samanvitaḥ*

*samāśrito nirbhara-śānta-dāntatām \|\|91\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Most learned, he was attached to Mahāprabhu’s lotus feet and not attached to anything in the material world. Intelligent, he had control of his senses and mind.**

### Text 92

*pitā vicintyātha vivāha-maṅgalaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**His father, thinking of an auspicious marriage, began searching for a bride suitable to his form and qualities. Viśvarūpa instantly understood his intentions.**

### Text 93

**Перевод:**

**Seeing his father eager to fulfill his desire for his marriage, Viśvarūpa left the house, crossed the Gaṅgā and departed, renouncing everything.**

### Text 94

**Перевод:**

**Fulfilling all his duties, all-attractive Viśvarūpa, an ocean of good qualities, took sannyāsa. An intelligent person with no material desires does not fall into illusion and pursue great projects in a hopeless world.**

### Text 95

*tadaitad āśrutya pitā prasūś ca sā*

**Hearing of this, his father and mother lamented loudly and fainted. Recovering, they gave blessings to their son, desiring his benefit.**

### Text 96

**“Our son has taken sannyāsa at an early age. The Lord should be merciful so that he will always be engaged in proper dharma.”**

### Text 97

*itīha bhūyo’tivilapy duḥkhitau*

**Перевод:**

**In sorrow they lamented in this way. Taking Gauraṅga in their arms, they became joyful by the auspicious association with his body.**

### Text 98

*uvāca vācāmṛta-pūra-pūrṇayā*

*tathaiva tātaṁ ca sadā dravan-manāḥ \|\|98\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With an affectionate heart, the ocean of mercy, taking shelter of his mother’s limbs, spoke to his father and mother with sweet words, which deliver the jīva from death.**

### Text 99

*gato’grajo me bhavatīm upekṣya yat*  
*titikṣayāsau pitaraṁ ca śāntimān \|*

**Перевод:**

**“My peaceful brother, ignoring his father, has left with great forbearance. I will soon do all services to you and mother.”**

### Text 100

*tadā tadākarṇayator vaco’mṛtaṁ*  
*kalasvareṇāti-gabhīram arthataḥ \|*  
*tadaiva pitror abhavat pariplutaṁ*  
*sukhair anekair vapur uttanūruham \|\|100\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When they heard these sweet words endowed with great meaning, his parent’s hairs stood on end because of great happiness.**

### Text 101

*tad-aṅga-saṅgāmṛta-dhārayā tayā*  
*manas tayor āplutam eva niścitam \|*

*yad-īkṣaṇa-dvandva-pathena nirgatā \|\|101\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Their minds were filled with streams of nectar through association with his body. The streams flowed out through their eyes.**

### Text 102

*paṭhan saparyāpara eva sarvadā*

*nirantaraṁ khelati khelayaty api \|\|102\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Serving his parents, at all times, being most merciful to them and bringing them joy, he began studies and played constantly with boys his age in a friendly manner.**

### Text 103

*svatantram ālokya kadācid ātmajaṁ*  
*pitā vacobhir nirabhartsayan muhuḥ \|*  
*tato rajanyāṁ śayito’tiśuddha-dhīr*  
*dadarśa saṁsvapnam adabhra-bhāgyavān \|\|103\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the independent nature of his son, the father scolded him constantly. While sleeping, the most fortunate, pure minded father then had a dream.**

### Text 104

*sutaḥ svatantro mama kiṁ sadā bhaved*  
*atīva-khelākula-lola-mānasaḥ \|*  
*itīva kṛtvā bahu-manyate bhavān*  
*na caivam āviṣkṛta-gaura-vigraham \|\|104\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You think, ‘My son is independent, and is absorbed in playing all the time.’ You do not respect him since he has not revealed his golden form.**

### Text 105

*yathā sparśa-sukhaṁ mahā-maṇer*  
*paśur bhajann apīmaṁ parilokayann api \|*  
*na vetti tat-tat-sad-asad-vivecanāṁ*  
*svabhāva-mugdhasya vivecanā kutaḥ \|\|105\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“An animal can touch and see a valuable gem but cannot understand its value. How can an innate fool have power of discernment?”**

### Text 106

*itthaṁ vacobhir bata bharsayann amuṁ*  
*dvijo jagādātiruṣāruṇekṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*prabuddha āsīt tata eva san-manāḥ*  
*suvismitas tat sakalaṁ jagāda ca \|\|106\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With red eyes, that brāhmaṇa scolded Jagannātha Miśra with these words. The father woke up and, joyful with astonishment, told everyone what he had dreamed.**

### Text 107

*niśamya tat svapnam atīva vismitā*

*mahāśayo’sāv iti sādhu menire \|\|107\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing about his dream and being astonished, people eagerly regarded the child, the Lord and best of men, as a great person, by their mind and by their words.**

### Text 108

*tataḥ kadācin nivasan sva-mandire*  
*samudyad-āditya-mahojjvalaḥ \|*  
*sva-tejasā dhvasta-tamisra-sañcayo*  
*jagāda devo jananīṁ pura-sthitām \|\|108\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One day, sitting his house and shining like the sun, destroying all darkness by his effulgence, he spoke to his mother present there.**

### Text 109

*saṁśrūyatāṁ mātar idaṁ vadāmi yat*  
*tatheti tasyoditam ādade śacī \|*

*kariṣyate tad vada tāta bhāṣitam \|\|109\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O mother! Please listen to what I say.” Śacī answered, “Whatever you say, I will do. Please speak.”**

### Text 110

*kadāpi mātar hari-vāsare tvayā*  
*na kāryam evādanam ity asau punaḥ \|*

*samādade nirbhara-bhāgya-bhūṣitā \|\|110\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He said, “O mother! Never eat on Ekādaśī day.” From that time, the fortunate Śacī followed that instruction.**

### Text 111

*punaś ca tāmbūla-phalādi śuddhiman*

*sutasya dehaṁ calito’ham añjasā \|\|111\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One day, rejecting pure betel and fruit offered to him, he said, “O mother! My body is trembling. Please take care of me.”**

### Text 112

*sa ittham utthāya mahāprabhuḥ kṣitau*  
*papāta śampāyuta-koṭi-koṭivat \|*  
*itīmam ālokya visaṁjñam ākulā*  
*siṣeca gaṅgā-salilaiḥ śacī ciram \|\|112\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He stood up and then fell down, like a million bolts of lightning striking the ground. Seeing him unconscious, his mother sprinkled him with Gaṅgā water for a long time.**

### Text 113

*tataḥ prabodha-sthirayā dhiyā samaṁ*  
*nava-prabodhāmbuja-rājad-īkṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*samutthito’sau mahasā nisargiṇā*  
*samāvṛtaḥ śārada-candravad babhau \|\|113\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With eyes like newly blooming lotuses, he recovered consciousness with and alert mind and stood up. He was like the autumn moon surrounded with natural light.**

### Text 114

*tadā tad āśrutya pitāpi tādṛśaṁ*  
*jagāma bhūyaḥ saha vismayaṁ svayam \|*  
*uvāca vācaś ca sad-artha-vācikāḥ*  
*kim etad etat kim itīti rītitaḥ \|\|114\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing about this, his father came with astonishment and spoke to him some serious words. “What is this behavior?”**

### Text 115

*tad-āśayaṁ tac-caritaṁ tad-iṅgitaṁ*  
*vidanti tad-vibhramam atra ke janāḥ \|*  
*nahi svayambhūḥ śrutayaś ca tāḥ svayaṁ*  
*bhavo’pi tāvat prabhavo bhaviṣṇavaḥ \|\|115\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What person can understand his intentions, his character, his gestures and his pastimes? Brahmā, Śiva, the Vedas and even persons born in the future cannot understand him.**

### Text 116

*guror gṛhe saṁvasatā mahā-dhiyā*  
*samasta-vidyāh sakṛtārthatāḥ kṛtāḥ \|*  
*kṣaṇena tasmin viviśuś ca tāḥ svayaṁ*  
*payonidhau nadya ivotsukā bhṛśam \|\|116\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The intelligent boy, staying at his teacher’s house, learned all knowledge with its meaning. Instantly he absorbed it all, like an ocean receiving eager rivers.**

### Text 117

*tataḥ pitā tasya nivṛtta-yauvano*  
*jarāṁ sa bheje jvarito’tidurbalaḥ \|*  
*tathāvidhaṁ taṁ parilakṣya sa prabhur*  
*nināya gaṅgā-taṭa-bhūmim ākulaḥ \|\|117\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With his youth dissipated, his father became old, weak and feverish. Seeing him in this condition, Gauraṅga took him to the bank of the Gaṅgā in grief.**

### Text 118

*pituḥ padaṁ vakṣasi duḥkhitātmanā*  
*nidhāya tepe nitarāṁ kṛpāvatā \|*  
*pitaḥ kva māṁ projjhya sudīnam ekakaṁ*  
*śiśuṁ kathaṁ hanta bhavān gamiṣyati \|\|118\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Holding his father’s feet to his chest, with sorrow in his heart, he said, “O father! Why are you giving up this miserable child and going away?”**

### Text 119

*niśamya vākyāmṛtam asya harṣadaṁ*  
*tato’nta-kāle dvija-puṅgavo’sakau \|*  
*samarpaṇaṁ te raghunātha-pādayoḥ*  
*kṛtaṁ sukhī syām iti putram abravīt \|\|119\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing these sweet words which gave bliss, his father spoke as he passed away. “I have given you to the lotus feet of Rāma. Please be happy.”**

### Text 120

*atha sā pati-pāda-paṅkaja-*  
*dvayam āliṅgya sa-gadgada-svaram \|*  
*paridevanayānayā muhur*  
*bahudhā netra-jalair asecayat \|\|120\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śacī held her husband’s lotus feet and soaked them with her profuse tears as she lamented with a choked voice.**

### Text 121

*api māṁ parihāya duḥkhitām*  
*atidīnāṁ kurarīm iva prabho \|*  
*kva nu samprati yāsi nīyatāṁ*  
*nija-dāsī bahu-duḥkha-karṣitā \|\|121\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I am like a suffering kurarī bird. Giving me up, where are you going? You should take your servant, thin with sorrow.”**

### Text 122

*divi deva-gaṇe nirantaraṁ*  
*sumano-varṣiṇi bhūriśaḥ sukhāt \|*  
*bhuvi kīrtana-tat-pare jane*  
*dyunadī-madhya-gataḥ sa nirvavau \|\|122\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The father left his body in the Gaṅgā, while devatās in the sky showered plentiful flowers in joy and people offered kīrtana on earth.**

## Chapter Three

### Text 1

*navīna-lāvaṇya-sudhāmbu-dhārā-*  
*bhṛtā navīnena sad-aṅgakena \|*  
*taṁ yauvarāṣṭre sakalasya yūnaḥ*  
*prasūna-cāpobhiṣiṣeca bhūyaḥ \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because his limbs flowed with the nectar of new youth, Cupid enthroned him as the king of the domain of all other youths.**

### Text 2

*papāṭha sat-paṇḍita-viṣṇu-nāmnaḥ*  
*sudarśanād apy atiharṣa-bhājaḥ \|*  
*gurutvam ākalpya mahānukampāṁ*  
*cakāra harṣād anayoḥ kim eṣaḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He accepted learned Viṣṇu and Sudarśana, who were endowed with great joy, as his teachers, but out of joy gave them great mercy.**

### Text 3

*tataś ca vaiyākaraṇāt sa gaṅgā-*  
*dāsād abhūt pratyanubhūta-vidyaḥ \|*  
*yad eṣa vidyām adadād dvijebhyas*  
*tenaiva puṇyena papāṭha so’tra \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He then learned grammar from Gaṅgā-dāsa. Since Gaṅgā-dāsa given knowledge to many brāhmaṇas, Gauraṅga studied under him because of that pious act.**

### Text 4

*satīrtha-vṛndaiḥ parihāsavadbhir*  
*hasan viśeṣaṁ savadāvadena \|*  
*tatāna līlā-pratibhāna-vārtām*  
*urvī sad-urvī-sura-vaṁśa-ratnam \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he joked with school mates loudly, the jewel among brāhmaṇas on earth spread his bold pastimes.**

### Text 5

*kadācanāsau vanamāli-nāmnā*  
*gṛhe sadācārya-varasya nāthaḥ \|*  
*jagāma sambhāṣa-rasena harṣād*  
*yadṛcchayā śrīmaya-gaura-dehaḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Beautiful Gaurāṅga one time by chance went to the house of Vanamālī, the best of ācāryas, out of joy in debating.**

### Text 6

*nivartamānena tataḥ sukhena*  
*sambhāṣya taṁ vartmani tena tatra \|*  
*akāri pīyūṣam iva kṣarantī*  
*netrātithiḥ kācana hema-vallī \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While returning home satisfied with the discussion, he saw a girl like a golden creeper flowing with nectar.**

### Text 7

*sā vallabhācārya-sutā calantī*  
*snātuṁ sakhībhiḥ sura-dīrghikāyām \|*  
*lakṣmīr anenaiva sahāvatīrṇā*  
*prabhor yayau locana-vartma tatra \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The daughter of Vallabhācārya, Lakṣmī who appeared with the Lord on earth, was going to bathe with her friends. Suddenly he saw her there.**

### Text 8

*vilokya sa prāktana-vallabhāṁ tāṁ*  
*sukhāmbudhau majjanam ātatāna \|*  
*naisargikam prema yathāvakāśam*  
*prasahya nāmodayatīhau1 kaṁ vā \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing his previous consort, he drowned in an ocean of happiness and spread natural prema. Would the occasion not give joy to any person?**

### Text 9

*tathāvidhāṁ tām avalokya rāmāṁ*  
*manasy abhūd ullasitaḥ kṛpābdhiḥ \|*  
*maṇiṁu2 vinā durlabham ābhirāmyaṁ*  
*na haiminī hāra-latā prayāti \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Looking at Lakṣmī, the ocean of mercy became overjoyed in his mind. A golden necklace does not reach its height of beauty without a gem.**

### Text 10

*sā śaiśavād eka-padena bālā*  
*samāgatā yauvana-sīmni kiñcit \|*  
*parituṭac-cāpala-jāyamāna-*  
*trapā tam ālokya nananda śaśvat \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The young girl who had entered into a portion of youth from childhood, on glancing at Gauraṅga with shyness which destroyed her fickleness, felt constant joy.**

### Text 11

*athājagāmaiṣa nirīkṣya kāntāṁ*  
*tais tair vayasyair viharaṁs tathaiva \|*  
*paṭhan sadodgrāha-paraḥ pareśo*  
*rarāja gūḍha-sthira-ramya-līlaḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing his beloved, he went home, playing with this friends and studying. Eager for marriage, the Lord performed pleasant pastimes without revealing this.**

### Text 12

*athāparedyur vanamāli-nāmā*  
*prabhoḥ ya ācārya upetya veśma \|*  
*namaścakāra praṇato mahātmā*  
*śacīṁ śuciḥ saṅkathayan vidhijñaḥ \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One day the ācārya named Vanamālī came to the Lord’s house. A great soul who was pure and knowlegeable of the rules, he offered respects and spoke to Śacī.**

### Text 13

*sutāya te devi vṛtāsti kācit*  
*kanyātidhanyā guṇa-rūpa-śīlaiḥ \|*  
*sā vallabhācārya-sutā varāṅgī*  
*mūrteva lakṣmīḥ kṣitito’vatīrṇā \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O respectable lady! A young girl, most fortunate, and endowed with beauty and good qualities, has accepted your son as her husband. She is the daughter of Vallabhācārya. She has perfect limbs and is Lakṣmī herself appearing on earth.**

### Text 14

*vidhīyatāṁ tatra laghu prayatnas*  
*tanūja-ratnasya vivāha-kārye \|*  
*yadīcchasi śrīmati tāṁ sad-aṅgāṁ*  
*śriyaṁ vadhū-ratnam anindya-śīlām \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If you desire this beautiful girl, a jewel of a bride possessing faultless character as his wife, then quickly make arrangements for the marriage ceremony with your jewel-like son.”**

### Text 15

*ity asya saṁśrutya vacomṛtaṁ sā*  
*tuṣṇīm abhūn naiva kim apy uvāca \|*  
*aśraddadhānā vacane’sya tasmin*  
*sute’pi tal-lakṣaṇa-lakṣaṇārthā \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing his sweet words, she remained silent and did not speak. She wanted to confirm with her son whether he desired the marriage.**

### Text 16

*naivākalayyāśu vacāṁsi śacyā*  
*yayau sa ācārya-varo’tiduḥkhī \|*  
*vilokayāmāsa manaḥ-kathābhiḥ*  
*kaṣāyitāsyo’tha mahāprabhuṁ tam \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Not hearing words of consent from Śacī, Vanamālī became very sad. With withered face, he went to see Mahāprabhu to explain his mind.**

### Text 17

*asau navadvīpa-kiśora-candraś*  
*candrānanaś candra-sahasra-kāntaḥ \|*  
*ācāryam ālokya nanāma hṛṣṭo*  
*dṛḍhaṁ pariṣvajya ca dhīram ūce \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The youthful moon of Navadvīpa, endowed with a moon-like face and a thousand moons for limbs, seeing the ācārya, joyfully offered respects, embraced him firmly, and spoke gravely.**

### Text 18

*āsīḥ kva gantā tvam aye mahātman*  
*kathaṁ nu vā tvaṁ vimanāḥ prayāsi \|*  
*sa āha mātuś caraṇau tavaiva*  
*draṣṭuṁ gataḥ samprati yāmi duḥkhī \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O great soul! Why have you come? Why are you so depressed?” He said, “I went to see your mother and now I come in sorrow to you."**

### Text 19

*na kiñcid ūce tam idaṁ sa śṛṇvan*  
*svam eva gehaṁ prayayau kṛpābdhiḥ \|*  
*tadīyayā tad-vimanas tayāsīt*  
*svayaṁ dayā-vārinidhiḥ suduḥkhī \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Gauraṅga, the ocean of mercy, did not speak but went to his house. Distressed by Vanamālī’s words, he became unhappy.**

### Text 20

*āgatya gehaṁ jananīṁ tato’sau*  
*papraccha nāthaḥ stanayitnu-dhīram \|*  
*kim uktam ācārya-varāya mātas*  
*tvayā yato’sau vimanāḥ prayāti \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Coming to his house, he questioned his mother in a voice as deep as thunder. “Mother! What did you say to the best of ācāryas, so that he became unhappy?**

### Text 21

*kathaṁ na tasyānumatau matis te*  
*babhūva nāmoditam uktam asya \|*  
*prītir yathā syāt sujanasya sādhos*  
*tathaiva kartuṁ sujanaḥ pramāṇam \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**"Why did you not agree with him and accept what he said? Doing what will please the great devotee is proof of one’s good behavior.”**

### Text 22

*vijñāya putrānumatiṁ mudāsau*  
*prasthāpayāmāsa tad-ātma-lokam \|*  
*ācārya-varyānayanāya śīghraṁ*  
*niṣpādyate kiṁ na tad-icchayā yat \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding that her son agreed to the proposal, she became happy and immediately send a servant to Vanamālī. Why should his proposal not be accepted?**

### Text 23

*drutaṁ sa āgatya śacīṁ praṇamyā-*  
*vadat kim ājñāpayatīśvarī me \|*  
*vidhīyate’sau śirasā niyogo*  
*niyujyatāṁ tat tava kiṁ karomi \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Quickly Vanamālī came and offered respects to Śacī. He said, "O noble lady! I will do whatever you command, holding that order on my head. Please engage your servant. What can I do for you?”**

### Text 24

*vijñāpitaṁ yo’sti yad atra tāta*  
*tad eva kartuṁ tvam iha pramāṇam \|*  
*tvaṁ vatsalo’tīva suhṛt-kuṭumbaṁ*  
*snigdhaḥ svayaṁ cety atha sā jagāda \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She said, “What I will order, only you can do. You are affectionate, a friend, a family member, and gentle.”**

### Text 25

*tataḥ samākarṇya vacaḥ sa dhīraḥ*  
*svadhīta-sarvāgama eva tūrṇam \|*  
*śacīṁ namaskṛtya śucir jagāma*  
*vidhitsur etasya vivāha-kāryam \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing those words, the sober, pure brāhmaṇa, who had studied all the Vedas, quickly offered respects to Śacī and went to arrange for Gaurāṅga’s marriage.**

### Text 26

*sa-vallabhācārya-gṛhe’tiharṣāt*  
*jagāma kautūhala-pūrṇa-cetāḥ \|*  
*tūrṇaṁ vilokayainam asāv udasthāt*  
*pratyudgamo’grārcanam eva sādhoḥ \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With a happy heart, he went quickly to Vallabhācārya’s house. Seeing him, Vallabhācārya stood up to welcome Vanamālī, who was praising him.**

### Text 27

*sa vallabho bhūmisuraika-ratnaṁ*  
*dideśa tasmai varam āsanaṁ tat \|*  
*papraccha paścāc ca vinīta-ceṣṭaḥ*  
*sadaiva dhīro vinayena bhāti \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vallabha gave the order to offer the jewel among brāhmaṇas a fine seat. With polite behavior he then inquired from him. The sober person always shines with proper behavior.**

### Text 28

*anugraho’yaṁ mayi te babhūva*  
*sphuṭaṁ yad atrāgamanaṁ tvadīyam \|*  
*kāryaṁ kiyad vāpy avaśiṣyate tad*  
*vaktuṁ mahā-dhīs tvam iha pramāṇam \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“It is great mercy that you have come to my house. Please say what remains for me to do. O intelligent one! You are the proof of an intelligent person.”**

### Text 29

*itthaṁ niśamyāśu mahānubhāvaḥ*  
*prabhor vivāhe ghaṭanāṁ vidhitsuḥ \|*  
*uvāca harṣodgata-roma-vṛndaḥ*  
*śubha-svarāṁ vācam aninditātmā \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing Vallabhācārya’s words, the great, faultless devotee, his hairs standing on end, desiring to inform him of the Lord’s marriage proposal, spoke in a pleasant voice.**

### Text 30

*guṇair varo miśra-purandarātmajaḥ*  
*śarīravattām atanuḥ kim āśritaḥ \|*  
*ya eṣa saundaryamayīṁ tanūm imāṁ*  
*jagat-trayī-loka-vimohinīṁ śritaḥ \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The excellent son of Jagannātha Miśra is endowed with all good qualities. It seems that Cupid has taken a human form. He has a body which bewilders the three worlds with its beauty.**

### Text 31

*ya eṣa niṣṇātatayā tayā vidher*  
*vidhāna-dakṣasya vidhāna-karmaṇi \|*  
*vidhāya saundarya-samūham agrataḥ*  
*sudhāmayaḥ ko’py atanur vinirmame \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The expert creator, in carrying out creation, taking all beauty, produced this inconceivably beautiful body with great skill.**

### Text 32

*yad āsya-candraṁ vidhinā vidhāya taṁ*  
*cirāya bhūyiṣṭham ivātma-sauṣṭhavam \|*  
*vidāmbabhūva gadatā pratikṣaṇaṁ*  
*caturbhir āsyair api sādhu sādhv iti \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Making his moon-like face, the creator admired his own skill for a long time, saying repeatedly ‘Very good! Very good!’**

### Text 33

*ataḥ sutāyās tava yogya-vibhramaḥ*  
*sa kalpa-vallyā iva kalpa-bhūruhaḥ \|*  
*yogo’stu muktā-maṇirvaryayor iva*  
*priyākaraḥ sarva-jagaj-janasya saḥ \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Her beauty is suitable for him, just as desire creeper is suitable for a desire tree or a pearl is suitable for the best of jewels. The whole world will be pleased with this union.”**

### Text 34

*niśamya saumyo’tha sa vallabha-dvijo*  
*dvijaika-ratnaṁ tam uvāca harṣataḥ \|*  
*vicintya bhūyo manasā śubhaṁyunā*  
*sakhyena vikhyāta-yaśaḥ-samuccayaḥ \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, the gentle Vallabha, the sum total of all fame, spoke with joy, with a happy mind and affection.**

### Text 35

*bhāgyātibhāgyena mahānubhava me*  
*yogena tat samprati tena bhūyate \|*  
*tathāvidhasyāsya samaṁ tathāvidhair*  
*yathātathaṁ syād ghaṭanā manoramā \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O great soul! If this marriage can happen by good fortune, it is as if the impossible were to happen.**

### Text 36

*yadīśvaraḥ syān mayi suprasāda-bhāk*  
*bhāgyodayo vā yadi me mahān bhavet \|*  
*yad asti putryāḥ sukṛtaṁ mahattaraṁ*  
*tadedṛśaṁ tat patir eva niścayaḥ \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If the Lord is merciful to me, or if I have great fortune, if my daughter has good fortune, then certainly she could have such a husband.**

### Text 37

*yathā guṇaiḥ kāñcana-hāra-vallī*  
*ratnena san-nāyakatāṁ gatena \|*  
*niṣpannatāṁ yāti tathā tvadīyair*  
*guṇais tayoḥ saṅghaṭanā ghaṭeta \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“As a golden necklace becomes furnished with a great center jewel by means of strings (*****guṇaiḥ*****), by your good qualities (*****guṇaiḥ*****), the combination of these two can take place.”**

### Text 38

*ity ūcivāṁs tāṁ vinayoktavattayā*  
*tayā mahāprīta imaṁ jagāda saḥ \|*  
*tavedṛśā sad-vinayena satvaraṁ*  
*sampatsyate sarvam aśeṣa-maṅgalam \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When he spoke in this pleasing manner, Vanamālī was very pleased and said, “By your pleasing behavior all unlimited auspiciousness is accomplished for you.”**

### Text 39

*itthaṁ sa sambhāṣya mitho dvijāgro*  
*jagāda bhūyo nilayeṣu śacyāḥ \|*  
*nyavedayat sarvam adabhra-bhāgyo*  
*vivāha-kautūhala-lola-cittaḥ \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Speaking in this way, Vanamālī again went to Śacī’s house. With his heart intent on the marriage, the fortunate brāhmaṇa informed her of everything.**

### Text 40

*śacī tathā tat sakalaṁ viditvā*  
*harṣeṇa pūrṇām avidat tanūṁ svām \|*  
*vicintya mūrtiṁ nija-bhāgya-rāśiṁ*  
*tanūja-ratnaṁ nibhṛtaṁ jagāda \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding everything, she felt her body becoming full of bliss. Thinking herself most fortunate, she spoke to her son privately.**

### Text 41

*vivāha-māṅgalya-viśeṣa-dakṣiṇo*  
*vidhīyatāṁ tat-samayaḥ sukhāvahaḥ \|*  
*tadā tad ākarṇya sa citta-vṛttibhiś*  
*cakāra nāthaḥ kalanāṁ kalā-nidhiḥ \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You should fix the proper time which will be auspicious for the marriage.” Hearing this, the ocean of all arts made the calculation in his mind.**

### Text 42

*drayvāṇy adabhrāṇi manojña-vibhramo*  
*mātur nideśād aharat tadā rahaḥ \|*  
*cakāra kālaṁ śubha-lagna-bhūṣitaṁ*  
*so’yaṁ tadā kiṁ svayam eva bhūṣitaḥ \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Directed by his mother, the attractive, beautiful Gaurāṅga privately collected countless ingredients for the marriage. He fixed the date with an auspicious ascendant. That time seemed to be decorated with all auspiciousness by itself.**

### Text 43

*mṛdaṅga-cāru-dhvani-bhaṅgi-saṅgī*  
*saṅgīta-kolāhala ucchrito’bhūt \|*  
*tathaiva tatrātiśayo garīyān*  
*nṛtyodgamo harṣita-nartakānām \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The time resounded loudly with sweet mṛdaṅgas and music. Joyful dancers began dancing with great intensity.**

### Text 44

*bhūdeva-veda-dhvanibhiḥ samantān*  
*mṛdaṅga-nādair jaya-nāda-miśraiḥ \|*  
*sa-candanair āguravaiḥ pradhūpair*  
*auśīravadbhiḥ sa rarāja kālaḥ \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That time was glorious with sounds of brāhmaṇas chanting the Vedas, mṛdaṅgas sounding along with shouts of “Jaya,” and incense made of āguru, uśīra and sandalwood.**

### Text 45

*ūrvī-sad-ūrvī-sura-ratna-dattāṁ*  
*jājvalyamānām adhivāsa-lakṣmīm \|*  
*āsādya bhāti sma sa-rohiṇīko*  
*yathā sudhā-raśmir athaiṣa nāthaḥ \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having attained the glorious adhivāsa ceremony of Lakṣmī performed by the best of brāhmaṇas, the Lord shone like the moon with Rohiṇī constellation.**

### Text 46

*tato dvijebhyaḥ pradaduḥ prakāmaṁ*  
*tāmbūla-mālyāny api candanāni \|*  
*rejus tathā te sakalā mahāntaḥ*  
*smerānanā harṣa-samudra-magnāḥ \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He then gave to the brāhmaṇas betel nut, garlands and sandalwood in plenty. The great brāhmaṇas, immersed in an ocean of joy, shone with smiling faces.**

### Text 47

*sa vallabho’bhyetya tadā sad-urvī-*  
*gīrvāṇa-ratnair dvija-sundarībhiḥ \|*  
*mahāprabhor gandha-sugandhi-mālyaiḥ*  
*śubhādhivāsaṁ vidadhe vidhijñaḥ \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vallabha, knowledgeable of the rules, then performed the adhivāsa of Mahāprabhu, with the assistance of brāhmaṇas, using perfumed garlands and scents.**

### Text 48

*atha prabhāte vimalārka-bhūṣite*  
*svayaṁ kṛta-snāna-vidhir yathā-vidhi \|*  
*prabhuḥ pitṝn arcayituṁ yathā tathā*  
*nāndīmukha-śrāddham athākarod asau \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the dawn, ornamented with the spotless sun, after bathing according to the rules, the Lord performed nāndīmukha-śrāddha to worship the forefathers according to scriptural rules.**

### Text 49

*tato dvijāti-śruti-pāṭhanadyair*  
*mṛdaṅga-nādaiḥ paṇava-svanaiś ca \|*  
*varāṅganā-vaktra-vinirgatais tair*  
*ulūla-śabdais tumulo maho’bhūt \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There was a great uproar with brāhmaṇas chanting the Vedas, with mṛdaṅgas and paṇavas sounding, and with women uttering auspicious sounds.**

### Text 50

*śacī dvijānāṁ mahilā yathāyathaṁ*  
*tat-tat-saparyā-grahilās tadāvadat \|*  
*alaṁ mayā bhartṛ-padābja-hīnayā*  
*kartavyam etad bhavatībhir eva hi \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śacī spoke to the wives of brāhmaṇas who had been properly worshipped. “I am unfortunately without a husband. You should perform the rites.”**

### Text 51

*sa mātur itthaṁ karuṇoditaṁ prabhur*  
*niśamya tāta-smṛti-duḥkha-vihvalaḥ \|*  
*muktā-phala-sthūla-vilocanāmbhasāṁ*  
*bindūn uvāha pravaroru-vakṣasi \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing these pitiful words of his mother, the Lord became filled with sorrow on remembering his father. From his eyes he shed tears, like heavy pearls, which flowed on his eminent chest.**

### Text 52

*tathāvidhaṁ tat-samaye vilokya sā*  
*sutaṁ sudīnāha sahāṅganā-gaṇaiḥ \|*  
*pituḥ kathaṁ maṅgala-karma kurvatā*  
*vimucyate vāri dṛśor amaṅgalam \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing her son in that condition, Śacī became sad and with the other women said, “Having done auspicious rites for your father, why do you cry inauspiciously?”**

### Text 53

*sa mātur itthaṁ vacanena nātho*  
*drāghīyasā niśvasitena tena \|*  
*mlānoru-vakṣāḥ karuṇaṁ babhāṣe*  
*prabhāta-candra-pratimāsya-candraḥ \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the words of his mother, the Lord released sighs and his chest withered. With face pale like the moon in the dawn, he spoke.**

### Text 54

*dhanāni kiṁ vā manujā na santi me*  
*yenedṛśaṁ mātar udīritaṁ vacaḥ \|*  
*tvayādya dainyena parāśrayāgraho*  
*vidhīyate kiṁ vada duḥkha-taptayā \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Do I not have wealth or followers? Why do you speak such words? Out of misery, why do you speak, lamenting and longing for other’s property?**

### Text 55

*tvayaiva dṛṣṭaṁ dvija-sajjanebhyaḥ*  
*prakāmam ṛkthaṁ rabhasād vikīrṇaḥ \|*  
*tāmbūla-mālyāni ca gandhavanti*  
*prakarṣato’laṅkaraṇāṁśukāni \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You have seen that I distributed wealth, betel, garlands, sandalwood, ornaments and cloth profusely to the brāhmaṇas.**

### Text 56

*pitrāpi hīno’ham akuṇṭha-śaktiḥ*  
*kiṁ mātar itthaṁ purato mamoktam \|*  
*amartya-kāryeṣu sadaiva śaktās*  
*tathāpi yal laukikam eva kurmaḥ \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Though without a father, I have unlimited powers. Why do you speak in this way in front of me? I have powers concerning spiritual matters, what to speak of material affairs.”**

### Text 57

*itīritaṁ tasya niśamya mātā*  
*taṁ sāntvayitvā madhurair vacobhiḥ \|*  
*sa-candanair āguruvānulepair*  
*lilepa vakṣaḥ-sthalam ātmajasya \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing him speak in this way, his mother pacified him with sweet words. She applied sandalwood and āguru to his chest.**

### Text 58

*trailokya-mādhurya-mayārya-kāntiḥ*  
*prasūna-mālyābharaṇānulepaiḥ \|*  
*vibhūṣitaḥ smera-mukho vireje*  
*saundarya-lakṣmy eva vṛtaḥ svayaṁ saḥ \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having the sweetest beauty in the three worlds, ornamented with flower garlands and unguents, with a smiling face, he appeared to be surrounded by a wealth of beauty.**

### Text 59

*tasmin kṣaṇe vallabha-bhūmidevaḥ*  
*samāpya kāryaṁ pitṛ-devatānām \|*  
*vibhūṣayāmāsa vibhūṣitāṅgāṁ*  
*sutām alaṅkāra-kulair mahārghyaiḥ \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The brāhmaṇa Vallabha completed the worship of forefathers and devatās and then decorated his daughter, who had natural beauty, with valuable ornaments.**

### Text 60

*varasya saundarya-bhṛtāṁ varasya*  
*dvijās tato’syānayanāya jagmuḥ \|*  
*sampreṣiṭās tena tatas tadaiva*  
*śubhasvarāṁ vācam amandam ūcuḥ \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The brāhmaṇas then went to bring the best of grooms, possessing the greatest beauty. Sent by Vallabha, they slowly spoke words in a beautiful voice.**

### Text 61

*vidhīyatāṁ samprati vatsa yātrā*  
*panthāna ete śubhadā bhavantu \|*  
*athaiṣa bandhu-dvija-sajjanādyair*  
*dolām adhiśritya yayau prasannaḥ \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Please make the journey. There will be auspicious chanting on the way.” He got in the palanquin and went happily with his friends, brāhmaṇas and other virtuous people.**

### Text 62

*pradīpta-dīpāvalibhir viśiṣṭaṁ*  
*tasyāviśat sad-bhavanaṁ manojñam \|*  
*tato’bhigamyālaya-madhyam evaṁ*  
*nināya vipro nija-bhāgya-rāśim \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He arrived at Vallabha’s attractive house, lit with rows of lamps. Vallabha welcomed him, and brought the Lord, who brought auspiciousness to Vallabha, into the house.**

### Text 63

*pādyādinā taṁ varayāmbabhūva sa*  
*dvijo navadvīpa-mahauṣadhīśvaram \|*  
*babhau vṛtas tena mahāprabhus tadā*  
*trailokya-lakṣmī-lalitāṁ tanuṁ śritaḥ \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He welcomed the Lord, the great remedy born in Navadvīpa, with foot wash and other items. Asked to marry his daughter, he shone brightly, as if his body had given shelter to the beauty of the three worlds.**

### Text 64

*sa gauracandraḥ kaṇakāṅgadābhir*  
*virājamānoru-bhujāntaraḥ svayam \|*  
*kalpa-druma-śrī-rucirasya vibhramaṁ*  
*jahāra hārī tapanīya-bhūbhṛtaḥ \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His broad arms shining with golden arm bands, Gauracandra stole the beauty of an attractive desire tree or an attractive golden mountain.**

### Text 65

*sutāṁ samānīya śaran-niśāpater*  
*jyotsnām iva snāpita-dig-vadhū-gaṇām \|*  
*prabhāva-nidhvasta-tamisra-sañcayāṁ*  
*svalaṅkṛtāṁ tāṁ prabhave dadau dvijaḥ \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vallabha brought his beautifully ornamented daughter, who destroyed all darkness and bathed all directions in her effulgence which resembled the light of the autumn moon, and gave her to the Lord.**

### Text 66

*cirāya sā labdha-phalaṁ manorathaṁ*  
*vilokya bālā caraṇāmbujaṁ prabhoḥ \|*  
*samāśritā dīptim uvāha bhūyasīṁ*  
*saundarya-lakṣmīr iva sā svayaṁvarā \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the Lord as the fulfilment of her desire after a long time, the young girl took shelter of the Lord’s lotus feet and shone like the Lakṣmī of beauty at her svayaṁvarā ceremony.**

### Text 67

*parasparaṁ tau sumanaḥ-samūhau*  
*vicakratuḥ prema-rasena sārdham \|*  
*tayor abhikṣā samam āvirāsīt*  
*tadaiva citrā-śaśinor ivāsau \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The two together, the sum total of beauty, shone with the rasa of prema. They were like the moon and Citra constellation.**

### Text 68

*athopaviśya prabhave pradātuṁ*  
*sutāṁ dvijo’sau vidhinā vidhijñaḥ \|*  
*varāya pādyaṁ vinivedya hṛdyaṁ*  
*hṛdi-sthitaṁ prema-vilocanābhyām \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sitting down, Vallabha, knowing the rules, offered his daughter to the Lord according to the rules. He offered the groom foot water consisting of heart felt affection by his eyes filled with prema.**

### Text 69

*tam arghyam arghyaṁ madhuparka-bhūṣitaṁ*  
*sa-viṣṭaraṁ sundaram āsanaṁ tataḥ \|*  
*krameṇa tasmai mahanīya-mūrtaye*  
*dadau varasya pravarāya śuddha-dhīḥ \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He offered a chair and the best arghya ornamented with madhuparka to the ideal groom, worthy of worship.**

### Text 70

*dattvā tanūjāṁ mahitāya tasmai*  
*babhāra harṣaṁ sadṛśaṁ samutsukaḥ \|*  
*imāni cāsau manasi prakāmaṁ*  
*bahir bibhedātha tanūruheṣu \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With happiness in giving his daughter to the great groom, he developed such joy that the joy became externalized as his hairs standing on end.**

### Text 71

*tato nivṛtte mahite mahotsave*  
*priyāṁ samādāya kṛpā-mahāmbudhiḥ \|*  
*rarāja rājan-mukha-padma-vibhramo*  
*yathā śaśī candrikayā samanvitaḥ \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the celebrations concluded, the ocean of mercy, accepting his beloved, radiating beauty from his glowing lotus face, shone like the moon with its light.**

### Text 72

*viśvambharo viśvajanāya kautukaṁ*  
*vikīrya viśvārti-bharair mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*lakṣmīṁ samādāya śarīriṇīṁ śriyaṁ*  
*saundarya-sārasya jagāma veśmani \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu or Viśvambhara, distributing joy to the people of the universe, accepting Lakṣmī, the most beautiful of forms endowed with the essence of beauty, along with the universe’s sufferings, returned to his house.**

### Text 73

*dvijāṅganānām atha sañcayaiḥ sā*  
*śacī sutodvāha-sukhair anekaiḥ \|*  
*asphūrtimattāṁ dhiyam etya gehe*  
*praveśayāmāsa vadhūṁ sutaṁ ca \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With all the brāhmaṇas’ wives, Śacī, happy on the occasion of her son’s marriage, came to receive the bride and groom who hid their emotions and brought them into the house.**

### Text 74

*dattvā dvijebhyo bahudhaiva harṣitā*

*samāpti-kṛtyā hi mahājanodyamā \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Joyfully giving the brāhmaṇas wealth, cloth and sandalwood, Śacī experienced the highest bliss. The endeavors of the great reach a perfect conclusion.**

### Text 75

*vasan sa itthaṁ nija-mandire prabhur*  
*mumoda lakṣmyā saha kāntayā tayā \|*  
*sadā jananyā paricintita-kriyo*  
*gṛhastha-dharmaṁ sad-udāram āvahan \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Living in his house with his beloved Lakṣmī, the Lord experienced bliss. Alert to follow whatever his mother requested, he observed all the rules of household life.**

### Text 76

*kāntāṅga-saṅgāmṛta-dhārayā tayā-*  
*bhiṣecayantī hṛdayeśaya-drumam \|*  
*mano’bhilāṣa-stavakoccayaṁ sukha-*  
*prasūna-vṛndaṁ virarāja sā bhṛśam \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sprinkling the desire tree lying in her heart with streams of nectar arising from his association, Lakṣmī shone with groups of flowers of joy and bud clusters of desires.**

### Text 77

*uras tarāgasya kim abja-korakau*  
*manohare hāra-latā-phale kim u \|*

*manoja-dantāvala-kumbhakau kim u \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Were there two lotuses on the lake of her chest? Were there two fruits nestled on an attractive creeper of her necklace? Were there two cakravāka birds floating on an ocean of beauty? Were there two protrusions on the head of Cupid’s elephant?**

### Text 78

*vidhor nijaṁ sauṣṭhavam unninīṣato*

**Were her breasts the creation of Brahmā who desired to display his skill? Were they two gold pots? Were they two new caskets? Endowed with these breasts, she gave joy to the Lord.**

### Text 79

*mukhena mandākṣara-vinamra-cakṣuṣā*  
*smitānupṛktena sadyoṣṭha-rociṣā \|*

*mano’dhirājasya mano jahāra sā \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She attracted his mind with her face endowed with soft speech and downcast eyes, her slight smile, her red lips, and smiling cheeks, pale like madhuka flowers.**

### Text 80

*susīma-bhājā stana-korakeṇa sā*

*nitambinā sajjaghanena sundarī*  
*sadā manonātha-manaḥ samādade \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the part in her hair, by her bud like breasts, by her slender waist with three lines, and by her hips and buttocks, she attracted the mind of her beloved.**

### Text 81

*tad-aṅga-saṁsarga-sudhāmbu-rāśeḥ*  
*pravāha-saṅgāhana-śītalasya \|*  
*lāvaṇyam atyanta-nitānta-kāntaṁ*  
*babhūva gaurāṅga-mahāprabhos tataḥ \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The beauty of Gaurāṅga, a combination of cool waves from the ocean of nectar derived from his beloved’s association, became extremely pleasing.**

### Text 82

*itthaṁ kiyanty atra dināni nātho*  
*nītvā kṛpāyai karuṇaika-sinduḥ \|*  
*yayau maghono diśi sajjanaughaiḥ*  
*sārdhaṁ samṛddhair nija-sat-kṛpābhiḥ \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Passing some days in this manner, the Lord, an ocean of mercy, out of mercy went to the east in the company of honest and wealthy men who had attained his mercy.**

### Text 83

*sa yatra yatra prabhur udgato’bhūd*

**Перевод:**

**Wherever the Lord went, with his extraordinary beauty, equal to a hundred moons, people became enchanted with his sweet form.**

### Text 84

*lāvaṇya-pīyūṣa-nidhau manuṣyā*  
*vilokya vaktrendum adṛṣṭa-pūrvam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing that extraordinary moon-like face in an ocean of sweet beauty, people drank it a hundred times with their eyes and could not be fully satisfied.**

### Text 85

*parasparaṁ te kathayāmbabhūvuḥ*  
*ka eṣa kasyaiṣa mahānubhāvaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**They talked among themselves. “Who is this? Whose son is he? Which fortunate woman gave birth to this youth by great pious acts?**

### Text 86

*vidhāya lakṣmīṁ dviguṇāṁ svakīyām \|*

*na gocaraḥ kutracid evam eṣaḥ \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“He is Cupid, with beauty twice the amount. We have not seen or heard about him anywhere.”**

### Text 87

*striyas tathocur nayanotpalābhyāṁ*

**Перевод:**

**The women spoke, drinking the nectar of his face with their lotus eyes. “This amazing person continually defeats the pride of Cupid completely by the beauty of his limbs.**

### Text 88

*saubhāgya-rāśeḥ katareva vallī*

**Перевод:**

**“What an amazing creeper of abundant beauty! What an incomparable pastime for this person full of pastimes! This creation is most astonishing. The creator created Rati for Cupid, but what woman would not criticize Rati (for marrying Cupid)?”**

### Text 89

*yan-nāma-mātra-śravaṇena dehinas*

**Перевод:**

**Just hearing his name, people crossed the fierce ocean of saṁsāra. He was personally visible to their eyes. Who can describe the mercy of Mahāprabhu?**

### Text 90

*padmāvatīṁ dvīpavatīṁ kṛpāvān*

**Merciful Gaurāṅga made the Padmāvatī River along with its islands auspicious by bathing there. Worshipped on its banks by devotees, Mahāprabhu remained there with a desire.**

### Text 91

*mahadbhir uccaiḥ pulinaiḥ suśobhais*

**The Padmāvatī river shining with beautiful, high banks, having performed austerities, immediately became equivalent to the Gaṅgā by touching the sweetness of his limbs.**

### Text 92

**The river, like a beauatiful woman, expressed her joy by making her waves into hands, śaphari fish into eyes, and her banks into hips.**

### Text 93

**Walking like an intoxicated king of elephants, performing pastimes that attracted the universe, endowed with lotus eyes, he created happiness for all great souls having pious credits.**

### Text 94

### Text 95

**His wife in her house thought only of the Lord of her life. She engaged in service to her mother-in-law, cleaning the house and massage her lotus feet.**

### Text 96

*nirantaraṁ prāṇapateḥ samāgamaṁ*

### Text 97

*suśītalābhiḥ śuci śīlatābhir*

**Śacī, by her calm demeanor, her sweet words and her gentleness, considered her to be another Lakṣmī and a second body of her son.**

### Text 98

### Text 99

*vijñāya kālād yathā-vihāriṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding after some time by her own power of mind the mind of her husband, who was wandering at will, she became pained by separation and made up her mind to overcome that sorrow.**

### Text 100

*daivād atho mandira-madhyam āgataś*

**Перевод:**

**By chance a cruel, low snake entered her house. With cruel intention, it bit her foot, fragrant as an autumn lotus, with its hard teeth.**

### Text 101

*tathā-vidhāṁ tām avalokya duḥkhitā*

*tathā prasaṅgottama-sañcayān asau*
