**Перевод:**

**Seeng her in that condition, Śacī, in sorrow, sought to counteract the poison. Affectionate to her, with care she brought many doctors.**

### Text 102

*anekadhā tair vihitāḥ prakārāḥ*  
*viṣasya dūrīkaraṇāya naiva \|*  
*śekus tadā daiva-kṛtaṁ viditvā*  
*mohaṁ samīyur vikalāś ca sarve \|\|102\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Using many methods, they could not counteract the poison. Understanding that this was an act of fate, they all lamented and fainted.**

### Text 103

*tad īśvareṇeritam eva matvā*  
*vadhūṁ vadhū-sneha-kṛśā kṛśāṅgīm \|*  
*gaṅgā-taṭe’nyām iva tatra gaṅgāṁ*  
*nināya dhanyām atiduḥkha-dagdhā \|\|103\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding it was an act of the Lord, Śacī, thin from affection for her, in great lamentation brought the fortunate daughter-in-law, like another Gaṅgā, to the bank of the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 104

*tato vimāne divi rājamāne*  
*prasūna-varṣair diviṣadbhir āptaiḥ \|*  
*patyuḥ padābjaṁ hṛdi gāḍham eṣā*  
*tataḥ pariṣvajya jahau tanūṁ svām \|\|104\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While the devatās arriving in the sky in vehicles showered flowers, Lakṣmī tightly embraced lotus feet of her husband in her heart and gave up her body.**

### Text 105

*tato’ṅkam āropya suduḥkhitā śacī*  
*vadhūṁ vimugdhā rudatī vilāpinī \|*  
*jagāda kṛcchrād vacasā garīyasā*  
*kṣobheṇa śokena ca gadgada-svaram \|\|105\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lamenting Śacī took the body of her daughter-in-law in her lap and wept in grief. With difficulty, in a choked voice, she uttered many words filled with sorrow and pain.**

### Text 106

*gataḥ suto me bhavatīṁ samarpya*  
*priyas tavāsau mayi duḥkha-bhāji \|*  
*hīnātvayātasya mukhaṁ kathaṁ vā*  
*drakṣyāmi duḥkhaika-nivāsa-bhūmiḥ \|\|106\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“My dear son left, leaving you in my protection, since I was suffering. How can I see his face without your presence? I am the abode of the greatest grief.**

### Text 107

*tvayā kṛtā prītir atīva gauravaṁ*  
*nirantaraṁ yatra viśeṣa-bhaktayā \|*  
*kathaṁ tv idānīṁ paridevanānvito*  
*vilokyate na kṣaṇam apy ayaṁ janaḥ \|\|107\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You had such constant affection and respect for me, with great devotion. Why do you now not look for one moment at me, overcome with grief?**

### Text 108

*āhūta-mātraiva mayā dadāsi*  
*praharṣa-bhīti-smita-bhakti-lajjam \|*  
*pratyuttaraṁ hanta kathaṁ tv idānīṁ*  
*na bhāṣase māṁ rudatīṁ sa-śokām \|\|108\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“On being called by me, you would answer, showing joy, fear, smiles, devotion and shyness. Why do you not speak to me, as I weep in sorrow?**

### Text 109

*yad vā mayi prīti-lavo’pi nāsti te*  
*babhūva daivena yad īdṛśī gatiḥ \|*  
*amuṁ tava prāṇa-patiṁ mamātmajaṁ*  
*na vīkṣya kiṁ vā vrajasi priyaṁvade \|\|109\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Perahps you do not have a particle of affection for me. But going in this manner by fate, dear woman who spoke so sweetly, without looking at your dear husband, my son, why did you leave?**

### Text 110

*asau tava prānapatiḥ priyaṅkaro*  
*nirantaraṁ prema-nava-prakāśinī \|*  
*amuṁ prati prīti-lavo’pi nāsti te*  
*kiṁ mātar itthaṁ kriyate yatas tvayā \|\|110\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Your husband had great affection for you at all times. O revealer of fresh love! O mother! Do you not have a speck of love for him, to act in this manner?**

### Text 111

*nirantaraṁ yā gamanāya patyur*  
*vicintayantī tvam udaśru subhru \|*  
*vilokya māṁ sādhvasam apūrvam āsīḥ*  
*salajjam aśrūṇy apasārayantī \|\|111\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O beautiful browed Lakṣmī, with tears in your eyes! Thinking of your husband’s return, on seeing me, you would become frightened and shy, shedding tears.**

### Text 112

*yā tvaṁ trapāyai mayi sādhvasāya*  
*svajīviteśasya viyoga-duḥkham \|*  
*dattvā bahiś cetasi tapyamānā*  
*lajjāvatī pratyaham evam āsīḥ \|\|112\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Being shy and frightened of me, grieving in separation from the lord of your life, you remained internally and externally pained and shy.**

### Text 113

*sā tvaṁ tadīyāsya-sudhā-mayūkhaṁ*  
*tavaiva cetaḥ-kumudaika-kāntam \|*  
*kaṭhora-citte tam avīkṣya sākṣāt*  
*kathaṁ kuto vā vrajasi prasahya \|\|113\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O hard-hearted Lakṣmī! Not seeing your husband with his moon-like face, dear to the lotus of your heart, why did you suddenly go away? Where did you go?**

### Text 114

*kathaṁ mahākrūra-mate vihāya māṁ*  
*svabhāva-mṛdvī bhavatā vadhūr iyam \|*  
*adaṁśi sarpa kṣaṇam apy asau dayā*  
*tvām eva pasparśa na sāmprataṁ nanu \|\|114\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O cruel snake! Why did you avoid me and bite my daughter-in-law endowed with a gentle nature? Compassion did not affect you even for a moment.**

### Text 115

*yad aṅgam etat kusumaiḥ sudūyate*  
*bāṣpoṣmaṇā cāpi śirīṣa-komalam \|*  
*kathaṁ nu vā te’sahatāti-duḥsahaṁ*  
*viṣāgni-tejas tad idaṁ hatāsmi tat \|\|115\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Her limbs, soft as the śirīṣa flower, could not endure the touch of a flower or the heat of tears. How could she endure the fire of your poison, impossible to tolerate? I am most unfortunate.”**

### Text 116

*itthaṁ sudīnā vilapanty anukṣaṇaṁ*  
*vilocana-dvandva-jalena bhūyasā \|*  
*cakāra sā kṣālitam eva santataṁ*  
*snehena vadhvā vadanendu-maṇḍalam \|\|116\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In a miserable condition, lamenting continually, she constantly bathed the moon-like face of Lakṣmī in her profuse tears out of affection.**

### Text 117

*samāpya kṛcchreṇa citocitāḥ kriyāḥ*  
*gṛhaṁ yayau rodanam eva kurvatī \|*  
*kathaṁ vapu-śūnyam avekṣayate gṛhaṁ*  
*tanūja-ratnaṁ ca tatheti-duḥkhitā \|\|117\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Completing the funeral rites with difficulty, she returned home while weeping. She lamented, “How will my jewel of a son see this house devoid of his wife?”**

### Text 118

*athāgato gaura-sudhā-mayūkhaḥ*  
*kiyad-dināntaram eva gehe \|*  
*nistārya tatratya-janān ajasraṁ*  
*sva-mātṛ-duḥkhāny apahartu-kāmaḥ \|\|118\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra, desiring to relieve his mother of sorrow, after some days returned home, after delivering many people where he had resided.**

### Text 119

*vilokya harṣaṁ na tathāvidhaṁ sā*  
*sutaṁ ciraṁ proṣitam apy agacchat \|*  
*vadhū-viyogena suduḥsahena*  
*tadā yad ādhikyam anena bheje \|\|119\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She did not express joy on seeing her son return after a long time. Rather she was more affected by the intolerable loss of his wife.**

### Text 120

*vidhāya bhūyo bhuvi daṇḍavan-natiṁ*  
*rajaḥ samādāya pada-dvayasya \|*  
*tathāvidhāṁ tām avalokya duḥkhitāṁ*  
*papraccha nātho manasā vadann api \|\|120\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After repeatedly offering respects on the ground, taking the dust of her feet, he saw that she was lamenting. He asked her the reason, though he knew in his mind the facts.**

### Text 121

*svakīya-vāṇī-sudhayāvagāhayann*  
*ayaṁ jananyāḥ sakalāṁ tanuṁ tataḥ \|*  
*jagāda mātar malineva lakṣyase*  
*kathaṁ tvam evaṁ nanu kathyatām iti \|\|121\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bathing his mother’s body in his sweet words, he said, “O mother! You appear to be withered. Why are you in this condition? Please tell me.”**

### Text 122

*itthaṁ samastaṁ bubudhe mahāprabhus*  
*tad apy anuktaṁ sahasā hasan muhuḥ \|*  
*tadīya-netra-dvaya-nirbharodgataiḥ*  
*payobhir ākhyānitam eva sākṣāt \|\|122\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu understood everything. He smiled constantly. Though she said nothing, the story was clearly told by the tears gushing from her eyes.**

### Text 123

*vadhūs tavāsau para-lokam āgatā*  
*mātas tad atrāsti mahad dhi kāraṇam \|*

*kasyāpi hetoḥ pṛthivī samāgatā \|\|123\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Your daughter-in-law has gone to another world. O mother! There is a good reason for this. She is not human. She came to earth with a purpose.**

### Text 124

*ahaṁ hi jānāmi tad etad asyā*  
*yat kāraṇaṁ bhūmim upāgatāyāḥ \|*  
*tathāgatāyāś samastam eva*  
*tat tyajyatāṁ mātar iha pramohaḥ \|\|124\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I know why she came to earth and why she left. O mother! Give up your lamentation.”**

### Text 125

*itthaṁ niśamyāśu vacaḥ sutasya*  
*śacī yayau nirvṛtim uttamāṁ sā \|*  
*nananda putreṇa samaṁ tathānyaiḥ*  
*sva-bandhubhiḥ svair vibhavaiḥ śacīva \|\|125\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the words of her son, Śacī was overjoyed. Happily she passed her time with her son, his friends, and her wealth, like Śacī, the wife of Indra.**

### Text 126

*tato’tivelaṁ manasā vicintya*  
*tanūja-ratnasya vivāha-kāryam \|*  
*samānayāmāsa tadaiva kāśī-*  
*nāthaṁ dvija-śreṣṭham adīna-sattvā \|\|126\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She began to think of another marriage for her son with great intensity. Thus she joyfully invited Kāśīnātha, a good brāhmaṇa to her house.**

### Text 127

*ānīya taṁ kṣipram uvāca vipraṁ*  
*tad-ātmajodvāha-vidhiṁ vivitsuḥ \|*  
*samucyatāṁ mat-tanujāya kanyāṁ*  
*sanātano vipra-varaḥ pradātum \|\|127\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bringing him, she spoke to him, desiring to know how to get her son married. “Sanātana, the best of brāhmaṇas, should give his daughter to my son.”**

### Text 128

*itthaṁ niśamyaiṣa vacāṁsi vipraḥ*  
*kṣipraṁ pramodena sanātanāya \|*  
*nyavedayan māṅgalikaṁ vidhitsur*  
*vaivāhikaṁ tat sakalaṁ vidhijñaḥ \|\|128\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing these words, Kāśīnātha, knowing all the rules, quickly went in joy to request to Sanātana the auspicious marriage.**

### Text 129

*tadā tadākarṇya vaco vimṛśya*

*nirṇīyatāṁ kāla idaṁ vidheyam \|\|129\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sanātana, hearing everything, considered the words with his friends and, thinking that the marriage should take place, spoke in joy, “Please decide the time.”**

### Text 130

*niśamya sarvaṁ vacanaṁ sa vipraḥ*  
*sukhena śīghraṁ samupetya śacyai \|*

*tutoṣa sānandam amanda-bhāgyā \|\|130\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing these words, Kāśīnātha, in joy quickly went to Śacī and informed her. Hearing the good news, she was completely satisfied with her good fortune.**

### Text 131

*sanātanena prahito’tha kaścit*  
*sametya tāṁ tatra jagāda natvā \|*

*sa yācate te tanayāya dātum \|\|131\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A messenger sent by Sanātana arrived, and, offering respects, spoke to her. “Sanātana requests to give his beautiful daughter endowed with excellent qualities and form to your son.**

### Text 132

*viṣṇupriyāṁ prāpya tavātmajaḥ priyāṁ*

*umā-maheśāv iva tau parasparam \|\|132\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If dear Viṣṇupriyā marries your son, her name will be successful. They should endure like Śiva and Parvatī in a happy marriage.”**

### Text 133

*gatvā sa sarvaṁ dvija-puṅgavāya*

**Перевод:**

**The messenger went to Sanātana and related Śacī’s consent. The learned Sanātana manifested great joy.**

### Text 134

*dravyāṇi bhadrāṇi sa śuddha-kīrtiḥ*

*vidhātukāmo mumude sutāyāḥ \|\|134\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sanātana, of pure fame, gathered the auspicious ingredients with a joyful heart. Quickly determining the time for the adhivāsa ceremony of his daughter, he became happy.**

### Text 135

*śubhena lagnena vibhūṣite tataḥ*  
*prakāśamāne samaye samantataḥ \|*  
*śubhādhivāsaṁ vidadhe mahāmatir*  
*mahādhiyām āptaphalā manorathāḥ \|\|135\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the auspicious ascendant arrived, the intelligent Sanātana performed the auspicious adhivāsa ceremony. The desires of the intelligent are always fulfilled.**

### Text 136

*tato dadau bhūsura-puṅgavebhyas*  
*tāmbūla-mālyāni sa-candanāni \|*  
*sampreṣitais tair api kārayitvā*  
*jāmātur agre mudito’dhivāsam \|\|136\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He gave betel, garlands and sandalwood to the best of brāhmaṇas. He sent these brāhmaṇas to Śacī’s house and had them first perform the adhivāsa of the groom.**

### Text 137

*atha prabhāte prabhur āhnikīṁ kriyāṁ*

*harṣād alaṁcakrur alaṁ prasādhanaiḥ \|\|137\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having bathed in Gaṅgā water, the Lord performed his daily rites. Shortly after, the brāhmaṇas decorated him in joy with ornaments.**

### Text 138

*ūcuś ca sādho vijayasva sādhu*  
*sādhur vivāhasya babhūva kālaḥ \|*  
*itthaṁ niśamyāracayat kṛpālur*  
*yātrāṁ samāruhya manojña-dolām \|\|138\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They said, “The auspicious time for marriage has come.” Hearing this, the merciful Lord mounted the attractive palanquin and made the journey to the bride’s place.**

### Text 139

*santapta-cāmīkara-gaura-deho*  
*dolām upetaḥ śarad-abhra-śubhrām \|*  
*dugdhāmbu-rāśer upari prarūḍhaṁ*  
*śṛṅgaṁ sumeroḥ sa jigāya sadyaḥ \|\|139\|*

**Перевод:**

**With a complexion like molten gold, he mounted the palanquin white as an autumn cloud. He defeated the peak of Sumeru towering above the Milk Ocean.**

### Text 140

*jāmātaraṁ vīkṣya samīpam āgataṁ*  
*prodgamya harṣeṇa tanūruhaiḥ samam \|*  
*pādyāsanādyair varayām babhūva*  
*kṣaṇena kanyāṁ ca dadau sa kūkudaḥ \|\|140\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the groom arrive, Sanātana came to welcome him with hairs standing on end. He accepted the groom with foot water and a seat. Quickly he offered his daughter.**

### Text 141

*dvija-striyaḥ svastika-dhūpa-dīpair*  
*amuṣya nirmañchanam ādareṇa \|*  
*cakruḥ samānīya tataḥ sa kanyāṁ*  
*prādāt dvijas tasya padāmbujebhyaḥ \|\|141\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wives of the brāhmaṇas performed ārātrika with svastikas, incense and lamps. Sanātana brought his daughter and offered her to Gauraṅga’s lotus feet.**

### Text 142

*unmīlat-paṭu-paṭaha-prakṛṣṭa-ḍakkā-*

*śrīmadbhir jaya-ninadaiḥ prasūna-vṛṣṭyā*  
*rejāte smita-sumukhau parasparaṁ tau \|\|142\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The smiling couple shone brightly with the tumult of various drums, shouts of “Jaya” and showers of flowers.**

### Text 143

*ity evaṁ gṛham anayat vadhūṁ mahadbhir*

*strī-ratnaṁ mudita-manāḥ śacī sva-geham \|\|143\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With music and shouts of “Jaya” Śacī brought the bride to the house and in joy had the jewel among women enter the house.**

## Chapter Four

### Text 1

*atha kṛpā-rasa-vāri-nidhīndunā*  
*svajana-mānasa-kairava-bandhunā \|*  
*dayitayā saha tatra virājitā*  
*nija-gṛhe jagṛhe gṛha-medhitā \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu, the moon in the ocean of mercy, friend of the lotuses of his followers’ minds, lived in household life with his wife.**

### Text 2

*druta-suvarṇa-suvarṇa-rucaḥ śucer*  
*madhura-komala-śītala-vibhramaḥ \|*  
*śriyam asau madhurām atisundarīm*  
*avapuṣo vapuṣo’nurucāharat \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With a complexion of attractive molten gold, his beauty was cool, tender and sweet. It defeated the attractive, sweet beauty of Cupid.**

### Text 3

*amūm avekṣya hṛdā hṛdayeśayaḥ*  
*sapadi niścitam ittham amanyata \|*  
*imam ṛte mama manmathatā janair*  
*anukathaṁ nu kathaṁ na hasiṣyate \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Cupid, seeing him, immediately concluded that his own beauty would be mocked by men repeatedly in comparison to his beauty.**

### Text 4

*nija-padābja-rasair atiśītalair*  
*jagad apḹrayad ātta-kṛpā-rasaḥ \|*  
*ya iha tat-kathane viramanty aho*  
*tanu-dharā nu dharāsu vasanti te \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Being most merciful, he filled the universe with the cooling rasa of his lotus feet. Persons who did not speak of him lived in the world worthlessly.**

### Text 5

*atha gurutvam upetya vikasvarā-*  
*mbuja-vilola-vilocana-khelanaiḥ \|*  
*dvija-gaṇaṁ samapāṭhayad eṣa yat*  
*pratibhayātibhayākulito guruḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Becoming a teacher, he taught brāhmaṇas while his eyes moved about playfully like blossoming lotuses. Because of his prowess, even Bṛhaspati became frightened.‘**

### Text 6

*vividha-śiṣya-sadasy api rājataḥ*  
*kanaka-gaura-tanor madhura-dyuteḥ \|*  
*sukhavataḥ paripāṭhayato’sya sā*  
*surucirā rucir āsa sudhā-rasam \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he joyfully taught, shining in the assembly of various students, with his golden body and sweet effulgence, his attractive beauty became the taste of nectar.**

### Text 7

*daśana-raśmibhir accha-rada-cchadau*  
*snapayatā satataṁ vadanendunā \|*  
*smita-sudhā-madhureṇa mahāprabhur*  
*ghana-rucā nava-cāru-rucir babhau \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the rays from his teeth, by his moon-like face bathing his lips, by the sweetness of his smile, and by his intense complexion, Mahāprabhu, endowed with fresh, attractive beauty, shone.**

### Text 8

*sakala-śiṣya-mukhāni mahāprabhoḥ*  
*kalayataḥ kakubhaḥ satatāruṇāḥ \|*  
*vidadhire bahu-vibhramayā balat-*  
*karuṇayāruṇayā nayana-śriyā \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When Mahāprabhu glanced at all the faces of the students with his beautiful eyes, red with strong mercy, filled with beauty, all the directions became red.**

### Text 9

*kara-talena girāṁ guru-vibhramair*  
*bhramavatā sa babhau paritaḥ sphuran \|*  
*kanaka-śaila ivodgata-gairiko-*  
*daya-latā laya-tāṇḍava-khelanām \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When he spoke, moving his hand with grave, graceful gestures, he shone like a dancing golden mountain slowing with red gairika.**

### Text 10

*ayam ayaṁ nu kim asya kim asya vā*  
*kim ayam artha uta svid ayaṁ tv iti \|*  
*kala-kalo’sya babhūva sukhāya sa*  
*tricaturaiś caturaiḥ paritaḥ kṛtaḥ \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sounds of three or four students surrounding him as they exclaimed, “This is the meaning or what is the meaning?” gave him pleasure.**

### Text 11

*prabhu-mukhe yugapat patayālubhir*  
*visṛmarair alibhir vidadhe muhuḥ \|*  
*vitata-pakṣa-vidhūnana-sakṣaṇair*  
*dig-abalā-gavalāvali-vibhramā \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When many bees flew towards the Lord’s face and spread out, vibrating their wings in joy, it appeared that buffalo herds were moving about in all directions.**

### Text 12

*kisalayaṁ salayaṁ kim u kāraṇaṁ*  
*sa-kamalaṁ kam alaṁ kim u vety aliḥ \|*  
*iha tadā hata-dākṣya iva prabhoḥ*  
*kara-dalaṁ rada-laṅghanayābhajat \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing his hand, the bees, losing their intelligence, thought it was a new shoot with a resting place or a lotus in the water and attempted to land on his hand without stinging.**

### Text 13

*nakha-sudhāṁśu-sudhāṁ śucim ukṣatā*  
*surucireṇa cireṇa madhuvrataḥ \|*  
*kara-dalena dalena rucāpy abhūd*  
*dhavalatā valatā suciroṣitaḥ \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the pleasant shower of white nectar from his moon-like nails, by the beauty of the stem of his hand, the bees gave up attachment to white creepers for a long time.**

### Text 14

*sa paritaḥ paritakṣya dhurīṇatāṁ*  
*madhuri-mādhurimānavatāṁ śriyām \|*  
*bhuvi hitaṁ vihitaṁ racayan satām*  
*agamad āgama-dākṣyam amuṣya kim \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up the beauty and sweetness of honey in the best of flowers, did the bee attain skill in the scriptures, rendering benefit to the devotees on earth?**

### Text 15

*prabhu-mukhendu-galad-vacanāmṛtaṁ*  
*mṛta-janasya ca jīvita-dāyi tat \|*  
*śruti-pathena nipīya cireṇa te*  
*mudam itā damitākhila-kalmaṣāḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Drinking the nectar of words flowing from the moon-like mouth of the Lord, who gave life to the dead, as the Lord recited scriptures, the students after some time had all their impurities destroyed and became joyful.**

### Text 16

*dhavala-pakṣa-sapakṣa-rug aṁśukaḥ*  
*śuka-cañcū-rucaṁ culūkīkṛtām \|*  
*madhurayor nu dadhe’dharayor asau*  
*madhurayor yad ayaṁ parijṛmbhate \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His white cloth, his parrot-like nose, and his two sweet lips produced sweetness that spread everywhere.**

### Text 17

*nava-vikasvara-paṅkaja-bhāsvaraṁ*  
*smita-madhu-drava-viśva-vilobhanam \|*  
*jahasur asya mukhendum avekṣya te*  
*rasa-mayaṁ samayantam aśoṇatām \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the Lord’s moon-like face which bewildered the universe with the liquid honey of his smile, shining like a newly blooming lotus, whose pleasing nature defeated all reddish objects, the students smiled.**

### Text 18

*vidhura-sauṣṭhavatāṁ labhatāṁ muhur*  
*vidhur asau valatā vadanāṁśunā \|*  
*madhu-rasānvita-puṣpa-manoramo*  
*madhur asādhur asāv abhivartatām \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Looking at his face, the moon lost its beauty. The sweet pleasure of a flower full of honey became improper.**

### Text 19

*iti janaḥ paripāṭhayati prabhau*  
*prabhavatā pratibhāna-rasābdhinā \|*  
*madhurimānam avekṣya samujjagau*  
*nava-sudhā vasudhām iva kiṁ śritā \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord taught his students with an ocean of great intelligence. Seeing his sweetness, people said, “Has fresh nectar taken shelter of this earth?”**

### Text 20

*iti kiyanti dināni mahāprabhuḥ*  
*samanayat paripāṭhya kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*nija-tanor mahasā sa dinaṁ dinaṁ*  
*prabhavatā bhava-tāpa-cayān api \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For some days Mahāprabhu taught, and daily, by the great beauty of his body, destroyed all the sufferings of this world.**

### Text 21

*sa jananī-bhaginī-patinā gayāṁ*  
*samam upaitu-manās tad-anantaram \|*  
*nija-manorama-ceṣṭita-vibhramaiḥ*  
*sumanasāṁ manasāṁ mudam āvahat \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He desired to go to Gayā with the husband of his wife’s sister. He gave joy to the mind of the gentle people by his graceful, attractive activities.**

### Text 22

*prathamam ullasito vijayodyame*  
*parisamāpta-vidhir mahito muhuḥ \|*  
*dvija-gaṇena sukhair vavṛdhe jaya-*  
*svanavatā nava-tāmarasekṣaṇaḥ \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**First joyfully completing the śrāddha rites on arriving there, the Lord, with eyes like lotuses, increased his happiness as brāhmaṇas shouted “Jaya.”**

### Text 23

*dvija-gaṇair bhagaṇair iva saṅkṣaran*  
*dyuti-sudhā vasudhāsu śaśīva saḥ \|*  
*sukathitaiḥ pathi tair vilasan prabhur*  
*asamayaṁ samayaṁ tam amanyata \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Together with the brāhmaṇas he was like a moon dispersing nectar among the stars on the earth. As he conversed with them on the road, it appeared to be a most pleasurable gathering.**

### Text 24

*kva ca vilokya manojñatamāṁ sthalīṁ*  
*sthala-payoruha-pāda-payoruhām \|*  
*upataraṅgiṇi tena viśa-vibhrame*  
*na madhupā madhu pātum anutsukāḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When he saw an attractive place on the bank of the river, the bees became eager to drink the honey for his lotus feet, thinking them to be lotuses.**

### Text 25

*nibhṛta-nīla-madhuvrata-locanair*  
*lalita-keśara-danta-vikasvaraiḥ \|*  
*vikasitāmburuhānana-maṇḍalair*  
*madhura-sādhu-rasā madhu-śālinī \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By bees as eyes, filaments as teeth, and blossoming lotuses as faces, the place became concentrated sweetness with sweet rasa to be experienced by the devotees.**

### Text 26

*madana-manthara-haṁsa-vadhū-gati-*  
*pratipadollasitā madhurākṛtiḥ \|*  
*kamalinī-tatir asya mudaṁ dadhe*  
*sarasi ko rasiko vilasen na hi \|\|26\|\| (yugmakam)*

**Перевод:**

**The host of lotuses, a form of sweetness, joyous with the movement of female swans intoxicated with love, gave the Lord joy. What enjoyer of rasa would not enjoy in this pond?**

### Text 27

*madhukarā madhupāna-madonmadāḥ*  
*kim idam eva gadanti muhur muhuḥ \|*  
*sphuṭa-saroja-vanīṣv avanī-tale*  
*kalabhatāṁ labhatāṁ rasiko janaḥ \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The bees, intoxicated with drinking the honey, constantly spoke as follows. “What is this? The enjoyer of rasa should become a young elephant in the forest of blossoming lotuses on this earth.**

### Text 28

*sarasi kā rasikā viratā bhaved*  
*urasi ko rasiko’dhṛta-tat-kucaḥ \|*  
*nanu kathaṁ nu kathañcana tau matau*  
*kamala-komala-korakavan na yau \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“What enjoyer of rasa would give enjoying in the pond? What enjoyer of rasa would not hold the lotus breasts to his chest? Would he not give regard to two tender lotus buds as breasts?”**

### Text 29

*iha dṛśāṁ sudṛśāṁ suṣamāṁ samāṁ*  
*tulayituṁ layituṁ ca muhur muhuḥ \|*  
*kuvalayaṁ valayaṁ pavanair vanair*  
*akati vā kati kā madhurā dhurāḥ \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the pond, all the lotuses had beauty like the eyes of beautiful-eyed women and had developed intense affection for the winds passing through the lotus clusters.**

### Text 30

*tanu-taraṅga-taraṅgam avīkṣya sā*  
*‘tanu-taraṅga-gataṁ priyam ākulā \|*  
*tanu-taraṅgam ayanty akhilaṁ payo’*  
*tanu-taraṅgam amuṣya sita-cchadī \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The female swans, not seeing their dear husbands playing, became disturbed, and making waves in the pond, moved quickly towards their mates.**

### Text 31

*kala-rutā garutām avadhūnanaṁ*  
*vidadhatī dadhatī praṇayaṁ priye \|*  
*akṛtakā kṛtakāpi mudaṁ vibhor*  
*mada-kalodaka-lolita-cakrikā \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The intoxicated swans, moving in the water, chortling softly, expressing affection for their mates, shook their wings and gave joy to the Lord.**

### Text 32

*anu taraṅga-javena taraṅgitaṁ*  
*nikaṭagaṁ sarasaḥ sarasaṁ taṭam \|*  
*parivilokya yayau mudam uttamāṁ*  
*surucire rucir eva hi kāminaḥ \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the waves disturb the bank, the Lord experienced the highest bliss. The pleasure seeker derives happiness from seeing something attractive.**

### Text 33

*amala-śīkara-śīla-meduraḥ*  
*kuvalayaṁ kalayan valayākṛti \|*  
*valaya-sādhvasa-sādhv atimantharaḥ*  
*śrama-hatīr mahatīr vidadhe marut \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Full of pure water drops, moving the host of lotuses, expert at disturbing them, the wind relieved the Lord of fatigue by blowing gently.**

### Text 34

*atha pathi prathitātisukhodgamaṁ*

*kim anurāga-rasair atilohito*  
*dina-patir na patiṣyati vihvalaḥ \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the sun move slowly, since it experienced joy on seeing the Lord on the path, people thought, “Has the sun, becoming red out of great attraction to the Lord, now fallen down out of unsteadiness?”**

### Text 35

*atha vilokya gataṁ caramācale*  
*pipatiṣuṁ paripakva-phalākṛtim \|*  
*dina-karaṁ bhramaraiḥ saha niḥsṛtair*  
*gata-rasā tarasā bhavad-abjinī \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the sun, like a ripe fruit, desiring to set on the horizon, along with the bees which had swarmed about, the lotuses suddenly became sad.**

### Text 36

*apatatā kvacanāpi ca niryatā*  
*mada-kalāli-kulena samantataḥ \|*  
*sahaja-vairavatī ca babhūva sā*  
*kumudinī mudinī rajanī tadā \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The night, which gives joy to the night lotus, showed enmity to the swarms of bees which flew towards the day lotuses.**

### Text 37

*nava-vikāśa-parāpi kumudvatī*  
*madhukaraiḥ sutarāṁ paribodhitā \|*  
*balavatā dayitena yathā bhavet*  
*priyatamāyata-māna-virāmataḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As a lover awakens his beloved to see her face, the bees attempted to awaken the night lotuses which were about to blossom in the night.**

### Text 38

*avirataṁ nalinī madhu-mādhurī*  
*madamadā api puṣpaliho muhuḥ \|*  
*kumudinīm abhajan nirata-spṛhā*  
*nava-rasā vara-sādhu-janāḥ khalu \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The intoxicated bees, which constantly sought the sweet honey of the lotuses, gave up their desire for the day lotuses and went to the night lotuses, just as the best of people seek out a new taste.**

### Text 39

*śiṣayiṣur niśi kāruṇikas tataḥ*  
*kvacana nīvṛti-nirvṛti-mānasaḥ \|*  
*saha-samasta-janena sunidratā-*  
*ghaṭanato’ṭanato virarāma saḥ \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The merciful Lord, desiring to sleep at night, giving up the crowds of people, enjoyed deep sleep.**

### Text 40

*dina-mukhasya vilokayatas tato’*

*hṛdayam uddharam utsukatā yayau \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When dawn came the next day, a breeze was blowing. The Lord, an attractive ocean of mercy, derived great enthusiasm from this.**

### Text 41

*sapadi saṅkucatā dalatā navaṁ*  
*dala-cayena tataḥ sama-vibhramā \|*  
*udayatāviśatāli-kulena ca*

**Перевод:**

**The lotuses were most beautiful with bees awakening, covered with pollen, and freshly entering the petals of the closed lotuses.**

### Text 42

*dina-mukhe’sya tatāna mahāprabhor*  
*mudam anekatamāṁ pathi gacchataḥ \|*  
*kumudinī nalinī ca samantato*

**Перевод:**

**At the beginning of the day, the colorful day lotuses and night lotuses, chief of Brahmā’s creations, gave the greatest bliss to Mahāprabhu as he walked on the path.**

### Text 43

*sa hṛdaye hṛdayepsitam īkṣaṇād*  
*akṛtako’kṛtako na hi vibhramaḥ \|*  
*smaraṇato raṇato’pi mudaṁ prabhor*  
*divi ratā viratā vitatir dadhe \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From seeing the pleasing scene, the Lord was joyful in his heart. As he wandered in the forest, the birds, giving up flying, gave him joy as he derived happiness from remembrance.**

### Text 44

*ciram iva pratibodham upāgatā*  
*giribhuvo vibhu-locana-vartma-gāḥ \|*

*sapadi sampadi santatam ādadhuḥ \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The mountainous grounds woke up after a long time on being seen by the Lord and immediately shouted “Jaya, jaya” in joy through the various chirpings of the birds.**

### Text 45

*suharitā haritāla-rucāṁ cayaiḥ*  
*kvacana kāñcana-kānta-ruciḥ kvacit \|*

*kva ca sitā ca sitāccha-śilā-cayaiḥ \|\|45\|\|*

**The earth was sometimes green like haritāla and sometimes yellow like gold. Sometimes it was black like a testing stone. Sometimes it was white like moonstones.**

### Text 46

*vikasitaiḥ kasitaiḥ kusumoccayair*  
*iva darī badarī-vidhurāyitā \|*  
*vihasatīha satīkṣaṇage prabhāv*

**The beautiful earth at the base of the mountain, with caves devoid of badarī plants, as if laughing with blossoming, bright flowers, became most beautiful for the Lord as he cast his glance on it.**

### Text 47

*agavayair gavayaiḥ śaraṇīkṛtaṁ*  
*visṛmaraiḥ sṛmarair upaśobhitam \|*

**The Lord gazed at the firm place on the road, beautiful with gavayas, cows, and various deer which it sheltered.**

### Text 48

*anṛju-locana-locana-vibhramair*

**Перевод:**

**The herds of deer, lying down and filled with desire, with graceful movements and side glances at every moment, quickly produced sweet rasa in the Lord.**

### Text 49

*iti sa vartmani gaura-sudhānidhir*  
*vividha-kautuka-vīkṣaṇa-kautukī \|*  
*viruruce sukha-magna-manā vrajan*  
*vividha-sad-vidha-sat-paripālitaḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Overjoyed on seeing various interesting things, the golden ocean of nectar, his mind absorbed in bliss, protected by devotees of various good qualities, shone brightly.**

### Text 50

*pathi sa cīranade prabhur ātanot*  
*plavana-tarpaṇa-pūjanam utsukaḥ \|*  
*jvaritam asya vapuḥ samabhūt tato*  
*na caritaṁ caritaṁ bhavati prabhoḥ \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The eager Lord, walking on the path, then bathed and performed tarpaṇa and worship at the Cīra River. He developed a fever but he did not fail to perform his daily rites.**

### Text 51

*pathi śarīra-gateyam asusthatā*  
*katham abhūt pratikūla-karī mama \|*  
*iti vicintayatā dvija-sañcayo*  
*nijagade jagad-eka-kṛpālunā \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“How have I become sick on the road? I must have done some unfavorable act.” Thinking in this way, the Lord who is most merciful to the world, spoke to the brāhmaṇas.**

### Text 52

*atha vicintya bhṛśaṁ manasātmano*  
*jvara-śamāya mahāprabhur auṣadham \|*  
*kṣiti-surāṅghri-payo nyadiśat svayaṁ*  
*nahi kṛpāṁ hi kṛpāmbudhir ujjhati \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking deeply in his mind, Mahāprabhu at the time of the fever stated that the cure for his fever was the foot water of the brāhmaṇas. The ocean of mercy never rejects mercy.**

### Text 53

*jvara-śamo’tha babhūva mahāprabhoḥ*  
*sapadi tena tadīya-padāmbunā \|*  
*jagati tac caritāni vidantu ke*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu’s fever then immediately disappeared by taking the foot water of the brāhmaṇas. What humble people in the three worlds can understand the humble actions of the Lord?**

### Text 54

*atha sametya sa rāja-giriṁ prabhur*  
*dvija-gaṇena mudā vyatanot tadā \|*  
*pitṛ-samarhaṇam uttamam ādarād*  
*uparame parameṣṭhi-sarasy api \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord went to Rājagiri with the brāhmaṇas and spread bliss. At Brahma-sarovara he completed excellent rites for his forefathers with respect.**

### Text 55

*akhila-tīrtha-vareṣu pitṛ-kriyāḥ*  
*sa kṛta-sad-vidhi tatra samāpayan \|*  
*atha gayāṁ saha bhūsura-sañcayair*  
*aviśad āviśadātmabhir utsukaiḥ \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At all the excellent holy places he completed rites for the forefathers according to the rules. With the pure, zealous brāhmaṇas, he entered Gayā.**

### Text 56

*atha sa gaura-kiśora-sudhākaraḥ*  
*prathitam īśvara-pūrva-purīti tam \|*  
*sapadi vīkṣya mudaṁ nirapāyinīṁ*  
*hṛdi tadādi tadāpi yayau prabhuḥ \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The youthful golden abode of nectar then saw the famous Īśvara Purī and experienced permanent bliss in his heart immediately, at that time and forever after.**

### Text 57

*tam avanamya nipatya ca bhūtale*  
*bahula-harṣa-paripluta-mānasaḥ \|*  
*atha jagāda gabhīra-ghana-svarāṁ*  
*vinayato nayatoṣakarīṁ giram \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He fell on the ground to offer respects. His mind was filled with great bliss. He spoke in a deep voice, with humility, using words pleasing with their politeness.**

### Text 58

*tava padāmbuja-yugmam idaṁ prabho*  
*bahula-bhāgya-bhareṇa vilokitam \|*

*prabhavato bhavatoyadhi-śoṣaṇam \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O master! By great fortune I have seen your lotus feet. Please tell me how, by the power of bhakti’s qualities, I can dry up the ocean of material existence.”**

### Text 59

*iti niśamya mahāprabhu-bhāṣitaṁ*  
*mudam avāpya yatiḥ sa mahāśayaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing Mahāprabhu’s words, the great sannyāsa and ocean of mercy became happy and gave the mantra to the Lord. The Lord gave respect to he who had given him mercy.**

### Text 60

*amum avāpya manuṁ vraja-bhāvinī-*  
*janapateḥ pulakāṅkura-śobhinā \|*  
*vigalad-aśru-bhṛtā vinayād ayaṁ*  
*nijagade jagad-eka-kṛpāvatā \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On receiving the Kṛṣṇa mantra, the Lord, merciful to the universe, with hairs standing on end and tears flowing from his eyes, spoke.**

### Text 61

*yati-pate bhavataḥ pada-saṅgamāt*  
*sumahatīha babhūva kṛtārthatā \|*

*vicakare ca kare padajaṁ rajaḥ \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O master of sannyāsīs! By association with your feet, I have become greatly successful.” With devotion to guru, he took the dust of his feet in his hands.**

### Text 62

*atha sa phalgu-nadī-plavane yathā-*  
*vidhi vidhāya pitṝn samatarpayat \|*  
*śava-mahībhṛti piṇḍam adād atho*  
*karuṇato’ruṇato’py aruṇekṣaṇaḥ \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At the Phalgu River, the Lord whose eyes were redder than the dawn, out of compassion, offered tarpaṇas to his forefathers according to the rules. At Preta-śīlā he offered piṇḍas.**

### Text 63

*samavatīrya tato vyatanot kriyāḥ*  
*pitṛ-gaṇasya sa dakṣiṇa-mānase \|*  
*dvija-gaṇaiś ca tathottara-mānase*  
*sahṛdayair hṛdayaika-sudhākaraḥ \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Leaving there, the Lord, the sole abode of nectar for the heart, went to Dakṣiṇa Mānasa and Uttara Mānasa with affectionate brāhmaṇas and performed rites for his forefathers.**

### Text 64

*pitṛ-gaṇasya gayā-śirasi kriyā*  
*atha vidhāya hareḥ pada-paddhatim \|*

*sa sahasā saha sādhu-janair yayau \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At Gayā Śiras he performed rites for his forefathers. Seeing the Lord’s feet in joy, he immediately left with the brāhmaṇas.**

*katham abhūn nṛhareḥ pada-paddhatiṁ*  
*samavalokayato mṛdutaiva na \|*  
*iti vicintayato’sya dṛśor jharo*  
*vipulakaḥ pulakaś ca tadābhavat \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He thought, “How is it that I saw the feet of the Lord (Nṛ-hari) and my heart did not melt?” Then his hairs stood on end and tears came to his eyes.**

### Text 65

*iti tathā-vidhayā nija-ceṣṭayā*  
*sapadi mukta-samasta-jana-prabhuḥ \|*  
*abhavad ullasitaś calituṁ tadā*  
*madhuvane dhuvanena calat-tanuḥ \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With such actions, he then gave up association of everyone and with trembling body became eager to go to Madhuvana (Mathurā).**

### Text 67

*atha divaḥ samabhūd aśarīriṇī*  
*sapadi gīr nava-megha-varākṛtiḥ \|*  
*punar upaiṣyati tatra mahāprabhuḥ*  
*sva-bhavanaṁ bhava nandayituṁ punaḥ \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A voice spoke from the sky, without body, in the form of a new cloud. “Mahāprabhu should go home. Go and experience happiness in your house.”**

### Text 68

*iti niśamya divo giram uttamāṁ*  
*pramuditena mahāprabhunā tataḥ \|*  
*nija-gṛhe calituṁ mahitāśayaiḥ*  
*pravavṛte’vavṛtena mahīsuraiḥ \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the excellent divine voice, Mahāprabhu became happy to go home, surrounded by the great brāhmaṇas.**

### Text 69

*atha kiyad-dina-mātra-vilambato*  
*nikaṭam āgata ātmaja ity asau \|*  
*nija-gṛhān samapurayad utsavaiḥ*  
*sumahatām ahatā hi manorathāḥ \|\|69\|*

**Перевод:**

**After some days, Śacī saw her son return. She filled the house with festivals. All her desires were fulfilled.**

### Text 70

*mṛdu-mṛdaṅga-yaśaḥ-paṭṭahollasat-*  
*paṇava-kāhala-kāṁsya-sumardalaiḥ \|*  
*yugapadena bhṛśaṁ paritāḍanāt*  
*dhvanir abhūn nirabhūta ivocchritaḥ \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**An amazing sound rose up by the simultaneous playing of attractive mṛdaṅgas, famous paṭtahas, paṇavas, kāhala, cymbals and excellent mardalas.**

### Text 71

*atisukhena paripluta-mānasā*  
*surucireṇa cireṇa tanūbhuvā \|*  
*gṛham upetya tato dadṛśe mudā*  
*sva-jananī jana-nītiṣu kovidā \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After many days, with joyful face, he had arrived at the house and saw his joyful mother, who was expert at bringing others there.**

### Text 72

*prabhur atho jananī-padajaṁ rajaḥ*  
*kara-talena śirasy adadhān muhuḥ \|*  
*atha papāta sa daṇḍavad utsuko*  
*bhuvi nayaṁ vinayaṁ vidadhan muhuḥ \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord took her foot dust with his hand and placed it on his head constantly. Eagerly he fell on the ground, showing humility constantly.**

### Text 73

*sughanaṁ sutataṁ sughanaṁ sutataṁ*  
*sahasā suṣiraiḥ sahasā śuṣiraiḥ \|*  
*atha vādyam abhūd atha vādyam abhūd*  
*rabhasodyama-bhū rabhasodyama-bhūḥ \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Suddenly, wind instruments of various sorts sounded deeply with great passion.**

### Text 74

*atha kāñcana-kāñcana-navya-latāṁ*  
*mṛdulāṁ mṛdulāñcita-śubhra-paṭām \|*  
*muditām uditām atha vīkṣya tanuṁ*  
*vasu tasya sutasya sasarja śacī \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing her joyful son, like a new golden creeper, wearing soft, pleated white cloth, who just become visible, Śacī distributed wealth.**

### Text 75

*dvija-gaṇāya sanartaka-vādaka-*  
*prabhṛtaye’pi ca bhikṣu-gaṇāya sā \|*  
*nija-sutāgamanollasitā dadau*  
*nibhṛta-sambhṛta-sampadijaṁ vasu \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Overjoyed by the arrival of her son, she gave wealth which had arisen by good fortune and had been gathered in one place, to brāhmaṇas, dancers, musicians, and beggars.**

### Text 76

*gayāyā ity evaṁ sva-gṛham agamad bhūri-karuṇaḥ*  
*prabhuḥ pauṣasyānte sakala-tanu-bhṛt-tāpa-śamanaḥ \|*  
*tato māghasyādau niravadhi nijaiḥ kīrtana-rasaiḥ*  
*prakāśaṁ cāveśaṁ bhuvi vikirati smānudivasam \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The merciful Lord, who destroyed the suffering of all beings, returned home at the end of Pauṣa month. At the beginning of Māgha month he constantly, daily, began spreading knowledge and devotion through the rasa of his kīrtana to the world.**

### Text 77

*iti kṣaṇotkṣipta-samasta-ceṣṭitaḥ*  
*pratikṣaṇaṁ gāyati nirbharaṁ muhuḥ \|*  
*pade pade roditi romaharṣaṇair*  
*vimukta-kaṇṭhaṁ karuṇā-payonidhiḥ \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With all his actions emanating festivity, the ocean of mercy at every moment sang loudly with ardor and sometimes wept, with hairs standing on end.**

## Chapter Five

### Text 1

*āgatya sva-gṛham atha svakīrtanādyaiḥ*  
*saṁreje niravadhi rodanair vibhinnaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭvaivaṁ vidhim aniśaṁ savismayāsīd*  
*ity etat kim iti kim ity atha prasūḥ sā \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Returning home, the Lord, having changed, shone with continual kīrtana and crying. Seeing this behavior, Śacī was constantly astonished. “What is this? What is this?” she asked.**

### Text 2

*yāminyāṁ śayitavataḥ śacī kadācit*  
*putrasya prathamam avekṣya rodanaṁ sā \|*  
*brūhītthaṁ kim ahaha tāta rodiṣi tvaṁ*  
*sāśaṅkaṁ tam iti jagāda bhūri-bhāgyā \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Fortunate Śacī one time saw him lying down and crying in the evening. She first said in anxiety, “Please tell me why you are crying.”**

### Text 3

*tac chrutvā na kim api ced uvāca nāthaḥ*  
*premārdro nayana-jalāsikta-sarva-gātraḥ \|*  
*sātyantaṁ niravadhi cintitā tadāsīt*  
*premety etad api viveda daiva-yogāt \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, soft with prema, his tears soaking his body, hearing this, said nothing. She began thinking constantly. She understood by good fortune that this was prema.**

### Text 4

*jñātvaitat vimala-manāḥ śacī tanūjaṁ*  
*sārdrākṣī vinaya-parā bhṛśaṁ yayāce \|*  
*mahyaṁ yad dhanam akhilaṁ prayacchasi tvaṁ*  
*premākhyaṁ kim u na dadāsi sāmprataṁ tat \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pure-minded Śacī, understanding about her son, with tears in her eyes, with humility requested him again and again, “Will you not now give me the treasure called prema?**

### Text 5

*devānām aviditam etad atyalabhyaṁ*  
*premedaṁ yad avagataṁ tvayā gayāyām \|*  
*dīnāyai tad iha ha me prayaccha tāta*  
*snehas te yadi mayi tiṣṭhati kṣaṇaṁ ca \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If you have a little affection for me, please give to this fallen soul that prema which you obtained at Gayā, which is rare and unknown even to the devatās.”**

### Text 6

*ity asyā giram adhigamya gauracandraḥ*  
*snehārdraḥ prativacanaṁ dadau jananyai \|*  
*tan mātas tava bhavitā cireṇa nūnaṁ*  
*yat te syād gurutara-vaiṣṇavānukampā \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing her words, Gauracandra, with affectionate heart answered his mother. “O mother! When I am very merciful to the devotees after some time, this will happen.”**

### Text 7

*tac chrutvā mudam adhikāṁ yayau tataḥ sā*  
*tan nūnaṁ mama bhaviteti hṛṣṭa-cittā \|*  
*gaurāṅgo’pi tad adhigamya mātṛ-cittaṁ*  
*viprendrān vinaya-paro jagāda bhūyaḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, she became very happy. She thought joyfully, “This will happen to me.” Gaurāṅga, understanding the mind of his mother, then spoke to the brāhmaṇas with humility.**

### Text 8

*premāyaṁ niravadhi mṛgyate jananyā*  
*bhaktiś ca prabhu-caraṇe garīyasīyam \|*  
*te syātāṁ sapadi yathāśiṣo bhavadbhir*  
*yujyantāṁ tad anu tathocur evam ete \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“My mother is continually seeking prema and intense devotion at the lotus feet of the Lord. You should bless give her blessings so that this will happen quickly.” They then gave her blessings.**

### Text 9

*ity evaṁ kvacana rudan vilocanābhyāṁ*  
*dhārāṇāṁ śata-śatam ādadhāty uraḥsu \|*  
*śleṣmāṇaṁ kṣipati muhur muhuḥ sthaviṣṭhaṁ*  
*nāsābhyāṁ bhuvi viluṭhan kvacit sa nāthaḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the blessing, he wept, and tears flowed from his eyes in hundreds of streams onto his chest. Mucus poured abundantly from his nose and he rolled on the ground.**

### Text 10

*pratyūṣa-prabhṛti dinaṁ samastam eva*  
*premāśru-pracura-varai rudan vinīya \|*  
*yāminyāṁ bhavati sati prabhuḥ prabodhe*  
*vaikalyād dinam iti tarkayāmbabhūva \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From the dawn, all day he wept with abundant tears of prema. When evening came he recovered and wondered whether it was still day time, out of confusion.**

### Text 11

*sandhyāyāṁ kim api rudan vimukta-kaṇṭhaḥ*  
*prātaḥ syāt katham api ced bahiḥ prabodhaḥ \|*  
*tan naktaṁ vrajati kiyat kadeti gauro*  
*vaikalyād vadati na tasya kāla-bhedaḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Crying loudly in the evening, he returned to external consciousness. Out of confusion he said, “It was morning. How did night appear?” He could not distinguish the passing of time.**

### Text 12

*nāmaikaṁ śravaṇa-pathaṁ yadaiva gacchet*  
*tat so’yaṁ bhuvi viluṭhan bala-prakāmam \|*  
*dāghiṣṭhaiḥ śvasana-samīraṇaiḥ sakampair*  
*netrānta-prasṛmara-dhārayā ca reje \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the Lord heard just one utterance of the name, he would roll on the ground with force. He shone brightly with heavy breathing, trembling, and profuse tears.**

### Text 13

*sotkaṇṭhaṁ niravadhi kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇety*  
*ājalpan kvacana vibhinna-sanna-kaṇṭhaḥ \|*  
*harṣordhvais tanuruha-sañcayair vibhāti*  
*prāyo’yaṁ pratidinam evam eva bhūtvā \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With great enthusiasm he constantly chanted the name of Kṛṣṇa, sometimes with a choked voice. He shone, with hairs standing on end. This happened almost every day.**

### Text 14

*sa snātvā divasa-mukhe karoti pūjām*  
*aśnāti pratidivasaṁ mudā nivedya \|*  
*sad-viprān api paripāṭhayann udārān*  
*māghādyān iti caturo nināya māsān \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He would bathe at dawn, perform worship and eat prasādam daily in joy. He taught excellent, pure brāhmaṇas boys. In this way, four months starting with Māgha passed.**

### Text 15

*premārdraḥ sapulakam ekadā murārer*  
*vaidyasyālayam agamat kṛpā-samudraḥ \|*  
*tatrāsau sapadi niveśya deva-gehe*  
*sambhinno nayana-jalaiḥ samadhyavātsīt \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One day, the ocean of mercy, filled with prema and hairs standing on end, went to the house of the physician named Murārī. Entering his temple, immediately he stood there with tears in his eyes and a contracted body.**

### Text 16

*āścaryaṁ daśana-yugena gāṁ balīyān*  
*vārāhaṁ vapur idam āvahan ka eṣaḥ \|*  
*marma-spṛk tudati mahāmahīdhra-tulyo*  
*bhūyo’sāv iti nigadan sasarpa paścāt \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“How amazing! This strong body of Varāha is carrying the earth on his two tusks. Like a great mountain he attacks, touching the vital points.” Saying this, he retreated.**

### Text 17

*ity uktvā sapadi tathā tadīya-bhāvaṁ*  
*saṅgṛhṇan bhuvi bhuja-jānubhir vrajan saḥ \|*  
*ghūrṇābhis taralatareṇa dṛg-yugena*  
*drāghiṣṭhām api vidadhe ca hūṅkṛtiṁ tām \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On saying this, immediately he took on the identity of Varāha and moved about on his hands and knees. He roared for a long time while rolling his eyes.**

### Text 18

*dantāgraiḥ sapadi sa paittalāmbu-pātraṁ*  
*dhṛtvāsau bahu-bhayam unmukho’tidūre \|*  
*saṅkṣipyaṁs tad anu murāri-guptam ūce*  
*rūpaṁ me sahajam udīrayeti śaśvat \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He quickly took a brass water pot in his teeth and, as if facing something fearful, threw it far away. He then said repeatedly to Murārī Gupta, “Describe my natural form.”**

### Text 19

*tac chrutvā bhuvi nipatan sa bhīta-bhīto*  
*no vidmo vayam iha te svarūpam etat \|*  
*ātmānaṁ svayam evam ātmanaiva vetthety*  
*ūce’sau prativacanaiś ca gītayoktaiḥ \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Murāri fell on the ground in fear and said, repeating words from the Gītā, “I do not this form of yours. You alone know yourself by yourself.”**

### Text 20

*bhūyo’sau sa hasitavan madhu-dravais taiḥ*  
*pratyūce prativacanaiḥ prabhus tam enam \|*  
*vedo’yaṁ nanu kim u vetty ayaṁ vimugdha*  
*saṁmohād avacinute’ndhavat sa nityam \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Again the Lord spoke with a smile, using sweet words. “The bewildered Vedas do not know my glories at all. Like blind persons, they search for me constantly in ignorance.”**

### Text 21

*ity uktā śruti-gaditaṁ nipaṭhya bhūyaḥ*  
*sotprāsaṁ sa parihasann uvāca nāthaḥ \|*  
*vedānām iha khalu nāsti śaktir eṣā*  
*jñātuṁ mām iti nigadan yayau sva-geham \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Uttering this, he recited the Vedas, laughed in derision and said, “The Vedas do not have the power to know me.” He then went home.**

### Text 22

*anyedyuḥ sva-gṛham abhi kṣapeśa-koṭi-*  
*śrī-yuktaḥ para-para-bhāga-bhāk pratīkaḥ \|*  
*śrīvāsaṁ nija-purataḥ sthitaṁ mahasvān*  
*abhyūce saha bala-hūṅkṛtair vacobhiḥ \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On another day, while going home, the most auspicious Lord endowed with the beauty of a million moons spoke to Śrīvāsa near his house in a loud voice, with great roaring sounds.**

### Text 23

*tvaṁ bhoḥ paśyasi na kim atra pañca-vaktrān*  
*ṣaḍ-vaktrān api ca caturmukhān sametān \|*  
*sopy ūce na khalu vilokyate mayāsau*  
*ṣaḍ-vaktra-prabhṛti-janaḥ samāgato’yam \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Do you not see Śiva, Kartikeya and Brahmā gathered here?” Śrīvāsa said, “I do not see Kartikeya and the others gathered here.”**

### Text 24

*ity ukte sati tad-anūpatasthirāṁsaṁ*  
*nāmnā śrīpatim anujaṁ dadarśa vipraḥ \|*  
*so’bhyetya śruti-nikaṭeṣu dhīram ūce*  
*‘dvaitasyāgamana-kathāṁ prabhuṁ didṛkṣoḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After saying this, Śrīvāsa saw his younger brother Śrīpati situated behind him. Approaching him, he said gravely, “Advaita has come to see the Lord.”**

### Text 25

*ācāryaḥ kim iha samāgato’sti tasyai*  
*taj jñātvā sapadi samutthito’jireṣu \|*  
*āgatya pratipada-hūṅkṛtāṁ sa vāṇīṁ*  
*pratyūce mahita-mahā-mahaḥ-samūhaḥ \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding that Advaita had come to see him, the Lord, endowed with the greatest powers, immediately rose, approached, and spoke in a loud voice.**

### Text 26

*te jñāsyanty ahaha sapady amutra ye ye*  
*yāsyanti kṣām adhunādhikāra-hīnāḥ \|*  
*ity uktvā gurutara-hūṅkṛtair vibhinnaḥ*  
*śrīvāsālayam agamat drutaṁ prabhuḥ saḥ \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“People without qualification should know that they will quickly take another body on earth in their next life.” Saying this with loud roaring, the Lord quickly altered his mood and went to Śrīvāsa’s house.**

### Text 27

*tatraiva drutam adhigatya gāḍha-bandhaṁ*  
*sambadhyārgalam avara-dvaye vikurvan \|*  
*bahv-āviṣkṛta-sahaja-prakāśa-bhāsvān*  
*āvāse rahasi rarāja gauracandraḥ \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Quickly going there, he then locked the door firmly, while his legs trembled. Gauracandra remained there alone, shining like the sun, emanating profuse natural light.**

### Text 28

*advaito nija-nilayāt samāgato’sau*  
*saprāduṣkṛta-sahajo vilokitavyaḥ \|*  
*ity evaṁ manasi vidhāya sat-pratijñāṁ*  
*tat-kāle bahir udabhūt kavāṭayos tat \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Advaita came from his house and, making up his mind to see the Lord who had manifested himself in a natural way, stood outside the door panels at that time.**

### Text 29

*śrīvāsa-dvija-kula-candramaḥ kanīyān*  
*eṣaḥ śrīpatir atha tat-samāgamaṁ tam \|*  
*sāśaṅkaṁ sapadi nivedayāñcakāra*  
*jñātvaivaitaṁ svayam amucat prabhuḥ kavāṭam \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa’s younger brother Śrīpati came in anxiety and quickly informed the Lord about Advaita. The Lord then unlocked the door.**

### Text 30

*saṅkalpo manasi kṛto yathaiva tena*  
*śrībhājaṁ prabhum avalokya taṁ tathaiva \|*  
*advaitas tṛṇa-nicayaṁ radair gṛhītvā*  
*susnigdho bhuvi nipapāta daṇḍavat saḥ \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I have seen the Lord endowed with beauty just as I have imagined.” Saying this, Advaita, with grass in his teeth, fell to the ground with great affection.**

### Text 31

*taṁ dṛṣṭvā prabhur api dor-dvayena śīghraṁ*  
*śrī-bhājaṁ svayam iva unnināya paścāt \|*  
*harṣeṇāśithilitam āśliṣad viśeṣaṁ*  
*premāśru-sravaṇa-jharaiḥ siṣeca bhūyaḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Advaita, the abode of beauty, the Lord quickly raised him up with his two arms. He embraced him tightly with joy and moistened him profusely with tears of prema.**

### Text 32

*ity evaṁ-vidha-vividhollasad-vihārair*  
*viśrānto’bhavad ṛtu-nāyako vasantaḥ \|*  
*atrānte prabhu-naṭanāvaloka-hṛṣṭaḥ*  
*kiṁ grīṣmaḥ prahasati mallikā-vikāsaiḥ \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the pastimes of bliss, the spring season became exhausted. Summer began smiling with blossoming jasmine flowers, overjoyed on seeing the Lord dance.**

### Text 33

*vicchedād iva surabher dināny amūni*  
*pratagrād atividhurāṇi saṁśrayante \|*  
*uddīpyad dinakara-jāta-vedasaḥ kiṁ*  
*jvālābhir niravadhi deha-dāha-vartma \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With the departure of spring, the days became more and more hostile. The sun, like fire, constantly burned the living entities with its fiery rays.**

### Text 34

*uddāma-dyumaṇi-ruco muhur jvalantyo*  
*yadyapy āśraya-mayam ambu śoṣayanti \|*  
*padminyās tad api dadhāty atīva saukhyaṁ*  
*duḥkhaṁ ca priya-vihitaṁ priyaṁ tanoti \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The hot sun’s rays, constantly burning, dried up the water which was the shelter for lotuses, but the lotuses were very happy. Suffering gives joy because it is ultimately meant to give pleasure.**

### Text 35

*naidāghaṁ nija-mahasā nidāgha-raśmiṁ*  
*nyakkurvan satataṁ nava-navena gauraḥ \|*  
*anyedyur dvija-tanūjān vipāṭhayan sa*  
*prodbhinna-prakaṭa-nija-prakāśa āsīt \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gaurāṅga continually defeated the sun with the sun-like rays of his ever fresh effulgence. Another day, teaching the sons of brāhmaṇas, he emanated the most dazzling light.**

### Text 36

*ity etad vidha-sahaja-prakāśa-bhāsvān*  
*nirbhinnaḥ sad-aruṇa-sarva-gātra-yaṣṭiḥ \|*  
*pratyagronmiṣad-aruṇotpalāṅghri-yugmaḥ*  
*śrīvāsālayam agamad vimukta-saṅgaḥ \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Like the sun shining with natural light, with his limbs colored bright pink, with his feet pink like freshly blossoming lotuses, he went alone to the Śrīvāsa’s house.**

### Text 37

*unmīlad-dyumaṇi-gaṇa-prakāśa-bhājaṁ*  
*pratyagra-sphuṭatara-śoṇa-sārasākṣam \|*  
*gacchantaṁ drutam aruṇāṅghri-padmayos tair*  
*vinyāsaiḥ pathi dadṛśur janāḥ sa-citram \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**People looked with astonishment at the Lord, whose eyes were like red lotuses freshly blossoming, like light from the rising sun, as he moved quickly on the path with feet like red lotuses.**

### Text 38

*tat-puryāṁ sapadi niveśya deva-gehasy-*  
*ālindopari paritasthivān pareśaḥ \|*  
*dhyāyantaṁ gṛham adhi nirbharaika-tānaṁ*  
*śrīvāsaṁ prakaṭa-prakāśam ājuhāva \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Entering Śrīvāsa’s house, the Lord sat on the veranda of the temple. He called out loudly to Śrīvāsa who was meditating in the room with great concentration.**

### Text 39

*tac chrutvā sapadi gṛhād bahir babhūva*  
*dhyānādi-prakaṭam apohya vipra-mukhyaḥ \|*  
*unmīlaṁ guru-mahasaṁ mahāyatāṅgaṁ*  
*so’drākṣīn nija-purataḥ sthitaṁ pareśam \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing his call, Śrīvāsa, the best of brāhmaṇas, immediately came out of the room, breaking his meditation. Before him he saw the Lord with beautiful limbs and a great effulgence.**

### Text 40

*udbhrāntaḥ prakaṭa-nija-prakāśa-vegair*  
*ajyadbhir mahita tanūr navair mahobhiḥ \|*  
*pāthobhiḥ sura-sarito mamābhiṣekaṁ*  
*śīghraṁ kurv iti nijagāda gauracandraḥ \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra, shining with profuse, unrestricted bright light, with fresh splendor, with a pure form, said “Quickly perform my abhiṣeka using Gaṅgā water.”**

### Text 41

*tac chrutvā sapadi sahodarair amuṣya*  
*śrīrāma-prabhṛtibhir utsukair mahadbhiḥ \|*  
*tac-ceṣṭā-sukha-vivaśais tad āhriyanta*  
*dravyāṇi svayam iva jagmur āhṛtatvam \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, immediately Śrīvāsa’s brothers headed by Śrīrāma, with great enthusiasm, absorbed in the happiness of the activities, gathered the items. The items seemed to gather themselves and come.**

### Text 42

*tat kaiścin nava-kalasī-śataṁ samantād*  
*ājahre jhaṭiti tathā jalaiḥ pupūre \|*

**Перевод:**

**The brothers quickly brought a hundred new pots and women who had quickly gathered filled them with water, going quickly to the Gaṅgā to take the water.**

### Text 43

*gambhārī-viracita-pīṭha-madhya-rājī*

**Перевод:**

**Gaurāṅga, desiring to be bathed, shone in the midst of the dais made of gambhāri wood in the courtyard. He was like Mount Meru situated in the Milk Ocean.**

### Text 44

*ānītair atilaghu-jahnu-kanyakāyāḥ*  
*pāthobhiḥ surabhi-suvāsitaiḥ prakāmam \|*

*śrīvāsas tam abhiṣiṣeca hṛṣṭa-cittaḥ \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa then joyfully bathed the Lord with pleasing Gaṅgā water brought by the women, which was scented with fragrant camphor, āguru, and other precious items.**

### Text 45

*drāghiṣṭhair niravadhi-śaśvad-unmiṣadbhis*

*gaurāṅgī-kriyata ivābhiṣeka-vārām \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The streams of water that fell on Gaurāṅga’s body became golden because of the intense golden effulgence emanating from his limbs.**

### Text 46

*gaṅgānāṁ kalasa-śatena saj-jalānāṁ*  
*seko’yaṁ jhaṭiti paṭīvad aṅga-bhājam \|*

*ucchvāsaiḥ subahu-kṛtārthatāṁ jagāma \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The pure Gaṅgā bath water from a hundred pots touched Gaurāṅga’s limbs. The earth took that water as her clothing and felt increasingly successful.**

### Text 47

*snānānte vara-vasanena sārayitvā*  
*gātrāmbhaḥ kara-yugalena tasya paścāt \|*  
*śrīvāsas tanutara-śubhra-śuddha-vāso-*  
*dvandvena prasaravatā sukhena bheje \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After the bath Śrīvāsa removed the water from his Mahāprabhu’s using his hands and the best cloth. He attained happiness by offering two pieces of thin, pure, white cloth to him.**

### Text 48

*śrī-gauras tanu-vasana-dvayaṁ gṛhītvā*  
*nīhāra-pracaya-supṛkta-meru-śobhām \|*  
*jagrāhodbhaṭa-mahasā mahīyasāsau*  
*sambhinno drutam aviśac ca deva-veśma \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Wearing the two pieces of fine cloth, Gaurāṅga was like Meru Mountain wrapped in a mist. Shining with great effulgence, he quickly entered the temple.**

### Text 49

*tasyāntaḥ sapadi niviśya gauracandraḥ*  
*paryaṅke lalita-rucau mahāmahasvān \|*  
*devānāṁ pratikṛti-sañcayaṁ samantād*  
*ākṣipya svayam akarot sukhopaveśam \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Entering within, radiant Gauracandra comfortably sat on an attractive cot, defeating the deities residing there by his beauty.**

### Text 50

*aprāpyāvisaram amuṣya veśma-madhye*  
*tejobhir bahir api sandhibhir vyabhedi \|*  
*tat-kāle jana-nicayasya harṣa-rāśiḥ*  
*svāntāntaḥ pulaka-bharair bahir babhūva \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Not finding sufficient space inside the room, the effulgence left the room through the cracks. It seemed that the joy of the people in the room externalized as hair standing on end.**

### Text 51

*sarve tat-samayam avāpya harṣa-magnā*  
*gaurāṅgaḥ parivividus trilokanātham \|*  
*śrī-vaṁśī-dhvanim atha śuśruvuś ca sarve*  
*ramyaṁ tan-mukha-kamalodgataṁ cirāya \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the people became absorbed in bliss. They understood that Gaurāṅga was the Lord of the three worlds. For a long time, they heard the sound of a flute emanating from his lotus mouth.**

### Text 52

*tat tāpe sukham atulaṁ samasta-lokair*  
*āsede pulaka-kulair athoñcad-aṅgam \|*  
*sambheje nayana-jalaiḥ sa romaharṣaḥ*  
*śrī-gaure jayati tathā-vidhe tadānīm \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Feeling unparalleled happiness, all people’s hairs stood on end. At that time, their hairs standing on end mixed with their tears and with shouts of “Jaya” directed to Gauracandra.**

### Text 53

*gaurāṅgo’vadad atha bhūsuraika-ratnaṁ*

*etasmād bhavana-varād bhavad-gṛhāntar*  
*yāsyāmīty atha satata-prakāśa-ramyaḥ \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gaurāṅga, emanating great splendor and effulgence, then said to Śrīvāsa, the jewel among brāhmaṇas, “I will go from this excellent room to your house.”**

### Text 54

*tac chrutvā jhaṭiti sahodaraiḥ samastais*  
*tad-gehaṁ sukha-vivaśaiḥ samaskṛtoccaiḥ \|*  
*madhya-dvāri ca bahu-veṣṭanais tathā tair*  
*āvavre bhavati sugopitaṁ yathā tat \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Śrīvāsa’s younger brothers, quickly cleansed and decorated with room in joy, and covered the middle door, so that it one could not see inside.‘**

### Text 55

*śrīvāsas tad anu gadādharaṁ babhāṣe*  
*khaṭvādyaṁ sakalam amutra nīyatāṁ tat \|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa said to Gadādhara, “Bring all the furniture include the bed from your house.” Gadādhara with great prema, forgetting his actions constantly, brought everything there.**

### Text 56

*sac-candrātapam upari pratatya tūrṇaṁ*  
*tasyānte surucira-cāmarāṇi tene \|*  
*paryaṅkopari kaśipūttamaṁ nipātya*  
*śrīmadbhir vara-vasanair athānuvavre \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the room, they quickly spread a canopy above and arranged attractive cāmaras. They placed a cushion on the couch with beautiful cloth and offered this to the Lord.**

### Text 57

*gaurāṅgas tad atha gṛhaṁ vrajan vireje*  
*tejobhir laghu tirayan vivasvad-ojaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Gaurāṅga entered the room and defeated the sun with his effulgence. He was like a thousand lightning bolts lighting up the earth.**

*pādāmbhoruha-yugalaṁ vilāsa-pūrvaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**Gracefully, the glorious Lord placed his feet on the ground and sat on the attractive couch. He was like the pinnacle of Meru Mountain putting other mountains behind.**

### Text 59

*sad-gauraiḥ parama-mahobhir unmiṣadbhiḥ*

*nyakkurvanty aniśam ilāvṛtasya śobhām \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The whole town, pervaded by his great pure golden effulgence, shone. This place criticized with destructive intent the beauty of Ilāvṛta-varṣa created by Brahmā.**

### Text 60

*kaiścid vā paripipiṣe na gandha-sāras*

**Перевод:**

**Did not someone grind sandalwood? Did not someone prepare betel? Did not someone collect hundreds of flowers at that time? Did not the whole earth become filled with festivals?**

*karpūrair marica-sitābhir apy akhaṇḍā-*

**Because of joy did they not spread a continuous experience of bliss like the taste produced by mixing milk with camphor, pepper and sugar?**

### Text 62

*sotkaṇṭhaṁ sapadi gadādhareṇa puṣpaiḥ*

*sva-svāntair iva sa manorathair jugumphe \|\|62\|\|*

**Gadādhara was absorbed with longing in skillfully stringing garlands using attractive, fragrant flowers. It seemed he was stringing a garland of desires with his mind.**

### Text 63

*uttaṁsaṁ kuṭila-kacocitaṁ vataṁsau*

**He skillfully made a crown for the Lord’s curly hair, two attractive earrings, an attractive flower necklace and a beautiful forehead ornament.**

**He made an attractive belt, anklets and ornaments for the Lord’s toes in an instant. With longing, he decorated Gaurāṅga’s body with these, taking a long time.**

### Text 66

**The infinitely fortunate Gadādhara with longing then applied camphor, āguru, sandalwood and kuṁkuma thickly to the Lord’s body from his toes to forehead.**

### Text 69

*dvārāgre’jira-bhuvi veṣṭanāni dṛṣṭvā*

**Seeing the crowd spread everywhere in the yard, the wives of Śrīvāsa and others felt pain, thinking, “We will not be able to see the Lord.”**

### Text 70

*gaurāṅgaḥ sapadi tathāvidhā viditvā*

**Understanding their thoughts, Gaurāṅga considered, “Will all the pious wives of the brāhmaṇas not be able to enter the house?” He then gave the order, “Let them come in.”**

*śrīvāsas tad anu nideśam etadīyaṁ*

**Understanding Gaurāṅga’s order, Śrīvāsa immediately called all the women in joy. In a flurry they entered the room shyly with joy in order to see the Lord.**

*āviśya prakaṭita-sat-prakāśa-ramyaṁ*

**Entering the room, seeing the Lord manifesting his pure, attractive form, incomparable and most astonishing, they became overjoyed and, fully satisfied, fell on the ground, surrendering fully to his lotus feet.**

**Перевод:**

**Saying repeatedly, “Concentrate your mind on me always,” Gaurāṅga, the ocean of the greatest qualities, soft with a sprinkling of the sweet rasa of mercy, offered his lotus feet to their heads.**

### Text 74

*tair etair atimahatāṁ satāṁ mahadbhiḥ*

**When Śrīvāsa and others, the greatest among the great devotees, saw the pure manifestation of the Lord, they began to think that he was the personification of all the joy in their hearts.**

*sarve tac-caraṇa-saroruhāṁ samīpaṁ*

**They offered gold articles to his lotus feet. By that offering, the cot suddenly became a desire tree flowing with jewels.**

*kārpāsaṁ vasana-yugaṁ jahau nivīya*

**Taking off his two pieces of cotton cloth, golden Gaurāṅga donned beautiful silk cloth. The ocean of mercy gave the cotton cloth to the brāhmaṇas’ wives with affection.**

### Text 77

*bhūyo’nyac chuci vasanaṁ dadau prasannaḥ*

*paryaṅkopari paritasthivān vilāsī*

**Перевод:**

**Pleased, he gave other pieces of cloth to the dearest devotees as prasādam. Shining on the cot, he made all the articles shine brightly.**

**He quickly took off the unguents and put on fragrant sandalwood. He took off the garlands worn for an instant and by himself put on new ones.**

**Chewing betel, he gave it up after chewing it thoroughly and filled the spittoon. Repeatedly the wives of brāhmaṇas emptied the spittoon.**

**The Lord, the ocean of mercy, smelled articles with good scent, ate foods which were worthy of eating, and accepted suitable articles. Gaurāṅga performed all actions with grace.**

### Text 81

**The merciful Lord gave the order to Advaita, “You should all immediately dance.” Hearing this, joyful Advaita danced with great, overjoyed devotees, who were singing.**

*śrīvāsodita-samupāgatā sakampaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa brought Śacī, famous as Mahāprabhu’s mother, who was the mother of the whole universe. She trembled in fear in front of the Lord at that time.**

### Text 83

**Перевод:**

**Seeing his mother, Mahāprabhu suddenly bent his lotus face in alarm. Śrīvāsa, on seeing this, felt sorrow in his heart and spoke to Gauracandra timidly.**

### Text 84

*naivedaṁ parama-dayasya te kṛpālor*  
*yogyaṁ ced vayam api kutra te bhavāmaḥ \|*

*tat paścāt tvaritam uvāca tāṁ ca vipraḥ \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“This is not suitable for you, the most merciful Lord. If it is, what are we to you? O best master! Showing your powerful aspect is not proper.” Then he quickly spoke to Śacī.**

### Text 85

*āgaccha prathamaṁ nipatya bhūmi-pṛṣṭhe*
