*nāyaṁ te suta iti manyatāṁ nipatya*  
*kṣmā-pṛṣṭhe tvaritam iti priyaṁ jagāda \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Come and first fall on the ground.” Hearing this, she delayed. He said with affection, “Falling on the ground immediately, do not consider him to be your son.”**

### Text 86

*ity evaṁ parikalayanty asau nipatya*  
*kṣmā-pṛṣṭhe prabhum anamat tadaiva devī \|*  
*śrīvāsas tad-avasare jagāda nāthaṁ*  
*sāśaṅkaṁ druta-hṛdayo bhayena dhīraḥ \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this Śacī fell on the ground and offered respects to the Lord. Wise Śrīvāsa then spoke in the Lord in fear.**

### Text 87

*kāruṇyaṁ kuru bhagavan prabho tad asyai*  
*yeneyaṁ tvayi na karoti putra-bhāvam \|*  
*yeneyaṁ tava caraṇe bhavet prapannā*  
*tenaiva prabhavati nirvṛtir mamāpi \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord! O master! Show mercy to Śacī so that she does not think of you as her son and so that she will surrender to you. Then I can be happy.”**

### Text 88

*ity ukte sati sahasā mahāśayo’syā*  
*mūrdhni śrī-yuta-pada-paṅkajaṁ sa nāthaḥ \|*  
*ādhāya prathita-kṛpas tathaiva tasyai*  
*kāruṇyaṁ parikalayann uvāca hṛṣṭaḥ \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When he said this, the merciful Lord suddenly placed his lotus feet on her head. Showing mercy to her, he spoke in joy.**

### Text 89

*spṛṣṭvaitat-pada-kamale tadaiva ciraṁ*  
*netrābhyām abhidadhatī jalaṁ garīyaḥ \|*  
*vibhrāntā pulakita-deha-yaṣṭir āsīt*  
*soddāmaṁ naṭana-parā hata-trapaiva \|\|89\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“On touching my lotus feet, immediately she cries profusely. Confused, her hairs stand on end. Without shyness she dances with enthusiasm.”**

### Text 90

*etaiḥ sā bahuvidha-ceṣṭayā prasahya*  
*vyāvṛttā suciram ivāpa citta-dhairyam \|*  
*krandantī nayana-jalena dhauta-dehā*  
*sambhinnā sabhayam asau jagāma geham \|\|90\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After a long time, Śacī recovered her composure with great endeavors of many types. Crying, with tears washing her body, she returned home alone in fear.**

### Text 91

*unnidra-prathama-saroja-patra-netro*  
*gaurāṅgaḥ parama-vilāsavān kṛpāvān \|*  
*yāminyā vigata-kṛśa-dvi-yāmavatyās*  
*tad yāma-dvayam anayat tathā vihāraiḥ \|\|91\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With eyes like freshly blossoming lotuses, most attractive, merciful Gaurāṅga, after two praharas of the night had passed, spent two more prahāras relaxing with the devotees.**

### Text 92

*āśleṣaiḥ kati ca tathaiṣa kāṁścid anyān*  
*ācumbais tad anu ca carvitais tathānyān \|*  
*ity evaṁ parama-kṛpā-nidhiḥ sutṛptān*  
*cakre sad-vilasita-līlayā mahatā \|\|92\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great ocean of mercy satisfied some by embraces, some by kisses and some by giving chewed items as part of his glorious, beautiful pastimes.**

### Text 93

*ity evaṁ punar api devatālaye’sau*  
*saṅgatya kṣaṇam avatasthivān vireje \|*  
*tat-paścād atikaruṇaḥ kramāc ca tūrṇāṁ*  
*bhrātṝṇām api caturo gṛhān jagāma \|\|93\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He again went to the temple and remained there for a short time. Then the merciful Lord went swiftly to the houses of the four brothers.**

### Text 94

*ity evaṁ bahu vilasan kṛta-prakāśo*  
*bhūyo’pi prabhur adhigamya deva-geham \|*  
*tān sarvān avadad alaṁ vilambitais tad*  
*gacchāmīty atikamanīya-gaura-dehaḥ \|\|94\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having revealed himself and having performed many pastimes, the beautiful golden Lord again entered the temple. He spoke to all present. “Without delay I will go away.”**

### Text 95

*tac chrutvā vacanam amuṣya te samastā*  
*advaita-prabhṛtaya evam evam ūcuḥ \|*  
*evaṁ ced vayam api tad-gale kṛpāṇaṁ*  
*baddhaitat sapadi śarīram ājahīmaḥ \|\|95\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing these words Advaita and all others said, “If you do this, we will all put swords to our throats and immediately give up our bodies.”**

### Text 96

*gaurāṅgo’py atha hasitaṁ vidhāya sa drāg*  
*ity etat kim iti kim āttha vākyam etat \|*  
*uktvaivaṁ kṣaṇam avatasthivān dharaṇyāṁ*  
*huṅkāraiḥ saha nipapāta citram etat \|\|96\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Smiling, Gaurāṅga immediately said, “What are you saying?” Remaining there, he fell on the ground roaring for a long time.**

### Text 97

*ity evaṁ bhuvi suciraṁ viluṭhya nātho*  
*niśceṣṭaḥ samajani hema-gaura-dehaḥ \|*  
*tat-kāla-cyutam iva kāñcanācalasya*  
*kṣmā-pṛṣṭhe jvalad-aniśaṁ manojña-śṛṅgam \|\|97\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord with golden body remained on the ground for a long time without moving at all. He was like an attractive peak of the constantly shining Golden Mountain, which had fallen on the ground.**

### Text 98

*bhūyo’yaṁ mṛdi ca viluṭhya catvarāntaḥ*  
*saṁmūrcchann iva virarāma ramya-mūrtiḥ \|*  
*ceṣṭādyaṁ na kim api nottaraṁ ca kiñcinn*  
*aspandaḥ śvasita-samīraṇaś ca naiva \|\|98\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the yard the beautiful form lay on the ground, in a deep faint. There was no movement, no response, no pulse and no breathing.**

### Text 99

*cikṣepa kṣitisu yathā bhujau tathā tau*  
*tādṛkṣāv iva kila tasthatuś cirāya \|*  
*tasthau śrī-pada-yugalaṁ tathā yathāsau*  
*cikṣepa kṣaṇam anu vismṛtāṅga-ceṣṭaḥ \|\|99\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His arms remained on the earth just as when he had fell for a long time. His feet remained just as when he fell. For some time he lost complete use of his limbs.**

### Text 100

*ity evaṁ bhavati sati kṣapāvyapāye*  
*paryāsīt sapadi raviḥ samudgato’bhūt \|*  
*mūrcchābhir gata-sakala-kriyaḥ prakāmaṁ*  
*naivāyaṁ prakṛtim avāpa gauracandraḥ \|\|100\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this condition, night ended, and the sun quickly rose. All his activities had ceased because of his fainting. Gauracandra still did not regain consciousness.**

### Text 101

*te sarve param aparaṁ sahasra-bhārair*  
*duḥkhānāṁ kim iti kim ity udīrayantaḥ \|*  
*niśceṣṭaṁ prabhum avalokya bhūmi-pṛṣṭhe*  
*svinnāṅgāḥ parimumuhur drutaṁ samantāt \|\|101\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They all said loudly in pain, “What has happened?” Seeing the Lord motionless on the ground, they perspired and became completely disturbed.**

### Text 102

*yātaiṣā sapadi niśā samudgato’rkaḥ*  
*sampanno’pi ca ghaṭikārdha eṣa so’pi \|*  
*yāmārdhas tad anu ca yāma eṣa bhūto*  
*hā hā kiṁ tad api bubodha naiṣa nāthaḥ \|\|102\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Night has passed and the sun has risen. Half a ghaṭika has passed. Half a yāma has passed. Now a yāma has passed. What has happened? The Lord has not woken up.”**

### Text 103

*ity etat satatam udīrayanta ete*  
*duḥkhārtāś calita iti pratepur uccaiḥ \|*  
*saṁruddhe parama-dṛḍhe kavāṭa-bandhe*  
*tat-puryāṁ tam abhinveśya te niṣeduḥ \|\|103\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this constantly, filled with sorrow, they loudly lamented, “He has gone.” They placed him in a sturdy room with a door in the house and remained waiting.**

### Text 104

*advaitas tv atha śata-huṅkṛtaiḥ kareṇa*  
*kṣiptāmbho vadanam amuṣya saṁsiṣeca \|*  
*gaurāṅgas tad api na bodhatām avāpa*  
*spandaṁ niḥśvasita-samīraṇaṁ na cāpi \|\|104\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Advaita, roaring loudly, sprinkled water on his face with his hand but the Lord did not recover consciousness, tremble or begin breathing.**

### Text 105

*cintābhir manasi vibhāvya kīrtanaṁ tac*  
*cakrus te madhu-madhuraṁ sudhīra-dhīram \|*  
*tac chrutvā sa tu cira-kālam eva nātho*  
*nahy eva prakṛtim iyāya gauracandraḥ \|\|105\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The worried devotees began to perform soft, sweet kīrtana. Hearing that for a long time, the Lord still did not recover.**

### Text 106

*aśrāntaṁ śravaṇa-pathaiḥ praviśya cetas*  
*tasyaitat sumadhura-kīrtanāmṛtena \|*  
*taiḥ sārdhaṁ sukhita-manobhir atra bhūyaḥ*  
*santene sapadi tanūruheṣu harṣaḥ \|\|106\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Tirelessly, the nectar of sweet kīrtana entered by the ears into his heart. Suddenly his hairs stood on end, along with theirs, as they rejoiced.**

### Text 107

*yad dharṣaiḥ samam udbhūtaṁ sa romaharṣo*  
*gaurasya prakṛtim upeyuṣaḥ samantāt \|*  
*tad-duḥkhaiḥ samam api nibharair vivṛttiṁ*  
*pārśvasya prabhur akarot krameṇa tatra \|\|107\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gaurāṅga had regained consciousness, with hairs standing on end in bliss. But then, the Lord turned over while they lamented to the extreme.**

### Text 108

*gaurāṅgaś ciram anubhūya kīrtanaṁ tat*  
*pravyaktaṁ dṛḍha-śayitaḥ śanair udasthāt \|*  
*tair bhūyas tyajati sati prabhau prakāśā-*  
*viṣkāraṁ vyaghaṭi tadāsya veśa-bhūṣā \|\|108\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A long time later, after manifesting deep sleep, Gaurāṅga became aware of the kīrtana and slowly got up. When the Lord had finished his revelation as the Lord, his ornaments and clothing had come undone.**

### Text 109

*utthāya prabhur atha deva-geha-bhittiṁ*  
*saṁhṛtya prakaṭa-nija-prakāśa-tejaḥ \|*  
*bhūyo’sau mṛdu-madhurāṁ dadhāra lakṣmīṁ*  
*naidāgho ravir iva śāradendur āsīt \|\|109\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Climbing onto the partition of the temple, the Lord withdrew his effulgence and again showed his soft, sweet beauty, like the summer sun becoming the autumn sun.**

### Text 110

*āśvasya kṣaṇam atha danta-sat-prasūna-*  
*dyotais tair adhara-dale vibhedayan saḥ \|*  
*pratyūce cira-śayito yathā prabuddho*  
*nidrānte kim api kathañcanāpy ajānan \|\|110\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Recovering slightly, he parted his lips, showing the luster of his flower-like teeth, and answered as if he had woken up after a long sleep, without being aware of anything.**

### Text 111

*etāvān kim u samayaḥ suṣupti-bhājā*  
*nidrāyām ati gamito mayā cirāyava \|*  
*pratyūṣe yad aham apāṭhayaṁ dvijātīn*  
*āścaryaṁ kim iti tad eva saṁsmarāmi \|\|111\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O devotees! Was I in deep sleep for all this time? I slept for a long time. I remember that every morning I have taught the brāhmaṇas. How astonishing!”**

### Text 112

*sotprāsaṁ tad anu jagāda gauracandraṁ*  
*śrīvāso vimalaṁ manāg vihasya \|*  
*nedānīṁ prabhavitum arhati tvadīyā*  
*māyeyaṁ viditatamā babhūva bhūyaḥ \|\|112\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He laughed loudly. Śrīvāsa laughing slightly then spoke to Gauracandra. “You do not need to show your power. I have understood well this māyā.”**

### Text 113

*tac chrutvā kim iti kim āttha kiṁ nu vā mām*  
*ity evaṁ parihasasi prakāmam eva \|*  
*nojāne kṣaṇam api kiñcid etad etat*  
*pratyūce sacakitam eva gauracandraḥ \|\|113\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Gauracandra said timidly, “What is this? What did you say? You laugh loudly. I do not for a moment understand this at all.”**

### Text 114

*yāmānāṁ trayam iti sambabhuva tatrā-*  
*tītair aṣṭabhir api sārdham atra yāmaiḥ \|*  
*na snānaṁ na ca gṛha-karma nānya-ceṣṭā*  
*no nidrā na ca śayanaṁ tadā janasya \|\|114\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Three praharas of the day and eight praharas of the previous day had passed. The devotees during this time had not bathed, performed household duties or other actions. They had not slept or rested.**

### Text 115

*ity ekādhika-daśabhiḥ sudīrgha-dīrghair*  
*yāmais tair nimiṣa ivābhavat sa kālaḥ \|*  
*eteṣu kṣaṇam api pakṣaṇāṁ vivṛttir*  
*naivāsīt sukha-mahatāṁ tadā janānām \|\|115\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The long duration of eleven praharas had passed in a second. The devotees however in great happiness had not blinked their eyes at all.**

### Text 116

*netrābhyāṁ ciram upavāsa-saspṛhābhyāṁ*  
*śrotrābhyāṁ vadhiratayā vivarjitābhyām \|*  
*svāntena prathama-samudgatena lokā*  
*nispandā iva satataṁ babhūvur ete \|\|116\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All people became motionless for a long time, their eyes not seeing, their ears not hearing, and their minds inoperable like those of new born infants.**

### Text 117

*aśrāntaṁ gata-nimiṣaṁ vilokayantyo*  
*gaurāṅgāhita-parama-prasāda-mugdhāḥ \|*  
*dehādi kṣaṇam api naiva sasmarus tā*  
*bāhyāntaḥ-pramada-bhareṇa vipra-patnyaḥ \|\|117\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wives of brāhmaṇas looked at the Lord with unblinking eyes continually. Happy with the great mercy given by Gaurāṅga, with external and internal joy, they forgot their bodies for a short time.**

### Text 118

*ity evaṁ parama-rahasyam īkṣamāṇāḥ*  
*kṣut-tṛṣṇā-paribhavam eva nāpur ete \|*  
*kiṁ caitat kṣaṇam iva ced dina-dvayaṁ syāt*  
*tat kiṁ kṣut-prabhṛtibhir atra deha-dharmaiḥ \|\|118\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Beholding this great secret, the devotees did not have urges of hunger of thirst. Two days felt like a moment. What is the use of hunger, thirst and other bodily urges?**

### Text 119

*atrānte parama-sukhena sajjayitvā*  
*gātrodvartana-para-vastu-datta-cittaiḥ \|*  
*snānāya prati vidadhe tathodyamaṁ tair*  
*gaurāṅgaḥ parama-kṛpā-rasāmbu-rāśiḥ \|\|119\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Remembering to collect excellent ingredients for massaging his body, the devotees made preparations with great happiness. Gaurāṅga, the ocean of great mercy, then prepared to bathe with the devotees.**

### Text 120

*snānānte nija-nija-veśma jagmur ete*  
*gaurāṅgaḥ punar api tasya veśma gatvā \|*  
*śrī-rāma-prabhṛti-sahodaraiś caturbhis*  
*tat-patnībhir api samarhito rarāja \|\|120\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After bathing, they all returned to their houses but Gaurāṅga went to Śrīvāsa’s house and was nicely worshipped by the four brothers and their wives.**

### Text 121

*srag-gandhair vara-vasanaiś ca bhūṣaṇaiś ca*  
*śrīkhaṇḍa-drava-sahitaiś ca dhīra-paṅkaiḥ \|*  
*snehena pratidina-nūtanena dattair*  
*gaurāṅgaḥ sukham atulaṁ jagāma bhūyaḥ \|\|121\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gaurāṅga became very satisfied when they daily offered, with fresh affection, garlands, incense, excellent cloth, ornaments and sandalwood and saffron paste.**

### Text 122

*pratyaṅgaṁ tanum anulipya candanena*  
*srag-vṛndair api vapur asya bhūṣayitvā \|*  
*sad-vāso’pi ca paridhāpya sūkṣma-śubhraṁ*  
*yad yogyaṁ tad api sukhena bhojayitvā \|\|122\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They anointed his limbs with sandalwood, decorated his body with many garlands, dressed him in fine, white cloth and fed him suitable food in joy.**

### Text 123

*pratyagrāṁ pratidivasaṁ tad arpayitvā*  
*tāṁ prītiṁ dvija-vṛṣabhāś ca tat-striyaś ca \|*  
*āsedur nirupama-bhāgya-sindhu-pūrair*  
*aśrāntaṁ parimilitaṁ pramoda-vṛndam \|\|123\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The best brāhmaṇas and their wives offered this with affection every day and attained continual bliss, with an ocean of incomparable good fortune.**

### Text 124

*ity evaṁ sahaja-nija-prakāśa-tejaḥ*  
*sandarśya sthira-karaṇaś ciraṁ vilasya \|*  
*svaṁ gehaṁ madhura-mukho yayau tato’yaṁ*  
*mātus tāṁ mudam atinirbharāṁ vitanvan \|\|124\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Showing his natural bright effulgence, dallying for a long time, fixed in his mind, the sweet-faced Lord then returned to his house and made his mother extremely happy.**

### Text 125

*ity evaṁ pracura-kṛpāmṛtaṁ vitanvan*  
*jyaiṣṭhādy-aṣṭabhir atisammadena māsaiḥ \|*  
*pauṣāntaṁ naṭana-rasair nidāgha-varṣair*  
*hemantaṁ saha śaradā nināya nāthaḥ \|\|125\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord spread the nectar of his deep mercy from Jyeṣṭha month for eight months till Pauṣa, with the rasa of dancing, during summer, monsoon, autumn and winter.**

### Text 126

*ṛtūnām eteṣāṁ pratidinam athānukṣaṇam asau*  
*prabhur māsaṁ māsaṁ prati yad akaron nartana-rasam \|*  
*tad etan naivāyaṁ kathayitum alaṁ kiṁ punar aho*  
*manuṣyās tu kṣudrāḥ sura-guru-sahasraṁ kva nu punaḥ \|\|126\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Every day during those four seasons, continually the Lord, month after month, displayed the rasa of dancing. Even Mahāprabhu cannot describe this, what to speak of puny humans. Even Bṛhaspati cannot describe it.**

### Text 127

*śrīvāsālaya eva nṛtyati sadā tad-bhrātṛbhir nirbharaṁ*  
*gāyadbhir hari-kīrtanāmṛta-rasaṁ śrī-gauracandraḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*tat-saṅgāmṛta-dīrghikā-niravadhi-snātās tad-āsyodgataṁ*  
*vāk-pīyūṣam amī nipīya bahudhā nityaṁ vijahrus tathā \|\|127\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord always danced at Śrīvāsa’s house with the four brothers, who sang kīrtana with sweet rasa. They continually bathed in the lake of his sweet association and drank the nectar of his words emanating from his mouth. In this way they continually performed pastimes.**

### Text 128

*sa tu gadādhara-paṇḍita-sattamaḥ*  
*satatam asya samīpa-susaṅgataḥ \|*  
*anudinaṁ bhajate nija-jīvata-priya-*  
*tamaṁ tam atispṛhayā yutam \|\|128\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great devotee Gadādhara was always in close association with the Lord. He daily worshipped the Lord, his dear life, with intense desire.**

### Text 129

*niśi tadīya-samīpa-gataḥ sthiraḥ*  
*śayanam utsuka eva karoti saḥ \|*  
*viharaṇāmṛtam asya nirantaraṁ*  
*sad-upabhuktam anena nirantaram \|\|129\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At night Gadādhara would fondly sleep near the Lord. The Lord continually enjoyed his continually sweet pastimes.**

## Chapter Six

### Text 1

*śrīvāsa-geham upagamya kadācid eṣa*  
*vyākhyāṁ cakāra tad-anantaram eva nāmnām \|*  
*māhātmyam udbhaṭam idaṁ puruṣārtha-sarva-*  
*śreṣṭhaṁ śruti-prakara-durlabha-modam ādau \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One day the Lord came to Śrīvāsa’s house and explained the holy name and its great glories. The name was the best of the puruṣārthas and the rarest joy in the Vedas.**

### Text 2

*svīye vilāsa-rasa-navya-mahāmbu-rāśau*  
*nityaṁ kutūhala-paro vijihīrṣur eṣaḥ \|*  
*ādau sva-nāma-mahimāmṛta-ramya-pūraṁ*  
*harṣād vaco’ñjali-puṭair jagati vyakārīt \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Desiring to play in the great, fresh ocean of his playful rasa, first in joy he scattered over the universe the pleasant nectar of the glories of his name, by an offering of words.**

### Text 3

*harer nāma harer nāma harer nāmaiva kevalam \|*  
*kalau nāsty eva nāsty eva nāsty eva gatir anyathā \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In Kali-yuga there is no other way, no other way, no other way, than chanting the name of the Lord, chanting the name of the Lord, chanting the name of the Lord.**

### Text 4

*nādyaḥ pumān ayam udeti sadaiva bhūmau*  
*nāma-svarūpam iti taṁ tu kalau vidantu \|*  
*vāra-traye ca punar-uktir athaiva-kāro*  
*dārḍhyāya sarva-jagato bahu-jāḍya-bhājaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This form of the Lord or ādi-puruṣa does not manifest at all times on earth. Let the people understand the real nature of the name in Kali-yuga. The repetition of** ***nāma*** **three times and the repetition of the word** ***eva*** **three times are used to emphasis the process to the whole universe, which is filled with great ignorance.**

### Text 5

*kaivalyam eva tad idaṁ tv iti kevalasya*  
*śabdasya dārḍhya-manane pratipādanaṁ tat \|*  
*yas tv anyathā vadati tasya gatir hi nāsti*  
*nāsty eva niścitam idaṁ punar eva-kārāt \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The word** ***kevalam*** **is used in the sense of “only” to reinforce the idea. If one speaks of some other method (*****anyathā*****), that does not exist (*****nāsti*****). The word** ***eva*** **is used to confirm that there is no other way.**

### Text 6

*ity ūcivān atha kṛta-prakaṭa-prakāśaḥ*  
*śrīmad-varāsanam upetya kṛpā-samudraḥ \|*  
*pādāravinda-yugalena manorameṇa*  
*śrī-rāma-paṇḍita-mukhān sama aspṛśad drāk \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, the ocean of mercy who had revealed himself then sat on an excellent seat and touched Śrī-rāma and others with his attractive lotus feet.**

### Text 7

*te’pi praṇamya sahasā nata-kandhareṇa*  
*prema-svarūpa-bhajanaṁ muditāḥ samīyuḥ \|*  
*tebhyo dadāv abhimataṁ bhagavān prakāmaṁ*  
*śrīmān sva-bhakta-jana-vatsalatātiramyaḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Immediately offering respects with lowered shoulders, they joyfully accepted this worship which is the form of prema. Pleased with the affection of those devotees, the Lord bountifully gave them what they had desired.**

### Text 8

*śuklāmbaro dvija-ravaḥ subhago’tha kaścid*  
*ūce prabhuṁ prakaṭitātiśaya-prakāśam \|*  
*dvārāvatīṁ ca mathurāṁ ca sadaiva gatvā*  
*māṁ duḥkhinaṁ kṣaṇam avekṣya dayasva nātha \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The best of brāhmaṇas, fortunate Śuklāmbara, then spoke to the Lord, who clearly manifested brightness. “O Lord! I always go to Dvārakā and Mathurā. Seeing that I am suffering, be merciful to me.”**

### Text 9

*kiṁ tatra santi na śṛgāla-cayās tataḥ kiṁ*  
*teṣāṁ bhavet kim atha te na punaḥ śṛgālāḥ \|*  
*ity uktavaty atha vibhau dvija-puṅgavo’yam*  
*uccaiḥ papāta bhuvi daṇḍavad utsukātmā \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Are there not many jackals there? What is the use of the holy places to them? Are they not just jackals?” The brāhmaṇa then eagerly fell on the ground.**

### Text 10

*bhūyaś ca bhūri-karuṇo nijagāda vipraṁ*  
*dīnānukampita-hṛdayo hṛdayaika-vettā \|*  
*adyaiva te’tra bhavitā prabhupāda-padme*  
*sa-prema-bhaktir iti gaura-sudhā-mayūkhaḥ \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra, full of mercy, whose heart is merciful to the miserable, who knows the heart of each person, said to the brāhmaṇa, “Today you will have prema for the lotus feet of the Lord.”**

### Text 11

*sadyo’tha tasya caraṇeṣu nipatya bhūyaḥ*  
*svidyan-manāḥ pulaka-sañcaya-pūritāṅgaḥ \|*  
*uccaiḥ-svareṇa bahulāśru-bharair vibhinno*  
*vāg-gadgadena ca ruroda mahānubhāvaḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great devotee with affectionate heart, immediately fell at his feet, with hairs standing on end all over his body. With tears and choked voice, speaking loudly, he cried.**

### Text 12

*śrīmān gadādhara-mahāmatir atyudāra-*  
*śīlaḥ svabhāva-madhuro bahu-śānta-mūrtiḥ \|*  
*ūce samīpa-śayitaḥ prabhunā rajanyāṁ*  
*nirmalyam etad urasi pratisāryam ebhyaḥ \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord spoke to intelligent, generous Gadādhara who had a sweet nature and was most peaceful, who slept near him at night. “Distribute the old garlands to them.”**

### Text 13

*itthaṁ sa yad yad adadāt pramadena yasmai*  
*yasmai janāya tad idaṁ sa gadādharo’pi \|*  
*prātar dadau satatam ullasitāya tasmai*  
*tasmai mahāprabhu-vimukta-mahā-prasādam \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gadādhara would joyfully give to the devotees Mahāprabhu’s remnants—whatever the Lord joyfully permitted.**

### Text 14

*saṅgrathya mālaya-nicayaṁ paricarya yatnāt*  
*sad-gandha-sāra-ghanasāra-varādi-paṅkam \|*  
*aṅgeṣu tasya pariyojayati sma nityaṁ*  
*sotkaṇṭham atra sa gadādhara-paṇḍitāgrataḥ \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Daily Gadādhara would eagerly make garlands, carefully prepare paste of sandalwood and kuṁkuma with scents, and apply these to his limbs.**

### Text 15

*sāyaṁ kadācid atha taiḥ sva-padābja-bhaktaiḥ*  
*śrī-gauracandra udito nija-kīrtanābdhau \|*  
*ākasmikair gagana-maṇḍalam ambuvāhair*  
*vyāptaṁ nirīkṣya karuṇo’jani vighna-bhītyā \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One time in the evening Gauracandra appeared in the ocean of kīrtana with his lotus devotees. Seeing the sky suddenly become completely covered with clouds, fearing an obstacle, he showed mercy.**

### Text 16

*ādāya pāṇi-kamaleṣv atha mandirāgryaṁ*  
*rāgān svarāṁś ca sakalān sa kṛtārthayitvā \|*  
*uccair jagau svaguṇa-sañcayam eva hṛṣṭaḥ*  
*śrīmān anaṅga iva vigrahavān pṛthivyām \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Like Cupid personified on earth, he took in his lotus hands excellent cymbals and in joy loudly sang the Lord’s glories with rāga and correct notes.**

### Text 17

*sadyas tadā jalamuco marutā prakīrṇā*  
*bhejur diśaṁ diśam amī saha citta-khedaiḥ \|*  
*vyomātinirmalam abhūd udiyāya candraḥ*  
*sārdhaṁ samasta-bha-gaṇena tamo’pahatyai \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Immediately the clouds, losing power, were scattered in all directions by the wind. The sky became clear and the moon rose with the stars to extinguish the darkness.**

### Text 18

*rajyan prasārita-karaḥ parirabhya gāḍhaṁ*  
*ramyāṁ kṣapā-nava-vadhūṁ vitamo’ntarīyām \|*  
*ānanda-sindhu-laharī-cayam ucchalantaṁ*  
*jyotsnām iṣād iva ramaty ayam oṣadhīśaḥ \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The shining moon, spreading its hands, firmly embraced its attractive new bride, the night, with a cloth composed of absence of darkness. The moon took pleasure in the light, which agitated the waves in the ocean of bliss.**

### Text 19

*gīrvāṇa-vartmani tadā vimale sadṛkṣaiḥ*  
*pīyūṣam udgirati tatra sudhā-mayūkhe \|*  
*śrī-gaura-śīta-kiraṇo’py avanau sva-loke*  
*saṅkīrtanāmṛta-rase ramati sma bhūyaḥ \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the moon with the stars poured out nectar in the clear sky, Gauraṅga with his shining rays, enjoyed the rasa of sweet kīrtana with his devotees on earth.**

### Text 20

*śrīmat-padābja-padavī-vara-haṁsakādyaiḥ*  
*pāṇi-pravāla-yugalaṁ valayair layaiś ca \|*  
*lātsyodgame sapadi manmatha-manmathasya*  
*śrī-gaura-śīta-kiraṇasya rarāja bhūyaḥ \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the bewilderer of Cupid, with golden rays began dancing, the place shone with the anklets on his lotus feet, with the bracelets on his pink arms and with the rhythm.**

### Text 21

*viprāṅganā-gaṇa-mukhendu-vinirgatais tair*  
*uccair ulūlu-ninadair jaya-nāda-miśraiḥ \|*  
*khe’vasthitasya diviṣan nicayasya harṣa-*  
*svānair atīva-tumulaḥ sumahotsavo’bhūt \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There was a tumultuous festivity with joyful voices of the devatās situated in the sky, mixed with the loud shouts of “Jaya” and ulū-dvāni emanating from the mouths of the wives of brāhmaṇas.**

### Text 22

*kundāravinda-karavīra-navīna-malli-*  
*jātyādi-puṣpa-nivahai rava-kḷpta-mālyaḥ \|*  
*śrīkhaṇḍa-kuṅkuma-lasan-mṛga-nābhi-paṅkair*  
*ālipya sarva-tanum eva rarāja nṛtyan \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he danced with garlands made of jasmine, karavīra, fresh malli and jāti flowers, his whole body shone with ointments of musk, sandalwood and kuṁkuma.**

### Text 23

*śuklāmbaraḥ sa tu nipatya dharātalāntaḥ*  
*śrī-gauracandram avadat sabhayaṁ mahātmā \|*  
*he nātha samprati kṛtā bhavatā navīna-*  
*dvīpaṁ navaiva mathurā vividhair vihāraiḥ \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śuklāmbara fell on the ground and said to the Lord with apprehension, “O Lord! With your many pastimes you have made Navadvīpa into a new Mathurā.”**

### Text 24

*ity uktavān bahala-gadgada-gadya-padya-*  
*vākyena bhūmim abhito galad-aśru-pūraḥ \|*  
*vaihvalya-dainya-hṛdayaḥ satataṁ vimukta-*  
*kaṇṭhaṁ ruroda bahuśaḥ stavanena tasya \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, with choked words, shedding tears on the earth, with confused, miserable heart, he loudly wept while praising the Lord profusely.**

### Text 25

*nṛtyan vayasya-rucirāṁse’taṭe’tipīnaṁ*  
*do-stambham arpayati sa kṣaṇam apy udāram \|*  
*uddāma-vepathu-calat-sakalāṅga-yaṣṭir*  
*bhūmau skhalaty anupadaṁ vivaśaḥ kṣaṇaṁ ca \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In dancing the Lord rested his generous, full arms for a moment on the attractive shoulder of a friend. Like a pole with hairs standing on end, he then stumbled on the ground out of control for a moment.**

### Text 26

*tebhyo varān kṣaṇam apīśvara-bhāva-ramyo*  
*bhūyo dadāti sadayaṁ sadayaika-sindhuḥ \|*  
*nānā-vidhair atikṛpā-rasa-sindhu-candro*  
*lokān aśikṣayad aśeṣa-vilāsa-bhāvaiḥ \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Enjoying the mood of being the Lord, Gaurāṅga, the ocean of mercy, mercifully gave gifts to the devotees. The moon in the ocean of mercy taught people in various ways by his unlimited moods during his pastimes.**

### Text 27

*āruhya sa kṣaṇam api svapadābja-bhakta-*  
*skandhaṁ mahāprabhur atīva-vikāra-ramyaḥ \|*  
*ākrīḍati svajana-harṣa-samudra-pūram*  
*ullāsayan niśi niśākara-koṭi-kāntaḥ \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu, attractive with extreme ecstatic symptoms, climbed the shoulders of his devotees who served his lotus feet. The Lord, more beautiful than a million moons, performed his pastimes, increasing the full ocean of joy of his devotees.**

### Text 28

*anyedyur udyad-ahimāṁśu-sahasra-bhāsvān*  
*bhūmau vasan kara-tala-dvaya-tāla-pūraiḥ \|*  
*sarvā diśaḥ pratiravonmukharāḥ samantāt*  
*kurvann uvāca nija-pāda-payoja-bhaktān \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Another day, shining like a thousand rising suns, he sat on the ground, filling the directions with the sound of karatālas held in his two hands. He then spoke to his devotees who served his lotus feet.**

### Text 29

*bhoḥ paśya paśya bhuvi ropitam āmra-bījaṁ*  
*cūtasya paśya punar aṅkura eṣa jātaḥ \|*  
*paśyaiṣa samprati babhūva vitasti-mātro*  
*bhūyo’pi paśya viṭapo’sya babhūva śīghram \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Look! Look! I planted a mango seed in the earth. The mango has sprouted. Look! Now it has become a foot high. Now look! It has manifested branches quickly.**

### Text 30

*śākhā babhūvur iha paśya nimeṣa-mātrāt*  
*paśyāsya pallava-cayaḥ parito babhūva \|*  
*paśyaitad eva paripakvam abhūd athāsya*  
*paśyābhavad grahaṇam apy aticitram etat \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Look! It has branches. In a second it has buds everywhere. Look! The fruit have become ripe. It is most astonishing to see.**

### Text 31

*vṛkṣaś ca sarva-viṭapaś ca phalaṁ ca sarvaṁ*  
*māyā-kṛtaṁ sakalam eva kuto’pi nāsti \|*  
*śailūṣa-ceṣṭitam idaṁ vitathaṁ yad etat*  
*tat-prāpta-vaikṛtam anarthakatāṁ prayāti \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The tree, all its branхches and the fruit are all illusory. They have no existence. They are illusions caused by a magician. They change and become useless.**

### Text 32

*etat tad apy amṛtam eva yad īśvarasya*  
*kautūhalāya purataḥ kurute janaughaḥ \|*  
*prāpnoti sad-vasanam ṛktham atiprakāmaṁ*  
*māyā-kṛtena ca phalaṁ labhate vicitram \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If men perform actions for the Lord’s pleasure, those actions becomes eternal. People obtain abundant fine cloth and great wealth by māyā but the result is amazing.**

### Text 33

*evaṁ hi viśvam akhilaṁ vitathaṁ yad etan*  
*niṣpādyate satatam īśvara-sevanāya \|*  
*tat sārthakaṁ bhavati samyag asatyam etat*  
*satyaṁ bhaved aśuci yat tad idaṁ śuci syāt \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If one offers the whole illusory universe for the service of the Lord constantly, it becomes meaningful. The false becomes real. The impure becomes pure.**

### Text 34

*tasmāj janaiḥ sakalam eva pareśvarasya*  
*sevārtham apy anṛtam etad ihāvaceyam \|*  
*saṁsāra eṣa nahi tasya bhaved virodhī*  
*sevā-paras tu nahi bādhyate eva kaiścit \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If one gathers everything false for the service of the Lord, saṁsāra no longer acts contrary to the person. The person engaged in service is never obstructed by anything.”**

### Text 35

*atrāntare sva-purataḥ sthitam atyudāraṁ*  
*proce mahā-karuṇa eṣa mukunda-dattam \|*  
*brahmeti kiṁ nu bhavatātra nirūpyate tad*  
*itthaṁ nigadya ca papāṭha punaḥ svayaṁ saḥ \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After this, the greatly merciful Lord then spoke to generous Mukunda Datta in front of him. “How do you define brahman? Saying this, he began to recite a verse.**

### Text 36

*tathā hi-ramante yogino’nante satyānanda-cid-ātmani \|*  
*iti rāma-padenāsau paraṁ brahmābhidhīyate \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The yogīs take pleasure in the Lord who is eternity, knowledge and bliss. Thus the supreme brahman is called Rāma.**

### Text 37

*bhūyo’pi taṁ samanuśiṣya jagāda nāthaḥ*  
*kiñcit krudhādhara-dala-dvaya-kampitena \|*  
*rūpaṁ caturbhujam atīva-varaṁ tato’nyan*  
*nyūnaṁ kiyad dvibhujam ity ayi kiṁ mataṁ te \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Teaching him again, the Lord spoke with his lips twitching in anger. “The four armed form of the Lord is supreme. The two armed form is somewhat inferior. What is your opinion?**

### Text 38

*yady ātmanor hitam avaiṣi tadā parasmât*  
*tad dvaibhujyaṁ varam iti pratikīrtaya tvam \|*  
*śrutvaiṣa tan nigaditaṁ karuṇā-vilāsi*  
*bhūmau nipatya nijagāda saharṣa-śaṅkam \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If you desire your benefit, then you should proclaim that the two armed form is best.” Hearing what the Lord said out of his mercy, Mukunda fell on the ground and spoke with joy and fear.**

### Text 39

*snātaṁ mayā sura-nadī-payasi prakāmaṁ*  
*śrī-vaiṣṇavāṅghri-rajasāṅgam alaṅkṛtaṁ ca \|*  
*śrīman tvadīya-pada-padma-yugātapatraṁ*  
*mūrdhni prayaccha kuru dāsya-pade’bhiṣekam \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I bathed plentifully in the waters of Gaṅgā and my limbs are ornamented with the dust from devotees’ feet. Please place the umbrella of your lotus feet on my head. Make me your servant.”**

### Text 40

*evaṁ niśamya karuṇā-rasa-pūrṇa-cetās*  
*tad vāk-sudhā-pramuditena tataḥ pareśaḥ \|*  
*śrīmat-padāmbuja-yugaṁ nija-lokanātham*  
*asyādadhāc chirasi pūtatame prasannaḥ \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, his heard full of compassion, the supreme Lord, pleased by his sweet words, pleased by his purity, placed him lotus feet, master of the devotees, on his head.**

### Text 41

*romāñca-sañcaya-samañcita-deha-yaṣṭir*  
*niryad-vilocana-payojhara-vṛnda-dhautaḥ \|*  
*tat pāda-paṅkaja-yugasya tadaiva labdhvā*  
*sparśaṁ babhūva ka ivātiśayotsukātmā \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Attaining the touch of his lotus feet, Mukunda, full of enthusiasm, with hairs standing on end, became covered in tears streaming from his eyes.**

### Text 42

*bhūyo jagāda karuṇaika-nidhir murāriṁ*  
*śrī-gauracandra idam udbhaṭa-bhāva-ramyaḥ \|*  
*ādhyātmikaṁ kim u kṛtaṁ na tavāsti gītaṁ*  
*satyaṁ vadāśu tad idaṁ yadi vā kṛtaṁ bhoḥ \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra, the ocean of mercy, attractive with manifested bhāva, spoke to Murārī. “Have you performed ādhyātmika (impersonal jñāna) practices? Are you not glorified for this? If you have done this, please tell the truth immediately.**

### Text 43

*vāñchāsti cet tava jīvitam eva kiṁ vā*  
*premodayeṣu tad idaṁ capalaṁ vihāya \|*  
*śrīmat-kṛpā-rasa-paripluta-pāda-padma-*  
*māhātmya-rūpa-guṇa-varṇam ātanuṣa \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If you desire to live with the manifestation of prema, then give up this fickleness and distribute descriptions of the qualities, form and glories of the beautiful lotus feet of the Lord filled with merciful rasa.”**

### Text 44

*śrutvā mahāprabhu-vaco madhuraṁ tato’sau*  
*nārāyaṇo’vadad amuṁ prati vaidya-mukhyaḥ \|*  
*kāruṇyam īśvara vidhehi murāri-gupte*  
*vaktuṁ yathārhati tavaiva caritra eṣaḥ \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the sweet words of Mahāprabhu, the famous doctor named Nārāyaṇa said, “O Lord! Give mercy to Murāri Gupta so that he can describe your qualities.”**

### Text 45

*śrutvātha taṁ prati tadā parama-prahṛṣṭas*  
*taṁ prārthanaṁ sa nijagāda kṛpā-samudraḥ \|*  
*yad yad vadiṣyati tad eṣa samastam eva*  
*śuddhaṁ bhaviṣyati bhaviṣyati śaktir ugrā \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of mercy, overjoyed on hearing his request, said to him, “Whatever he speaks will be pure and filled with great power.”**

### Text 46

*śṛṇvann asau tad uditaṁ sumanāḥ prahṛṣṭaḥ*  
*protphulla-roma-nicayo mumude murāriḥ \|*  
*pīyūṣa-sindhuṣu nimagnam ivātivelam*  
*ātmānam udbhaṭa-sudhaika-vaśo viveda \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing what he said, Murārī became pleased and with hairs standing on end rejoiced. Overcome by the strong sweetness, he felt himself submerged in an ocean of nectar.**

### Text 47

*śrīvāsa-paṇḍita-mahāmatir atyudāra-*  
*śīlaḥ svabhāva-hari-bhaktir ato’tidhīraḥ \|*  
*śuddhaḥ svadharma-nirato bahu-śānta-dāntas*  
*tat sevanena mumude’nudinaṁ mahātmā \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The intelligent Śrīvāsa Paṇḍita, a great soul with generous character and natural devotion to the Lord, most serious and pure, engaging in his sva-dharma, in control of his mind and senses, rejoiced in serving the Lord daily.**

### Text 48

*evaṁ nirantaram upāsanayā ca nṛtyaiḥ*  
*saṅkīrtanair api tathā vividhaiś ca bhāvaiḥ \|*  
*śrīvāsa-paṇḍita-mahāśaya eva nityaṁ*  
*tat-saṅgato’tivilasan mumude mahātmā \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great soul Śrīvāsa took pleasure in the Lord’s association and performed pastimes constantly with him by worship, dancing, chanting and various sentiments.**

### Text 49

*adhyāpayan dvija-sutān aparedyur īśaḥ*  
*śaśvat sva-nāma-guṇa-kīrtanam ātatāna \|*  
*daivād uvāca purato dvija-sūnur eko*  
*nāthaṁ na kiñcid api jātu vidaṁs tad-ante \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Another day the Lord, teaching brāhmaṇas’ sons, began continuously glorifying the Lord’s name and qualities. One child by fate said to him finally, “We do not understand anything.”**

### Text 50

*nāmno ya eṣa mahimā khalu so’rtha-vāda*  
*itthaṁ khalasya parikarṇya sarvam \|*  
*karṇau pidhāya saha tena puraḥsareṇa*  
*gaṅgā-taṭaṁ samagamad ghṛṇayā mahatyā \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing that the glories of the Lord’s name was all exaggeration, Gaurāṅga shut his ears with great aversion and, putting the child in front, went to the bank of the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 51

*snātvā sa-cela udagāt saha cela-vṛndaiḥ*  
*śuddhaiḥ śucir nija-gṛhaṁ mudito jagāma \|*  
*yaḥ kīrtayaty anudinaṁ ya idaṁ śṛṇoti*  
*sa premṇi nāmni nitarāṁ bhavati pralīnaḥ \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After bathing in the Gaṅgā with his clothes on, having been purified, with his pure clothes, he happily went home. He who glorifies or hears this pastime daily will become continuously absorbed in prema and the name.**

### Text 52

*itthaṁ sva-nāma-mahimā prathamaṁ prakāmaṁ*  
*prakhyāpitaḥ kramata eva śanais tathaiva \|*  
*ādhyātmikaṁ padam apāsitam ātma-pāda-*  
*padmopasevana-rasena parameśvareṇa \|\|52\|*

**Перевод:**

**By first making the glories of the Lord’s name well known, gradually the ādhyātmika (impersonal) goal was destroyed by Gaurāṅga, by service to the Lord’s lotus feet.**

### Text 53

*nāthaḥ kadācid atha tair nija-pāda-bhaktaiḥ*  
*śrīvāsa-paṇḍita-mukhaiḥ sukha-sāgaraḥ saḥ \|*  
*advaita-candram avalokitum asya gehe*  
*śrīmān anaṅga iva vigrahavān pratasthe \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One time the Lord, the ocean of happiness, like Cupid incarnate, along with his devotees such as Śrīvāsa Paṇḍita, went to see Advaita Candra at his house.**

### Text 54

*gacchan pathi prathita-nartana-kīrtanādyair*  
*gāyan naṭann api jagāma tad asya veśma \|*  
*advaita-candram adhi bhūmiṣu daṇḍavat sa*  
*bhūyaḥ papāta nija-bhakta-mahattva-vedī \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Travelling on the path, singing and dancing with extended kīrtana and dancing, he came to Advaita’s house. Knowing the greatness of his devotee, the Lord fell on the earth repeatedly, after Advaita had offered his respects.**

### Text 55

*āliṅganāny atha parasparam utsukāṅgau*  
*tau cakratuḥ parama-kāruṇikau jagatsu \|*  
*advaita eva kim u kiṁ nu sa gauracandra*  
*ity ūhitau jana-cayena babhūvatuś ca \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The two most merciful Lords in the world, with eagerness, embraced each other. The crowd could not distinguish who has Advaita and who was Gauracandra.**

### Text 56

*śuddhāsane samupaviśya sa gauracandraḥ*  
*svacchāṁ kathām akathayat karuṇaika-rāśiḥ \|*  
*āviṣkṛta-svapada-bhakti-vilāsa-lolo*  
*nānā-vidhena nija-bhakti-nirūpaṇena \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sitting on a pure seat, Gauracandra, the sum of all mercy, spoke pure topics, defining bhakti in various ways, while playfully revealing his lotus feet in a pastime of devotion.**

### Text 57

*advaita eṣa nijagāda tato mahātmā*  
*bhaktiḥ kalau na khalu vartata eva mūḍhāḥ \|*  
*ye saṁvadanti kudhiyaḥ sakalās ta ete*  
*paśyantu tat tad aśṛṇot svayam eva nāthaḥ \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great soul Advaita said, “Look at all the unintelligent, foolish people who say that bhakti does not exist in Kali-yuga.” The Lord heard this.**

### Text 58

*nāstīti yo vadati tasya gatir hi nāsti*  
*tasyaiva janma viphalaṁ khalu so’ti pāpī \|*  
*bhaktir hi rājati kalau satataṁ tadāti-*  
*krodhāruṇākṣi-yugalo bhagavān jagāda \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With eyes red in anger the Lord said, “He who says that there is no bhakti has no destination. His birth is useless. He is a sinner. Bhakti exists at all times in Kali-yuga.”**

### Text 59

*śrīvāsa eṣa tad-anantaram ittham ūce*

*vighno babhūva nitarām ayam atra nūnaṁ*  
*saṅkīrtane katham ito bahir eṣa yāti \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After that, Śrīvāsa spoke, on seeing a non-devotee brāhmaṇa of fierce nature. “He has created an obstacle to the sankīrtana today. How can we exclude him?”**

### Text 60

*tvac cintayālam alam atra na caiṣa vipra*  
*āyāsyatīty avitathaṁ nijagāda nāthaḥ \|*

*atrāntare mudam iyāya sa bhūmi-devaḥ \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said, speaking the truth, “You need not worry. This brāhmaṇa will not come.” Accepting the mental order of the Lord, the brāhmaṇa did not come. Śrīvāsa came joyful in his heart.**

### Text 61

*śrīvāsa-vipra-tilakāṁsa-taṭe sa dakṣaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**Placing his right arm on the shoulder of the best brāhmaṇa, Śrīvāsa, and placing his left arm on the shoulder of Gadādhara, putting his lotus feet on the head of Śrīrāma Paṇḍita, the Lord, having the beauty of many moons, shone.**

### Text 62

*krīḍā-paro’sya nilaye sa maheśvarasya*  
*rājīva-locana-yugaḥ kala-dhauta-gauraḥ \|*  
*smerānanaḥ sapadi darpaka-darpa-hārī*  
*reje nijair jana-cayai racayan vihāram \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the house of Advaita, the Lord, inclined to pastimes, with lotus eyes, with a complexion like gold, a smiling face, and beauty which stole the pride of Cupid, shone as he performed pastimes with his devotees.**

### Text 63

*adhyātma-tattvam abhi gaura-mahāprabhuḥ sa*  
*vyākhyāṁ cakāra bahu-durgama-bodham anyaiḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu explained ādhyātma-tattva, very difficult to understand for others. “One ātmā remains here at all times. At creation, the ātmā alone manifests.”**

### Text 64

*itthaṁ prasārya sva-karau karuṇā-samudro*

*bhūyo jagāda jagad eka-gatiṁ prakāmam \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of mercy, spreading his hands and making a fist, danced quickly. Again he spoke profusely about the nature of sac-cit-svarūpa, the only goal.**

### Text 65

*bhāvo’pi niścitam anarthaka eva tasya*  
*sad-rūpam eva sudhiyām avadhāraṇīyam \|*  
*yad brahmaṇo bhavati naiva kadāpi muktir*  
*ekatvam etad avabodham ṛte hi sā syāt \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Material existence is absolutely meaningless. The actual existence, brahman, should be realized by the intelligent. Without understanding that there is only this one entity, one cannot attain liberation.**

### Text 66

*paśyāṅgulī karagate punar ekakasya*  
*saiko’mṛtena nicitāṁ parilocitāṁ ca \|*

**Перевод:**

**“See two fingers on one hand. One is dipped in nectar and is admired. The other is covered with sores and is disgraceful. One does not look at it for even a moment, being clearly pained with disgust.**

### Text 67

*itthaṁ sa eka iha śeṣa-padaṁ hy anādir*  
*ātmā sadaiva pariśiṣyata evam eṣaḥ \|*

*mukto’nyathā sa khalu kaścid apīha jīvaḥ \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“This one ātmā without beginning remains at all times in this world. If endowed with an upādhi, it should be freed from that upādhi. Otherwise (with the upādhi) it appears in the world as a jīva.”**

### Text 68

*itthaṁ prabhur bahu nirūpya nisarga-durgaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**In this way the Lord extensively explained jñāna, difficult to understand by its nature so that his followers could easily understand. Relaxing as tears streamed from his eyes and his hairs stood on end, he spoke sweetly.**

### Text 69

*snihyan-manāḥ pulakito virudan hasaṁś ca*

*mad-bhakta eva paripāti punāti nityam \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With affectionate mind, hairs standing on end, crying, laughing, paralyzed by the nectar of prema, forgetting the functions of the body, he sang and danced. “My devotee alone protects and purifies the three worlds constantly.”**

### Text 70

*vāg gadgadā dravate yasya cittaṁ*  
*rudaty abhīkṣṇaṁ hasati kvacic ca \|*

*mad-bhakti-yukto bhuvanaṁ punāti 70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee whose speech is choked up, whose heart is melted, who cries continually and sometimes laughs, sometimes feels ashamed, sometimes sings loudly and sometimes dances—a devotee thus fixed in loving service to me purifies the entire universe. SB 11.14.24**

### Text 71

*ity uktavān nija-janaiḥ karuṇaika-sindhuḥ*

*prema prakāśayati tat-karuṇaiva saiṣā \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Reciting this verse, the ocean of mercy with smiling face, danced sweetly in joy with his devotees. Revealing prema on earth as he personally danced was his mercy.**

### Text 72

*tatrāparedyur amala-dyumaṇi-prakāśo’*

**Another day, Advaita, shining like the pure sun, after bathing and performing worship, came to see Mahāprabhu. At that time, the Lord was going to Śrīvāsa’s house.**

**With a slight smile, putting flowers on a stick, roaring, the lotus eyed Lord said, “I have worshiped this club to punish the sinful.**

### Text 74

*eko’sti duṣṭatama eva madīya-bhakta-*

**“There is one great sinner who hates my devotees. I will make him suffer in hell with leprosy and sores all over his body. I will make his disciples food for dogs and jackals.**

### Text 75

**Hearing that Advaita was coming, he thought “Is he here? Why did he not come? There is some delay. I will go there.” But Advaita came, fell on the ground and offered respects repeatedly.**

**Raising him up, the Lord held his hand and said, “I was coming to see you.” The merciful Lord sat with him on a cot and shone brightly.**

**“People pray for this prema. O great soul! I have given it to you.” Advaita said, “The devotees of your lotus feet are truly candidates for that prema.”**

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Śrīvāsa, best of the brāhmaṇas, spoke to the Lord of the universe, full of mercy, in fear, with sweet words. “O Lord! Who can be a devotee? It is your mercy only.”**

### Text 82

*roṣeṇa kampa-daśana-cchadana-dvayas taṁ*

**Hearing this, Śrīvāsa was silent. The merciful Lord again spoke. “You should not for a moment speak about adhyātma.**

### Text 85
