### Text 101

*so’yaṁ papāṭha kavitāṁ svakṛtām anekāṁ*  
*śrī-rāghavendra-guṇa-rūpa-vilāsa-gāthām \|*  
*itthaṁ niśamya raghunandana-rāja-siṁha-*  
*ślokāṣṭakaṁ padam adhāt tad amuṣya mūrdhni \|\|101\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Murāri Gupta then recited many verses he had composed describing the pastimes, form and qualities of Rāmacandra. Hearing the eight verses describing Rāma, the lion among kings, the Lord placed his feet on Murāri’s head.**

### Text 102

*taṁ rāma-dāsa iti bho bhava mat-prasādād*  
*bhāle lilekha caturakṣaram etad eva \|*  
*paścāt papāṭha madhuraṁ madhurākṛtiḥ sa*  
*ślokaṁ mahāprabhur atīva kṛpā-samudraḥ \|\|102\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“By my mercy, become the servant of Rāma.” He wrote on his forehead four syllables (Rāma-dāsa). Then Mahāprabhu, the great ocean of mercy with sweet form, recited a sweet verse.**

### Text 103

*na sādhayati mā yogo na sāṅkhyaṁ dharma uddhava \|*  
*na svādhyāyas tapas tyāgo yathā bhaktir mamorjitā 103\|\| (bhā.pu.11.14.20)*

**Перевод:**

**“O Uddhava! I am attained by unmixed** ***bhakti.*** **I cannot be attained by mystic** ***yoga*****, Sāṅkhya philosophy,** ***dharma,*** **Vedic study, austerity or renunciation.”**

### Text 104

*itthaṁ papāṭha madhuraṁ tata āgatāṁs tān*  
*ūce dvijān dvija-mayūkha-samāplutoṣṭhaḥ \|*  
*śrīvāsa eva vadatīha yadā yathā vai*  
*kartavyam etad adhunā niyataṁ bhavadbhiḥ \|\|104\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Reciting sweetly this verse, he spoke with a smile to the brāhmaṇas who had come there. “You should do whatever Śrīvāsa says.”**

### Text 105

*śrī-rāma-paṇḍitam athāha sadaiva kāryaṁ*  
*jyeṣṭhasya sevanam idaṁ hi mamaiva sevā \|*  
*etena te sakalam eva śivāya bhūyād*  
*itthaṁ vadan sa ruruce rucirānanenduḥ \|\|105\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīrāma Paṇḍita said, “My service is to always serve my elder brother.” Saying “By doing this you will attain all auspiciousness,” the Lord shone, his face as beautiful as the moon.**

### Text 106

*śrīvāsa-paṇḍita-samarpita-dugdha-pūga-*  
*mālyāni tatra sa niṣevya tato’vaśeṣam \|*  
*tebhyaḥ prasāda-sumukho nija-pāda-padma-*  
*bhaktebhya eva bhagavān pradadau kṛpābdhiḥ \|\|106\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, the ocean of mercy, accepted betel, milk and garlands from Śrīvāsa and then, being merciful, distributed his remnants to the devotees who served his lotus feet.**

### Text 107

*itthaṁ nināya sakalāṁ sa niśāṁ niśeśa-*  
*koṭi-prakāśa-madhurānana-candra-bimbaḥ \|*  
*udyāti tigma-kiraṇe’tha mahāprabhuṁ taṁ*  
*sannamya veśmani yathātatham īyur ete \|\|107\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord with a face like a million moons spent the whole night in this way. When the sun rose, the devotees offered respects to Mahāprabhu and went to their houses.**

### Text 108

*bhūyaś ca deva-taṭinī-plavanena mugdhāḥ*  
*sampūjya deva-sadanāc ca yathāyathaṁ te \|*  
*ājagmur asya pada-paṅkaja-darśanārthaṁ*  
*tan-mātra-jīvana-mahauṣadhayo mahāntaḥ \|\|108\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Satisfied with bathing in the Gaṅgā, worshipping the Lord, they again came to see the lotus feet of Mahāprabhu. He was the only medicine to maintain their lives.**

### Text 109

*dṛṣṭvā mahāprabhur athaiva samāgatāṁs tān*  
*ūce payodhara-gabhīra-ravaḥ sudhīram \|*  
*atrāgato’sti matimān avadhūta-nityā-*  
*nandaḥ śrutaṁ katham amuṣya vilokanaṁ syāt \|\|109\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the devotees assemble, with a voice as deep as a rumbling cloud, he spoke wise words. “I have heard that the intelligent avadhūta Nityānanda has come here. How will we see him?**

### Text 110

*he rāma-paṇḍita mukunda murāri-gupta*  
*nārāyaṇa drutam itas tvaritaṁ prayāta \|*  
*atrāsti sa pracura-bhāgya-bharo mahātmā*  
*gatvā samānayata taṁ mahitānubhāvam \|\|110\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O Śrīrāma, Mukunda and Murāri Gupta! O Nārāyaṇa! Go quickly from here. Going to where that great soul of great fortune is residing, bring him here.”**

### Text 111

*ājñāpitā iti mahāprabhunā tatas te*  
*gatvā bhṛśaṁ pathi vicārya na taṁ vilokya \|*  
*bhūyaḥ sametya ca vilokita eṣa naiva*  
*kutrāpi kiṁ bata vidheyam itīdam ūcuḥ \|\|111\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Ordered by Mahāprabhu, they went and searched everywhere on the road but did not see him. Again they returned and said, “We could not find him anywhere. What should we do?”**

### Text 112

*bhūyas tathāha bhagavān adhunā na dṛśyaḥ*  
*so’yaṁ bhavadbhir iha sāyam avekṣitavyaḥ \|*  
*svān svān gṛhān sapadi gacchata tat tadānīm*  
*atrāgamiṣyatha tatheti yayur gṛhaṁ te \|\|112\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord again spoke. “You will not see him now. You will see him in the evening. Go home and come here in the evening.” They then went home.**

### Text 113

*sāyaṁ tataḥ pathi calan saha taiḥ kṛpālur*  
*vaidyaṁ murārim avalokya jagāda dhīram \|*  
*ācārya-nandana-gṛhe’sti hi so’vadhūtas*  
*tatra prayāhi capalaṁ tam ihānayeti \|\|113\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the evening, while walking on the path, the merciful Lord, seeing Murārī the doctor spoke in a serious tone. “The avadhūta is in Nandanācarya’s house. Go there and quickly bring him here.”**

### Text 114

*itthaṁ sa tatra samupetya dadarśa nityā-*  
*nandaṁ prabhuṁ ca samalokayad eṣa sākṣāt \|*  
*ānamya taṁ madhuram āha sudhāṁśu-kamraḥ*  
*kākvā nayena vinayena kṛpā-rasābdhiḥ \|\|114\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When Mahāprabhu went there, Mahāprabhu saw Nityānanda Prabhu. Gazing at him directly, the ocean of rasa and mercy, beautiful as the moon, spoke to him in a plaintive voice with great politeness.**

### Text 115

*tvaṁ bhūtale’tula-mahā-mahimārṇavo’si*  
*saṁsāra-sāgara-viśoṣaṇam ātanoṣi \|*  
*niḥśeṣa-dehi-kula-nandathum eva kurvan*  
*pāṣaṇḍināṁ hṛdayam ākulayasy aśeṣam \|\|115\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You are an incomparable ocean of greatness on this earth. You dry up the ocean of saṁsāra. You give joy to the unlimited living entities and have afflicted the hearts of the pāṣāṇḍīs without limit.**

### Text 116

*tvaṁ tyakta-loka-nicayo’pi samasta-loka-*  
*samyak-śritāṅghri-kamala-dvaya eva nityam \|*  
*vairāgyam āśrayasi santatam eva loke*  
*rāgo mahān pravirataḥ khalu lakṣyate’sau \|\|116\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You have renounced the world but you always give shelter to all the people with your lotus feet. As a renunciate, you take constantly shelter of detachment in this world, but great attachment (for the Lord) is seen in you.”**

### Text 117

*ity ūcivān saha nijāṅghri-saroja-bhaktaiḥ*  
*saṅkīrtanaṁ samakaron naṭanaṁ ca bhūyaḥ \|*  
*tatrāvadhūta-pada-dhūlibhir ātma-loka-*  
*śīrṣaṁ cakāra paripūtatamaṁ paraṁ saḥ \|\|117\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, the Lord performed kīrtana and danced with the devotees dedicated to his lotus feet. He purified the heads of the devotees with the dust from Nityānanda’s lotus feet.**

### Text 118

*itthaṁ vrajan pathi śacī-tanayaḥ sa tais tais*  
*tasyāvadhūta-paramasya kathāṁ jagāda \|*  
*jñānaṁ puro bhavati bhaktir atho viraktir*  
*itthaṁ vadaty ayam ataḥ paramo’yam eva \|\|118\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The son of Śacī went on the road with his devotees and talked about Nityānanda. He explained that Nityānanda was the best, being endowed with knowledge, devotion and detachment.**

### Text 119

*itthaṁ vicintya karuṇābdhir athāparedyur*  
*bhikṣārtham asya niyataṁ nirato babhūva \|*  
*sad-bhojitaṁ tad anu candana-kuṅkumādyaiḥ*  
*pratyaṅgam evam anulipya nananda nāthaḥ \|\|119\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking in this way, the ocean of mercy became engaged in begging for Nityānanda the next day. After offering him a fine meal, the Lord smeared sandalwood and kuṁkuma on all his limbs and satisfied him.**

### Text 120

*anyedyur eṣa bhagavān avadhūta-veśaḥ*  
*śrīvāsa-geham agamat kṣudhitaḥ prakāmam \|*  
*āmantrya so’numumude dharaṇī-surāgryo*  
*bhikṣāṁ dadau tad anu candanakair lilepa \|\|120\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Another day, Nityānanda came to Śrīvāsa’s house in great hunger. Śrīvāsa invited him in happily and gave him alms. After that he put sandalwood on his body.**

### Text 121

*viśrāmam atra sa cakāra tathaiva bhuktvā*  
*tatraiva so’pi karuṇā-nidhir udgato’bhūt \|*  
*āgatya deva-nilaye vara-kambalena*  
*ramyaṁ varāsanam upetya rarāja nāthaḥ \|\|121\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After eating there, Nityānanda took rest. The ocean of mercy arrived there and, entering the temple, sitting on an excellent seat with a good blanket, he shone.**

### Text 122

*ūce’vadhūtam atha gaura-sudhākaro’sau*  
*māṁ paśya paśya kṛtavān asi yac chramaṁ tvam \|*  
*ity ukte eṣa nahi kiñcana tasya dehe*  
*praikṣiṣṭa naiva tad abuddha mahānubhāvaḥ \|\|122\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra said to him, “Look at me! Look at me since you have rested!” Nityānanda looked at his body but could not see anything. Mahāprabhu understood.**

### Text 123

*jñātvā sa ittham atikāruṇikas tatas tān*  
*ūce bahir vrajata śīghram ito bhavantaḥ \|*  
*gacchatsu teṣu sa ca tatra dadarśa tasya*  
*dehe dineśa-śata-koṭi-maho mahīyaḥ \|\|123\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding, the most merciful Lord said to the devotees, “Quickly go outside.” When they went out, Nityānanda saw on Mahāprabhu’s body an effulgence like a hundred million suns.**

### Text 124

*puraḥ ṣaḍbhir dorbhiḥ parama-ruciraṁ tatra ca punaś*  
*caturṇāṁ bāhūnāṁ parama-lalitatvena madhuram \|*  
*tadīyaṁ tad-rūpaṁ sapadi parilocyāśu sahasā*  
*tad āścaryaṁ bhūyo dvibhujam atha bhūyo’py akalayat \|\|124\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**First seeing Mahāprabhu’s attractive form with six arms, then his sweet, attractive form with four arms, he then suddenly saw his remarkable two armed form again.**

### Text 125

*vilokyotthaṁ tat tat parama-ramaṇīyaṁ sumadhuraṁ*  
*kṛpāsindho rūpāmṛtam idam amandaṁ pramuditaḥ \|*  
*jahāsoccair nṛtyann atiśaya-sukhāsphālana-paro*  
*bhṛśaṁ nityānandaḥ sukha-jaladhi-samplāvita-tanuḥ \|\|125\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the sweet, attractive, shining forms of Mahāprabhu, the ocean of mercy, Nityānanda was overjoyed. Trembling with great happiness, he laughed loudly and danced, his body submerged in an ocean of happiness.**

## Chapter Seven

### Text 1

*aparedyur eṣa niśi suptim ito*  
*viruroda nirbharam atiprakaṭam \|*  
*tanayaṁ tathāvidham avekṣya śacī*  
*sabhayaṁ jagāda jagad-eka-patim \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On another day at night, after seeing her son weeping while asleep, Śacī in fear spoke to the Lord of the universe.**

### Text 2

*kim u tāta roditi bhavān avadat*  
*sa tatheti mātaram uvāca tataḥ \|*  
*ayi nidrayā vikalitena mayā*  
*sa vilokto’sti madhuro madhuraḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She said, “O son! Why are you crying?” He answered his mother. "While sleeping I saw the sweetest form.**

### Text 3

*nava-nīla-nīrada-samūha-rucir*  
*nava-nīla-kaṇṭha-dala-maṇḍanakaḥ \|*  
*ghana-medurātikuṭila-prasarat-*  
*kaca-sañcaya-prasṛta-bhāla-talaḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“His complexion was like a dark, new rain cloud. He had a peacock feather on his head. His thick, curly hair spread over his forehead.**

### Text 4

*surasuna-sañcaya-vataṁsa-rasa-*  
*pramada-bhramad-bhramara-vibhrama-bhṛt \|*  
*alasollasan-madhura-cilli-lataḥ*  
*śravaṇānta-sañcarita-netra-yugaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“There were bees hovering around his clove flower crown. He glanced with languid sweetness from eyes stretching to his ears.**

### Text 5

*aruṇāruṇākṣi-kamalaḥ pramado*  
*ghana-sāndra-dṛṣṭi-laharī-madhuraḥ \|*  
*sad-apāṅga-bhaṅgima-jagan-madanaḥ*  
*smita-gaṇḍa-maṇḍala-lasan-mukuraḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“His lotus eyes were pink like the dawn. His intense glance was like a wave of sweetness. He intoxicated the universe with his side glance. His smiling cheeks were like mirrors.**

### Text 6

*tapanīya-kuṇḍala-vilāsa-lasac-*  
*chravaṇa-dvayī-hṛta-jagad-dhṛdayaḥ \|*  
*nava-vidruma-druma-kaḍamba-lasan-*  
*madhurādhara-dyuti-sudhā-madhuraḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“He was decorated with gold earrings which attracted the whole universe. The beauty of his lips, shining like new coral trees, were sweet nectar.**

### Text 7

*daśana-prasūna-ruci-mañjarikā-*  
*dhara-pallavāruṇima-kamra-mukhaḥ \|*  
*madhu-mādhurī-madhura-sac-cibukaḥ*  
*śuci-kambu-kaṇṭha-taṭa-hāra-dharaḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“His face was charming with pink buds for lips, shining with flower-like teeth. His beautiful chin was as sweet as honey. He wore a necklace around his throat, pure as a conch shell.**

### Text 8

*nava-mauktika-prakara-hāra-latā-*  
*vilasad-galo vilasad-aṁsa-taṭaḥ \|*  
*tapanīya-sūtra-parikḷpta-lasad-*  
*vara-kaustubha-sphurad-uraḥ-saraṇiḥ \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The necklace was made of fresh pearls, shining on his throat and shoulders. On his chest was the Kaustubha gem, sparkling on a golden thread.**

### Text 9

*amara-prasūna-nava-mālya-kalā-*  
*lalitorupīna-sad-uro madhuraḥ \|*  
*vara-jānu-lambi-mṛdu-pīna-bhujā*  
*vilasad-varāṅgada-sukaṅkaṇakaḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“He was shining with a fresh garland of clove flowers on his broad chest. His arms which reached his knees were decorated with shining arm bands and bracelets.**

### Text 10

*karameya-madhyama-vilāsa-lasad-*  
*vara-bandhurodarakaṭīrataṭaḥ \|*  
*abhinābhi-vītatapanīya-dhaṭī-*  
*lasad-añcalāñcita-padāgra-taṭaḥ \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“His waist was as narrow as a fist and shone with a band tied around his stomach. Above his navel, he wore a dhotī with its golden border shining on his toes.**

### Text 11

*smita-dīdhiti-snapita-dig-valayaḥ*  
*karuṇā-kaṭākṣa-madhuraḥ kamalaḥ \|*  
*iti taṁ vilokya sahasāvirabhūt*  
*sukha-sañcayair mama suvihvalatā \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The effulgence of his smile filled the directions and his sweet, merciful glance was like a lotus. Seeing him, suddenly I became thrilled with all sorts of happiness.**

### Text 12

*atha rodimi pratimuhur vikalaḥ*  
*sukha-sāgare’smi kṛta-samplavanaḥ \|*  
*tanayoditāny atha niśamya śacī*  
*sahasābhavat sapulakaṁ muditā \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Confused, I cried continuously. I was thrown into an ocean of happiness.” Hearing the words of her son, Śacī’s hairs stood on end in bliss.**

### Text 13

*prabhur apy asau nayana-vāri-jharair*  
*jaladhi-dvayaṁ kim adadhād urasi \|*  
*kiyatā dinena samupetya babhau*  
*dvija-puṅgavālaya-varaṁ tad idam \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord made two oceans on his chest with the streams of his tears. For some days he remained in the house of Jagannātha Miśra.**

### Text 14

*mahanīya-mūrtir avadhūta-vibhuḥ*  
*paridhūta-sarva-kali-kāla-malaḥ \|*  
*sa punar eva tatra karuṇāmbunidher*  
*atisundarīṁ madhura-rūpa-sudhām \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great form of the avadhūta, who washed Kali of all contamination, gazed at that most beautiful, sweet form of nectar.**

### Text 15

*apibad vilocana-puṭena muhur*  
*na tṛṣo’sya pāram agamad vibhavaḥ \|*  
*vara-ṣaḍ-bhujaṁ tam atha dakṣiṇato*  
*dara-cakra-nirmala-gadāstra-dharam \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Constantly drinking the form with his eyes, he could not be fully satisfied. He then saw an excellent form with six arms, holding in his right arms a conch, cakra, and pure club.**

### Text 16

*muralī-varāmburuha-śārṅga-dharaṁ*  
*rucirair athāpara-bhuja-tritayaiḥ \|*  
*druta-śātakumbha-maya-bhūmiruhas*  
*taruṇāṅkuraṁ karuṇayāruṇitam \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In his other three attractive arms he was holding a flute, an excellent lotus and a bow. He was a young tree made of molten gold, brimming with mercy.**

### Text 17

*vara-kaustubha-dyuti-virājad-uraḥ*  
*sthala-śobhi-mauktika-saraṁ sarasam \|*  
*śravaṇa-dvayānta-vilasan-makarā-*  
*kṛti-kuṇḍala-sphurita-gaṇḍa-yugmam \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His chest shone with the effulgence of the excellent Kaustubha gem and an attractive necklace of pearls. His makara earrings, sparkling on his ears, illuminated his cheeks.**

### Text 18

*nava-nīla-ratna-vara-hāra-lasad-*  
*vara-kambu-kaṇṭha-ruciraṁ kamalam \|*  
*prathamoditārka-kara-gaura-varā-*  
*mbaram ullasad-guru-nitamba-taṭam \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On his pink neck, attractive as the best conch, was a sapphire necklace. His cloth around his heavy hips shone golden like the rays of the rising sun.**

### Text 19

*iti taṁ vilokya karuṇā-jaladhiṁ*  
*mumude’vadhūta-vibhur eṣa bhṛśam \|*  
*tad-anantaraṁ bhuja-catuṣṭaya-sat-*  
*kamanīya-rūpam atha bāhu-yugam \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing that form, the ocean of mercy, Nityānanda, rejoiced greatly. Then he saw the attractive four armed form, and finally the two armed form.**

### Text 20

*avalokya vismita-manāḥ sumanāḥ*  
*sumanaś cāyaṁ rahasi taṁ vyākirat \|*  
*tad-anantaraṁ ca bahu-harṣa-bharair*  
*vidalan-manā naṭitum ārabhata \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Astonished on seeing this, joyful Nityānanda showered flowers on that form in private. His mind bursting with great joy, he began dancing.**

### Text 21

*parirabhya nirbharam asau svajanān*  
*svajana-pramoda-bhara-kṛt karuṇaḥ \|*  
*bhṛśam eva nartana-kalākulito*  
*hari-kīrtanāmṛta-nadī-plavanāt \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Merciful Nityānanda, who gave great joy to his devotees, embraced them tightly. Possessed with dancing, he gave joy to the three worlds by flooding them in the river of sweet kīrtana of the Lord.**

### Text 22

*mudito babhūva jagatī-tritaye*  
*janam ātmanaḥ sama-manā kalayan \|*  
*pada-paṅkaja-dvaya-parāga-lava-*  
*grahaṇena yasya vidhurā vibudhāḥ \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Making the people like himself, he gave joy to the three worlds. The devatās, receiving a particle of pollen from his two lotus feet, became bewildered.**

### Text 23

*vividhāṁ śriyaṁ sapadi yat kṛpayā*  
*labhate sadā bhuvi samasta-janaḥ \|*  
*kim u tasya bhūri-mahimāmbu-nidher*  
*manujaiḥ kṣitau parimitiḥ kriyatām \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They were overwhelmed on seeing that, by his mercy, all the people on earth attained great wealth immediately. Can humans put a limit on that great ocean of mercy?**

### Text 24

*balarāma ity avani-madhyam adhi*  
*prathito ya eṣa mahanīya-guṇaḥ \|*  
*atha gaura-śīta-kiraṇaḥ svajanān*  
*nijagāda bhūri-karuṇaḥ kamanaḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda, possessor of great qualities, was thus known on earth as Balarāma. Merciful, attractive Gaurāṅga then spoke to his devotees.**

### Text 25

*avadhūta eṣa paribhoga-gataḥ*  
*kamalākṣa-deva-bhavane jhaṭiti \|*  
*amunā samaṁ vrajata tasya puro*  
*‘sya ca san-mahattvam upakīrtayata \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Nityānanda is absorbed in eating. Go quickly to Advaita’s house and glorify the six armed form as well as Nityānanda in front of Advaita.”**

### Text 26-27

*tam upetya te samam anena muhur*  
*bhuvi daṇḍavan-nati-tatiṁ vidadhuḥ \|*  
*bhuvi rudra ity adhigato’sti hi yaḥ*  
*kamalākṣa-saṁjña iha vipra-kule \|\|26\|\|*

*avatīrṇatām upagatantam amī*  
*parilokya nātha-gaditaṁ jagaduḥ \|*  
*sa niśamya ṣaḍ-bhuja-caturbhujatām*  
*avanītale vihita-gaura-tanoḥ \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Meeting him, they constantly offered respects on the earth as he also did. Seeing Advaita who was known as Śiva on earth and called Kamalāksa, living in a brāhmaṇa family, among those who made their appearance with the Lord, they informed him as Mahāprabhu had told them. Hearing about the six and four armed form, belonging to the Lord endowed a golden body when he descended on earth, Kamalākṣa was overjoyed.**

### Text 28

*karuṇālayasya mumude subhṛśaṁ*  
*sukha-sāgare vihita-samplavanaḥ \|*  
*atha tan nivedana-vaco-muditā*  
*vinivedya te hy upanatā anayan \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Overjoyed by the abode of mercy, he drowned in an ocean of happiness. The devotees, happy with his words, and, informing him of everything, offered their respects.**

### Text 29

*karuṇālayasya caraṇābja-rajaḥ*  
*parigṛhya tat-pada-yugānugatāḥ \|*  
*aparedyur apy ayam amanda-guṇaḥ*  
*kamalākṣa-deva udiyāya tataḥ \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They took the dust from Kamalāksa’s lotus feet and became obedient to those feet. On another day, Kamalākṣa, endowed with unlimited qualities, came to see Mahāprabhu.**

### Text 30-31

*avalokya gaura-śaśinaṁ ca tadā*  
*mada-siṁha-nāda-ruciraḥ samabhūt \|*  
*samupāgate’tra mahanīya-guṇe*  
*giriśa-prabhau prabhur asau jagatām \|\|30\|\|*

*sahasāvirātanuta bhūridayaḥ*  
*prakaṭa-prakāśam atha gauḍa-śaśī \|*  
*bhuvi nārado’yam iti yaḥ prathito*  
*bhavaneṣu tasya sa tu deva-gṛhe \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the golden moon, Mahāprabhu, Kamalākṣa roared like a mad lion. After the two, Śiva and the Lord, met, the merciful Lord, the moon of Gauḍa, master of the universe, then showed to Kamalākṣa (Advaita), who is Śiva, endowed with great qualities, his manifested form, in the house of Śrīvāsa, who is known on earth as Nārada.**

### Text 32

*prakaṭa-prakāśam avadarśya tadā*  
*sukham asya bhūri-karuṇo’tanuta \|*  
*atha taṁ tathā-vidham avekṣya bhṛśaṁ*  
*nanṛtur jagur mumudire bahu te \|*  
*paripūjya puṣpa-phala-pūga-dhanair*  
*bhuvi daṇḍavad bahu-sukhair anaman \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the most merciful Lord showed that form, he spread bliss. The devotees, seeing Mahāprabhu in this form, began dancing and singing in great bliss. They worshipped that form with flowers, fruit, betel and wealth and offered respects on the ground with great joy.**

### Text 33

*paritas tad-arcanam asau kṛpayā*  
*parigṛhya tebhya idam eva dadau \|*  
*vasanaṁ prasūnam api kāruṇikaḥ*  
*karuṇālayasya karuṇā mahatī \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Accepting that worship, the merciful Lord out of mercy gave items such as flowers and cloth to the devotees. This was the great mercy of Gaurāṅga, the abode of mercy.**

### Text 34

*jagatī-trayasya janatābhir ati-*  
*pratimṛgyam atyasulabhaṁ bahudhā \|*  
*apavargam apy atilaghuṁ sahasā*  
*sukha-tan-mayā vidadhur ittham amī \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees considered impersonal liberation, which was sought by many persons in the three worlds but difficult to attain, to be very insignificant, since they were filled with the happiness of bhakti.**

### Text 35

*na dinaṁ na rātrim atha te’tha vidur*  
*na sukhaṁ na duḥkham api te paramāḥ \|*  
*kim anīpsitāpi satanūn abhajaj*  
*jaḍatā-miṣeṇa bhuvi muktir amūn \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These great devotees did not know day or night, happiness or distress. Liberation, though not desired by them, served their bodies in the form of their senseless condition.**

### Text 36

*abhibhāsvad-udgamanam ittham amī*  
*nanṛtur jagur mumudire bahu te \|*  
*rajanīṁ vinīya sakalāṁ ca punar*  
*divasādim etya vivaśā abhavan \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They danced and sang in bliss till the sun rose. Passing the whole night, they remained powerless to do anything else, even with the coming of day.**

### Text 37

*dyunadī-jalaṁ samavagāhya tataḥ*  
*prathamaṁ dinasya muditās ta ime \|*  
*asudhāraṇaika-paramauṣadhivac*  
*caraṇaṁ prabhor mṛdutaraṁ dadṛśuḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After taking bath in the Gaṅgā, being joyful at the beginning of the day, they saw the Lord’s soft feet, the supreme medicine for maintaining their lives.**

### Text 38

*atha tasya nartana-vilāsam imaṁ*  
*parilokituṁ sarabhasaṁ muditaḥ \|*  
*mudiraḥ śanair nabhasi kiṁ vidadhau*  
*sahasodgamaṁ madhura-medura-ruk \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Did the cloud, beautifully dark in hue, becoming intensely joyful, suddenly and quietly appear in the sky to see the pastimes of the Lord’s dancing?**

### Text 39

*bhuvi bhāti gaura-hima-raśmir ayaṁ*  
*madhura-dyutiḥ kim adhunā bhavatā \|*  
*iti bhūriśo nabhasi candramasaṁ*  
*jaladodgamaḥ sapadi kiṁ pidadhe \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The cool, golden rays of Mahāprabhu, shine sweetly on the earth. Are you, O moon, any longer necessary?” Did the cloud say this and suddenly cover up the moon in the sky?**

### Text 40

*iha gauracandra-mahasā mahatā*  
*parinirjito dinapatir nabhasi \|*  
*trapayaiva kiṁ viniviveśa bhṛśaṁ*  
*jaladāvalīṣv avralāsu tataḥ \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**And did the sun in the sky, defeated by the great effulgence of Gauracandra, out of shame quickly enter the dense clouds?**

### Text 41

*vikasat-kadamba-nava-gandha-rasair*  
*aticāru-vāsitavatīḥ kakubhaḥ \|*  
*parirabhya harṣa-bhavam aśru-bharaṁ*  
*jaladodgamaḥ kṣaṇa-vaśād amucat \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The clouds, embracing the goddess of the directions who was attractively scented with the fragrant rasa of newly blossoming kadambas, shed profuse tears of joy at that time.**

### Text 42

*karuṇāsavena madhure madhure*  
*caraṇāmbuje’sya bhuvi rājati kim \|*  
*iha mādṛśair iti mamajja tadā*  
*sarasīruhāṁ tatir iyaṁ sarasi \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the Lord’s lotus feet shone sweetly with the nectar of mercy, did the lotuses, thinking, “We are no use at all,” submerge themselves in the water?**

### Text 43

*hariṇī-dṛśāṁ kuṭila-medura-sat-*  
*kaca-pāśa-bhāsura-ruco jaladāḥ \|*  
*capalācayair madhuratāṁ dadhire*  
*sphuṭa-ketakāṅkita-tamāla-taroḥ \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The clouds, colored dark like the thick, curly hair of doe-eyed women, became attractive with bolts of lightning, like a tamāla tree placing a blossoming ketakī creeper on its lap.**

### Text 44

*laghu-nṛtyato’sya caraṇāmburuhaṁ*  
*kṣiti-saṅgamo vyathayate bahuśaḥ \|*  
*iti cintayā jalamucaḥ salilair*  
*mṛdulāṁ sadaiva dharaṇīṁ vidadhuḥ \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking that the Lord’s feet were in pain from touching the earth while dancing, the clouds made the earth soft with water.**

### Text 45

*sa yadā sukhena tanute naṭanaṁ*  
*vilasat-padāmbuja-vilāsa-rasaḥ \|*  
*jaladās tadaiva karuṇaika-nidher*  
*lalitātapatra-suṣamāṁ dadhati \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the Lord danced, delightfully stepping with his attractive feet in joy, the clouds became an attractive umbrella for the ocean of mercy.**

### Text 46

*nava-vidruma-druma-kadamba-rucā*  
*pada-pallavasya madhura-cchaṭayā \|*  
*dharaṇīṁ cakāra karuṇābdhir asāv-*  
*aruṇāyitām aruṇa-pāṇi-talaḥ \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of mercy endowed with reddish palms made the earth turn red by the sweet effulgence of his lotus feet, which shone like a forest of fresh coral trees.**

### Text 47

*tapanīya-gaura-vapuṣo mahasā*  
*naṭato’sya vārida-balavat-timirāḥ \|*  
*kakubho vibhinna-rucayo militāḥ*  
*mṛganābhi-kuṅkuma-rucaṁ vidadhuḥ \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The directions changed color, becoming like musk and kuṁkuma, when the darkness of the clouds mixed with the effulgence of golden body of the Lord as he danced.**

### Text 48

*tata āgataṁ ca haridāsa-mahā-*  
*mahitāśayaṁ sumahanīya-guṇam \|*  
*nija-pāda-paṅkaja-madhūnmada-sad-*  
*bhramaraṁ vilokya mumude sa vibhuḥ \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the arrival of the exalted Hari-dāsa, endowed with the greatest qualities, who was like a bee mad for the honey of the Lord’s lotus feet, the Lord became happy.**

### Text 49

*parirabhya nirbharam amuṁ sahasā*  
*sva-padābja-bhaktam anuraktatamam \|*  
*varam āsanaṁ karuṇayā svajanair*  
*nayana-śriyānayad aneka-kṛpaḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The merciful Lord tightly embraced Hari-dāsa, a devotee attached to his lotus feet, and had his devotees, by a hint to his glance, offer him an excellent seat.**

### Text 50

*abhivādya tat tu śirasā praṇato*  
*varam āsanaṁ bhuvi cakāra padam \|*  
*prabhupāda-paṅkaja-parāga-cayaṁ*  
*parigṛhya bhakti-parayā sadhiyā \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari-dāsa saluted the seat and offered respects to it with his head. With great devotion, accepting the dust from the Lord’s lotus feet, he made a seat on the earth.**

### Text 51

*tanum asya candana-rasena tadā*  
*parilipya mālyam avayojya hṛdi \|*  
*sa caturvidhaṁ madhuram annam ataḥ*  
*paribhojya bhuri-karuṇo mumude \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The merciful Lord applied sandalwood to his body and placed a garland on his heart. He fed him tasty food of four types and rejoiced.**

### Text 52

*anunṛtya so’pi hari-kīrtanataḥ*  
*satataṁ prabhor nilaya eva babhau \|*  
*avalokya taṁ ca nija-pāda-yuga-*  
*priyam ānananda sa tu gaura-śaśī \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Dancing to kīrtana in the Lord’s house, Hari-dāsa shone. Seeing Hari-dāsa, who was fond of his lotus feet, Gauracandra became blissful,**

### Text 53

*atha tatra tena saha deva-ghaṭā-*  
*mukuṭārghya-ratna-ruci-rāji-padaḥ \|*  
*gamanāya geham abhitaḥ sahasā*  
*gatavantam āha giriśaṁ sa vibhuḥ \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, whose feet are lit by the priceless jewels in the devatās’ crowns, suddenly gave permission for Kamalākṣa (Advaita), who had come with Hari-dāsa, to go home (to Śāntipura).**

### Text 54

*sa tatheti tasya vacanād giriśaṁ*  
*pṛthivī-taleṣu kamalākṣa iti \|*  
*prathito ya eṣa bhavanaṁ muditaḥ*  
*sa yayau jagat-prabhu-girā parayā \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śiva, who was known as Kamalākṣa on earth, agreeing to his words, went home, blissful because of the words of the Lord.**

### Text 55

*avadhūtam īśvaram atho vinayān*  
*nijagāda taṁ jigamiṣuṁ yaminam \|*  
*samanuvrajann iti vidhehi vibho*  
*sumahā-prasādam amalaṁ vasanam \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord then spoke to Nityānanda, the avadhūta and Lord, controller of his senses, who wanted to go. Following after him, Gaurāṅga said, “O Lord! Take this pure cloth and give great mercy.”**

### Text 56

*iti tad-bahirvasanam ekam asau*  
*parigṛhya kāruṇikatāṁ racayan \|*  
*nija-pāda-jīvana-dhanebhya ṛte*  
*kamalākṣa-devam adadāt karuṇaḥ \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda accepted one piece of cloth as an outer garment and thus spread mercy. He gave the rest of the cloth to the devotees, whose lives and wealth were his lotus feet, except for Kamalākṣa (who was not present).**

### Text 57

*abhivādya tat tu śirasā ta ime*  
*prabhuṇā samaṁ sva-gṛham eva yayuḥ \|*  
*dyunadī-jaleṣu vihitāplavanāḥ*  
*kṛta-pūjanā api yathā-vidhi te \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Respecting the cloth with their heads, they left with Nityānanda to their homes. After taking bath in the Gaṅgā, they performed worship according to the rules.**

### Text 58

*anusandhyam āyayur atho nilaye*  
*parameśvarasya paramollasitāḥ \|*  
*sa upāgataḥ sahṛdayaiḥ paramair*  
*jagatāṁ prabhuḥ prabhavatā mahasā \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the evening, the joyful devotees went to Mahāprabhu’s house. Nityānanda, showing great effulgence, went with the affectionate devotees.**

### Text 59

*mahatā mahena mahanīya-tanur*  
*nija-kīrtanaṁ naṭanam apy akarot \|*  
*sa tu cakravad bhramaṇa-vibhrama-saṁ-*  
*prasaran-mahaḥ-samudayena tadā \|*  
*tirayann ilāvṛta-vilāsa-rucaṁ*  
*rucirānano rucira-vāg-amṛtaḥ \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With greatly radiant body the Lord, danced to the kīrtana. Spreading great effulgence as he whirled around like a wheel, with his attractive face and sweet words, he defeated the glory of Ilāvṛta-varṣa.**

### Text 60

*naṭanāntare nija-janān paritaḥ*  
*parirabhya nirbharam atho saha taiḥ \|*  
*viluṭhan karuṇāmbuja-yugena mudaṁ*  
*pravahan mṛgendra iva samprababhau \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After dancing, he strongly embraced the devotees and rolled with them on the ground. Spreading joy with his lotus glances, he shone like a lion.**

### Text 61

*ciram evam eva dharaṇīṣu vibhuḥ*  
*pariluṭhya bhakta-nicayāntarataḥ \|*  
*bhuvi nārado ya iha vipra-varaḥ*  
*parigṛhya taṁ prabhu-varo’ntaradhāt \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After rolling on the ground for a long time among the devotees, he took Śrīvāsa and disappeared.**

### Text 62

*na samīkṣyate’tha bhṛśam ākulitās*  
*tam itas tataḥ samanusandadhire \|*  
*ta itas tato’tha na samīkṣya bhṛśaṁ*  
*vikalā babhūvur atiduḥkha-bharaiḥ \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Not seeing him, the devotees became greatly afflicted. They searched everywhere for him. Not finding him anywhere, they were inundated in great sorrow.**

### Text 63

*atha tāṁs tathāvidha-hṛdaḥ karuṇān*  
*adhigamya bhuri-karuṇo madhuraḥ \|*  
*vikiran manojñatamadṛṣṭi-sudhāṁ*  
*sa tu gauracandra udiyāya tataḥ \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Merciful, sweet Gauracandra, understanding the suffering of the devotees, then appeared and sprinkled the nectar of his attractive glance upon them.**

### Text 64

*abhito’bhitas tam abhi gaura-rucaṁ*  
*kamalānanaṁ karuṇayā parayā \|*  
*parilokayantam atisāndra-mudaṁ*  
*nayanair nitāntam apibann iva te \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On all sides, continually they drunk with their eyes, Gaurāṅga, composed of concentrated bliss, who was looking at them with great compassion.**

### Text 65

*tad-anantaraṁ ca rabhasākulitaiḥ*  
*saha taiḥ sva-pāda-yuga-mātra-dhanaiḥ \|*  
*nija-kīrtanāmṛta-rasena muhur*  
*naṭanaṁ cakāra rasa-sindhu-śaśī \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The moon in an ocean of rasa danced for a long time, to the rasa of sweet kīrtana along with the devotees who took his feet as their only wealth and who became agitated with bliss.**

### Text 66

*atha karhicid bahu-vilāsa-nidhī*  
*rajanī-mukhe sukhamayāmbunidhiḥ \|*  
*avakṛṣya bhakta-jana-vastra-cayaṁ*  
*parito vilasya punar eva dadau \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On one evening, the ocean of many pastimes, the ocean of bliss, snatched the devotees’ clothing, and after thorough joking, gave the clothing back to them.**

### Text 67

*tad-anantaraṁ ca punar atīva-sukhād*  
*avadhūta īśvara upetya tataḥ \|*  
*avalokya gauram atisāndra-rucaṁ*  
*madhuraṁ jagau naṭanam apy akarot \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the avadhūta Lord, Nityānanda arrived with great happiness, and, seeing Gaurāṅga shining brightly, sang and danced sweetly.**

### Text 68

*naṭanāntare tu bhagavān jagatāṁ*  
*prabhur ādideśa nija-bhakta-janān \|*  
*avadhūta-pāda-kamalasya śubhāny*  
*avanejanāni pibata kramataḥ \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After the dancing was over, the Lord of the universe ordered his devotees, “Each of you should drink the foot water of Nityānanda.”**

### Text 69

*ta idaṁ niśamya vacanaṁ śirasā*  
*praṇatena tat-pada-payāṁsi dadhuḥ \|*  
*upajīvinaś caraṇa-paṅkaruho*  
*vacane bhavanti satataṁ niratāḥ \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing these words, the devotees took Nityānanda’s foot water on their bowed heads. The devotees, who took the Lord’s lotus feet as their life, always followed his orders.**

### Text 70

*vacasā vilāsa-gamanena kṛpā-*  
*mṛdunā vilokita-rasena tataḥ \|*  
*hasitena sāndra-madhureṇa sukhaṁ*  
*vidadhe janasya jagatāṁ karuṇaḥ \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Merciful to all people of the world, Mahāprabhu, gave happiness by his words, his coming to perform pastimes, his sweet glance filled with tender mercy, and his very sweet smile.**

### Text 71

*viharantam ittham avalokya sadā*  
*paramaṁ prabhuṁ nabhasi deva-gaṇāḥ \|*  
*dayitākulaiḥ pramada-matta-dhiyo*  
*divasaṁ niśāṁ ca gamayanti mudā \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Constantly gazing upon the Supreme Lord performing pastimes, the devatās in the sky, along with their wives, overflowing with pleasure, spent their days and nights in joy.**

### Text 72

*haridāsa īśvara iti prathitaḥ*  
*paramo jano dayita-gaura-padaḥ \|*  
*punar etya nūpura-manojña-pada-*  
*dvitayo nanarta parameśa-puraḥ \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari-dāsa, the supreme person, who cherished Gaurāṅga’s feet, called “the Lord,” came wearing ankle bells on his attractive feet and danced in front of the Lord.**

### Text 73

*punar āgataḥ sa kamalākṣa-vibhuḥ*  
*prabhupāda-paṅkaja-yugaṁ mṛdulam \|*  
*parilokya harṣa-vibhavāpluta-dhīḥ*  
*subhṛśaṁ nanada jagatī-sukhadaḥ \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great Kamalākṣa (Advaita) again came and, seeing the soft lotus feet of the Lord, his mind filled with bliss, he became overjoy, giving happiness to the universe.**

### Text 74

*lalitena pādya-salilena tataḥ*  
*saha dūrvayākṣata-cayaiś ca tataḥ \|*  
*sumanaś-cayair malaya-janma-rasaiḥ*  
*paripūjya taṁ prabhu-varo’nnam adāt \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord worshipped Kamalākṣa with pure foot water, dūrva, rice, flowers, and sandalwood, and then gave him food.**

### Text 75

*atisādhvasādara-rasena tataḥ*  
*sa mahā-prasādam avagṛhya mudā \|*  
*prabhunā samaṁ pari nanarta bhṛśaṁ*  
*hari-kīrtanāmṛta-sukhāmbu-nidhau \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With fear and respect, Kamalākṣa accepted the mahā-prasādam in joy and with the Lord danced intensely in an ocean of happiness and sweet kīrtana.**

### Text 76

*atha kaścanātiśaya-dīna-manās*  
*tanayena bhikṣur aṇusaṅgatayā \|*  
*naṭato’sya gaura-śaśinaḥ paramaṁ*  
*kim apīha vīkṣya vimumoha tataḥ \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One miserable beggar came with his son and, seeing the extraordinary actions of Gaurāṅga dancing, fainted.**

### Text 77

*ciram utthitas tu sa jagāda tadā*  
*kim aho vilokitam aho kim iti \|*  
*tad-anantaraṁ ca saha tair muditaḥ*  
*samakīrtayan lalita-gīta-kalām \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After a long time the beggar stood up and said, “What have I seen?” Then in joy he sang with the devotees an attractive song.**

### Text 78

*iti bhikṣur eṣa vipulaiḥ pulakair*  
*dviguṇībhavat-tanur atīva-sukhī \|*  
*nayanāmbubhiḥ satata-dhauta-tanū*  
*rasa-sāgare paramamarja bhṛśam \|\|78\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The beggar’s body expanded double in size as his hairs stood on end because of his happiness. His body was completely washed in his tears. He became submerged in an ocean of rasa.**

### Text 79

*atha karhicid dvija-kulaika-śaśī*  
*bhuvi yas tu nārada iti prathitaḥ \|*  
*apaṭhad bṛhat-pada-yutaṁ prathamaṁ*  
*sahasra-nāma-kṛta-paitra-kṛtiḥ \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One time, the moon among brāhmaṇas, known as Nārada on earth, Śrīvāsa, completing rites for the forefathers, began reciting a thousand names, using many verses.**

### Text 80

*sva-gṛhe sthitaḥ sa bhagavān nṛharer*  
*abhidhāṁ niśamya mahito mahasā \|*  
*narasiṁha-bhāvam adhigatya tataḥ*  
*puruṣarṣabho’gamad amuṣya gṛham \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing from his own house the name of Nṛsiṁḥa, Mahāprabhu assumed the mood of Nṛsimḥa and came to Śrīvāsa’s house.**

### Text 81

*mahatīṁ gadāṁ kara-payoruhayoḥ*  
*parigṛhya duḥsaham upetya mahaḥ \|*  
*abhidhāvati sma pathi bhūmi-talaṁ*  
*dalayan padāmbuja-valad-dalanaiḥ \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Shining with unbearable radiance, holding in his hands a great club, he ran on the path, making the earth tremble by the great pounding of his lotus feet.**

### Text 82

*atha taṁ tathā-vidham avekṣya janāḥ*  
*pathi dhāvanena paridīpta-javam \|*  
*abhito’bhito bhayam upetya bhṛśaṁ*  
*paridudruvur drutam atipracalāḥ \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing him in this state on the road, running at great speed, people because frightened and quickly ran here and there.**

### Text 83

*sa tu tān palāyana-parān manujān*  
*avalokayaṁs tad iha sausthyam adhāt \|*  
*parihāya tāṁ sumahatīṁ ca gadām*  
*agamac chanair bhavanam asya tadā \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the people running away, he recovered and gave up the huge club. He slowly went to Śrīvāsa’s house.**

### Text 84

*upagamya tatra manasā mṛdunā*  
*janatā-palāyana-vilokanataḥ \|*  
*aparādhavān aham amutra jane*  
*satataṁ kim ity atha jagāda vibhuḥ \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving there, on observing the people fleeing, with tender mind, the Lord said, “Did I commit a great offense to these people?”**

### Text 85

*na hi te kvacāpi bhagavan bhavitā*  
*nikhilāparādha-śamanasya vibhoḥ \|*  
*aparādha eṣa karuṇā-vibhavas*  
*tava satyam ittham avadat svajanaḥ \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His devotees said, “O Lord! You cannot commit an offense since you destroy all offenses. Saying it is an offense is merely your mercy. That is the truth.”**

### Text 86

*aparedyur asya karuṇāmbu-nidheḥ*  
*purataś ca kaścana sugāyanakaḥ \|*  
*śiva-gītam uttama-sukhena jagau*  
*karuṇāśayāsya karuṇasya vibhoḥ \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One day a great singer came before the Lord, the ocean of mercy, and sang a song about Śiva in great happiness for the Lord, the abode of mercy.**

### Text 87

*niśamayya gītam atidhīra-padaṁ*  
*lalitaṁ babhūva bhagavān muditaḥ \|*  
*adhiruhya tasya lasad-aṁsa-taṭaṁ*  
*naṭanaṁ cakāra sa ca dhūrjaṭivat \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the skillful song with clever verses, the Lord became joyful and mounting the shining shoulders of the singer, he danced like Śiva.**

### Text 88

*mada-ghūrṇitākṣi-yugalo vipulaiḥ*  
*pulakair atīva-ruciro rucimān \|*  
*sa tad-aṁsa-mūlam adhiruhya tadā*  
*śivavan nanarta karuṇāmbunidhiḥ \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With eyes rolling in intoxication, with hairs standing on end, shining attractively, the ocean of mercy mounted the singer’s shoulders and danced like Śiva.**

### Text 89

*bhuvi yas tu nārada iti prathitaḥ*  
*sa papāṭha tatra giriśa-stavanam \|*  
*atisusvaraḥ sa tu mukunda-bhiṣak-*  
*stavanaṁ mahimna iha hanta jagau \|\|89\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa, known as Nārada on earth, began to recite verses glorifying Śiva in a sweet voice. Mukunda, the physician, sang verses there with majesty.**

### Text 90

*tad-anantaraṁ sa tu tad-aṁsa-bhuvaṁ*  
*parimucya tatra rabhasād abhajat \|*  
*varam āsanaṁ nija-janān satataṁ*  
*pariharṣayan kumuda-bāndhavavat \|\|90\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then getting off the shoulders of the singer, the Lord quickly sat on an excellent seat, giving joy to his devotees constantly, as the moon gives joy to the night lotus.**

### Text 91

*naṭanāvasāna-samaye’nya-dine*  
*purataḥ sametya vinipatya bhuvi \|*  
*bhṛśam agrahīt pada-payoja-rajāṁsy*  
*atha kācana dvija-vadhū-pravarā \|\|91\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One day after the dancing was finished, one excellent wife of a brāhmaṇa approached the Lord and falling on the ground, took the dust of his lotus feet.**

### Text 92

*tad idaṁ vilokya sahasaiva tayā*  
*vihitaṁ babhāra bahu-duḥkha-bharam \|*  
*dyunadī-jale’tha nipapāta tadā*  
*capalaṁ prasṛtya bahudhā vikalaḥ \|\|92\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing her do that, he experienced great sorrow, became very disturbed and, quickly leaving, threw himself in the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 93

*tam amuṁ tathāvidham avekṣya balī*  
*samam uddadhāra payaso’tibalāt \|*  
*avadhūta-deva iha gaura-vibhuṁ*  
*guru-dor-dvayena sahasā vikalaḥ \|\|93\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Strong Nityānanda, best of the avadhūtas, seeing the Lord in this state, became agitated and, with great energy quickly pulled Gaurāṅga from the water using his huge arms.**

### Text 94

*haridāsaka-prabhṛtayo’nucarāḥ*  
*sahasā sametya bahudhā vidhurāḥ \|*  
*parivavrur enam atikāruṇikaṁ*  
*sabhayaṁ sa-gadgadam amī ruruduḥ \|\|94\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari-dāsa and other followers quickly gathered in great distress. They surrounded his pitiful figure in fear and with choked voiced wept.**

### Text 95

*sa murāri-gupta-nilayaṁ saha tair*  
*upagatya bhūri-karuṇaḥ prababhau \|*  
*punar apy agād dvija-geham atho*  
*rajanīṁ ca tatra karuṇo’gamayat \|\|95\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The merciful Lord then went to Murāri Gupta’s house with the devotees. He then went to a brāhmaṇa’s house and spent the night there.**

### Text 96

*bhagavān prabhāta-samaye’nya-dine*  
*dyunadīṁ pratīrya saha tair agamat \|*  
*taṭam uttaraṁ vikalitena hṛdā*  
*kṣaṇam eva viśramaṇam ātanuta \|\|96\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On another day in the early morning, the Lord crossed the Gaṅgā with the devotees and came to the northern bank. He rested there for a moment, with his heart agitated.**

### Text 97

*atha te bhayena mahatā vinayaiḥ*  
*parisāntvanaṁ kila vidhāya muhuḥ \|*  
*prabhum ālayaṁ samanayan muditā*  
*bhajatāṁ hi bhāva-vaśa eṣa khalu \|\|97\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In great fear the devotees constantly tried to calm him in a gentle way. Joyfully they brought him to a house. He was under the control of the mood of the devotees.**

### Text 98

*śrīvāsasya gṛhaṁ sametya sa punaḥ provāca dhīrākṣaraṁ*  
*sarveṣām avaśṛṇvatāṁ hi purataḥ śrī-gauracandraḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*tyaktvāhaṁ jananīṁ vrajāmi kila cet kutrāpi tasmāj janaḥ*  
*sarvo’yaṁ kṛtavān niruddham asakau nūnaṁ vadiṣyaty adaḥ \|\|98\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra came to Śrīvāsa’s house again and spoke seriously while all listened. “If I give up my mother and go somewhere, all people will say that I have done something contrary to dharma.”**

### Text 99

*murāri-gupto’tha jagāda vākyaṁ*  
*śrutvā tadīyaṁ sudhayaiva siktam \|*  
*na ko’pi nātheha bhavatsu tat tad*  
*vadiṣyati premada-pāda-padmaḥ \|\|99\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Murāri Gupta, hearing those words drenched in nectar, said, “No one will say this about you, since your lotus feet give prema.”**

### Text 100

*śrutvetthaṁ vacanam asau kṛpā-samudraḥ*  
*saṁhṛṣṭaḥ parama-sukhas tam āliliṅga \|*  
*so’py evaṁ pulaka-ghaṭā-vibhinna-dehaḥ*  
*ślokaikaṁ mudita-manāḥ papāṭha dainyāt \|\|100\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of mercy, hearing these words, became joyful and in great happiness embraced him. Murāri’s hairs stood on end and with a joyful mind he recited one verse in humility.**

### Text 101

*kvāhaṁ daridraḥ pāpīyān ity ādi (bhā.pu. 10.81.16) \|*  
*kvāhaṁ daridraḥ pāpīyān kva kṛṣṇaḥ śrī-niketanaḥ*  
*brahma-bandhur iti smāhaṁ bāhubhyāṁ parirambhitaḥ 101*

**Перевод:**

**Who am I? A sinful, poor friend of a** ***brāhmaṇa.*** **And who is Kṛṣṇa? The Supreme Lord, full in six powers. Nonetheless, he has embraced me with his two arms. SB 10.81.16**

### Text 102

*śrutvā sa ittham uditaṁ bhagavāṁs tadaiva*  
*svaiśvaryam uttamam upetya rarāja nāthaḥ \|*  
*ramyāsanopari pariṣṭhita udbhaṭena*  
*tejaś cayena dinanātha-sahasra-tulyaḥ \|\|102\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing these words, the Lord manifested great power and shone like a thousand suns as he sat on an attractive seat while radiating great effulgence.**

### Text 103

*idaṁ śarīraṁ paramaṁ manojñaṁ*  
*sac-cid-ghanānanda-mayaṁ mamaiva \|*  
*jānīta yūyaṁ nahi kiñcid anyad*  
*vināsti bhūmau sa itīdam ūce \|\|103\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He said, “You know that my body is most attractive, composed of eternity, knowledge and bliss. There is nothing other than this on earth.”**

### Text 104

*hṛṣṭās tat tan nātha-vākyaṁ niśamya*  
*prodyad-romāñcāñcitāṅgāḥ samantāt \|*  
*śrīvāsādyā netra-vāri-pravāhaiḥ*  
*samyak snātās tatra tatraivam āsan \|\|104\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the Lord’s words, the devotees headed by Śrīvāsa became joyful. Their hairs stood on end completely. They were bathed in rivers of tears. They simply stood there.**

### Text 105

*śrīvāso’sau pūrvavad gāṅga-toyaiḥ*  
*svaccha-svacchaiḥ snāpayāmāsa bhūyaḥ \|*  
*śrī-gaurāṅgaṁ tat-padaikāvalambaḥ*  
*premāmbhobhir dhauta-sarvāṅga-ramyam \|\|105\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa, who took shelter of his lotus feet, after washing all Gaurāṅga’s limbs with his tears of love, bathed him again with clear Gaṅgā water.**

### Text 106

*yāvat kumbhair gauracandraṅga-yaṣṭau*  
*gaṅgā-toyair bhūsuro’yaṁ siṣeca \|*  
*tāvat svāṅge netra-pāthoruhābhyāṁ*  
*premnā niryat-toyam udgīrṇavān saḥ \|\|106\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa bathed the limbs of Gaurāṅga with pots of Gaṅgā water while releasing water from his lotus eyes onto his limbs because of his prema.**

### Text 107

*evaṁ bhūyaḥ kautukaṁ te vilokya*  
*premodbhrāntāḥ kīrtanaṁ nartanaṁ ca \|*  
*uccair uccaiś cakrur unmatta-cittāḥ*  
*śrī-gaurāṅga-prema-mātrāvalambāḥ \|\|107\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the happiness, the devotees, agitated with prema, taking shelter of Gaurāṅga’s prema, loudly performed kīrtana and danced with crazed hearts.**

### Text 108

*anyedyur gauracandro nija-jana-sahito bhakti-śikṣāṁ vitanvann*  
*atyantāścarya-ceṣṭaḥ kamala-bhava-bhavādyair bhṛśaṁ bhāvanīyaḥ \|*  
*ku-jñānādyaiḥ samantāt sakala-manupuraṁ devatānāṁ niketaṁ*  
*sanmārjanyā ca cakre jagati suvidito mārjitaṁ śaśvad eva \|\|108\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On another day, the Lord, endowed with remarkable pastimes, whom Brahmā and Śiva contemplate, distributed teachings about bhakti along with the devotees. In this way he cleansed the universe completely of despicable jñāna, and cleansed the Lord’s temple with a broom. He became well known in the world for this eternally.**

## Chapter Eight

### Text 1

*kadācid atha taṁ prītyā gacchantaṁ paramaṁ prabhum \|*  
*praṇamya vinayāt kaścit provāca madhurākṣaram \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One day, some person saw the Supreme Lord coming and offered respects with affection. He spoke politely with sweet words.**

### Text 2

*sarve tvāṁ devadeveśaṁ saccid-ghana-śarīriṇam \|*  
*puruṣaṁ paramaṁ prāhus tan noddharasi kiṁ nu mām \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Everyone says that you are the Supreme Lord, lord of lords, with a body of eternity, knowledge and bliss. Will you not deliver me?**

### Text 3

*trāhi māṁ sarva sarveśa kuṣṭhāt parama-garhitāt \|*  
*dodhūyamāna-hṛdayaṁ kṛpāṁ kuru kṛpānidhe \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord of all beings! O complete being! O ocean of mercy! Trembling in my heart, mercifully deliver me from this most despised leprosy.”**

### Text 4

*śrutvedaṁ tad vacaḥ śrīmān krodhāruṇita-locanaḥ \|*  
*jagāda vadana-vyājād dvija-rājena śobhitaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing his words, beautiful Mahāprabhu, with moon-like face, his eyes red with anger, with an apparent grimace, spoke.**

### Text 5

*āḥ pāpātman durācāra mad-bhakta-dveṣa-kāraka \|*  
*tvām uddhariṣye cen nāham uddhariṣyāmi kaṁ tataḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O sinful, wicked man! You show hatred to my devotees. If I do not save you, is it that I will not save anyone else?**

### Text 6

*śrīvāsasya sadā dveṣaṁ yatas tvaṁ kṛtavān asi \|*  
*ata eva pratibhavaṁ kuṣṭhī khalu bhaviṣyasi \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Since you showed hatred to Śrīvāsa all the time, you will suffer from leprosy in every birth.**

### Text 7

*asmin dehe tu ye prāṇās te na lakṣyā kadācana \|*  
*bahiś-carā iva prāṇā vaiṣṇavā iti viddhi me \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“In this body one cannot see the prāṇa. But know that the devotees are like my prāṇa externalized.**

### Text 8

*ye ye yena prakāreṇa tān dviṣanti mama priyān \|*  
*teṣāṁ teṣāṁ pratibhavaṁ narake patanaṁ bhavet \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If in any way one offends the devotees who are dear to me, he will fall to hell in every birth.**

### Text 9

*vaiṣṇavebhyo natā ye ca ye tad-ājñā-parāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*te ta eva tariṣyanti saṁsārārṇavam utkaṭam \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who respect the devotees and follow their orders will cross the terrible ocean of saṁsāra.”**

### Text 10

*ity uktvā geham agamat śrīvāsasya mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*tena sārdhaṁ tadā reme bhagavān bhakta-bhaktimān \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Mahāprabhu went to Śrīvāsa’s house and, as the Lord who has devotion to his devotee, enjoyed time with him.**

### Text 11

*ekadā nṛtya-samaye draṣṭuṁ gaurāṅga-sundaram \|*  
*calito dvāra-pālena vārito dharaṇī-suraḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One time while they was dancing, a brāhmaṇa came to see beautiful Gaurāṅga, but was stopped by the door keeper and went away.**

### Text 12

*kruddho’para-dine so’yaṁ gaṅgāyās taṭa-sannidhau \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā jagat-prabhuṁ tatra durmukho roṣa-lohitaḥ \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Another day, full of anger, he saw the Lord of the universe on the bank of the Gaṅgā. His face was twisted and his eyes were red with anger.**

### Text 13

*upavītaṁ dvidhā cchittvā śāpaṁ dāsyann idaṁ jagau \|*  
*tvāṁ nṛtya-samaye draṣṭuṁ gatavān aham ekadā \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The brāhmaṇa broke his upavīta, and, cursing the Lord, said, “I came to see you once while you were dancing.**

### Text 14

*tavaiva dvāra-pālena vāritas tena duḥkhitaḥ \|*  
*śāpaṁ dadāmi tat tubhyaṁ saṁsārāc chittir astu te \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I was prevented from entering by the guard and became sad. I now curse you that your material life will be destroyed.”**

### Text 15

*tac chrutvā bhagavān nātho nananda manasā muhuḥ \|*  
*ruṣṭasya śāpo viprasya varo’bhūd iti harṣitaḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, the Lord was pleased in his mind. In joy he thought, “The curse of the angry brāhmaṇa is a blessing to me.”**

### Text 16

*iti śrutvā harau śāpaṁ brahma-śāpād vimucyate \|*  
*tad idaṁ śraddhayā lokaiḥ śrotavyaṁ śuddha-buddhibhiḥ \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If one hears the story of the brāhmaṇa’s curse, one will be free from curses of brāhmaṇas. Intelligent people should hear this story with faith.**

### Text 17

*anyedyuḥ padminīṁ mudrāṁ kareṇārko’pasārayan \|*  
*udayādreḥ samuttasthau vilāsī śayanād iva \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On another day, like a playful person arising from sleep, the sun arrived at the horizon, opening the closed lotuses with its rays.**

### Text 18

*tato gaurāṅga-candro’pi brāhmaṇān sajjanān bahūn \|*  
*pāṭhayan pūrṇa-pīyūṣa-raśmivat sa vyarocata \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra also, having many qualified brāhmaṇas recite, shone like the full moon.**

### Text 19

*kṣaṇād vaihvalya-sambhinnaḥ skhalat-sarva-tanuḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*madhūni dehi dehīti babhāṣe madhurānanaḥ \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For a moment, he became full of disturbance and stumbled about with his whole body. With a sweet face he said, “Give me honey! Give me honey!”**

### Text 20

*āścaryam āścaryam idaṁ caritaṁ paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*hāṭakācala-gauro’yaṁ raupyācala ivābhavat \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This action of the supreme Lord was most astonishing. The golden mountain of Gaurāṅga became like a silver mountain.**

### Text 21

*sīrapāṇiṁ nīlavāsaḥ samalaṅkṛta-vigraham \|*  
*ghūrṇāpūrṇākṣi-yugalaṁ mada-matta-viceṣṭitam \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He had a plough in his hand, wore blue cloth, and was decorated with many ornaments. He rolled his eyes and acted with the intoxication of liquor.**

### Text 22

*evaṁ tat tat kṣaṇe sarve dadṛśus te mudānvitāḥ \|*  
*rohiṇy-aṅga-bhuvo bhāvaṁ dadhānaṁ parameśvaram \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the Lord who had assumed the mood of the son of Rohiṇī for a moment, everyone became blissful.**

### Text 23

*kīrtayadbhis tataḥ sarvair janaiḥ saha mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*murāri-gupta-nilaye jagāma paramotsukhaḥ \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In great joy, Mahāprabhu then went to Murāri Gupta’s house with the devotees, who performed kīrtana.**

### Text 24

*madhūni dehi dehīti tatrāpi madhurākṣaram \|*  
*uktāmbu-pātraṁ hastena dhṛtvāmbūni bhṛśaṁ papau \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying the sweet words, “Give me honey” holding a water pot in his hand, he drank the water in plenty.**

### Text 25

*mada-ghūrṇita-lolākṣaḥ kṣaṇadā-nātha-sundaraḥ \|*  
*śuklair mahobhir gehasya śaityaṁ kurvan nanarta saḥ \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With eyes rolling from intoxication, the beautiful Lord who gives happiness, making the house white with his white rays, began dancing.**

### Text 26

*nāhaṁ sa kṛṣṇo vacasā yo’sau śīghraṁ sukhī bhavet \|*  
*tad ānayānaya bhṛśaṁ madhūny adya samarpaya \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The person who is happy with the words ‘I am not Kṛṣṇa’ should now bring honey and offer it to me.”**

### Text 27

*ity uktvaikena hastena dvijaikaṁ prākṣipat prabhuḥ \|*  
*ārād eva papātāsau mallo’pi balavattaraḥ \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, the Lord pushed a brāhmaṇa with one hand. That person, stronger than a wrestler, fell far away.**

### Text 28

*prātar eva balāveśa-vivaśo rajanī-mukhe \|*  
*prabuddhaḥ syāt tadā snānaṁ karoti kamalekṣaṇaḥ \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having assumed Balarāma’s mood in the morning, the lotus eyed Lord recovered in the evening and took a bath.**

### Text 29

*aparedyur dīpyamānas tejobhir atiduḥsahaiḥ \|*  
*muhur mumoha bhagavān vikīrṇa-kaca-sañcayaiḥ \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On another day, shining with intolerable radiance, the Lord, with hair scattered, fainted.**

### Text 30

*baladevāveśa-ramyaṁ matta-dvirada-gāminam \|*  
*matta-siṁha-samollāsaṁ mada-ghūrṇita-locanam \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He was pleasing in his form as Balarāma, walking like an intoxicated elephant. He was as regal as an intoxicated lion. His eyes rolled with intoxication.**

### Text 31

*rajyad-gaṇḍa-sthalaṁ caṇḍa-raśmi-koṭi-sama-prabham \|*  
*vaihvalya-nunna-hṛdayaṁ dṛṣṭvetthaṁ te tadā vadan \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His cheeks shone and his body shone like a million suns. Seeing this form endowed with an agitated heart, the devotees spoke.**

### Text 32

*kim idaṁ nātha ko vāyaṁ veśaḥ kiṁ vā paraṁ mahaḥ \|*  
*kim atra kāraṇaṁ brūhi bhagavān sarva-bhāvanaḥ \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord! What is this? Is it a possession? Is it a show of your greatness? What is the cause? Please tell us. You are the Lord, the creator of all things!”**

### Text 33

*evaṁ balāveśa-līlā-lalito lalitāspadam \|*  
*uvāca skhalitaṁ śaśvad vacanaṁ mada-ghūrṇitaḥ \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Attractive with his pastime as Balarāma, his eyes rolling with intoxication, the Lord spoke with a broken voice words of beauty.**

### Text 34

*dṛṣṭo mayā sīra-pāṇir nīlāmbara-dharaḥ pumān \|*  
*ropyācala iva śrīmān ko’py asau mādṛśair iha \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I have seen the person with a plough in his hand, wearing blue cloth. He is beautiful like a silver mountain. He is amazing for persons like me in this world.”**

### Text 35

*śrī-candraśekharācārya-ratnaṁ tatra jagāda tam \|*  
*yas tvayā nātha dṛṣṭo’sau kutrāste balināṁ varaḥ \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Candraśekhara, the best of ācāryas, said to him, “Where does this Lord, best of the strong, whom you saw, stay?”**

### Text 36

*evaṁ vadan dadarśāsau tam eva halinaḥ prabhoḥ \|*  
*āveśāveśa-vinyāsaṁ bibhrataṁ gaurasundaraḥ \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he said this, he saw Gaurasundara assume the form of Balarāma.**

### Text 37

*tatas tad-bhāvam āpannaḥ śrīmān koṭīndu-sundaraḥ \|*  
*gaurāṅgo nartanaṁ cakre taiḥ sarvair muditātmabhiḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Assuming Balarāma’s mood, the Lord, beautiful as a million moons, danced with his overjoyed devotees.**

### Text 38

*nṛtyatas tasya pīyūṣa-drava-siktaiḥ pade pade \|*  
*jalpitais te svarga-sukham adharīcakrur añjasā \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees easily considered the happiness of Svarga insignificant in describing with words drenched in nectar the dancing of the Lord.**

### Text 39

*evaṁ dinaṁ sa nṛtyena nināya parama-prabhuḥ \|*  
*kīrtanāmṛta-vāpīṣu snātais taiḥ svajanaiḥ saha \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**During the day, the supreme Lord danced with his devotees who bathed in a lake of sweet kīṛtana.**

### Text 40

*tato’parāhne bhūyo’smin nṛtyati śrī-yute marut \|*  
*mada-gandhair diśaḥ sarvāḥ samantāt samapūjayat \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the afternoon, when the Lord danced, the wind worshipped all directions completely with the fragrance of musk.**

### Text 41

*taṁ taṁ gandhaṁ samāghrāya madotkaṭam atisphuṭam \|*  
*ākasmikair iva ghanair bhramaraiḥ pidadhe nabhaḥ \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Smelling that strong fragrance of musk, the bees suddenly covered the sky like a cloud.**

### Text 42

*śrī-rāma-nāmā viprāgryo dadarśākāśa-maṇḍalāt \|*  
*samāgatān mahā-kāntīn mahā-dīptīn mahājanān \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great brāhmaṇa named Śrī-rāma saw many beautiful, greatly effulgent beings come from the sky.**

### Text 43

*divya-gandhānuliptāṅgān divyābharaṇa-bhūṣitān \|*  
*divya-srag-vasanān divyān divya-rūpa-guṇāśrayān \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They were decorated with shining ornaments and anointed with heavenly sandalwood. They wore heavenly garlands and had heavenly forms and qualities.**

### Text 44

*eka-karṇa-dhṛtāmbhoja-karṇa-pūra-manoharān \|*  
*ūṣṇīṣa-paṭṭa-saṁśliṣṭa-mastakān hṛṣṭa-mānasān \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They were attractive with one earring in each lotus ear and heads furnished with silk turbans. They had joyful minds.**

### Text 45

*anye tasya mukhāc chrutvā nanṛtur jagur añjasā \|*  
*kīrtanena harer nāmnām āmnāya-sudhiyo bhṛśam \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When others learned in the Vedas heard this from Śrī-rāma, they danced and sang spontaneously with enthusiasm, performing kīrtana of the Lord’s name.**

### Text 46

*tatraiva kaścid viprāgryo vanamālī mahāśayaḥ \|*  
*apaśyat parvatākāraṁ halaṁ kāñcana-nirmitam \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A great brāhmaṇa named Vanamālī came there, and saw a plough, huge like a mountain, made of gold.**

### Text 47

*dṛṣṭvā sa-vismayo bhūtvā locanāśru-jharākulaḥ \|*  
*pulakaugha-parītāṅgo na sasmāra tadā tanum \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing this, he was amazed. He was overcome with torrents of tears. His hairs stood on end. He could not remember his body.**

### Text 48

*tato nanarta taiḥ sārdhaṁ nija-kīrtana-maṅgalaiḥ \|*  
*halāyudhāveśa-ramyo ramya-gaurāṅga-sundaraḥ \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then beautiful Gaurāṅga, attractive with his manifestation of Balarāma, danced with the devotees who performed kīrtana.**

### Text 49

*divi deva-gaṇāḥ sarve samahendrāḥ sapadmajāḥ \|*  
*praṇemuḥ kusuma-stomaṁ varṣanto nata-kandharāḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the sky all the devatās headed by Indra and Brahmā showered flowers and offered respects with bowed shoulders.**

### Text 50

*evaṁ niśāvaśeṣo’bhūn nṛtyati śrīyute prabhau \|*  
*candraś carama-śailāntaṁ cucumba śanakair iva \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the beautiful Lord danced, the night came to an end. The moon gradually kissed the Western Mountain.**

### Text 51

*nṛtyatas tasya naṭana-darśanārtham iyaṁ kim u \|*  
*purandarāśā taruṇī babhūvātyanurāgiṇī \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At this time did the young girl personifying the eastern direction became attracted to see the dancing of the Lord, by turning red?**

### Text 52

*manda-gandhavahaḥ śaśvat jyotsnayābhyupagūhitaḥ \|*  
*kumudāni samādhunvan gaurāṅghrim abhajat tataḥ \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The gentle, fragrant breeze, embraced by the moon light, shaking the lotuses, worshipped the lotus feet of Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 53

*tatas taiḥ svajanaiḥ sārdhaṁ svar-nadyāṁ jagatāṁ prabhuḥ \|*  
*upeyivān babhau nātho yathā meruḥ sahādribhiḥ \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord of the universe with his devotees approached the Gaṅgā and shone like Meru with other mountains.**

### Text 54

*avagāhya tato gaṅgāṁ gāṅgeyācala-sundaraḥ \|*  
*karavāribhir anyonyaṁ cakāra jala-khelanam \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The beautiful golden mountain, submerging himself in the Gaṅgā, then played in the water with the devotees by splashing water with his hands.**

### Text 55

*evaṁ nānā-prakārāṇi krīḍitāni samāpayan \|*  
*yayau gehaṁ nijaṁ gauro yathendur udayācalam \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After finishing various types of play, he went to his house just as the moon rises on the eastern horizon.**

### Text 56

*hasann asau sumadhuraṁ śrīvāsam avadat prabhuḥ \|*  
*veṇuṁ prayaccha me śīghraṁ kva so’sti na tu dṛśyate \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Smiling sweetly, the Lord said to Śrīvāsa, “Quickly bring my flute. Where is it? I do not see it.”**

### Text 57

*tato’yaṁ vipra-pravaro hasann idam abhāṣata \|*  
*veṇus tavāsti gopībhiḥ paritaḥ parirakṣitaḥ \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Smiling, the best of brāhmaṇas said, “The gopīs have hidden your flute somewhere.”**

### Text 58

*vṛndāvana-krīḍitāni smṛtvā smṛtvā kṛpā-nidhiḥ \|*  
*sāndrānandaika-sandoha-magnas tūṣṇīm abhūt kṣaṇam \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Constantly remembering the Vṛndāvana pastimes, the ocean of mercy, submerged in great bliss, remained silent for some time.**

### Text 59

*tataś cātiśayāviṣṭo hṛṣṭa-romā mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*brūhi brūhīti satatam uccais taṁ nijagāda saḥ \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then completely absorbed, with hairs standing on end, Mahāprabhu spoke constantly to Śrīvāsa in a loud voice. “Tell me, tell me.”**

### Text 60

*vṛndāvana-krīḍanaṁ ca yamunā-krīḍanaṁ tathā \|*  
*sarvaṁ tato’sau śrīvāso varṇayāmāsa bhūriśaḥ \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The brāhmaṇa Śrīvāsa then described all the Vṛndāvana pastimes and pastimes in the Yamunā.**

### Text 61

*purā vṛndāraṇye taruṇa-hariṇākṣībhir aniśaṁ*  
*tvayi premāviṣṭe vilasati ya āsīt sa vibhavaḥ \|*  
*tvayaivātṛptenājani na yadi tan nātha rabhasaḥ*  
*kathaṅkāraṁ nityaṁ nava nava ivāyaṁ samabhavat \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You could not be satisfied with the good fortune which existed previously when you performed pastimes with the young gopīs in Vṛndāvana in deep prema. O Lord! If that were not so, how could that joy appear always newer and newer?**

### Text 62

*āmañju-guñja-dali-puñja-nikuñja-ramyaṁ*  
*vṛndāvanaṁ nirupamaṁ sa purā praviśya \|*  
*krīḍāṁ cakartha rasa-kautuka-kāma-tantra-*  
*mantra-svarūpa iva yat tvam atipriyaṁ tat \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Previously, like the incarnation of the art of love who produces joyful rasa, you entered incomparable Vṛndāvana, most dear to you, endowed with many bowers covered with attractive bees and performed pastimes.”**

### Text 63

*evaṁ niśamya mada-matta-mṛgendra-nādaṁ*  
*bhūyo vadeti madhuraṁ nijagāda nāthaḥ \|*  
*atrāntare dvija-varaḥ sa ca tat-kṛpābhiḥ*  
*sarvaṁ tadīya-caritaṁ prakaṭaṁ jagāda \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, the Lord spoke sweetly, roaring like an intoxicated lion, “Speak more.” Then the best of brāhmaṇas by his mercy related with clarity all the Lord’s pastimes.**

## Chapter Nine

### Text 1

*ittham udbhaṭa-sukhāmbudhi-magnaṁ*  
*gauracandram atha yathā so’bhijagāda \|*  
*śrūyatāṁ prabhuvara sva-vihāraṁ*  
*prāk-kṛtaṁ svayam ahaṁ kathayāmi \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa said to Gauracandra, who was submerged in an extraordinary ocean of happiness, “O Lord! Please hear of your pastimes that you previously performed. I will narrate them.”**

### Text 2

*vīkṣya tad-vadanam anirvacanīyaṁ*  
*ramya-ramyam api valgu-manobhiḥ \|*  
*śreyasā saha vilāsavatībhiḥ*  
*svāṅga-vallibhir akāri vicitram \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the indescribable, attractive face of the Lord, the playful gopīs, with beautiful minds, eager to play with him, performed astonishing actions with their limbs.**

### Text 3

*preyasā saha navīna-tamāla-*  
*śyāmalena vipinaṁ praviśantaḥ \|*  
*tat-puro nava-ghanena vilāsaṁ*  
*vidyutāṁ dadhur amūr vraja-vadhvaḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With their lover, dark like a tamāla tree, the women of Vraja entered a grove, and in front of him acted as lightning with a fresh cloud.**

### Text 4

*rāmaṇīyakam avekṣya ramaṇyo*  
*mānasena manasijena lasantyaḥ \|*  
*ceṣṭayā rucirayālasa-bhājo*  
*bhāvitāḥ samabhavann adinātham \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The attractive women, seeing their attractive Lord, dancing in their minds with Cupid, desiring attractive activity, developed loving sentiments for their Lord.**

### Text 5

*sābhilāsam atha bhāvavatīnāṁ*  
*kṛṣṇa-candram abhimugdha-vadhūnām \|*  
*sādhvasaṁ prathamajaṁ tirayitvā*  
*manmathena hṛdaye samudāse \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The beautiful women, possessing love filled with desire, surpassed the fear that first arose in their hearts, by the influence of Cupid.**

### Text 6

*aṁśukaṁ śithilitaṁ draḍhayitvā*  
*bibhratī sacakita-trapam ekā \|*  
*sasmita-priya-sakhī-jana-pārśve*  
*līlayā laghu calanty abhireje \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One gopī, full of fear, tightly tied up her loose cloth and moved quickly and playfully towards a smiling friend.**

### Text 7

*kāpi mandamayatā parivṛtte*  
*mārutena kuca-yugmaka-cele \|*  
*sambhramāt priya-sakhī-janam uccair*  
*āliliṅga paripaśyati kṛṣṇe \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the gentle breeze moved the cloth on her breasts, one gopī hastily embraced her friend tightly, while Kṛṣṇa watched.**

### Text 8

*unnamayya bhuja-yugmam athānyā*  
*pīvara-stana-yugonnamanena \|*  
*sāṅga-bhaṅgam alasena lasantī*  
*jṛmbhate sma purato dayitasya \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Another gopī raised her two heavy breasts by raising her hands, and lazily posturing her body, yawned in front of her lover.**

### Text 9

*pīvarorasija-kuṭmala-kāntāṁ*  
*pāṇi-dhūta-nava-pallava-kāntim \|*  
*projjya kānana-latāṁ vara-nārī-*  
*deha-vallim abhajan madhupaughaḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Swarms of bees gave up the forest creepers and enjoyed the creeper-like bodies of the excellent women, whose full breasts were as beautiful as buds and whose hands defeated the beauty of new shoots.**

### Text 10

*subhruvāṁ tanu-latāsu latānāṁ*  
*śrīr iyaṁ saparitoṣam abhūt kim \|*  
*sarvataḥ sapadi tāsu yad etan-*  
*mañju-guñja-dalināṁ kulam āsīt \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Were the bees satisfied with the beauty of the creeper-like bodies of the beautiful women since they flocked completely to those creepers with loud buzzing?**

### Text 11

*ekayauṣṭha-patane’mṛtapatvaṁ*  
*prepsur unmadataro madhupāyī \|*  
*oṣṭha-daṁśana-ratasya sa-toṣaṁ*  
*preyasaḥ smaraṇato na nirāse \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The bees, desiring to drink nectar, became insane on touching their bodies with their lips. Remembering with pleasure their lover’s touch with his teeth and lips, the gopīs did not drive the bees away.**

### Text 12

*mantharaṁ madana-vihvala-haṁsī-*  
*lāsya-śaṁsi madhura-krama-ramyam \|*  
*ādadhuś caraṇa-paṅkaja-ramyaṁ*  
*subhruvo’tha laghu tatra vihartum \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**More graceful than swans intoxicated with love, the beautiful women lightly and slowly walked with their attractive lotus feet in order to enjoy pastimes.**

### Text 13

*ullasan-madana-manthara-pāda-*  
*nyāsa-bhāji-gamane ramaṇīnām \|*  
*śroṇi-bimba-kucayoḥ pariṇāhaḥ*  
*khedayann api babhūva sukhāya \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Moving with graceful steps that delighted Cupid, the women were in difficulty because of the size of their hips and breasts, but went in order to give happiness to Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 14

*vīci-bhaṅga iva kāñcana-kāñcī-*  
*kāma-ḍiṇḍima-raveṇa nitambaḥ \|*  
*subhruvāṁ gamana-vibhrama-bhūṣo*  
*manda-mandam alasena nanarta \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With sounds like small drums from the jingling bells on their golden belts, like waves breaking on the shore, the hips of the women, decorated with graceful movements, danced slowly and indolently.**

### Text 15

*komalaṁ caraṇa-padmam aśaktaṁ*  
*mā sma gā drutataraṁ madirākṣi \|*  
*ity atīva vivaśau rudataḥ kiṁ*  
*nūpurau praṇayato ramaṇīnām \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O women with fickle eyes! Your soft lotus feet are incapable. Do not go quickly!” Did their ankle bells weep in helplessness out of affection for the women?**

### Text 16

*tat-tad-aṅghri-kamalasya vilāse*  
*saspṛhaṁ kathayatīva mahāntam \|*  
*svānurāgam anurāgavatīnāṁ*  
*yāvakair aruṇitā vana-bhūmiḥ \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The forest earth became red with the lac from the feet of the gopīs filled with anurāga as they moved their lotus feet. Did this redness (rāga) indicate the earth’s great anurāga for the Lord, filled with desire?**

### Text 17

*kṛṣṇa-pṛṣṭha-taṭa-lagna-kucāgrā*  
*tat-tad-aṁsa-vilasad-bhuja-mūlā \|*  
*sāci-tad-vadana-cumbita-vaktrā*  
*kāpi tatra ruruce’nucalantī \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One gopī touched the tip of her breast to Kṛṣṇa’s back and placed her arm on his shoulder. She kissed his face while turning her head and then followed after him.**

### Text 18

*pṛṣṭhataḥ priyatamena bhujābhyāṁ*  
*śliṣṭa-vakṣasi ruhāmburuhākṣī \|*  
*indranīlamaṇi-hāram ivāsyā*  
*kaṇṭha-sīmni dadhatī calitāsīt \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One gopī was embraced on her chest by Kṛṣṇa from behind. She held him on her throat like a sapphire necklace.**

### Text 19

*keśavāṁsa-taṭa-rāji-bhujāyā*  
*manthāralasa-gateḥ saha yāntyāḥ \|*  
*tan-nitamba-bhuvi lagna-vilagno*  
*vīcivat kila rarāja nitambaḥ \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With her arm on his shoulder, one gopī moved slowly and gracefully with him. Her hips, attached to his hips, appeared like waves merging together.**

### Text 20

*prāṇanātham adhi kāpi sakhibhir*  
*bibhratī gatim anaṅga-vibhaṅgyā \|*  
*sāṅga-bhaṅgam anugāṁsa-taṭe’dhād*  
*bāhu-mūlam udayat-kuca-mūlam \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One gopī with her friends moved about, making amorous gestures directed to Kṛṣṇa. She raised her arm attached to her breast and placed it on the shoulder of a friend with graceful movements of her limbs.**

### Text 21

*tat-kṣaṇe kṣaṇata eva vadhūnāṁ*  
*manmathena bahudhā vivaśānām \|*  
*āyayau sapadi kānana-lakṣmīḥ*  
*sā yathepsitam upāyana-bhāram \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At that moment, the Lakṣmī of the forest suddenly arrived bearing desirable gifts for the gopīs who had become helpless because of Cupid.**

### Text 22

*mā sma mānini kṛthāḥ śramam uccais*  
*tyajyatāṁ vivaśatāṁ sarasākṣi \|*  
*hema-gauri garimāṇam upeto*  
*māna eṣa bhavitaiva cariṣṇuḥ \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**She said, “O proud women! You have become very tired. O women with pleasing eyes! Give up your helpless condition. O golden women! Your deep pride should vanish.**

### Text 23

*paśya matta-hāriṇākṣi dhunānā*  
*pallavaṁ tava karasya samānam \|*  
*mādhurī kusuma-yauvana-ramyā*  
*bādhyate madhukarair atilubdhaiḥ \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Look, doe-eyed women! Shaking its shoots, similar to hands, the mādhavī creeper, attractive with the flower of youth, is being attacked by the greedy bees.**

### Text 24

*dharṣitāpi madhupair iha mallī-*  
*vallir ullasita-kuṭmala-ramyā \|*  
*pāṇivat kiśalayaṁ vidhunānā*  
*kiṁ śaśāka parimarda-śamāya \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The jasmine creeper, attractive with blossoming buds, is being attacked by bees. Shaking its twigs like hands, can it alleviate the attack?**

### Text 25

*paśya bhṛṅga-lulitā dala-kampair*  
*evam eva parivakti lateyam \|*  
*naiva naiva mada-bhāji riraṁsau*  
*subhruvo manasi tiṣṭhati mānaḥ \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Look! This creeper with trembling leaves is attacked by the bees. It says, ‘If the proud woman desires to enjoy, she can never keep pride in her mind.’”**

### Text 26

*āśravaṁ tam imāśliṣa kāntaṁ*  
*muñca muñca sakhi mānam asantam \|*  
*kāpi bhāva-caturā parihāsaiḥ*  
*prāṇanātham abhi kāñcid avādīt \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One gopī, skillful in love, while laughing to another gopī, in the presence of Kṛṣṇa, said, “Embrace the compliant lover. Rid yourself of unsteady pride, O friend!”**

**When one gopī had her clothing stolen by Kṛṣṇa, she desired to block out all the light. But the jewels reflected her form, and she became embarrassed.**

*susmitair hima-mayūkha-mayūkhaiḥ*  
*saṁśravanty aniśam indu-maṇīnām \|*

*sphāṭikair viraciteti vadhūbhiḥ \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Moonstone melts in the smiling moonlight. Seeing the platform, the gopīs thought it was made of crystalline water.**

*subhruvāṁ caraṇa-pallava-pātair*  
*bimbavaty anavagāham agādhāt \|*

*dṛśyate sphaṭika-bhūr api yatra \|\|82\|\|*

**The crystal floor appeared to be made of red gems when it reflected the women’s soft red feet to its very depths.**

*yatra citra-likhitair maṇi-bhittau*

*jīvavadbhir iva geha-sado’nye*  
*te ta eva sahasaṁ pralapanti \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the jeweled walls were painting of parrots, cuckoos, peacocks and doves, so life-like that the residents would suddenly talk to them.**

*bāla-cūta-taru-mañjari-kālir*  
*gṛhyatām iti muhuḥ pralapanti \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In that place, the gopīs became enchanted with the beautiful beaks in a painting of female cuckoos. They repeated said, “Please accept these young mango buds.”**

*unmiṣad-vividha-ratna-mayūkhair*

**There, the rays from the various jewels mixed together and effortlessly produced attractive articles useful in pastimes.**

*ullasan-marakatāśma-maṇīnāṁ*

**The woman, with fawns in their laps, happily sent the fawns to rows of emeralds with gestures of their arms, thinking that they could eat grass there.**

*āṅga-rāgam anurāgata īyuḥ \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Happy to decorate herself with clusters of red gems, one gopī saw her red reflection in the gems. She thought, “I will never decorate myself with kuṁkuma again.”**

*yatra kalpa-taravo vividhānāṁ*  
*jyotiṣāṁ vyatikaraiḥ sumaṇīnām \|*  
*uccakair jvalad-amanda-śikhāgrair*  
*maṇḍitā iva babhur vara-dīpaiḥ \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The desire trees which were decorated with the bright light of various excellent gems, like the best of lamps, shone with brilliance.**

### Text 89

*pakva-dāḍima-dhiyā śuka-śāvās*  
*teṣu śoṇa-maṇiṣūpacarantaḥ \|*

*cañcum ādadhati yatra kadāpi \|\|89\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Parrots and śarika birds approached the red gems, thinking them to be ripe pomegranates, but not tasting fruit, they gave up going there and pecking with their beaks.**

*puṣpam icchati na hīraka-buddhyā*  
*hīrakaṁ śrayati puṣpa-dhiyaiṣaḥ \|*  
*yatra daiva-vaśato madhupatvaṁ*  
*gacchati sma madhupaḥ khalu maugdhyāt \|\|90\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The bees did not desire the flowers, thinking they were diamonds, and they went to the diamonds, thinking they were flowers. By fate, the bees went to drink honey in bewilderment.**

*ekataḥ sphaṭika-pāṭala-gaurair*  
*anyato marakata-dyutibhinnaiḥ \|*

*yatra cāru-salilaiḥ kila vāpī \|\|91\|\|*

**There was a channel of clear water, radiating white light of crystals in one place and green light of emeralds in another place, like lines of moonlight and darkness.**

*sphāṭikaṁ taṭam abhi pratimagnaḥ*  
*protthito dyupati-ratna-taṭānte \|*

*yatra cāru sarasīm abhi kokaḥ \|\|92\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A cakravāka bird, thinking a crystal slab was water, tried to enter into it. Going to a sunstone stab, thinking it was a lake, the bird began to call for his mate and lament.**

*candrikā iva sudhāmaya-sindhau \|\|93\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**To decorate themselves, the women with eyes pure as blue lotuses slowly entered this huge, delightful, colorful pavilion, like moonlight in the ocean of nectar.**

*(pañcadaśabhiḥ kulakam)*

*anyonya-svaparivardhato’tyapūrvaṁ*  
*sambheje śravaṇa-rasāyaṇatvam uccaiḥ \|\|94\|\|*

**The beautiful cuckoos, peacocks, parrots, swans, pigeons, bees and doves created pleasure for the ears by their competitive, unprecedented singing.**
