**“O Lord! Please explain to me this one verse. I am afraid to speak about it. I have explained it, but I no longer have faith in that explanation.”**

### Text 80

*ity ūcivān padya-yugaṁ pramodād*  
*ekādaśa-skandha-bhavaṁ papāṭha \|*  
*niśamya taṁ kāruṇikāgra-gaṇyo*  
*vyākhyāṁ cakārāti-sudurgamārtham \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, he then recited two lines in bliss from the Eleventh Canto of Bhāgavatam. ??Hearing the verse, the most merciful Lord explained the verse having a most difficult meaning.**

### Text 81

*pṛthak pṛthaktvān navadhā cakāra*  
*vyākhyāṁ sapadya-dvitayasya śaśvat \|*  
*aṣṭādaśārthān ubhayor niśamya*  
*mahā-vimugdho’bhavad eṣa vipraḥ \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing nine meanings of the first line and nine meanings of the second line, totally eighteen meanings, the brāhmaṇa was overjoyed.**

### Text 82

*bhūtvā vimugdho’tiśayaṁ mahātmā*  
*tuṣṭāva kurvann adhikaṁ sva-nindām \|*  
*aho vimūḍho nṛpaśur na mādṛk*  
*tavānubhāvaṁ praviveda deva \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Overjoyed, the great soul praised him and intensely criticized himself. “I am a fool, a human animal. I could not understand your power, O Lord!”**

### Text 83

*iti prakāmāṁ stavanaṁ vidhāya*  
*kaṁcit prabhoḥ pāriṣadaṁ gṛhītvā \|*  
*yayau sva-gehaṁ tad-anantare ca*  
*vilikhya patrīm anavadya-padyām \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Profusely praising the Lord, taking a follower of the Lord with him, he went home and there wrote a flawless verse as a letter.**

### Text 84

*bhikṣārtham asyaiva mahā-kṛpālor*  
*mahā-prasādānnam ananya-dṛṣṭam \|*  
*dattvā tam enaṁ prabhave tu patrī*  
*deyeti prasthāpya nananda vipraḥ \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He gave mahā-prasādam which had not been seen by others to the follower, for the Lord’s meal. With joy he sent the follower back, telling him to deliver the letter.**

### Text 85

*mukunda-datto’tha vilokya patrīṁ*  
*nipaṭhya ca śloka-yugaṁ tadīyam \|*  
*bhittau vilikhyāpi ca nātha-haste*  
*dadau sa cālokya papāṭha mandam \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mukunda Datta, seeing the letter, read it and wrote the two verses on the wall. He gave the letter to the Lord. The Lord, seeing the letter, carefully read it.**

### Text 86

*vairāgya-vidyā-nija-bhakti-yoga-*  
*śikṣārtham ekaḥ puruṣaḥ purāṇaḥ \|*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya-śarīra-dhārī*  
*kṛpāmbudhir yas tam ahaṁ prapadye \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I surrender to the one eternal person, appearing in the body of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, the ocean of mercy, to teach renunciation, knowledge and devotion to the Lord.**

### Text 87

*kālān naṣṭaṁ bhakti-yogaṁ nijaṁ yaḥ*  
*prāduṣkartuṁ kṛṣṇa-caitanya-nāmā \|*  
*āvirbhūtas tasya pādāravinde*  
*gāḍhaṁ līyatāṁ citta-bhṛṅgaḥ \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa Caitanya has appeared in the world to reveal bhakti-yoga, which had been destroyed for a long time. May the bee of my heart deeply enter the lotus of his feet!”**

### Text 88

*iti prapaṭhyaiva vihasya dorbhyāṁ*  
*vidārayāmāsa kṛpāmbudhis tām \|*  
*bhittau vilokyātha samasta-lokaś*  
*cakāra kaṇṭhe maṇivat tad eva \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Reading the verses, the ocean of mercy laughed and tore up the letter with his two hands. All the people, seeing the verses written on the wall, wore them on their throats like a jewel.**

### Text 89

*śrī-gauracandrasya kṛpā tu saiṣā*  
*vācā kathaṁ tat-pratisaṅga-leśāt \|*  
*anyaiva rītiḥ khalu cetasaḥ syād*  
*anyac ca janmānyad ivāpy adṛṣṭam \|\|89\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The mercy of Gauracandra received from even a moment’s association cannot be described by words. One’s behavior changes. One’s birth changes. One’s karma changes.**

### Text 90

*yato’yam adhyātma-pathaika-pānthaḥ*  
*sa vipramukhyaḥ prabhupāda-saṅgāt \|*  
*mokṣasya nāmāpi na karṇa-vartma*  
*nayaty asau gaura-vibhoḥ kṛpaiṣā \|\|90\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The best of brāhmaṇas was a follower of impersonalism (ādhyātma), but by the association of the feet of the Lord, he would not even hear the word “liberation.” Such is the mercy of Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 91

*kadācid eṣa prabhu-pūrvatas tu*  
*prastāvato bhāgavatīya-padyam \|*  
*nipaṭhya tan-mukti-pade sa dāya-bhāg*  
*ity atra bhaktīti paṭhan nananda \|\|91\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One time, when the opportunity arose, in front of the Lord, Sārvabhauma recited a verse from Bhāgavatam and instead of mukti-pade said bhakti-pade in bliss.**

### Text 92

*prabhus tad ākarṇya ca mukti-śabda-*  
*syānyārtham ādhāya tadaiva devaḥ \|*  
*samarthayāmāsa tathāpy uvāca*  
*so’yaṁ tadīya-prabhutābhiṣiktaḥ \|\|92\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, the Lord gave another meaning to the word “mukti.” Sārvabhauma said, “This meaning has been anointed with your power.”**

### Text 93

*tathāpy asabhya-smṛti-hetukatvād*  
*aślīla-doṣo’yam iti bravīmi \|*  
*ity ādi yasyokti madhu prasiddhaṁ*  
*sa sārvabhaumaḥ kathayā na kathyaḥ \|\|93\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I say that the phrase has the fault of inauspiciousness, since it gives rise to inferior meanings.” The statement whose words are perfect and eloquent, conquers the whole world by its fame, and cannot be described by words.**

### Text 94

*aṣṭādaśāhāni sa tatra nītvā*  
*vilokya taṁ devam atīva-harṣāt \|*  
*pracakrame caṅkramaṇāya nātho*  
*vimohayan kāṁścana viprayogaiḥ \|\|94\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Spending eighteen days there, Mahāprabhu saw Jagannātha in great joy and, bewildering the devotees with separation, prepared to leave.**

### Text 95

*dṛṣṭvā jagannātha-mahāprabhuṁ taṁ*  
*mahāprabhur gaura-sudhā-mayūkhaḥ \|*  
*ādāya tasyaiva nideśam ādau*  
*yayau pramodād diśi dakṣiṇasyām \|\|95\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After seeing the great Lord Jagannātha, Mahāprabhu, effulgent as gold nectar, took his permission and departed for the south in joy.**

### Text 96

*gacchantam itthaṁ sa tu sārvabhaumaḥ*  
*śokākulātmā karuṇaṁ babhāṣe \|*  
*kathaṁ prabho mām bahu-duḥkha-dagdhaṁ*  
*kṛtvā kuto vā prasabhaṁ prayāsi \|\|96\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sārvabhauma, seeing the Lord depart, in lamentation spoke pitifully. “O Lord! Why are you leaving so quickly, making me burn in great sorrow?**

### Text 97

*kathaṁ mamābhūn na hi putra-śokaḥ*  
*kathaṁ mamābhūn na hi deha-pātaḥ \|*  
*vilokya yuṣmat pada-padma-yugaṁ soḍhuṁ*  
*na śakto’smi bhavad-viyogam \|\|97\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord! Why do I not lament for a son? Why do I not give up my body? Having seen your lotus feet, I cannot tolerate separation from you.**

### Text 98

*bata kva gantāsi pathā nu kena*  
*kathaṁ pathaḥ kleśa-saho’tha bhāvī \|*  
*yady eva gantāsi tadā kṛpālo*  
*godāvarī-tīra-bhuvaṁ samīyāḥ \|\|98\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Where are you going? By which path are you going? How can you endure the difficulties on the road? O merciful Lord! If you go, then travel by the bank of the Godāvarī River.**

### Text 99

*tatrāsti kaścit paramo mahātmā*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇa-pādāmbuja-matta-bhṛṅgaḥ \|*  
*nopājihīthā viṣayīti rāmā-*  
*nandaṁ bhavānanda-tanūja-ratnam \|\|99\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“At that place lives one great soul, a mad bee at the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, named Rāmānanda, the son of Bhāvānanda. Do not reject him as a materialist.”**

### Text 100

*tatheti kṛtvā bhagavān kṛpāluḥ*  
*kaurme jagāma prathamaṁ pramodāt \|*  
*namaś cakārātha nijāṁ sa bhaktiṁ*  
*prakāśayaṁs tat karuṇaiva saiṣā \|\|100\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Agreeing to this proposal, the merciful Lord first went to Kūrma-kṣetra in bliss and offered respects to the deity. Showing bhakti to himself was his mercy.**

### Text 101

*dṛṣṭvā ciraṁ taṁ sa nijāvatāraṁ*  
*punar namaskṛtya kṛtī kṛtajñaḥ \|*  
*tat karma mādhyandinam asya mānaṁ*  
*cakāra śikṣā-gurutām upetaḥ \|\|101\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After gazing at the deity, his own avatāra, for a long time, the expert, grateful Lord offered respects again, and acting as a śikṣā-guru, performed his midday rituals with respect.**

### Text 102

*kṣetre ca tatrāti sudhīr mahātmā*  
*kūrmāhvayo bhūsura-vaṁśa-janmā \|*  
*vilokya taṁ bhūyaśa eva natvā*  
*sa bhīta-bhīto madhuraṁ jagāda \|\|102\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At Kūrma-kṣetra, a great soul, who was very intelligent, named Kūrma, born in a brāhmaṇa family, seeing the Lord, offered repeated respects and in fear spoke sweet words.**

### Text 103

*adyaivam etat saphalā janiḥ syād*  
*adyaiva me tat saphalaṁ samastam \|*  
*yad asya pādāmburuha-dvayasya*  
*rajaḥ-prapāto bhavitālaye’smin \|\|103\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Today my birth has become successful. Today everything has become successful, since my house has obtained the dust of your two lotus feet.”**

### Text 104

*sa kūrma-nāmā dvija-puṅgavāgryo*  
*bahu prakārārjita-puṇya-puñjaḥ \|*  
*vidhṛtya pādau sva-gṛhaṁ nināya*  
*prakṣālayāmāsa ca tau payobhiḥ \|\|104\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The best of brāhmaṇas, Kūrma, who had accrued abundant pious results, holding the Lord’s feet, brought him into his house and washed his feet with water.**

### Text 105

*tathaiva kṛtvā paramaḥ kṛpālur*  
*nananda tasyaiva śubhālaye’sau \|*  
*bhikṣāṁ ca tatraiva tadopanītāṁ*  
*cakāra nāthaś ca tataḥ pratasthe \|\|105\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The merciful Lord was satisfied with his actions and ate at his auspicious house. He then departed.**

### Text 106

*śrutvety ayaṁ śrī-puruṣottamāt sa*  
*mahāprabhor dakṣiṇato jagāma \|*  
*śrī-vāsudevāhvaya eka vipro*  
*‘kasmāt kathañcit tata āgato’bhūt \|\|106\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One brāhmaṇa named Vāsudeva heard that Mahāprabhu was going south from Jagannātha Puri and approached to him suddenly.**

### Text 107

*śvitreṇa śaśvad galad-aṅga-yaṣṭir*  
*mahāśayo’sau sumahāturo’pi \|*  
*tat kūrma-nāmno dvija-puṅgavasya*  
*jagāma gehaṁ mahitānubhāvaḥ \|\|107\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In great pain because of white leprosy on his limbs, the great soul came to Kūrma’s house.**

### Text 108

*gatvā ca papraccha mahāprabhuṁ taṁ*  
*taṁ kūrma-nāmānam upetya dhīraḥ \|*  
*so’py etad ūce sumahāśayāya*  
*tasmai samastaṁ karuṇālayasya \|\|108\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Coming there, Vāsudeva asked Kūrma about Mahāprabhu. Kūrma told him everything concerning the abode of mercy.**

### Text 109

*ihaiva devaḥ samuvāsa bhikṣāṁ*  
*cakāra mādṛśy akarot kṛpāṁ ca \|*  
*yady āgamiṣyaḥ kṣaṇam atra śīghraṁ*  
*tad āvalokayiṣya ihaiva nātham \|\|109\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord had stayed there and taken food. He gave mercy to me. If you go quickly you can see the Lord.”**

### Text 110

*niśamya so’yaṁ sakalaṁ mahātmā*  
*gataḥ sa ity ākulam eva bhūmau \|*  
*papāta mūrcchām adhigamya tatra*  
*nivṛtya bhūyaḥ prabhur ājagāma \|\|110\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Vāsudeva went outside in confusion and, fainting, fell on the ground. Understanding this, the Lord returned.**

### Text 111

*āgatya dorbhyāṁ parirabhya vipraṁ*  
*kuṣṭhaiḥ samaṁ moham apācakāra \|*  
*sa-cetanāṁ cārutarāṁ tanuṁ ca*  
*prāpyānamat taṁ dhṛta-harṣa-śokaḥ \|\|111\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Coming there, the Lord embraced the brāhmaṇa in his arms and released him from illusion along with his leprosy. Receiving a conscious, attractive body, he offered respects to the Lord in joy and lamentation.**

### Text 112

*kvāhaṁ daridraḥ pāpīyān kva kṛṣṇaḥ śrī-niketanaḥ*  
*brahma-bandhur iti smāhaṁ bāhubhyāṁ parirambhitaḥ (112)*

**Перевод:**

**“Who am I? A sinful, poor friend of a brāhmaṇa. And who is Kṛṣṇa? The Supreme Lord, full in six powers. Nonetheless, he has embraced me with his two arms.” SB 10.81.16**

### Text 113

*ity ādi padyaṁ paripaṭhya coce*  
*nānugraho’yaṁ bata nigraho me \|*  
*dainyaṁ kṛthā mā nirahaṅkṛtaḥ san*  
*mām eṣyatīty antaradhāc ca devaḥ \|\|113\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Reciting this verse he said, “This is not mercy but punishment to me.” The Lord said, “Do not feel miserable. Devoid of pride, a person will attain me.” He then disappeared.**

### Text 114

*vilokya so’py atra tathā-vidhaṁ taṁ*  
*mumoha kūrmaḥ sthita-marma-duḥkhaḥ \|*  
*utthāya bhūyaḥ karuṇaṁ cakāra*  
*vilāpa-mālām api vaiṣṇavāgryaḥ \|\|114\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing this, Kūrma, feeling pain in his body, fainted. Then standing up, the best of brāhmaṇas lamented.**

### Text 115

*atraiva bhāgyodaya īdṛśo’bhūn*  
*mahāprabhuḥ sarva-jagat-prabhuḥ saḥ \|*  
*sthitaḥ samāgatya tatheśa-buddhyā*  
*na jñāta eṣa kṣaṇa-mātram eva \|\|115\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He had attained great fortune. “Mahāprabhu, the lord of all the universe, having stayed there, returned. But I did not realize he was the Lord for even a moment.**

### Text 116

*aho mahā-mūḍha-matir manuṣyaḥ*  
*kṣudro nṛśaṁsaḥ paramāgha-kārī \|*  
*amūlya-ratne sva-karopalabdhe*  
*na rakṣitaṁ tad bata helayaiva \|\|116\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Man is a great fool, low, cruel and sinful for having held a priceless jewel in his hand. He does not protect it, being careless.**

### Text 117

*svabhāva-mūḍhas tṛṇa-mātra-bhoktā*  
*paśuḥ sudhāsvāda-rasaṁ na vetti \|*  
*spṛṣṭe’pi ca sparśa-maṇau na vetti*  
*maṇir mahān ity asakṛd vimugdhaḥ \|\|117\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“An animal, by nature foolish, chews grass and cannot relish the taste of nectar. Though touching a touchstone, a man does not know that it is a great gem. He is foolish at all times.”**

### Text 118

*aho mahā-kāruṇikasya tasya*  
*jagat-pater eṣa viyoga-duḥkham \|*  
*asahyam etan na śaśāka soḍhum*  
*atipramugdho bahudhā mumoha \|\|118\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kūrma could not endure the pain of separation from the most merciful Lord of the universe. He fainted, becoming unconscious many times.**

### Text 119

*athaiṣa tasmāt paramaḥ kṛpālur*  
*vrajan nṛsiṁhaṣ sa tu nārasiṁhe \|*  
*kṣetre samāgatya nṛsiṁha-devaṁ*  
*namaścakāra stuvam apy akārṣīt \|\|119\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The most merciful Lord, Nṛsiṁha, then arrived at Nṛsiṁha-kṣetra, offered respects to Nṛsiṁha, and praised him.**

### Text 120

*sadā madonmādakarīndra-gāmī*  
*mahā-vilāsī vara-pīna-bāhuḥ \|*  
*nakhendu-pīyūṣa-nadī-pravāha-*  
*dhārābhir āplāvya rasāṁ jagāma \|\|120\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Moving like an intoxicated elephant, with most graceful gestures, with attractive, full arms, flooding the earth with streams of nectar flowing from his toenails, the Lord travelled.**

### Text 121

*rāma rāghava rāma rāghava rāma rāghava pāhi mām \|*  
*kṛṣṇa keśava kṛṣṇa keśava kṛṣṇa keśava trāhi mām \|\|121\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O Rāma! O Rāghava. Please protect me! O Kṛṣṇa! O Keśava! Please deliver me!”**

### Text 122

*saṅkīrtayann ittham amandam uccaiḥ*  
*pathi prakāmaṁ pulakācitāṅgaḥ \|*  
*ārta-svaraṁ kutra ca vīkṣya bhīmaṁ*  
*vanaṁ pareśaḥ pariroditi sma \|\|122\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he chanted this loudly and intensely on the road, his hairs stood on end. Seeing a wild forest in one place, the Lord cried in a painful voice.**

### Text 123

*godāvarī-tuṅga-taraṅga-śīte*  
*marudbhir āśliṣṭa-latā-samūhaiḥ \|*  
*itas tato bhūri sametam antar-*  
*vanaṁ vilokyaiṣa nananda nāthaḥ \|\|123\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the middle of the forest endowed with creepers embraced by the wind from the cool huge, waves of the Godāvarī River, the Lord felt bliss.**

### Text 124

*kadamba-vīthīṣu nadan-mṛdaṅgaiḥ*  
*samullasat-tāṇḍava-sat-kalāpaiḥ \|*  
*viśrabdham unnetra-yugaiḥ kṛpālur*  
*nananda bhūyo hariṇaiḥ sakāntaiḥ \|\|124\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The merciful Lord became blissful on seeing the deer with their mates, eyes raised in faith, and joyful peacocks displaying their tails and dancing on hearing the sounds of mṛdaṅgas (clouds rumbling) in the kadamba groves.**

### Text 125

*niṣkūja-śāntāḥ kva ca caṇḍa-śabda-*  
*pratidhvani-grasta-diśaḥ kva cāpi \|*  
*kva ca prasuptoru-karāla-sattva-*  
*śvāsāgni-dīptā vana-bhūmi-bhāgāḥ \|\|125\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some places in the forest were devoid of birds’ chirping and some places echoed with their loud cries. Some places of the forest was agitated by the fiery breathing of ferocious animals who were sleeping.**

### Text 126

*godāvarī-vega-mahā-ninādā*  
*bhīmā giri-prasravaṇā raveṇa \|*  
*śrī-gauracandrasya vitenur uccaiḥ*  
*sukomalaṁ cittam anāpta-dhairyam \|\|126\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra’s soft heart became unsteady by the fierce rumbling of the Godāvarī’s torrents along with the roaring of waterfalls.**

### Text 127

*kṣaṇāt skhalat-pāda-vikampra-pakṣaiś*  
*cañcūpatad-bīja-cayaiḥ prapūrṇaiḥ \|*  
*śukair dalad-dāḍima-cumbavadbhir*  
*godāvarī-tīra-vane sa reme \|\|127\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He enjoyed that forest on the bank of the Godāvarī, where trembling birds stumbled about in bliss. The forest was filled with seeds which had fallen from the birds’ beaks and with parrots pecking at split pomegranate fruits.**

### Text 128

*tāmbūla-vallī-dala-vrndam uccair*  
*bhindadbhir ugraiḥ krakacair asadbhiḥ \|*  
*ajasra-dīrgheṇa vimugdha-jhillī-*  
*jhaṅkāra-rāveṇa nikāma-ramye \|\|128\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The parrots pecked at the leaves of the tāmbūla creepers with loud, sharp sounds. The forest was filled with the pleasant, drawn-out chirping of infatuated crickets.**

### Text 129

*jyotir-gaṇācumbibhir ambudābhais*  
*tamāla-mālārjuna-kovidāraiḥ \|*  
*nānā-vidhaiḥ patra-rathair asadbhiś*  
*camūru-vṛndaiś camaraiś ca juṣṭaiḥ \|\|129\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It was filled with tamāla, arjuna and kovidāra trees, like clouds touching the stars. It was filled with various wild birds and with flocks of deer and camaras.**

### Text 130

*arka-prabhā-parka-vihīna-sāndra-*  
*snigdhāti-sac-chītala-cāru-bhūmau \|*  
*akṛtrimālepa-nipīta-mūle*  
*vāpī-taḍāgādi-nirantarāle \|\|130\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There were cool places with dense forest, devoid of contact with sunlight, covered with natural unguents and there were places with canals and ponds everywhere.**

### Text 131

*tataḥ sa godāvarikām upetya*  
*manasy athāndolitatāṁ jagāma \|*  
*sambhāṣitavyaḥ kim asau na veti*  
*śrīmad-bhavānanda-suto mahātmā \|\|131\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving at this place on the bank of the Godāvarī, the Lord felt bliss in his mind. He thought, “Will I be able to converse with the saintly son of Bhāvānanda?”**

### Text 132

*tathāpy abhivyajya vibhur virāgaṁ*  
*na taṁ vilokyaiva yayāv avācīm \|*  
*nānā-vanālokana-komalātmā*  
*kvacit praviśyātiśayaṁ ruroda \|\|132\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Showing some discontent on not seeing him, the Lord continued south. Becoming soft-hearted on seeing various forests, he entered one forest and cried intensely.**

### Text 133

*kvacit kvacid gāyati mukta-kaṇṭhaḥ*  
*kvacit kvacin nṛtyati ca svayaṁ saḥ \|*  
*kvacit kvacid roditi hṛṣṭa-romā*  
*rātrindivaṁ naiva viveda gacchan \|\|133\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sometimes singing loudly, sometimes dancing and sometimes crying, with hairs standing on end, he travelled about, unaware of day or night.**

### Text 134

*kanaka-kari-varo’yaṁ kiṁ cironmukta-bandhaḥ*  
*kim u jhaṭiti cariṣṇur merur eṣaḥ prabhāti \|*  
*atha kim u cira-rociḥ puñja eṣa prakāmaṁ*  
*sphurati cira-vilāsaḥ ko nu vāyaṁ prapañcaḥ \|\|134\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Was he a golden elephant set loose for a long time? Was he a swiftly moving, shining Mount Meru? Was he a permanently shining mass of light? Was this some permanent manifestation of beauty?**

### Text 135

*iti sakala-nṛ-loko dākṣiṇātyaḥ sa-toṣaṁ*  
*vinimiṣam anuvelaṁ locanābhyāṁ piban saḥ \|*  
*jaḍima-jaḍita-cetā dūram apy atra deve*  
*gatavati yati-candre sthāṇuvat tatra tasthau \|\|135\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking in this way, all the people of the south constantly drank his form with their unblinking eyes to full satisfaction. When he was going far away, they remained standing like pillars, as if giving up consciousness.**

## Chapter Thirteen

### Text 1

*evaṁ sa tīrthāṭana-kautukena*  
*dīnaika-bandhuḥ karuṇaika-sindhuḥ \|*  
*tato yayau bhāgyavatīm avācīṁ*  
*sva-nāma-ratna-grahaṇotsavotkaḥ \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Desiring to go on pilgrimage, anxious the chant the name, the friend of the suffering, the ocean of mercy, went to the fortunate southern area.**

### Text 2

*vilokya taṁ vartmani kṛṣṇasārās*  
*tṛṣṇāvatākṣṇor yugalena bhūyaḥ \|*  
*rūpāmṛtaṁ pātum ivāpta-dhairyāḥ*  
*samaṁ samantāt savidhaṁ samīyuḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing him on the road, the Kṛṣṇa-sāra deer, as if intelligent, gathered from all around and came near to drink the sweetness of his form with their thirsty eyes.**

### Text 3

*śrī-raṅga-kṣetram asau dayāluḥ*  
*kāveri-kāveṣṭitam ucca-deśam \|*  
*āsādya tatratyam avekṣya devaṁ*  
*priyākaro’sau mudam āpi tuṅgām \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The merciful Lord arrived at Śrī-raṅgam, an elevated area surrounded by the Kāverī River. Seeing the deity there, he, endowed with a pleasing form, became very happy.**

### Text 4

*trimalla-bhaṭṭasya mahāśayasya*  
*gṛhe kṛtāvāsa-vidhiḥ kṛpāluḥ \|*  
*kutūhalenaiva nināya cātur-*  
*māsyaṁ sa āvaśyaka-karma kurvan \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The merciful Lord stayed at the house of Trimalla Bhaṭṭa and passed four months there performing necessary rites with great happiness.**

### Text 5

*kāverikāyāṁ vihitāplavo’yaṁ*  
*cakāra tasyā bahu-pāvanatvam \|*  
*śrī-raṅga-saṅgaṁ pravilokya devaṁ*  
*nināya māsāṁś caturaḥ kṛpāluḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bathing in the Kāverī, becoming purified, seeing the deity, the merciful, charming Lord passed the months.**

### Text 6

*varṣāśaran-madhya-gataḥ sa kālaḥ*  
*pariṣvajantīṁ śaradaṁ cucumbe \|*  
*kāntā-dvayāntaḥ-śayito vilāsī*  
*pārśvāvivṛttāv iva vīta-nidraḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The time between the monsoon and autumn seasons, sleeping between two women (the two seasons) kissed the autumn while embracing it, as if giving up sleep and turning over.**

### Text 7

*tato navotphulla-saroruhāsyā*  
*navotpalākṣī gata-paṅka-jālā \|*  
*sujīvanā tat-karuṇāpti-kāmā*  
*dāsīva bheje śarad-īśvaraṁ tat \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The autumn season whose face was blossoming lotuses, whose fresh water lilies were the eyes, whose water became pure, devoid of mud, desiring to obtain his mercy, worshipped the Lord like a female servant.**

### Text 8

*athāta ānanda-samūha-magno*  
*yayau prahṛṣṭo diśi dakṣiṇasyām \|*  
*mahāprabhuḥ svīya-guṇānugāthā-*  
*nirantarotkīrtana-mugdha-vaktraḥ \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then, absorbed in bliss, Mahāprabhu, his face joyful with continuous, loud kīrtana describing the Lord’s qualities, went in the southern direction.**

### Text 9

*tatra kvacit śrī-raghunātha-bhaktaṁ*  
*praśānta-cittaṁ dvija-puṅgavaṁ saḥ \|*  
*sītā daśāsyāpahṛteti śokād*  
*bahir vrajat-prāṇam ivāluloke \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There he saw one excellent brāhmaṇa, a devotee of Rāma, who was about to give up his life because Rāvaṇa had stolen Sītā.**

### Text 10

*lakṣmīr iyaṁ rākṣasa-hasta-yātā*  
*kim etad ity asya mano viditvā \|*  
*āśvāsayann eva tam abravīd bho*  
*maivaṁ svarūpaṁ śṛṇu yad bravīmi \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He thought, “How can Lakṣmī be touched by the hand of a demon?” The Lord pacified him and said, “That is not so. Hear the truth that I will speak.**

### Text 11

*yad vā madīye vacasi pratītir*  
*na te bhavitrī tad idaṁ nu paśya \|*  
*purāṇa-padya-dvayam ity akasmād*  
*adarśayat svāñcalato vikṛṣya \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If you do not believe my words, see the two verses from the Purāṇa. He suddenly pulled the two verses from his cloth and showed them to the brāhmaṇa.**

### Text 12-13

*sītayārādhito vahniś chāyā-sītām ajījanat \|*  
*tāṁ jahāra daśagrīvaḥ sītā vahni-puraṁ gatā \|\|12\|\|*  
*parīkṣā-samaye vahniṁ chāyā-sītā viveśa sā \|*  
*vahniḥ sītāṁ samānīya sva-purād udanīnayat \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“When he was petitioned by mother Sītā, the fire-god, Agni, brought forth an illusory form of Sītā, and Rāvaṇa, who had ten heads, kidnapped the false Sītā. The original Sītā then went to the abode of the fire-god. When Lord Rāmacandra tested the body of Sītā, it was the false, illusory Sītā that entered the fire. At that time the fire-god brought the original Sītā from his abode and delivered her to Lord Rāmacandra.”**

### Text 14

*athātra kañcid yatināṁ variṣṭhaṁ*  
*dadarśa nātho bahu-hṛṣṭa-cittam \|*  
*mahānubhāvaṁ paramaṁ purastād*  
*ānanda-madhyaṁ ca purīṁ tad-antam \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord then saw there the excellent, noble sannyāsa, Paramānanda Purī, endowed with a joyful heart.**

### Text 15

*vilokya sambhāṣya sujāta-harṣau*  
*babhūvatus tau parama-prabhāvau \|*  
*anyonya-samprīti-vaśau kṛpālū*  
*tasmāt prayātuṁ dadhatuś ca cetaḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The two most powerful persons, with mutual affection and mercy, in joy saw each other, conversed, and then decided to depart.**

### Text 16

*eko gato gaura-śaśītva-vācīm*  
*anyaḥ samāgāt puruṣottamaṁ ca \|*  
*setuṁ samuddiśya calann athāsau*  
*rarāja rājīva-dalāyatākṣaḥ \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra went south and Paramānanda went to Puri. Going to Setu-bandha, the Lord with lotus eyes shone.**

### Text 17

*gacchan pathi prema-vibhinna-cetā*  
*hasaty alaṁ roditi nirbharārtaḥ \|*  
*vibhinna-dhairyaś calitas tato’sau*  
*dadarśa saptocchrita-tāla-vṛkṣān \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Going on the road, his heart bursting with prema, he laughed and in great pain cried. Without control, he moved about and saw seven tāla trees.**

### Text 18

*vilokya tāṁs tāla-tarūn kṛpāluḥ*  
*pratyekam evāśliṣad ātta-harṣaḥ \|*  
*atrāntare te divam īyivāṁ saḥ*  
*śūnyā sthalī sā sahasaiva yātā \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the trees, the merciful Lord in joy embraced each one. Immediately the trees rose into the sky and the place became suddenly empty.**

### Text 19

*ka eṣa gaurāṅga-mahāprabhos tat*  
*vicitra-nānānubhavasya loke \|*  
*atarkanīyo mahimā kṛpāloś*  
*citraṁ kṛpāyāḥ kim aśakyam āste \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What is the inconceivable greatness of Gaurāṅga, Mahāprabhu, performing many astonishing acts in this world? He who is merciful has astonishing mercy. What is impossible for him?**

### Text 20

*atha vrajan dakṣiṇa-dig-vibhāge*  
*vilokayan kautuka-ceṣṭitāni \|*  
*akhaṇḍa-pāṣaṇḍa-patha-praviṣṭān*  
*dadarśa nānā-vidha-liṅga-saṅghān \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Going south, he saw some curious activities. He saw a group of ascetics absorbed in unauthorized methods.**

### Text 21

*nikāma-vāme pathi vartamānāḥ*  
*pāṣaṇḍinas te parilocya nātham \|*  
*nānā-vidhena sva-matena śaśvad*  
*vilobhayāñcakrur adabhra-pāpāḥ \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These greatly sinful heretics on the wrong path saw the Lord and tried for a long time to lure him with their opinions.**

### Text 22

*yadīya-māyaika-vijṛmbhitena*  
*svaṁ cātipāṣaṇḍa-patha-pravṛttam \|*  
*paśyanti naite tam imaṁ kathaṁ vā*  
*kurvantu nānā-kuhakair vimugdham \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because of the Lord’s illusory power, they had deviated from the correct path. They could not see the Lord at all. They were bewildered by various illusions.**

### Text 23

*athāsya saṅge jagadīśvarasya*  
*vrajantam ekaṁ parilola-cittam \|*  
*taṁ kṛṣṇa-dāsākhyam amī vilokya*  
*vilobhayāñcakrur atīva-mandāḥ \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They then saw one fickle hearted servant named Kṛṣṇa-dāsa who had come with the Lord. These wicked persons bewildered him.**

### Text 24

*are kuto gacchasi duḥkha-mātraṁ*  
*sādhyaṁ tad asmāsu kuruṣva maitrīm \|*  
*tatas tu tenaiva śarīrakeṇa*  
*svargaṁ gamiṣyatha no vicāraḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Where are you going? You will obtain only suffering. Make friends with us. Go to Svarga in this body. This is certain.**

### Text 25

*astv eka evātra sa ko’pi panthāḥ*  
*kiyad vidūre’khila-loka-durgaḥ \|*  
*tad ehi tenaiva pathā bhavantaṁ*  
*samprāpayiṣyāma itaḥ khalu svaḥ \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“There is one path, long and difficult to traverse for all people. Come! By this path we will take you from here to Svarga.”**

### Text 26

*ity eṣa pāṣaṇḍa-patha-praviṣṭais*  
*tair mohito dolita-citta-vṛttiḥ \|*  
*śaithilyam īśasya pathi prayāte*  
*cakāra kiñcit kramato vimugdhaḥ \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bewildered by these sinful deviants, weak in his heart and fooled, he lost enthusiasm to go with the Lord.**

### Text 27

*prabhus tad ājñāya durātma-bhājāṁ*  
*viceṣṭitaṁ tasya ca lolatāṁ ca \|*  
*kṛpaika-sindhur jagad eka-bandhur*  
*durāśayais tair akarod vivādam \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding the wicked intentions of the sinful persons and the weak nature of Kṛṣṇa-dāsa, the ocean of mercy, the friend of the universe, argued with them.**

### Text 28

*bho nyāsinaḥ kiṁ mama dāsa eṣa*  
*pralobhya bālaḥ khalu nīyate kva \|*  
*naitac chivaṁ vo na ca sādhu-ceṣṭā*  
*tat tyajyatām eṣa vidūram ādhvam \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O sannyāsīs! What is this? You have lured my young servant and are taking him somewhere. This is not proper. This is not a correct action for you. Give him up and go away.”**

### Text 29

*itthaṁ vivādī na ciraṁ kṛtena*  
*kathaṁ kathañcid vimukhīcakāra \|*  
*nija-prabhāvena kṛpāmayābdhis*  
*taṁ suprasanne hi vidhau tathā syāt \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Arguing in this way, by his power, the ocean of mercy made them give up their attempts. When the Lord is pleased, this can happen.**

### Text 30

*itthaṁ vilokyāpatha-vartinas tat*  
*kuceṣṭitaṁ kiñcid asau vihasya \|*  
*na kiñcid ūce khalu kṛṣṇa-dāsaṁ*  
*setuṁ samudiśya tato jagāma \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the wicked acts of the deviants, the Lord laughed and said nothing to Kṛṣṇa-dāsa. He then headed towards Setu-bandha.**

### Text 31

*pathi prabhuḥ svair guṇa-nāmadheyair*  
*nirantaraṁ kīrtanam eva kṛtvā \|*  
*premāśrubhir dhauta-samasta-dehaś*  
*cakāra pūtām aṭavīṁ samastām \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the road, the Lord performed continuous kīrtana, glorifying the Lord’s qualities and names. Washing his body with tears of prema, he purified the whole forest.**

### Text 32

*evaṁ sa setuṁ prayayau kṛpāluḥ*  
*kṛpā-pariplāvita-sarva-deśaḥ \|*  
*rāmeśvaraṁ rāma-samarhitaṁ taṁ*  
*dṛṣṭvā nanāma stavam apy akārṣīt \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The merciful Lord, who flooded all places with his mercy, arrived at Setu-bandha and, seeing Rāmeśvara, who had been worshipped by Rāma, he offered respects and recited praises.**

### Text 33

*vilokya setuṁ raghunātha-kīrtiṁ*  
*setos tataḥ śrī-maya-gauracandraḥ \|*  
*nivartituṁ tatra kṛpā-samudraś*  
*cakāra cittaṁ parama-prabhāvaḥ \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the bridge, which gave fame to Rāma, beautiful, powerful Gauracandra, the ocean of mercy, decided to return from there.**

### Text 34

*sa tena tenaiva pathā vilokya*  
*śrī-raṅgadevaṁ punar ārdra-cittaḥ \|*  
*godāvarīm etya tathaiva rāmā-*  
*nandasya sandarśanam eṣa cakre \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Going by the path, with melted heart he saw Raṅga again. He came to the Godāvarī and there he saw Rāmānanda Rāya.**

### Text 35

*upetya godāvarikāṁ sa nāthaḥ*  
*pramodatas tat-parilocanāya \|*  
*jagāma tad-veśmani śīta-raśmir*  
*ivodayādriṁ jaladāgamās te \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving at the Godāvarī, the Lord in joy went to Rāmānanda’s house to introduce himself. He was like the autumn moon rising on the eastern horizon.**

### Text 36

*vilokya nāthaṁ sa tu kṛṣṇa-citto*  
*nanāma harṣād bhuvi saṁnipatya \|*  
*anantare koṭi-guṇa-pravṛddhām*  
*āhlāda-lakṣmīm uditāṁ babhāra \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the Lord, Rāmānanda, who always remembered Kṛṣṇa, joyfully offered respects, falling on the ground. After that, he experienced the greatest bliss, which had increased by millions of times.**

### Text 37

*īśas tu tad-darśana-mātrato’sau*  
*druto bhavac-cetasi harṣa-bhāraiḥ \|*  
*athopariṣṭāj jagad-eka-kāntir*  
*babhrāja kandarpa-samūha-kamraḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Just on seeing him, the Lord’s heart melted in bliss. The Lord, most beautiful in the universe, shone with the beauty of many Cupids.**

### Text 38

*uvāca kiñcit stanayitnu-dhīraṁ*  
*sa-kaitavaṁ bhoḥ kavitāṁ paṭheti \|*  
*tadā tad ākarṇya mahārasajñaḥ*  
*papāṭha vairāgya-rasāḍhya-padyam \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said in a voice like a rumbling cloud, with some deception, “Read some poetry.” Hearing this, Rāmānanda, who knew about the greatest rasa, recited a verse filled with the rasa of renunciation.**

### Text 39

*vairāgyaṁ cej janayatitarāṁ pāpam evāstu yasmāt*  
*sāndraṁ rāgaṁ janayati na cet puṇyam asmāsu bhūyāt \|*  
*vairāgyeṇa pramudita-mano-vṛttir abhyeti rāgaṁ*  
*rāgeṇa strī-jaṭhara-kuhare tāmyati brāhmaṇo’pi \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If detachment manifests it is good, for sin is destroyed. If it does not manifest, then deep material attachment arises. We will attain great advantage by detachment. An enjoying mind produces attachment. By attachment even a brāhmaṇa enters a womb.**

### Text 40

*itīdam ākarṇya sa gauracandro*  
*bāhyātibāhyaṁ bata bāhyam etat \|*  
*iti sphurad-vāg-vibhavottha-tāpod-*  
*gamānta-kṛn nātimudaṁ prapede \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Gauracandra said, “That is external.” The austerity arising in the heart from these words did not produce bliss.**

### Text 41

*tataś ca saṁśuddha-matiḥ sa rāmā-*  
*nando mahānanda-pariplutāṅgaḥ \|*  
*papāṭha bhakteḥ pratipādayitrīm*  
*ekānta-kāntāṁ kavitāṁ svakīyām \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pure in mind, Rāmānanda, feeling bliss in all his limbs, recited his own attractive verse which explained bhakti.**

### Text 42

*nānopacāra-kṛta-pūjanam ārta-bandhoḥ*  
*premṇaiva bhakta-hṛdayaṁ sukha-vidrutaṁ syāt \|*  
*yāvat kṣud asti jaṭhare jaraṭhā pipāsā*  
*tāvat sukhāya bhavato nanu bhakṣya-peye \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“As long as there is hunger and thirst within the stomach, varieties of food and drink make one feel very happy. Similarly, when the Lord is worshiped with pure love, the various activities performed in the course of that worship awaken bliss in the heart of the devotee.”**

### Text 43

*itthaṁ ca saṁśrutya tathaiva bāhyaṁ*  
*bāhyaṁ tad etac ca paraṁ paṭheti \|*  
*jagāda nātho’tha kacaiḥ sudīrghaiḥ*  
*saṁveṣṭya nāthasya padau papāta \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, the Lord said “This is irrelevant. Reciting something else.” Rāmānanda fell at the feet of the Lord, surrounding his feet with his long hair.**

### Text 44

*nikāma-sammoha-bharālasāṅgo*  
*gāṅgeya-gauraṁ tam anaṅga-ramyam \|*  
*prabhuṁ praṇamyātha padābja-mūle*  
*nipatya samprotthita ānananda \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His limbs slack with great bewilderment, he offered respects to the golden Lord attractive as Cupid. Having fallen at his feet, he rose and felt great bliss.**

### Text 45

*tataḥ sa gītaṁ sarasāli-gītaṁ*  
*vidagdhayor nāgarayoḥ parasya \|*  
*premno’tikāṣṭhā-pratipādanena*  
*dvayoḥ paraikya-pratipādy avādīt \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He then recited an attractive song which described the supreme prema of the loving couple.**

### Text 46

*bhairavī rāgaḥ—*  
*pahilehi rāga nayana-bhaṅge bhela \| anudina bāḍhala, avadhi nā gela \|\|*  
*nā so ramaṇa, nā hāma ramaṇī \| duṅhu-mana manobhava peṣala jāni' \|\|*  
*e sakhī, se-saba prema-kāhinī \| kānu-ṭhāme kahabi vichurala jāni' \|\|*  
*nā khoṅjaluṅ dūtī, nā khoṅjaluṅ āna \| duṅhukeri milane madhya ta pāṅca-bāṇa \|\|*  
*ab sohi virāga, tuṅhu bheli dūtī \| su-purukha-premaki aichana rīti \|\|*  
*vardhana rudra narādhipamāna \| rāmānanda-rāya kavi bhāṇa \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend! First, by glances of the eyes, pūrva-rāga had arisen. Day by day it increased without limit. He is not my husband and I am not his wife, but Cupid has joined our minds. All this is the effect of prema. Do not forget to tell this fact to Kṛṣṇa. I did not search for a messenger. I did not search for any one. Cupid himself was the mediator for our meeting. That lover is now indifferent to me. Therefore you should be the messenger. This is the nature of that lover’s prema. Rāmānanda Raya has composed this poem in honor of King Vardhana Rudra.**

### Text 47

*tatas tad ākarṇya parātparaṁ sa*  
*prabhuḥ praphullekṣaṇa-padma-yugmaḥ \|*  
*prema-prabhāva-pracalāntarātmā*  
*gāḍha-pramodāt tam athāliliṅga \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this supreme description, the Lord, with blossoming lotus eyes, shivering with intense prema, embraced Rāmānanda in deep bliss.**

### Text 48

*itthaṁ dṛḍhāśleṣa-kalā-kalāpa-*  
*kallola-lolāntarayoḥ sa ko’pi \|*  
*kālas tadāsīt sukha-sāgarormi-*  
*kadambakaiḥ parvatayā parītaḥ \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As they tossed around in the waves of firm embraces, with whirlpools in the ocean of happiness, like a great festival, time passed.**

## Text49

*iti svabhāva-praṇayāyutena*  
*cirād gatenānubhavasya vartma \|*  
*sambhāṣya taṁ katy api vāsarān*  
*sa nītvā jagannātha-didṛkṣur āsīt \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the Lord conversed for long periods with Rāmānanda, endowed with natural prema, some days passed. Then the Lord desired to see Jagannātha.**

### Text 50

*athāyayau kṣetram adabhra-bhūṣaṁ*  
*bhramāpahaṁ gaura-sudhā-mayūkhaḥ \|*  
*pūrvaṁ tataḥ snāna-mahotsavasya*  
*dadarśa nīlācala-mauli-ratnam \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra arrived at Puri, the destroyer of illusion, which was decorated profusely and saw the deity before snāna-yatra.**

### Text 51

*nīlācale prodyati gauracandre*  
*payonidhiḥ pūram uvāha tuṅgam \|*  
*janāś ca vidhvasta-śugandhakārā*  
*babhūvur utphulla-dṛg-utpalāntāḥ \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the waves of the ocean increase when the moon rises, when Gauracandra appeared in Puri, the people had all their ignorance destroyed and their eyes blossomed like lotuses.**

### Text 52

*kecij jagannātha-vilokanāc ca*  
*kecit praṇāmād atha pūjanāc ca \|*  
*pradakṣiṇāt kecana sevanāc ca*  
*sarve samaṁ tat-savidhaṁ samīyuḥ \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though some were engaged in seeing Jagannātha, some were offering respects to the deity, some were performing worship, some were circumambulating the deity and others were serving the deity, they all approached Mahāprabhu.**

### Text 53

*prabhuś ca kāṁścid dhasitena kāṁścit*  
*vilokanena smita-sādareṇa \|*  
*kāṁścit samāśleṣa-rasena sarvān*  
*manorathaiḥ phulla-hṛdayaś cakāra \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He made everyone happy, some by laughter, some by glances, some by smiles, some by embraces, and some by fulfilling their desires.**

### Text 54

*athaiṣa nāthaḥ purato hy amīṣāṁ*  
*sākṣitvam ādhāya ca kṛṣṇa-dāsam \|*  
*tat kṣetram ānītam atiprayatnād*  
*gaccheti samyag visasarja tatra \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, making all these people the witness, addressed Kṛṣṇa-dāsa whom he had carefully brought back to Puri. He said, “Go.” He completely rejected him.**

### Text 55

*paśyan sa nīlācala-mauli-ratnaṁ*  
*gaurāṅga-candraḥ śata-ratna-ramyam \|*  
*svakīya-netrāmbu-jhareṇa bhūyo*  
*nijāṁ tanūm eva siṣeca hṛṣṭaḥ \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the crown jewel of Puri, Jagannātha, attractive as a hundred jewels, Gauracandra bathed his body in streams of his own tears in joy.**

### Text 56

*athāsakau snāna-mahotsavaṁ sa*  
*dadarśa ramyaṁ vibudhair durāpam \|*  
*ānanda-sandoha-samudram uccaṁ*  
*samudra-tīre’nyam ivekṣyamāṇam \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He then saw the attractive bathing festival, rare for the devatās to view, like another deep ocean of bliss, on the shore of the ocean at Puri.**

### Text 57

*atha prabhātāvasare tathaiva*  
*vilokituṁ taṁ gatavān kṛpāluḥ \|*  
*gūḍhaṁ tathā tatra vilokya nāsau*  
*babhūva duḥkhī kṛta-bāṣpa-mokṣaḥ \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Early in the morning merciful Gaurāṅga went to see the Lord, but could not see him, since he was hidden from view. He became sad and wept.**

### Text 58

*bahiḥ prayāya tvaritaṁ mahotko*  
*vicitra-ceṣṭo mada-siṁha-ramyaḥ \|*  
*ālālanāthaṁ prayayau tathāmī*  
*yayus tadānveṣaṇa-kātarāṅgāḥ \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Leaving the temple, he moved quickly like an infuriated lion, with great longing, and came to Ālālanātha temple. Searching for him with great anxiety, the devotees went out of the temple.**

### Text 59

*vicārya tasminn avalokya naiva*  
*pratepur uccair atha te’tikhinnāḥ \|*  
*athāyayuḥ kṣetram atīva-duḥkhaiḥ*  
*kṣaṇaṁ ca kalpān iva menire sma \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking and searching, they could not find him. They lamented loudly. In great sorrow they went to Puri. One moment seemed like a kalpa.**

### Text 60

*prabhus tathā tena pathaiva godā-*  
*varīṁ varīyān prayayau kṛpāluḥ \|*  
*tenaiva sārdhaṁ priya-bhāṣaṇena*  
*nināya māsāṁś caturo’parāṁś ca \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Later the Lord went on the road to the Godāvarī and spent more than four months talking to Rāmānanda.**

### Text 61

*hemanta-kāle’tha tathaiva tena*  
*samaṁ samantāt karuṇāṁ vitanvan \|*  
*samāyayau kṣetra-varaṁ varīyān*  
*jānātu kas tac caritaṁ vicitram \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the winter, the excellent Lord, spreading his mercy, returned to Puri with Rāmānanda. Who can understand the astonishing acts of the Lord?**

### Text 62

*sametya nīlācalam utsuko’sau*  
*hemācalābhaḥ kamanīya-dehaḥ \|*  
*śaśvaj jagannātha-mahāprabhuṁ taṁ*  
*vilokya harṣeṇa nināya kālam \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving at Puri, the joyful golden mountain, with attractive body, gazed continually at Jagannātha and passed his time in joy.**

### Text 63

*samāgataṁ taṁ parikarṇya kāśī-*  
*miśraṁ kṣatāgaḥ-paṭalī-tamisraḥ \|*  
*vilokya natvā mumude prakāmam*  
*abhīpsitaṁ bāhu-catuṣṭayāḍhyam \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing that Mahāprabhu had arrived, Kāśī-miśra, destroyer of all sin and darkness, saw him with four arms, as he desired. He offered respects and became blissful.**

### Text 64

*tat-kṛpābhir abhicumbita eṣa*  
*śrīmad-aṅghri-kamalasya rajobhiḥ \|*  
*rañjitaḥ pulaka-kaṇṭakitāṅgaḥ*  
*sāndra-saukhya-vivaśaḥ sa rarāja \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Touched by the Lord’s mercy and the dust from his lotus feet, with hairs standing on end, overwhelmed by intense bliss, Kāśī-miśra shone.**

### Text 65

*yo yadīya-kṛpayā sumahatyā*  
*nīla-śaila-tilakālaya-lakṣmīm \|*  
*sve vaśe prakurute sma garīyāṁs*  
*tasya kena mahimāparimeyaḥ \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By Gaurāṅga’s great mercy, Kāśī-miśra brought the gṛha-Lakṣmī of Jagannātha under his control. Who can measure his great glory?**

### Text 66

*gauracandra-caraṇa-dvitayasyā-*  
*jñāpanaṁ sakalam ātanute yaḥ \|*  
*īpsitaṁ parikalayya sa kāśī-*  
*miśra eṣa kathayā kim u vedyaḥ \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Who can describe in words Kāśī-miśra, who carried out all the orders of Gaurāṅga’s lotus feet, understanding his desires?**

### Text 67

*yo mahotsava-vidhau vividhāni*  
*prāyaśo nija-matāni viśeṣāt \|*  
*nirmitāni vidadhe prabhu-cittaṁ*  
*prākalayya kim ayaṁ jana-vedyaḥ \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Who can understand this person who, understanding the Lord’s mind, made various detailed arrangements as he saw fit, following the festival rules?**

### Text 68

*kaścanaiṣa paramo’tha mahātmā*  
*viṣṇu-dāsa iti nirmala-buddhiḥ \|*  
*sarvam eva parihāya dadarśa*  
*śrī-śacīsuta-padāmbuja-yugmam \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One very great soul named Viṣṇu-dāsa, with pure intelligence, gave up everything and saw the lotus feet of Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 69

*sadya eva sa tadīya-kṛpābhir*  
*vyānaśe sukṛta-sañcaya-dhanyaḥ \|*  
*locana-dvaya-galaj-jala-dhārā-*  
*dhauta-sarva-tanur eva tadāsīt \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Most fortunate with heaps of good acts, he was immediately inundated with the Lord’s mercy. His tears began to bathe his body.**

### Text 70

*ko’pi bhūri-sukṛtaḥ subhagaḥ pra-*  
*dyumna-miśra iti bhāgya-mayābdhiḥ \|*  
*gauracandra-caraṇāmbuja-yugmaṁ*  
*locanātithiṁ sukhena cakāra \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One very fortunate devotee named Pradyumna Miśra, an ocean of good qualities, saw with joy the lotus feet of Gauracandra.**

### Text 71

*locanātithitayaiva tad asminn*  
*asya kāruṇikatā kalitāsīt \|*  
*yad vilocana-gatā jala-dhārā*

**Just by seeing him he obtained the Lord’s mercy. His eyes, like clouds in the monsoon season, gushed with tears.**
