### Text 128

*sa tu dīna-dayārdra-mānasaś*  
*caraṇāṅguṣṭha-dalena tac-chiraḥ \|*  
*muhur aspṛśad ūcivān idaṁ*  
*nanu jānāmi bhavantam ity api \|\|128\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His mind soft with mercy to the fallen, the Lord touched his head with his toe many times and said, “I know you.”**

### Text 129

*sukṛtī kṛta-puṇya-sañcayas*  
*tad-anuprema-mayaḥ sa rāghavaḥ \|*  
*rabhasena dadarśa taṁ kṣaṇāt*  
*karuṇārdraḥ karuṇāṁ cakāra saḥ \|\|129\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee named Rāghava, having accumulated piles of good fortune, having great prema for the Lord, saw the Lord and at that moment, the Lord, tender with mercy, immediately gave him mercy.**

### Text 130

*atha śuddha-matir mahāśayaḥ*  
*sa tu govinda iti prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*bahu-tīrtha-paribhramād bahiḥ*  
*sumahān puṇya-payonidhir yayau \|\|130\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee known as Govinda, endowed with a pure mind, and ocean of good fortune, eternally huge from wandering in many holy places, went to Puri.**

### Text 131

*puruṣottamam eva tatra taṁ*  
*dayitaṁ gaura-kṛpā-mahānidhim \|*  
*sa dadarśa ca pāda-padmayoḥ*  
*paricaryāsu rato’bhavan muhuḥ \|\|131\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There he saw the merciful Gaurāṅga, the ocean of mercy and became attached to serving his lotus feet constantly.**

### Text 132

*ayam apy atibhāgyavāṁs tataḥ*  
*prabhṛti śrī-prabhu-pāda-padmayoḥ \|*  
*nikaṭastha ito divāniśaṁ*  
*paricaryām akarod gata-kriyaḥ \|\|132\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From that time onwards, the very fortunate Govinda remained near the Lord’s lotus feet and, having given up all other activities, served him day and night.**

### Text 133

*atha śuddha-matir mahāśayo*  
*guṇavān sac-caritas tadā prabhum \|*  
*pradadarśa sukhaugha-bhūṣitaḥ*  
*sa bhavānanda iti prakīrtitaḥ \|\|133\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee named Bhavānanda, of pure intelligence, full of good qualities, of good character, ornamented with great happiness, saw the Lord.**

### Text 134

*prabhur apy atiśuddha-mānasaṁ*  
*bhuja-yugmena dṛḍhaṁ samāśliṣat \|*  
*ayi pāṇḍu-samo’si bhāgyavān*  
*iti vācaṁ madhurāṁ jagāda ca \|\|134\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord firmly embraced pure-minded Bhavānanda with his two arms. He told him in a sweet voice, “You are very fortunate. You are the same as Pāṇḍu.”**

### Text 135

*athāsya putrā api pañca rāmā-*  
*nandādayo’syaiva mahā-kṛpāloḥ \|*  
*atipriyā eva babhūvur añjaḥ*  
*pārśva-sthitāḥ sevanam eva kṛtvā \|\|135\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His five sons headed by Rāmānanda, staying by Mahāprabhu’s side, became very dear to the Lord, performing service to him.**

### Text 136

*mṛdur mahātmā parama-priyo’sau*  
*śāntaḥ suhṛt sarva-janasya śaśvat \|*  
*caitanya-candrāṅghri-rataś ca vāṇī-*  
*nāthas tam eva pratisevamānaḥ \|\|136\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vāṇinātha, a soft devotee, very dear to the Lord, peaceful and a friend to all people, attached to Mahāprabhu’s lotus feet, served him exclusively.**

### Text 137

*ācārya-yuktaḥ puruṣottamākhyo*  
*mahāmatiḥ kaścana cāru-śīlaḥ \|*  
*śrutvā tadīyaṁ caritaṁ prayatnād*  
*yayau tam evekṣitum utsukātmā \|\|137\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Puruṣottamācārya, a very intelligent devotee with an attractive nature, hearing of Mahāprabhu’s activities, with effort eagerly came to see him.**

### Text 138

*puruṣottamam etya vihvalaḥ*  
*pradadarśātha kṛpānidheḥ padam*  
*sa tu darśana-mātra-kautukād*  
*abhavat kīdṛśa eva sammataḥ \|\|138\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving at Puri, in great bewilderment, he saw the lotus feet of the ocean of mercy, and from the joy of merely seeing the Lord, became completely changed.**

### Text 139

*tanur apy ahahaiva vismṛtā rasa-*  
*mātraṁ sukha-mātram īkṣitam \|*  
*api jīvita-nātha-darśanāj*  
*jaḍatā tena sadaiva saṁśritā \|\|139\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He forgot about his body. Only rasa and happiness could be seen in him. From seeing the Lord of his life, his body became stunned at all times.**

### Text 140

*atha nayane jala-nirjharākule*  
*vapur udyat-pulakaika-bhūṣitam \|*  
*pṛthu-vepathu-bhaṅga-bhaṅguraṁ*  
*gurum uru-dvitayaṁ tadā dadhe \|\|140\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His eyes poured torrents of tears. His body was decorated with goose bumps. His thighs shook intensely and became crooked.**

### Text 141

*dayitekṣaṇa-bhāva-bhāvitā*  
*dayitevābhavad eṣa bhāvitaḥ \|*  
*ayam apy atikomalo’bhavat*  
*priyatābhiḥ priyataika-sāgaraḥ \|\|141\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the Lord, he was overcome with emotion. He developed emotions like the Lord. The Lord, an ocean of affection, became most tender because of his affectionate nature.**

### Text 142

*bahudhā madhurāṁ śriyaṁ prabhuḥ*  
*parilocyāśu babhūva komalaḥ \|*  
*nitarām akarod amutra ca*  
*prathitaṁ prema-mahā-rasāmbudhiḥ \|\|142\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The tender Lord displayed great sweetness. The ocean of rasa distributed prema constantly to Puruṣottamācārya.**

### Text 143

*abhajiṣṭa tadā sadāśayaḥ*  
*sa tu sannyāsam adabhra-bhāgyavān \|*  
*agamat tu rasa-svarūpatām*  
*iha dāmodara ity udīritaḥ \|\|143\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The greatly fortunate Puruṣottamācārya, the abode of truth, took sannyāsa and became the very form of rasa. He was called Svarūpa Dāmodara.**

### Text 144

*iti tena nirantaraṁ prabhoḥ*  
*pada-pāthoja-samīpa-saṅgataḥ \|*  
*nimiṣaṁ sahate sma no dṛśoḥ*  
*paripaśyann iva tṛṣṇayā piban \|\|144\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Staying near the Lord’s lotus feet constantly, he drank the nectar with thirst. He could not tolerate not seeing the Lord for a moment.**

### Text 145

*śrī-vakreśvara-paṇḍito’timadhuraḥ kaścin mahātmā sadā*  
*sāndrānanda-rasāmṛtodadhir iti premāspadaṁ śrī-prabhoḥ \|*  
*āgatyātha vilokya cābhavad ayaṁ yasyāsya nṛtyodgame*  
*so’yaṁ gaura-mahāprabhuḥ pravaṇatāṁ yātaḥ svayaṁ sarvadā \|\|145\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vakreśvara Paṇḍita, was a very sweet devotee. He was an ocean of sweet rasa and condensed bliss at all times. Arriving at Puri and seeing the Lord, he began dancing. He thus became the object of Mahāprabhu’s prema. Gaurāṅga favored him at all times.**

### Text 146

*śrī-vāsudeva iti datta-kulaika-ratnaṁ*  
*gaurāṅga-candram avalokya jhaṭity amandam \|*  
*śaśvad babhūva khalu jīvana-nirviśeṣo*  
*niḥśeṣa-tat-praṇaya-sindhu-nimagna eṣaḥ \|\|146\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vāsudeva Datta, the jewel in his dynasty, seeing Gauracandra, immediately took him as his very life and became submerged in an ocean of unlimited affection for him.**

### Text 147

*athānya eko bhagavān itīha*  
*khyātaḥ sadācārya-varo mahātmā \|*  
*śrī-gauracandra-praṇato’nuvelaṁ*  
*śrīmaj-jagannātha-prabhuṁ siṣeve \|\|147\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhagavānācārya was a great devotee. Surrendering to Gauracandra, he served Lord Jagannātha constantly.**

### Text 148

*itthaṁ śrī-puruṣottame sthitavati pratyāsam āsīd dhvaniḥ \|\|*  
*sarvāsāṁ vidiśāṁ diśāṁ ca janatā sotkaṇṭham evāgatā \|*  
*ye cānye khalu satyarāja-sumatis tad-bhrātṛ-putrādayo*  
*ye cānye raghunandano narahariḥ śrīman-mukundādikaḥ \|\|148\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While the Lord lived in Puri, the sound of people arriving spread in all directions. Intelligent Satyarāja, his brothers and sons as well as Raghunandana, Narahari, Mukunda and others had assembled there with longing.**

## Chapter Fourteen

### Text 1

*Snāna-yatra*  
*ekadā prāha nātho’yaṁ nija-pāda-payoruham \|*  
*draṣṭuṁ tatrāgatān svīyān advaita-pramukhān janān \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One time, the Lord, spoke to the devotees headed by Advaitācārya, who had come see his lotus feet.**

### Text 2

*ācārya he mahābuddhe he paṇḍita mahāśaya \|*  
*yad vadāmi śṛṇu śrīmaj-jagannātha-viceṣṭitam \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O ācārya! O most intelligent, learned one! Hear what I speak about the activities of Jagannātha.**

### Text 3

*śrī-jagannātha-devo’sau sadā sarva-rasāśrayaḥ \|*  
*karoti guṇḍicā-yātrāṁ vilāsa-parayā dhiyā \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Jagannātha is always the shelter of all rasas. He goes to Guṇḍicā for special pastimes.**

### Text 4

*guṇḍicāgāra-gamane vartmanaḥ pārśvayor dvayoḥ \|*  
*ya eṣa puṣpitārāmo rāmaṇīyakavān iha \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“There is an attractive flower garden on either side of the path going to Guṇḍicā.**

### Text 5

*vṛndāraṇya-smṛti-karam enaṁ viddhi viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*tatra gatvā jagannātho guṇḍicā-maṇḍape prabhuḥ \|*  
*ekādhikāṣṭa-divasaṁ viharaṁs tatra tiṣṭhati \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Know that it particularly gives remembrance of Vṛndāvana. Going to the Guṇḍicā hall Jagannātha stays and performs pastimes there for nine days.**

### Text 6

*tad imāṁ paramāṁ yātrāṁ devādyair api durlabhām \|*  
*draṣṭuṁ pratyabdam evātrāgantavyaṁ hi bhavādṛśaiḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Every year you should all come to see this great festival, rare even for the devatās to see.”**

### Text 7

*iti svīya-vilāsānāṁ darśanāya mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*tān uvāca kṛpāmbhodhau rathayātrā-cchalena saḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu, the ocean of mercy, in this way, on the pretext of rathayātra, allowed the devotees to see his own pastimes.**

### Text 8

*tataḥ prabhṛty evam ete rathasya samaye prabhum \|*  
*śrī-gauracandraṁ draṣṭuṁ taṁ pratyabdaṁ yānti sa-spṛham \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus from that time onwards, they would eagerly come to see Gauracandra at the time of rathayātra every year.**

### Text 9

*yat pratyabdaṁ prayānty ete draṣṭuṁ gaurāṅga-sundaram \|*  
*tat kathāṁ kiṁ sura-guroḥ śataṁ kathayituṁ bhavet \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**How can hundreds of Bṛhaspatis describe these devotees who came to see Gauraṅga every year?**

### Text 10

*tathāpy utkaṇṭhayā śaśvat prathayann aviśeṣataḥ \|*  
*ekavārasya gamanaṁ samantād varṇayāmahe \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will describe how they went there one time, with constant eagerness, to give a general idea.**

### Text 11

*advaitācārya-devo’sau śrīmac-chrīvāsa-paṇḍitaḥ \|*  
*gṛhītvānekaśo lokānanyābde gamanotsukaḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Advaitācārya and Śrīvāsa with many people were eager to go one year.**

### Text 12

*pravṛtte mādhave māsi vahan malaya-mārute \|*  
*rute kokila-bhṛṅgādyaiś cāru te gantum udyatāḥ \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In Vaiśākha month, with the Malaya wind blowing, with the chirping of cuckoos and humming of bees, they decided to go.**

### Text 13-14

*prathamaṁ hṛṣṭa-hṛdayaḥ śrīmān śrīvāsa-paṇḍitaḥ \|*  
*śrī-gauracandra-premāti-nirbhara-snigdha-mānasaḥ \|\|13\|\|*

*śrī-vāsudeva-dattaṁ taṁ śrī-śivānanda-senakam \|*  
*hṛṣṭe ūce sva-hṛdayaṁ modayann anayor api \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa full of joy, his heart soft with affection for Gauracandra, spoke to Vāsudeva Datta and Śivānanda Sena, giving their hearts joy.**

### Text 15

*āgato’yaṁ sa samayo rathasya tad dinaṁ kuru \|*  
*praśastam asmad-gamane yuvayor api sāmpratam \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Find out the day of rathayātra, and decide what day is most suitable for us to depart for Puri.”**

### Text 16

*tato yātrā-dinaṁ kṛtvā sarve parama-saspṛhāḥ \|*  
*śrī-navadvīpa-gamane babhūvur atisotsukāḥ \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Having fixed the day of going, they all with great longing and desire went to Navadvīpa.**

### Text 17

*śrī-śacīṁ tāṁ bhagavatīṁ viṣṇu-bhakti-svarūpiṇīm \|*  
*mātaraṁ sarva-jagato dadṛśuḥ paramāśayāḥ \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great devotees saw Śacī, the form of Viṣṇu-bhakti, mother of the whole universe.**

### Text 18

*sthitvā dina-dvayaṁ tatra tat-sneha-bhara-nirvṛtāḥ \|*  
*śrīmad-advaita-devaṁ taṁ dadṛśur bahudhotsukam \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying there a few days, with bliss and affection, they went to see Advaitācārya, who was filled with longing.**

### Text 19

*tato jagāda madhuram advaitācārya īśvaraḥ \|*  
*yātrā-dinaṁ yad yuṣmākaṁ praśastaṁ tan mamāpi ca \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He said in a sweet voice, “The day of departure that is suitable for you is suitable for me also.”**

### Text 20

*tataḥ pramuditāḥ sarve nṛtya-kīrtana-tatparāḥ \|*  
*babhūvus tatra gaurāṅga-caraṇa-sneha-nirvṛtāḥ \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They danced and sang happily, blissful with affection for Gaurāṅga’s lotus feet.**

### Text 21

*śrīmad-advaita īśo’pi calitaḥ paramotsukaḥ \|*  
*bhakti-līlā-rasasyeva maryādā-parvato mahān \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Advaita, the greatest mountain range of rasa for bhakti pastimes, also departed in great happiness.**

### Text 22

*tataḥ śrī-haridāso’sau bhakti-līlā-mahāmbudhau \|*  
*magno mahā-parvatavan maināka iva vāridhau \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari-dāsa, absorbed in the great ocean of bhakti pastimes, was like a great mountain which had sunk in an ocean.**

### Text 23

*guṇa-kīrtanam evāsya santataṁ mahimārṇavāt \|*  
*āhṛtya saspṛhaṁ cakre yaḥ so’py atraiva sammataḥ \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Extracting kīrtana glorifying the Lord’s qualities from the great ocean continually, he agreed with great desire to go to Puri.**

### Text 24

*tata ete mahātmāno haridāsādayo janāḥ \|*  
*ācārya-paṇḍitāv ādau puraskṛtya yayuḥ sukham \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari-dāsa and other great devotees, putting Advaita and Śrīvāsa in front, happily proceeded to Puri.**

### Text 25

*śrī-vāsudeva-datto’pi śrī-śivānanda-senakaḥ \|*  
*anyonyaṁ parama-prītau tat-saṅge yayatur mudā \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vāsudeva Datta and Śivānanda Sena, with great affection, happily went with them.**

### Text 26

*śrīvāsa-paṇḍitasyāyād anujo rāma-paṇḍitaḥ \|*  
*yasya gānena gaurāṅgaḥ satataṁ tad-vaśo’bhavat \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The younger brother of Śrīvāsa, Śrirāma, who controlled Gaurāṅga by his singing at all times, also went.**

### Text 27-28

*śuciḥ snigdha-matiḥ śrīmān mukundaḥ paramaḥ priyaḥ \|*  
*madhuraḥ śāntimān sāntva-vacāḥ parama-komalaḥ \|\|27\|\|*

*tato murāri-guptaś ca prema-bhakti-rasārṇavaḥ \|*  
*dvitīya iva tat-saṅge dvitīyaḥ san mudaṁ yayau \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(yugmakam)**  
**Mukunda, pure, affection, most dear to the Lord, sweet, peaceful, mild speaking and very soft, joyfully went with Murāri Gupta, an ocean of prema-bhakti.**

### Text 29

*atha te śrīla-gaurāṅga-caraṇa-prema-vihvalāḥ \|*  
*tasyaiva guṇa-nāmādi kīrtayanto mudaṁ yayuḥ \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**These devotees, trembling with prema for Gaurāṅga’s lotus feet, joyfully went while chanting the Lord’s name and qualities.**

### Text 30

*kīrtanaṁ prātar ārabhya sandhyāyām athavā niśi \|*  
*kurvanti te’tha viśrāmaṁ pathi kṛtyaṁ tathā tataḥ \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Singing kīrtana from morning, they took rest in the evening or night, performing necessary activities on the road.**

### Text 31

*evaṁ dinaṁ kīrtanena nṛtyena ca mahāśayāḥ \|*  
*vinīya vartmani yayuḥ paramotsuka-cetasaḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Passing their days in singing and dancing, the devotees travelled on the road with greatly anxious hearts.**

### Text 32

*teṣāṁ teṣāṁ vāsarāṇāṁ varṇanīyaṁ na kiṁcana \|*  
*sukha-sāgara evāsīt sarvā viplāvayan diśaḥ \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One cannot properly describe their days. The ocean of happiness spread in all directions.**

### Text 33

*evaṁ te harṣa-pāthodhi-kallolākula-mānasāḥ \|*  
*lālasā gaura-caraṇe remuṇāyāṁ yayur mudā \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With minds aroused by huge waves in an ocean of joy, greedy for Gaurāṅga’s lotus feet, they happily came to Remuṇā.**

### Text 34

*astādri-mastake nyasya samasta-karam eva saḥ \|*  
*arko viṣīdati muhus teṣāṁ dṛṣṭim anāpnuvan \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sun, lamenting on not seeing the devotees, placed his rays on Sunset Mountain.**

### Text 35

*tatra te nagare śrīmad-gopīnāthaṁ samīkṣitum \|*  
*viviśus tat-purīṁ ramyāṁ pulakāktāṅga-yaṣṭayaḥ \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees, with hairs standing on end, entered the attractive town to see Gopīnātha.**

### Text 36

*dṛṣṭvā tan-mukha-candraṁ te paramāṁ prītim āyayuḥ \|*  
*namaskṛtya mahātmānaḥ kṛcchrān nivṛtur bahiḥ \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the moon-like face of the deity, they experienced the greatest satisfaction. Offering respects, the great devotees exited with difficultly.**

### Text 37

*prātaḥ pratasthire sarve sarvadotsuka-cetasaḥ \|*  
*śrī-gauracandra-caraṇa-darśanārtā mahāśayāḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the morning they departed with eager hearts, longing to see the lotus feet of Gauracandra.**

### Text 38

*teṣām oghaḥ sa paramaḥ satataṁ sukha-tanmayaḥ \|*  
*pārāvāra ivāreje pārāvāra-vivarjitaḥ \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With their blissful bodies, the great crowd shone like a second ocean.**

### Text 39

*‘advaito’yaṁ nidhir abhūt śrīvāso bhakti-parvataḥ \|*  
*amṛtaṁ kīrtanam abhūt haridāso mahā-maṇiḥ \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Advaita was the water, Śrīvāsa was a mountain of bhakti. Kīrtaṇa was the nectar arising in the ocean. Hari-dāsa was the Kaustubha jewel.**

### Text 40

*teṣām anyonya-samprītir lakṣmīr abhavad uttamā \|*  
*hiṇḍīro yaśasāṁ rāśis tejaś ca baḍavānalaḥ \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Their mutual affection became excellent Lakṣmī. Its great fame was the foam and its effulgence was the baḍava fire.**

### Text 41

*kallolo jaya-nisvānas taraṅgo nirbharāplutiḥ \|*  
*mīnāś ca pādāṅgulayo muktās tan-nakha-paṅktayaḥ \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Shouts of “Jaya” were the waves and flooding rip tides. Their toes were the fish and their toe nails were the pearls.**

### Text 42

*sarpā api bhujā āsan raktāṁsi dvīpa-sañcayāḥ \|*  
*āścarya-kamalāny āsan vadanāni vibhānty api \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(yugmakam)**  
**Their arms were the serpents, their chests were the islands and their faces were the astonishing lotuses.**

### Text 43

*tato jayapure grāme sārvabhaumo mahāmatiḥ \|*  
*samāgamena tatraiva paramotsuka āgataḥ \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great minded Sārvabhauma with great eagerness came to Jayapura village to meet them.**

### Text 44-45

*muñcan nayanayor vāri tān prati sneham eva tat \|*  
*bibhrat-pulaka-saṅghena samantād ākulāṁ tanum \|\|44\|\|*

*advaitaṁ tatra dṛṣṭvāsau mahātmānaṁ mahāśayaḥ \|*  
*astuvac choka-bandhena sva-kavitvena sat-kaviḥ \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While he shed tears out of affection for them, his body became covered with goose bumps. After seeing the great devotee Advaita, the great poet began reciting verses of praise he had composed.**

### Text 46

*advaitāya namas te’stu maheśāya mahātmane \|*  
*yat-prasādena gaurāṅga-caraṇe jāyate ratiḥ \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I offer respects to Advaita, the great devotee, the great Lord, by whose mercy attachment to Gaurāṅga’s lotus feet arises.”**

### Text 47

*evam uktvā papātāsau daṇḍavad dharaṇī-tale \|*  
*pulaka-prema-jaḍito mahātmā bhāgya-toyadhiḥ \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, his hairs standing on end, and stunned, the ocean of good fortune fell on the ground, offering respects.**

### Text 48

*haridāsaṁ samālocya bhaktimān abhavan mahān \|*  
*daṇḍavad bhuvi hṛṣṭo’sau patitvā pulakācitaḥ \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Hari-dāsa, filled with great devotion, in great joy, he fell on the ground with hairs standing on end.**

### Text 49

*cakāra bhūyaśaḥ śrīmān praṇāmān natakandharaḥ \|*  
*kula-jāty-anapekṣāya haridāsāya te namaḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He offered respects many times with lowered shoulders saying, “I offer respects to Hari-dāsa, whose family and birth are not considered.”**

### Text 50

*tataḥ sagadgadāṁ vācam uvāca dvija-puṅgavaḥ \|*  
*pulakaiḥ kaṇṭakī-bhūtaṁ vapur bibhrat galat-klamaḥ \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The best of brāhmaṇas, recovering, with hairs standing on end, then spoke in a choked voice.**

### Text 51-52

*śrī-gaurāṅga-candra-caraṇa-kamalasyāpy ājñayā \|*  
*vedāntānyārtha-kṛtaye taj-jñānāṁ tāraṇāya ca \|\|51\|\|*

*cirād adhyātma-yogasya bhāvanā-śuṣka-kaṭhinaḥ \|*  
*etayā bhakti-sudhayā jīvayāmīti gamyate \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Following the order of Gauracandra’s lotus feet, I will bring to life with the nectar of bhakti to those persons who have dried up by thoughts impersonal brahman, by delivering knowledge, giving another meaning to the Vedānta.**

### Text 53

*atra prabho mat-pratijñā-śravaṇānantaraṁ yathā \|*  
*vāco-vilāsaṁ mākārṣīr vṛthā-śramam atisphuṭam \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“After hearing my proposal, the Lord said playfully, ‘Do not make useless efforts.’**

### Text 54

*athāpy utkaṇṭhayā gantu-kāmaṁ māṁ karuṇā-nidhiḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca na te śaktir bhaviṣyati kathañcana \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The ocean of mercy, seeing me anxious to go, said, ‘You will not at all have that power.**

### Text 55

*mā sma gā mā kṛthā vyartha-pariśramam imaṁ dvija \|*  
*yasya no vartate bhāgyaṁ kiṁ tu tvaṁ kārayiṣyasi \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Do not go. Do not have wasted effort, O brāhmaṇa! What can you do, you who do not have good fortune?’**

### Text 56

*tathapy utkaṇṭhayā yāmi kāśīṁ parama-nistrapaḥ \|*  
*manoratho me saphalo yathā syāt tat-kṛpaṁ kuru \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I am eagerly going to Kāśī without shame. Be merciful so that my desire will be fulfilled.”**

### Text 57

*ity uktavān sārvabhaumo bhūmi-gīrvāṇa-paṇḍitaḥ \|*  
*namaskṛtvā mahābhāgo jagāma sukha-tanmayaḥ \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sārvabhauma, Bṛhaspati on earth, saying this, offered respects and, his body filled with happiness, left.**

### Text 58

*tata ete mahātmāno ramyāṁ yājapurīṁ yayuḥ \|*  
*kṛtvā vaitaraṇī-snānaṁ jagmur nagara-madhyataḥ \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees came to attractive Yājapurī and after bathing in the Vaitaraṇī River, entered the town.**

### Text 59

*atha pratāparudreṇa svapnaṁ dṛṣṭvā mahātmanā \|*  
*preṣito yānam utthāpya tadīyo’dvaitam ānayat \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pratāparudra, after having a dream, sent a palanquin and put Advaita on it, and brought him.**

### Text 60

*rāja-sambhāṣaṇaṁ kartuṁ gantuṁ mām iti saṁvidan \|*  
*kiṁ vadiṣyati nātho’sāv iti cintākulo’bhavat \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Advaita was worried. “Knowing that I am conversing with a king, what will Gauracandra say to me?”**

### Text 61

*īśvaro’py eṣa gaurāṅga-candra-bhītyāśu vepitaḥ \|*  
*śrī-vāsudeva-dattaṁ taṁ nināya nija-saṅgataḥ \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(yugmakam)**  
**Though Advaita was the Lord he trembled in fear of Gauracandra. He brought Vāsudeva Datta with him.**

### Text 62

*kecit tat-saṅgato jagmur advaitānugatā janāḥ \|*  
*kaṭakasya pathā te ca śrī-gaura-caraṇāśrayāḥ \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some followers of Advaita, surrendered to Gaurāṅga’s lotus feet, went with him on the road to Kaṭaka.**

### Text 63

*anye ca haridāsādyā mahātmāno mahāśayāḥ \|*  
*śrīvāsaṁ purataḥ kṛtvā haṁseśvara-pathair yayuḥ \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hari-dāsa and other great devotees, letting Śrīvāsa go ahead, went to Haṁseśvara.**

### Text 64

*tad dinaṁ tatra saṁnīya dṛṣṭvā ca tam umāpatim \|*  
*prātar utthāya sukhitā paritas te mudā yayuḥ \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Spending the day there, they saw the deity of Śiva, and the next morning rose thoroughly refreshed and continued walking in joy.**

### Text 65-66

*kiyad-dūre hi te tiṣṭhan śrīvāsa-pramukhā janāḥ \|*  
*nikaṭaṁ gacchatāṁ teṣām utkaṇṭhā dviguṇābhavat \|\|65\|\|*

*At some distance, Śrīvāsa and others who had gone ahead were waiting. Their joy doubled as the other devotees approached.*  
*vilokitavyā gaurāṅga-nakha-candra-cchaṭā iti \|*  
*advaito’pi tatas tatra milito’bhūn mahāmatiḥ \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great-minded Advaita met with them, while thinking, “We will see the effulgence of Gaurāṅga’s toe nails.”**

### Text 67

*ekatraiva militvā te yayuḥ kamalake pure \|*  
*mudā paramayā yuktāḥ kīrtayanto’bhito’bhitaḥ \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Together then went to Kamalaka-pura in great joy, spreading kīrtana everywhere.**

### Text 68

*nadīm āsādya susnātāḥ prāsādaṁ dadṛśur muhuḥ \|*  
*auttuṅgena vivasvantaṁ nabhasthaṁ pātayann iva \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Coming to the river, they bathed and gazed at the temple, for a long time. With its towering pinnacle, it seemed the sun had fallen from the sky.**

### Text 69

*tejasā koṭi-sūryābhaḥ sudhayā ca samanvitaḥ \|*  
*sa nīlaparvata-pateḥ prāsādaḥ sukha-darśanaḥ \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With joy they saw the temple of Jagannātha shining like a million suns, endowed with nectar.**

### Text 70

*sukhadaḥ sarva-bhūtānāṁ tair adarśi mahāśayaiḥ \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great devotees gazed at the temple which gives bliss to all beings.**

### Text 71

*dṛṣṭvā prāsādam uttuṅgaṁ tuṅga-romāñca-sañcayaiḥ \|*  
*harṣas teṣāṁ samajani tat-samo bhṛśam ucchritaḥ \|\|71\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the temple, their hairs stood on end in joy as great as the size of the temple.**

### Text 72

*vilokya harṣa-sandoha-nirbharāḥ sphūrti-vihvalāḥ \|*  
*namaścakrur mahātmāno hari-kīrtana-tatparāḥ \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the temple, they felt an ocean of happiness. They trembled and offered respects while performing kīrtana.**

### Text 73

*atha prāpya mahātmāsau mālāṁ parama-pāvanīm \|*  
*śrī-gauracandra-prahitāṁ mumude’dvaita īśvaraḥ \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Receiving a pure garland sent by Gauracandra, Advaita was overjoyed.**

### Text 74

*kīrtayadbhir niravadhi prema-hṛṣṭair mahātmabhiḥ \|*  
*advaito’pi sukhāviṣṭo naṭanāyopacakrame \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Overcome with happiness, Advaita danced along with the devotees who, joyful with prema, were doing constant kīrtana.**

### Text 75

*nṛtyann asau kīrtayantas te’pi gaurāṅga-lālasāḥ \|*  
*narendrākhya-saras-tīram āsādya sukham āyayuḥ \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Dancing and chanting, greedy for Gaurāṅga, they arrived at the bank of Narendra Lake and proceeded happily.**

### Text 76

*atha bhūyo’pi govindān mālām āsādya pāvanīm \|*  
*advaitas tan nigaditaṁ śuśrāva bhṛśam utsukaḥ \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Advaita again received a pure garland from Govinda. He heard Govinda’s words with eagerness.**

### Text 77

*samudra-taṭa-saṁsthasya nideśo’yaṁ mahāprabhoḥ \|*  
*upavāso’sti vihito nātra yuṣmākam āgamaḥ \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Mahāprabhu is now at the ocean. He says that you should fast (with your eyes) and should not come to the ocean to see him.**

### Text 78

*bhaviṣyati hi tatraiva puṇḍarīkākṣa īkṣyatām \|*  
*ahaṁ tatraiva yāsyāmi vilambena suniścitam \|*  
*bhaviṣyati samālāpas tatra miśrālayāntare \|\|78\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You should see Jagannātha. He will come definitely after some delay. You can talk together in Kāśī-miśra’s house.”**

### Text 79

*iti śrutvādvaita īśo māyaiṣeti vitarkayan \|*  
*tathaivānumatiṁ cakre tad-vaśo’sau yataḥ svayam \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Advaita thought, “This is his māyā.” He inferred this, since the Lord was under his control.**

### Text 80

*murāri-gupto’tha mahā-nirveda-parayā dhiyā \|*  
*patitvā daṇḍavad bhūmau rudann idam abhāṣata \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Murāri Gupta, his mind afflicted with depression, fell on the ground like a stick and, crying, spoke.**

### Text 81

*dīno’yaṁ duḥkhitatamo jīvalokaḥ supāmaraḥ \|*  
*etāvad dūram ānīto bhavadbhir mahitāśayaiḥ \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I am miserable, full of sorrow, the most fallen person. You great devotees have brought me this far.**

### Text 82

*na pāraye’haṁ vrajituṁ na śaktir mama vartate \|*  
*na sāhasaṁ me’sti tāvad draṣṭuṁ jagad-īśvaram \|*  
*bhavadbhir jñāpite paścād gantuṁ śaktir bhaviṣyati \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I do not have the strength to go further. I do not have the rashness to see Jagannātha. If you inform Mahāprabhu of this, then I will gain the strength to go later.”**

### Text 83

*ity uktvā bahu-nirviṇṇo duḥkhī tatraiva susthiraḥ \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, in great depression and sorrow, Murāri Gupta remained fixed in his determination.**

### Text 84

*tad-anantaram advaita-pramukhās te mahāśayāḥ \|*  
*puṇḍarīkākṣa-yugalam īkṣāṁ cakrur jagatpateḥ \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After that, the devotees headed by Advaita saw Jagannātha, endowed with lotus eyes.**

### Text 85

*mahorasaṁ mahābāhuṁ viśālāyata-locanam \|*  
*taṁ vilokya jagannāthaṁ mudam āpur mahattarām \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Jagannātha with his mighty chest, his huge arms and his blossoming eyes, they were overjoyed.**

### Text 86

*atha śrī-śrī-gauracandraś candra-koṭir mahojjvalaḥ \|*  
*udiyāya sukhāviṣṭaḥ sravad-aśru-bhara-plutaḥ \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then Gauracandra, shining like a million moons, overflowing with happiness, tears flowing from his eyes, arrived.**

### Text 87

*pāda-nyāsair dalan bhūmiṁ matta-padmīndra-vikramaḥ \|*  
*matta-siṁha-mahollāso lasad-ājānu-dor-dvayaḥ \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He made the earth tremble with his footsteps. He had the gait of an intoxicated elephant. He had the enthusiasm of a mad lion. His gleaming arms extended to his knees.**

### Text 88

*jaṅgamaḥ kāñcana-giriḥ sākṣād iva sudhākaraḥ \|*  
*galad-aśru-jharāsāra-jhara-nirjhara-sañcayaḥ \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He was a moving mountain of gold, a mine of nectar, gushing with waterfalls of tears.**

### Text 89

*sudhāṁśu-koṭir yugapad ekībhūya samudgataḥ \|*  
*vikiran satatāsārāṁ pīyūṣa-drava-dīrghikām \|\|89\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He was like a million moons rising simultaneously, emitting constantly long streams of nectar.**

### Text 90

*sindūrāruṇa-kaupīna-bahirvāsaḥ suśobhitaḥ \|*  
*ūru-dvandva-vinirdhūta-rambhā-stambha-yuga-dyutiḥ \|\|90\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He shone with a red kaupīna and outer cloth. His effulgent thighs conquered two banana trunks.**

### Text 91

*nakhendu-sundara-jyotsnā-pīyūṣa-cchaṭayā tayā \|*  
*prakāśayan puṇyavatīṁ rasāṁ rasa-payonidhiḥ \|\|91\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With rays of sweet light emanating from his beautiful toe nails, the ocean of rasa illuminated the fortunate earth.**

### Text 92

*mukha-candra-snigdha-sāndra-jyotsnā-snapita-diṅ-mukhaḥ \|*  
*sukha-sāgara evānyo mūrtimān kambu-kandharaḥ \|\|92\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All directions were bathed in the intense light of his moon-like face. His conch-like neck was the personification of an ocean of happiness.**

### Text 93

*siṁha-grīvo mahā-pīna-vakṣaḥ-sthala-vilobhanaḥ \|*  
*kṣīṇāvalagna-saṁlagna-kaṭi-sūtra-manoharaḥ \|\|93\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He had the neck of a lion, and a broad, beguiling chest. He had a thin waist tied with a waist cloth.**

### Text 94

*naumīḍya te’bhra-vapuṣe iti brahma-stavaṁ paṭhan \|*  
*svayam advaita-devaṁ taṁ praṇanāma mahāprabhuḥ \|\|94\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Reciting Brahmā’s prayer starting with naumīḍya te ‘bhra-vapuse, Mahāprabhu offered respects to Advaita.**

### Text 95

*advaito’pi sukhāviṣṭo hṛṣṭa-romā nanāma tam \|*  
*dvayoḥ stavana-natyādau dvau na prabhavataḥ kṣaṇam \|\|95\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Overjoyed, Advaita, his hairs standing on end, offered him respects. They praised each other and offered respects continually.**

### Text 96

*tayor galad vāridhārā-lakṣa-muktā-srajo muhuḥ \|*  
*āsīt praṇāma-stutibhiḥ ko’pi kālaḥ sukhāvahaḥ \|\|96\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Continuously they shed tears which formed pearl garlands. They spent a long happy time offering respects and praises.**

### Text 97

*tato mahāprabhur dhṛtvā śrīvāsasya padāmbujam \|*  
*bahudhā vihvalo bhūtvā cakāra stutim uttamām \|\|97\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then Mahāprabhu, seeing the lotus feet of Śrīvāsa, became overjoyed and praised him profusely.**

### Text 98

*so’pi dvijāgryo vikalo martu-kāma ivābhavat \|*  
*nanāma bhūri-sukṛto vacanenāstuvad bhṛśam \|\|98\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa, becoming confused, seemed like he desired to die. The fortunate devotee offered respects and praises profusely.**

### Text 99

*tato’syāvarajo rāma-paṇḍito’timahāśayaḥ \|*  
*śrī-vāsudeva-datto’pi nematur yugapat prabhum \|\|99\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His younger brother Śrīrāma and Vāsudeva Datta offered respects to the Lord together.**

### Text 100

*tau jagrāha bhuja-stambha-yugalena mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*śrī-śivānanada-seno’pi tat-paścād anaman mudā \|\|100\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu embraced them with his pillar-like arms. Śivānanda Sena then offered respects in bliss.**

### Text 101

*gaṅgā-jalasya ca puro bhāṇḍa-dvayam athānayat \|\|101\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He brought two jars of Gaṅgā water to Mahāprabhu.**

### Text 102

*tat tu dṛṣṭvā kṛpāmbhodhir gaṅgā-māhātmyam ujjagau \|*  
*uvāca madhuraṁ cānudanta-dyotojjvalādharaḥ \|\|102\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the water, Mahāprabhu, the ocean of mercy, sang its praises. His teeth gleaming between his lips, he spoke sweetly.**

### Text 103

*snānotsavāyaikam idaṁ mahyam ekaṁ ca dīyatām \|*  
*tad dvayaṁ śrī-vāsudeva-śrī-śivānandayoḥ pṛthak \|\|103\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Give one jar to me, for the bathing ceremony of Jagannātha. Separate these two jars of Vāsudeva and Śivānanda.”**

### Text 104

*ubhayor eva vijñāya vāsanāṁ punar uktavān \|*  
*tayor ardhaṁ vibhajyādau jagannāthāya dīyatām \|*  
*anyad ardhaṁ tato’traiva sthāpyatām iti sa prabhuḥ \|\|104\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He understood the desire of Vāsudeva Datta and Śivānanda Sena. He said “Divide the water and give half to Jagannātha and leave the other half here.”**

### Text 105

*atha śrīmān kṛpāmbhodhiḥ papraccha vismayānvitaḥ \|*  
*murāriḥ kva murāriḥ kva kvāsau satvaram ānaya \|\|105\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of mercy then inquired with astonishment, “Where is Murāri? Where is Murāṛī? Bring him quickly.”**

### Text 106

*iti śrutvā pradhāvantaḥ śataśo bhṛśam utsukāḥ \|*  
*satvaraṁ tatra gatvā ca narendra-sarasas taṭe \|\|106\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, many devotees ran with zeal and came quickly to the bank of Narendra Sarovara.**

### Text 107

*vihvalaṁ patitaṁ bhūmau rudantaṁ dīna-cetasam \|*  
*dadṛśus te tathaivocuḥ śīghram āgamyatām iti \|\|107\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They saw Murāri lying on the ground, trembling and crying with a miserable heart. They said, “Quickly come.”**

### Text 108-109

*tathā niśamya tad vākyaṁ murāriḥ paramotsukaḥ \|*  
*vihvalo’śru-jalaiḥ śaśvad āpluto dhūli-dhūsaraḥ \|\|108\|\|*

*tathaiva virudan bhūri-kākū-proktair mahāśayaḥ \|*  
*yayau parama-nirviṇṇaḥ prāṇa-prabhum avekṣitum \|\|109\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(yugmakam)**  
**Hearing these words, Murāri in great joy, trembling, with eyes full of tears, covered with dust, crying with pitiful words, completely despondent, went to see the Lord.**

### Text 110

*stambha-gharmāmbubhiḥ śaśvat skhalat-pada-yugaḥ patan \|*  
*saṁvītasyaiva celasya gale baddhvārdham añcalam \|\|110\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Perspiring and stunned, he stumbled about continually. He covered his throat with the border of his cloth.**

### Text 111

*dante nidhāya bahudhā tṛṇāni tṛnavad vrajan \|*  
*galad-aśru-payo-yukta-vakṣo-mauktika-hāra-dhṛk \|\|111\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Holding grass in his teeth, he moved like lowly grass. He wore a pearl garland of tears on his chest.**

### Text 112

*premāndha iva tatraiva ciraṁ prabhum alokayat \|*  
*sa-bāṣpa-kaṇṭhaṁ kim api vaktuṁ śakto na ca kṣaṇam \|\|112\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As if blinded by prema, he gazed at the Lord for a long time. He could not speak because tears choked his throat for some time.**

### Text 113

*tathāpi gadgadodgāra-lakṣa-kākūktivān asau \|*  
*dadhāra caraṇāmbhoje prabhoḥ parama-dīna-dhīḥ \|\|113\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then with choked, pitiful voice, feeling most miserable, he uttered words and held the lotus feet of the Lord.**

### Text 114

*tat-pādāmbuja-yugmaṁ tat siṣeca khalu bhūyaśaḥ \|*  
*locana-dvaya-nirgacchad-aśru-dhārā-samuccaye \|\|114\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He repeatedly sprinkled the tears flowing from his two eyes upon the Lord’s lotus feet.**

### Text 115

*so’pi prabhus tasya pṛṣṭhaṁ siṣeca nayanodbhavaiḥ \|*  
*ambhobhir āyatārakta-locanāmburuha-dvayaḥ \|\|115\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, with two blossoming lotus eyes, also sprinkled Murārī’s back with his tears.**

### Text 116

*tatrasthaḥ sakalo lokas tasya rodana-kākubhiḥ \|*  
*arudat tat-sama iva tanmayaḥ samayo’bhavat \|\|116\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The people present there cried along with his crying. The time became similar to him, absorbed completely in his emotions.**

### Text 117

*prabhuś ca tat kāku-vādaṁ rodanaṁ ca mahattaram \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā śrutvā kṣaṇam api na sehe vikalo’bhavat \|\|117\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, seeing and hearing his pitiful words and intense crying, could not endure it for a moment, and felt great sorrow.**

### Text 118

*tato babhau tatra nātho’dvaitādika-samanvitaḥ \|*  
*snigdho rākā-niśānātha iva nakṣatra-maṇḍitaḥ \|\|118\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the Lord, with Advaita and others, became charming and affectionate, like the moon decorated with stars.**

### Text 119

*udyad-vibhrama-śoṇāsya hāsya-rañjita-candrikaḥ \|*  
*svāṅga-jyotsnācchaṭā-śaśvat-snāpitāśā-vadhū-mukhaḥ \|\|119\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The moon of Gaurāṅga, smiling, tinged with beautiful red lips, bathed the faces of the directions in the effulgence of his limbs.**

### Text 120

*atha te kṛṣṇa-caitanya-caraṇāsava-lampaṭāḥ \|*  
*snāna-yātrā-darśanāya babhūvur aniśotsukāḥ \|\|120\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Greedy for the nectar of Caitanya’s feet, the devotees became anxious to see the bathing festival of Jagannātha.**

### Text 121

*ekādaśyāṁ ca dadṛśur vivāhotsavam utsukāḥ \|*  
*tataś ca pūrṇimāyāṁ te snāna-yātrāṁ ca pāvanīm \|\|121\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On Ekādāśī the devotees happily saw the marriage festival and on the full moon day they saw the purifying bathing festival.**

### Text 122

*tatra nīla-girau ramye saudhāṭṭālika-gopure \|*  
*pure mahita-saundarye ramaṇīye sukhāvahe \|\|122\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The city of Puri was most attractive and pleasing with mansions, watchtowers and gates.**

### Text 123

*śubhrāvabhraṁliha-saśrīka-prāsādavati kaścana \|*  
*snāna-mañcaḥ sañcarati sudhābhir anurañjitaḥ \|\|123\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It had beautiful palaces which touched the white clouds. In that city, the bathing platform, permeated with nectar, seemed like a moving person.**

### Text 124

*tataḥ pūrvedyus tatrādriṁ dyumaṇau yāti sundaram \|*  
*taṁ mañcaṁ maṇḍitaṁ kartum ārebhe tat-paro janaḥ \|\|124\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the previous day, when the sun set, some people had started to decorate the platform.**

### Text 125

*tathaiva tatra kalayā hīnaḥ pūrṇavad udgataḥ \|*  
*rarāja rajanīkāntaḥ kāntayaṁs tat puraṁ mahat \|\|125\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the full moon rose, it shone, making the great city beautiful.**

### Text 126

*snāna-mañcam api śrīmān sudhāṁśuḥ sudhayānvitaḥ \|*  
*karau saṁmārjayāmāsa sevā-para iva prabhoḥ \|\|126\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The moon, full of nectar, as if serving the Lord, cleansed the platform with its hand-like rays.**

### Text 127-128

*jālena mahatā rājat-kṣudra-ghaṇṭā-ughargharaiḥ \|*  
*satoraṇena dīvyena puṣpa-mālyair anekadhā \|\|127\|\|*

*maṇḍite snāna-nilaye tac-chobhānāṁ samudgame \|*  
*abhūt ka iva nirvācyo jagaj-jana-manoramaḥ \|\|128\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The bathing platform was decorated with abundant flower garlands, with an attractive archway, with a huge network of flowers, and with small shining, jingling bells. Its beauty became attractive to the whole world.**

### Text 129

*tato gaurāṅga-candrasyājñāpanena mahāśayāḥ \|*  
*snāna-sandarśanotkaṇṭhāḥ prākāropari susthirāḥ \|\|129\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the order of Gaurāṅga, the devotees, anxious to see the bathing of Jagannātha, fixed themselves on top of a wall.**

### Text 130

*virejur antarīkṣa-sthā devā iva hareḥ puraḥ \|*  
*śrī-gaurāṅga-karālipta-candanai rājitorasaḥ \|\|130\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Their chests shining with sandalwood applied by the hand of Gaurāṅga, they shone like devatās in the sky in front of Mahāprabhu.**

### Text 131

*yāminyāś carame kāle āgate dayitādayaḥ \|*  
*sannāha-paṭṭaṁ vimalaṁ śrīmad-aṅge nyayojayan \|\|131\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When dawn came, the servants of Jagannātha fastened the pure ropes on Jagannātha’s body.**

### Text 132

*tataḥ pūrvaṁ haladharo vijayodyamam āvahan \|*  
*siṁhāsanād avataran babhau koṭīnduvad vibhuḥ \|\|132\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**First, Balarāma desired to go. He came off his throne, shining like a million moons.**

### Text 133

*tato bhagavatī devī subhadrātha jagatpatiḥ \|*  
*jagannātho’py avataran vicitrāṁ śriyam āyayau \|\|133\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then Subhadrā, and then Jagannātha, came off their thrones, producing astonishing beauty.**

### Text 134

*tato gaurasudhā-raśmiḥ purataḥ purato vrajan \|*  
*dadarśa vartma-vijayaṁ kramaśas taṁ trayasya ca \|\|134\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra, came in front and saw the victory path of the three deities, one by one.**

### Text 135

*pāda-nyāsair dalan bhūmiṁ kaśipoḥ kaśipūttamam \|*  
*vrajan babhau jagannātho yathā bhād bhāntaraṁ śaśī \|\|135\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Shaking the earth with his footsteps, moving from pillow to pillow, Jagannātha appeared like the moon moving from constellation to constellation.**

### Text 136

*taṁ sopāna-paramparābhir amalaṁ svaccha-dyutiṁ maṇḍapaṁ*  
*cañcad-vīci-paramparā-pravilasat-kṣīrābdhi-śobhā-muṣam \|*  
*ghaṇṭā-gharghara-nāda-lakṣita-jaya-dhvānaiś ca jāloccayaiḥ*  
*samyag bhūṣitam āruroha bhagavān nīlādri-cūḍāmaṇiḥ \|\|136\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, the crown jewel of Nīlādri, ascended the spotless platform, which was decorated with networks of flowers, which shone brightly with a series of steps, and which surpassed the beauty of the milk ocean with trembling waves, while people shouted of “Jaya” and rang bells.**

### Text 137

*śrī-caitanya-mahāprabhuś ca purato bhaktair janair āvṛtaḥ*  
*śaśval-locana-paṅkaja-dvaya-galad-dhārāṁ vahan vakṣasi \|*  
*dhārābhir vilasann asāv api jagannāthaḥ svayaṁ snāpito*  
*reje’nyonya-samāna-vibhrama-samālokena harṣākulaḥ \|\|137\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Caitanya Mahāprabhu, surrounded by devotees, was in the front. Streams of tears fell from his lotus eyes onto his chest. Shining Jagannātha was also bathed in tears, overcome with joy on seeing that they had similar beauty.**

### Text 138

*ucair uccavad ullasaj jaya-jaya-svānaiḥ samānotthitaiḥ*  
*puṣpa-stoma-samāna-vṛṣṭibhir api śrīmān mahān utsavaḥ \|*  
*āsīt sarva-janasya locana-yugānandāmṛtāyāsphuṭaṁ*  
*brahmādyair api durlabho ‘sita-giri-śrīman-maṇeḥ sāmpratam \|\|138\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great festival of Jagannātha, rare for the devatās like Brahmā to see, clearly meant for giving sweet bliss to the eyes of all people, was beautiful with simultaneous loud shouts of “Jaya, jaya,” ever increasing in joy, and with showers of flowers similar to verses of praise.**

### Text 139

*snānāmbu-dhārāpluta eṣa nīla-*  
*girīśvaro gaura-sudhākarasya \|*  
*viccheda-bhāvena rudan vireje*  
*cirāya gupto bhaviteti devaḥ \|\|139\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Covered with the bathing water, Jagannātha seemed to be crying in separation from Gaurāṅga, thinking “He will be hidden from my vision for a long time.”**

### Text 140

*evaṁ snāna-mahotsavāmṛta-rasa-snigdhoru-vakṣaḥ-sthalaḥ*  
*śrī-nīlācala-mauli-ramya-tilakaḥ sthitvā kṣaṇaṁ sakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*ārebhe punar apy asau kaśipubhir gacchan śubhaṁ dakṣiṇā-*  
*vartaṁ sevaka-sañcayair vṛta-bhuja-stambha-dvayaḥ śrī-yutaḥ \|\|140\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His chest and thighs were pleased with the sweet liquids of the bathing festival. Jagannātha remained there for some time in bliss. Again, his two arms were held by groups of servants. Endowed with beauty, he began to move gloriously on pillows.**

### Text 141

*kurmaḥ sīdati śeṣa eṣa calitaḥ sarvaiḥ phaṇā-maṇḍalaiḥ*  
*kṣauṇī kṣubhyati bhūbhṛto vidalitā brahmāṇḍam utkhaṇḍitam \|*  
*maryādām api sāgaro’py atigato dudrāva bhāsvān asau*  
*prasthāne muravairiṇo vijayino nīlādri-cūḍāmaṇeḥ \|\|141\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At the departure of Jagannātha, Kūrma seemed to sink, Śeṣa seemed to tremble with all his hoods, the earth seemed to quake, mountains seemed to explode, the universe seemed to break apart, the ocean seemed to flood its shores, and the sun seemed to flee away.‘**

## Chapter Fifteen

### Text 1

*asita-giri-patis tato’yam antaḥ-*  
*pura-paricārikayā śriyā sametaḥ \|*  
*anavasaram upetya gūḍha-veśo*  
*vasati janasya vilocanātidūraḥ \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jagannātha, wearing the cloth of invisibility, then disappeared from men’s vision with Lakṣmī, servant of the palace, for repairs.**

### Text 2

*asita-giri-nivāsi-bhakta-lokān*  
*atiśayitārti-parān vidhātu-kāmaḥ \|*  
*sa nibhṛtam athavā śriyā vihartuṁ*  
*rahasi nilīya rarāja deva eṣaḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jagannātha disappeared to play with Lakṣmī alone, desiring to produce great pain of separation in his devotees living in Puri.**

### Text 3

*atha tad anavalokanāti-duḥkha-*  
*kṣubhitatamāni manāṁsi vibhratas te \|*  
*asita-giri-nivāsino mahānto*  
*bhṛśam atapan prabhu-darśanena hīnāḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Their minds disturbed with sorrow on not seeing the Lord, the devotees living in Puri suffered greatly on being deprived of the Lord’s presence.**

### Text 4

*prabhur api sa śacī-suto’tha duḥkhī*  
*bhṛśam abhavad vikalo na taṁ vilokya \|*  
*prakaṭayati ca tac-chalena vṛndāvana-*  
*ramanī-jana-viprayoga-duḥkham \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu, not seeing Jagannātha, was greatly pained. On this occasion, he manifested the sorrow of the gopīs in separation from Kṛṣṇa in Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 5

*niravadhi hṛdaya-sthitāni vṛndā-*  
*vana-ramaṇī-virahasya duḥkhitāni \|*  
*anubhavati sa tac-chalena labdhā-*  
*vasaram udeti hi cetaso vikāraḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He experienced the sorrow in the gopīs’ hearts constantly. When the opportunity arose in this condition, he experienced transformations in his heart.**

### Text 6

*niravadhi-galad-aśruṇo’vatārair*  
*urasi sasambhṛta-hāra-vibhramāḍhyaḥ \|*  
*kraśimabhir avaśiṣṭa-śiṣṭa-nāmā cira-*  
*virahād viṣasāda gauracandraḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra appeared to wear a necklace of constantly flowing tears on his chest. Becoming thin, present only by his name, he became despondent because of the long separation.**

### Text 7

*vikirati bahu-dīrgham uṣṇam uccaiḥ*  
*śvasita-samīraṇam ambu locanābhyām \|*  
*sad-aruṇa-kamala-dvayāruṇābhyāṁ*  
*kṛśa-tanur anvaham evam eva bhūtaḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He became thinner by the day, sprinkling tears from his pink lotus eyes while exhaling hot wind in long sighs.**

### Text 8

*asita-giri-pater adarśanena*  
*dviguṇita-duḥkha-davānalaḥ kṛpābdhiḥ \|*  
*kiyad iva sa jagāma tatra gopī-*  
*pati-vijayaṁ parilocya citta-dhairyam \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the forest fire of sorrow doubled by not seeing Jagannātha, the ocean of mercy attained some peace of mind on considering that the Lord would return.**

### Text 9

*sulalita-muralī-karaḥ sa dolām*  
*atimadhurām adhiruhya rājamānaḥ \|*  
*niravadhi vara-vāra-nāgarīṇāṁ*  
*naṭana-kalā-kuṭukī trisandhyam eva \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, with a flute in his hand, mounting an attractive swing, viewed the artistic dancing of the best temple women at the three junctures of the day.**

### Text 10

*vilasati paṭaha-prakṛṣṭa-bherī-*  
*madhura-mṛdaṅga-vibhaṅga-ramya-gītaiḥ \|*  
*niravadhi sumanaḥ-samūha-vṛṣṭyā*  
*guru-dhavalī-kṛta-veśma-madhya-bhūmau \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He enjoyed nicely with charming songs, while sweet mṛdaṅgas and other drums sounded. The floor of the room became white from the constant showers of flowers.**

### Text 11

*iti viraha viṣaṇṇa-citta-vṛttir*  
*nija-jana-vīkṣaṇa-kiñcid ātta-dhairyaḥ \|*  
*niravadhi virudan vimukta-kaṇṭhaṁ*  
*kati divasāni nināya gauracandraḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His heart despondent because of separation, Gauracandra passed his day crying loudly and incessantly and attained some relief by seeing his companions.**

### Text 12

*atha nija-caraṇāmbujaika-bhaktaiḥ*  
*saha sa tu gaura-śaśī samudyato’bhūt \|*  
*racayitum abhimārjanāṁ samantāt*  
*prathitavato bhavanasya guṇḍiceti \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra then decided to thoroughly clean the place known as Guṇḍicā along with the devotees dedicated to his lotus feet.**

### Text 13

*atha sakala-janaiś cakāra pūrve*  
*’hani śacitanujo vidhāya yuktim \|*  
*jhaṭiti rucira-mārjanī-samūham*  
*udita-manā bhavanasya mārjanārtham \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the day previous to the cleaning, Mahāprabhu, engaging all the devotees, joyfully gathered brooms for cleaning the place.**

### Text 14

*atha rajanī-virāma-kāla-pūrvaṁ*  
*rabhasa-vaśād udiyāya talpa-madhyāt \|*  
*vimala-salila-sañcayair vidhātuṁ*  
*snapanam atho bhagavān samudyato’bhūt \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The next day before the dawn, he rose quickly from bed, eager to take his bath with pure water.**

### Text 15

*vimala-surabhi-śītala-vāri-vṛndaiḥ*  
*snapanam athaiṣa vidhāya celam anyat \|*  
*sadaruṇam abhajat yathā sumerur*  
*niviḍam upāśliṣad utsukena sandhyām \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He took his bath with pure, fragrant, cool water and dressed himself in a fresh piece of red cloth. He was like Sumeru eagerly and tightly embracing the (red) twilight,**

### Text 16

*surucira-kaṭi-sūtrakeṇa baddhā*  
*vasanam atīva dṛḍhaṁ mahā-kṛpābdhiḥ \|*  
*malaya-ruha-viśeṣakaṁ vidhāya*  
*śriyam atinirbhara-sundarīm avāpa \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of mercy tightly tied another piece of cloth around his waist. Applying tilaka with sandalwood, he appeared most beautiful.**

### Text 17

*atha bahir upagatya sarva-lokān*  
*aruṇa-kaṭākṣa-taraṅgitena dṛṣṭvā \|*  
*nija-pura upanīya mārjanīnāṁ*  
*śatam adadāt kramataḥ pṛthak pṛthak saḥ \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Going outside, seeing all the people with the waves of glances from his red eyes, he brought a hundred brooms in front him, and distributed them one by one.**

### Text 18

*prabhu-caraṇa-payoja-bhakta-vargaḥ*  
*sa ca sukha-bhūruha-mañjarīm ivaitām \|*  
*prabhu-kara-kamalād āvāpya cārvīṁ*  
*sapadi raho’timārjanīṁ nananda \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Receiving the attractive brooms, like branches from the tree of happiness, from the lotus hands of the Lord, his dedicated devotees immediately felt great happiness.**

### Text 19

*atha mada-kari-rāja-rāji-gāmī*  
*kanaka-mahīdhra ivāti-jaṅgamo’sau \|*  
*parama-rabhasa-lola-citta-khelas*  
*tvaritam adhāvata mādhurī-dhurīṇaḥ \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Moving like a herd of mad elephants, like a mobile gold mountain, filled with sweetness, his heart bursting with joy, the Lord ran swiftly.**

### Text 20

*cira-samaya-niruddha-śīghra-muktaḥ*  
*pramada-karīva-niraṅkuśo’bhidhāvan \|*  
*pada-kamala-vihāra-bhūri-bhārair*  
*avani-talaṁ taralīcakāra śaśvat \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Running like a mad elephant suddenly released after being chained for a long time, he shook the ground with the heavy steps of his lotus feet.**

### Text 21

*druta-gatir atha guṇḍicālayasya*  
*prabhu-vara-gamya-samīpam utka-cittaḥ \|*  
*sukha-jaladhim ivāviśat puram tac*  
*cira-samayena tu te samīpam īyuḥ \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Going quickly with anxious heart, he came to Guṇḍicā, which was like an ocean of happiness, and entered the place. The devotees arrived later.**

### Text 22

*prathamam ayam atīva-harṣa-pūrṇaḥ*  
*puram abhivśya nijair janais tadaiva \|*  
*ita ita upagṛhya mārjanīṁ tāṁ*  
*sapadi mamārja pṛthak pṛthak krameṇa \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Filled with great joy, he entered the place and, with his devotees, holding brooms, quickly cleaned the place bit by bit.**

### Text 23

*atha yugapad ayaṁ pramārjanotko*  
*jana-nicayaḥ prabhu-kīrtanātimugdhaḥ \|*  
*anugṛham anubhitti cānv alindaṁ*  
*tv anuvaḍabhi pramamārja mārjanībhiḥ \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees were enthusiastic to clean and to hear the Lord’s kīrtana simultaneously. With the brooms they cleaned each room, each wall, each veranda and each upper room.**

### Text 24

*prabhu-vadana-nirīkṣaṇena mugdhā*  
*rahasi ca kecana mārjanīṁ gṛhītvā \|*  
*nayana-jala-jhareṇa dhauta-dehāś*  
*ciram iva vismṛta-mārjana-kriyāḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Joyful on seeing the Lord’s face, some devotees in private, washing their bodies with torrents of tears, remained motionless for a long time, forgetting to sweep the rooms.**

### Text 25

*supulakam api kecid apīśa-sūkti-*  
*śravaṇa-pareṇa hṛdā vinidritāṅgāḥ \|*  
*gṛham api ca tathaiva mārjayantaḥ*  
*kṛtam api karma na cāvidan vimugdhāḥ \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With hairs standing on end, some devotees, their minds absorbed in hearing the Lord’s beautiful words, lost control of their limbs and, though cleaning the rooms, in bewilderment did not know what they were doing.**

### Text 26

*prabhur api parama-praharṣa-mugdhas*  
*tvam ita itas tatas tatas tvam \|*  
*sulalitam iti mārjayeti lokān*  
*adiśad alaṁ sukhitān muhuḥ prakurvan \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord in great joy ordered the devotees, “You clean over there. You clean here. Clean nicely.” This made them very happy.**

### Text 27

*prabhu-vacana-vilāsate yad ete*  
*vidadhati karma tatas tato nikāmam \|*  
*dviguṇitam alabhanta saukhya-bhāraṁ*  
*na ca paritṛptim āptir ābabhūva \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Their joy doubled when the Lord gave directions on how to clean. They could not be fully satisfied however.**

### Text 28

*prabhur api ca vilambitena yo yaḥ*  
*purata upaiti sa tasya tasya pṛṣṭhe \|*  
*praṇaya-rasa-bhareṇa mārjanībhir*  
*bahutara-gāḍham atikrudhā jaghāna \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In anger and affection, with brooms he heavily beat on the back those who came late.**

### Text 29

*sa tu jana-nicayaś ca mārjanīnāṁ*  
*dṛḍhatara-ghāta-rujāpi saukhyam āyāt \|*  
*pariṇatir iyam eva hārda-rāśer*  
*yad alaghu duḥkham api priyaṁ tanoti \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**But these devotees felt happiness in the pain arising from being heavily beaten by the brooms. This is the effect of great affection. Great suffering gives pleasure.**

### Text 30

*kṣaṇam api bhagavān svayaṁ vidhatte*  
*sulalita-mārjanam ūrjita-praharṣaḥ \|*  
*kṣaṇam api ca vilokate’nya-karma*  
*kṣaṇam api ca kārayati priyair nideśaiḥ \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord himself cleaned joyfully for a short time. Sometimes he watched and sometimes he engaged others in actions by his orders.**

### Text 31

*sakala-jana-samīpam eva gacchann*  
*atiśaya-harṣa-bharaṁ cakāra teṣām \|*  
*smita-vacana-nirīkṣaṇābhimarśaiḥ*  
*śamita-samasta-śugaugha-datta-harṣaiḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Approaching all the devotees, he made them extremely blissful by his smiles, words, glances, and touch, which replaced all sorrow with joy.**

### Text 32

*svayam api katibhir janaiḥ sa siṁhā-*  
*sanam abhito’bhita eka-datta-cittaḥ \|*

### Text 86

*atha vijaya-rasotsuko niśānte*  
*parihita-sannahanocita-prakāśaḥ \|*  
*avataraṇa-miṣeṇa nīlacandro*  
*rucira-mahāsanato gireḥ śaśīva \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Filled with the rasa of his victorious passage, at the end of night, Jagannātha manifested his form covered in battle gear. He desired to descend from his seat, like the moon descending on the western horizon.**

### Text 87

*viracita-rucirāvatāra-madhye*  
*sahaja-padād vijayī sa gauracandram \|*  
*kanaka-mayam iva kṣiti-kṣid-agryaṁ*  
*nija-purataḥ sthitam eva manyate sma \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While descending attractively with his feet, victorious Jagannātha saw Gauracandra as the best golden mountain, situated in front of him.**

### Text 88

*atha dharaṇiṣu kramād upetaḥ*  
*kaśipu-cayair vihitāplutiḥ samantāt \|*  
*pratibham iva śaśī vrajan vireje*  
*dyuti-samudāya-vidūritāndhakāraḥ \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he descending using many pillows, Jagannātha appeared like the moon destroying darkness with his light, spreading to all the stars.**

### Text 89

*kramata ita itaḥ padāni jiṣṇuḥ*  
*kaśipuṣu nikṣipati kṣaṇād athaiṣaḥ \|*  
*druta-surapati-ratna-sāgarormi-*  
*pracaya-ruciṁ vijigāya tat prakāmam \|\|89\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he placed his feet on the pillows, he defeated the beauty of waves in an ocean of liquid sapphires.**

### Text 90

*kaṭi-taṭa-paribaddha-paṭṭa-ḍora-*  
*dvitaya-vijṛmbhita-sevakāvahṛṣṭaḥ \|*  
*sa jayati kim u nābhi-padma-nāla-*  
*dvaya-ja-vidhātṛ-sabhā rahaḥ samantāt \|\|90\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While he gave joy to his many servants, who clustered around two silk ropes tied around his waist, it appeared that he by himself conquered the assembly of Prajāpatis (the servants) who had arising from two stems (the ropes) in his lotus navel.**

### Text 91

*upari paridhṛtātapatra-vṛndair*  
*mukha-śaśi-sevana-tat-parendu-rūpaiḥ \|*  
*niravadhi sumanaḥ-samūha-vṛṣṭyā*  
*sita-raṇa-bhūr api nīla-śaila-nāthaḥ \|\|91\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jagannātha became a white battlefield by the constant showers of flowers and the many umbrellas held above Jagannātha’s head, like many moons serving his moon-like face.**

### Text 92

*anusarati puro yathāsitenduḥ*  
*kim api tathāpasaraty asau śacījaḥ \|*  
*abhimukham abhigacchatos tayos*  
*tat sulalita-kandūka-vibhramaṁ babhāra \|\|92\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As Jagannātha, the black moon, moved forward, the son of Śacī, facing him, retreated. He appeared like beautiful ball moving about.**

### Text 93

*asita-giri-patir yathā sva-bhṛtyaiḥ*  
*parikalitaḥ sa tathaiva gauracandraḥ \|*  
*surapati-maṇi-hema-ratna-bhāsau*  
*jana-caya-lakṣyatanū babhūvatus tau \|\|93\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As Jagannātha was surrounded by his servants, Gauracandra was surrounded by his servants. To the people they appeared to shine like a sapphire and golden jewel.**

### Text 94

*kvacid ayam api gauracandra-bhāsā*  
*bhavati suvarṇa-rucis tathaiva so’pi \|*  
*jagati tad ubhayoḥ sitetarādreḥ*  
*parivṛḍhatā paritaḥ prakāśitāsīt \|\|94\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sometimes Jagannātha assumed Gauracandra’s golden complexion and sometimes Gauracandra assumed a black complexion. From Puri, the two manifested their power throughout the universe.**

### Text 95

*gajapati-kara-daṇḍa-khaṇḍa-khaṇḍīkṛta*  
*sakalārir aśeṣa-vighna-hartā \|*  
*nṛpati-gaṇapatiḥ pratāparudro*  
*ravir iva yaḥ pratapaty asau sadaiva \|\|95\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pratāparudra crushed his opponents by his elephant trunk. He destroyed unlimited obstacles. Like the sun he shone at all times.**

### Text 96

*sa tu laghutara-sevakāyamānaḥ*  
*kara-kalitāmala-haima-mārjanīkaḥ \|*  
*kim api tad-ubhayor vihāra-līlāṁ*  
*parikalayan gata-sarva-ceṣṭa āsīt \|\|96\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(yugmakam)**  
**But he became a minor servant and held in his hand a golden broom. Seeing the pastimes of Jagannātha and Gaurāṅga, he became stunned with immobility.**

### Text 97

*satatam ubhayatojjvalan-maholkā*  
*vividha-mahātapa-vismṛta-kṣapāntaḥ \|*  
*paṭaha-paṭala-maṇḍu-ḍiṇḍimādyair*  
*ati-mahimā-samayo’yam evam āsīt \|\|97\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Like the night which is forgotten when two meteors blaze brilliantly in the sky constantly, this occasion became grand with the sounds of drums and various instruments.**

### Text 98

*iti ratha-nikaṭaṁ vrajan vireje*  
*parikalayan purataḥ sa gauracandraḥ \|*  
*ita ita ita etad etad etat*  
*parikalanīyam itaḥ svabhṛtya-nādaiḥ \|\|98\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Coming near the cart and looking at the Lord, Gauracandra shone as his servants exclaimed, “He can be seen from here, from here.”**

### Text 99

*atha ratham adhiruhya nīla-śaila-*  
*prabhur asakau rasa-kautukī rarāja \|*  
*pariṇata iva pūrva-parvatānte*  
*madhu-madhuro jaladātyaye himāṁśuḥ \|\|99\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Ascending the cart, Jagannātha shone with joyful rasa. He was like the sweet moon rising over the eastern mountain, in a cloudless sky.**

### Text 100

*iti pathi vihite’pi sad-vihāre*  
*ratham adhirohati nīla-śaila-nāthe \|*  
*nija-jana-nicayaiḥ sa gauracandraḥ*  
*snapana-vihāra-cikīrṣayā jagāma \|\|100\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After Jagannātha ascended to the car on the road, Gauracandra with his devotees desired to go for bathing pastimes.**

### Text 101

*atha laghu-vihitāvagāha-ramyā*  
*prabhu-purato militā babhūvur ete \|*  
*svayam api vihitāplavaḥ prakāmaṁ*  
*malayaja-paṅka-cayair lilepa tāṁs tān \|\|101\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Attractive after their quick bath, the devotees gathered in front of Mahāprabhu. He also bathed in delight and anointed them with sandalwood paste.**

### Text 102

*prathamam asakṛd advitīya-bhāvo-*  
*rasi rasikaḥ kara-pallavena hṛṣṭaḥ \|*  
*malaya-ruha-rasair lilepa tasya*  
*dviguṇitam utsukayan saromavṛndam \|\|102\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**First, the crown jewel of rasa, overjoyed, applied sandalwood on Advaita’s chest, making his hairs stand on end with double bliss.**

### Text 103

*tad anu ca bhuvi nārada-svarūpaṁ*  
*dvija-kula-candramasaṁ mahānubhāvam \|*  
*tad anu tad-anujaṁ tatas tathānyān*  
*kramata ito malayodbhavair lilepa \|\|103\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He then applied sandalwood on Śrīvāsa, the form of Nārada, the moon in his dynasty, and then his younger brother Śrīrāma. Then in order he applied sandalwood on the others.**

### Text 104

*tad anu sakala-gāyanān viśeṣaṁ*  
*pratijanam evam uraḥ-sthale kṛpāluḥ \|*  
*pramada-bhara-bharālasāṅga-yaṣṭir*  
*naṭana-kalākulito lilepa tais taiḥ \|\|104\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His body was slack from great bliss and trembling. Merciful Gauracandra applied sandalwood on each of the singers’ chests.**

### Text 105

*ye te śrīvāsa-rāmau svara-vijita-pikau vāsudevo mukundaḥ*  
*śrīmad-dāmodarākhyo yatir iti jagati khyātavān prema-puñjaḥ \|*  
*śrīmad-vakreśvaraś ca prathita-guṇa-gaṇaḥ śrīla-dāmodaro’sau*  
*bhūmī-gīrvāṇa-mukhyas tad anu sumadhuraḥ ko’pi nārāyaṇākhyaḥ \|\|105\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He applied sandalwood on Śrīvāsa and Śrīrāma, endowed with voices like cuckoos, on Vāsudeva Datta, on Mukunda, on the famous renunciate Dāmodara, filled with prema, on Vakreśvara, filled with good qualities, on Dāmodara, the best of brāhmaṇas, and on the sweet devotee named Nārāyaṇa.**

### Text 106

*śrīkānto makaradhvajaḥ sumadhuraḥ śuddhaḥ śubhānandakaḥ*  
*kāśīnāthaka-vallabhau ca haridāsākhyo raghuḥ śuddha-dhīḥ \|*  
*etāṁs tān sahasaiva candana-rasair liptvā sa svayaṁ śrīmatā*  
*gaurāṅgena dṛḍhaṁ nibadhya vasanaṁ śrīmat-kaṭī-rodhasi \|*  
*ājānu-dvaya-lambi-pīvara-bhuja-dvandvena mandollasad-*  
*romāñcāñcita-vigraheṇa paramāviṣṭena tair niryaye \|\|106\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauraṅga applied sandalwood on Śrīkānta, sweet Makaradhvaja, pure Ṣubhānanda, Kāśīnātha Miśra, Vallabhācārya, Hari-dāsa, and intelligent Raghu. He firmly tied a cloth around his waist. With his two thick arms which reached his knees, his hairs standing on end in complete bliss, he then departed with the devotees.**

### Text 107

*amanda-karatālaka-prakara-ramya-san-mandirā-*  
*svalaṅkṛtakarāmbujāḥ pulaka-vṛnda-sāndrāṅgakāḥ \|*  
*amī tad anu satvaraṁ pratipadaṁ padaṁ nirbharaṁ*  
*skhalat-pada-saroruhāḥ sukha-samudra-magnā yayuḥ \|\|107\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With shining karatālas and attractive cymbals in their lotus hands, with hairs standing on end, with lotus feet stumbling at every step, drowning in an ocean of happiness, they followed Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 108

*govindas tvaritaṁ sametya nitarāṁ naikaṭyam āsāditaḥ*  
*pārśvasthaḥ sukha-sāgareṣu satataṁ majjan pratasthe tataḥ \|*  
*ete ye ca samāgatāḥ pratipadollāsākulāḥ śrī-yujo*  
*naiṣāṁ harṣa-sudhāmbudhir niravadhir brahmāṇḍa-madhye’ñcitum \|\|108\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Govinda came quickly, approached close, remained beside the Lord, sank in an ocean of bliss, and then departed. The beautiful devotees gathered together, blissful at every step. Their ocean of bliss, without limit, could not be measured within the universe.**

### Text 109

*atha mada-mṛgendrālī-līlā-vilāsi-pada-kramaḥ*  
*pramada-vigalad-gharma-snāna-pracāyaka-pada-kramaḥ \|*  
*anupama-sudhārohādromodgamāñcita-vigrahaḥ*  
*pathi laghu yayau gauras tejo-nirasta-ravi-grahaḥ \|\|109\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Walking gracefully like a lion, with feet bathed in perspiration of joy, with hairs standing on end because of incomparable bliss, Gaurāṅga, brighter than the sun, went quickly on the path.**

### Text 110

*ratham abhi baladevasyāgrato gauracandraḥ*  
*pramada-mada-manojñaḥ śrī-virājat-tanūkaḥ \|*  
*druta-kanaka-mahīdhrair daṇḍavad bhūmi-pṛṣṭhaṁ*  
*saha nayana-jalena premataḥ prāpa bhūyaḥ \|\|110\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Attractive with joy and intoxication, with shining body, Gauracandra came in front of the chariot of Baladeva and offered respects on the ground like a stick, like a toppled golden mountain, flowing with tears of prema.**

## Chapter Sixteen

### Text 1

*atha pulaka-samūha-bhrājamānaṁ prarohan*  
*mukuka-kula-virājat-kāñcana-dru-prakāśam \|*  
*madhuram apaṭhad uccaiḥ pīnam unnīya bāhuṁ*  
*kanaka-girir ivāsau śṛṅga-lagnāntarīkṣaḥ \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu, like a golden mountain whose peak touched the sky, sprouting shining hairs on end, which made him look like a shining golden tree with buds, raising his broad arms, began to recite verses in a sweet voice.**

### Text 2

*jayati jayati devo devakī-nandano’sau*  
*jayati jayati kṛṣṇo vṛṣṇi-vaṁśa-pradīpaḥ \|*  
*jayati jayati megha-śyāmalaḥ komalāṅgo*  
*jayati jayati pṛthvī-bhāva-nāśo mukundaḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All glories unto the Supreme Lord, who is known as the son of Devaki! All glories to the Lord, who is known as the light of the dynasty of Vrishni! All glories to the Supreme Lord, whose bodily luster is like that of a new cloud, and whose body is as soft as a lotus flower! All glories to the Supreme Lord, who appeared on this planet to deliver the world from the burden of demons, and who can offer liberation to everyone!**

### Text 3

*jayati jana-nivāso devakī-janma-vādo*  
*yadu-vara-pariṣat svair dorbhir asyann adharmam \|*  
*sthira-cara-vṛjina-ghnaḥ su-smita- śrī-mukhena*  
*vraja-pura-vanitānāṁ vardhayan kāma-devam \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Œro K???a lives among the cowherd men and the Yâdavas, and is conclusively both the son of Devako and Yaœodâ. He is the guide of the Yadu dynasty and the cowherd men, and with His mighty arms He kills everything inauspicious, in Vraja, Mathurâ and Dvârakâ. By His presence He destroys all things inauspicious for all living entities, moving and inert, and the suffering of separation of the inhabitants of Vraja and Dvârakâ. His blissful smiling face always increases the desires of the** ***gopîs*** **of V?ndâvana and women of Mathurâ and Dvârakâ. He remains eternally in this situation. SB 10.90.48**

### Text 4

*nāhaṁ vipro na ca narapatir nāpi vaiśyo na śūdro*  
*nāhaṁ varṇī na ca gṛhapatir no vanastho yatir vā \|*  
*kintu prodyan-nikhila-paramānanda-pūrṇāmṛtābdher*  
*gopī-bhartuḥ pada-kamalayor dāsa-dāsānudāsaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am not a brāhmaṇa, I am not a kṣatriya, I am not a vaiśya or a śūdra. Nor am I a brahmacārī, a householder, a vānaprastha, or a sannyāsī. I identify myself only as the servant of the servant of the servant of the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the maintainer of the gopīs. He is like an ocean of nectar, filled with the highest bliss.**

### Text 5

*iti naṭana-kalādau śrīla-vṛndāvanendoḥ*  
*parama-mahimavattvaṁ nirbharārto nirūpya \|*  
*atiśaya-karuṇārdraḥ prema-bhaktiṁ vitanvann*  
*ayam atimadhurāṅgo harṣa-pūrṇo babhūva ha \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While dancing, Mahāprabhu, soft with great mercy, spreading prema-bhakti as he described the great glory of Kṛṣṇa with pain in his heart, with intensely sweet limbs, became filled with bliss.**

### Text 6

*āsphoṭya vāma-kara-kakṣa-taṭīṁ kareṇa*  
*rajyad-vapur-madhura-komalatātiramyaḥ \|*  
*līlā-vilola-mukha-candra-mayūkha-rociḥ*  
*śrīmac-chaṭājhalāmalāyita-dik-samūhaḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Waving his left hand, attractive with his sweet, tender red body, spreading rays from his moving moon-like face, he illuminated all direction with his effulgence.**

### Text 7

*uccair muhur jaya jayeti vimukta-kaṇṭham*  
*uccārayan saha tanūruha-vṛnda-harṣaiḥ \|*  
*muṣṭi-prameya-tanu-madhya-vilāsa-baddha-*  
*raktāmbara-dyuti-viḍambita-bandhu-jīvaḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Constantly chanting “Jaya jaya” in a loud voice with abandon, with hairs standing on end, he conquered the bandhu-jīva flower with the beauty of his red cloth bound around his charming waist, slender as a fist.**

### Text 8

*śrīmad-vilocana-jalāpluta-gaura-dehaḥ*  
*pratyagra-gharma-kaṇikā-khacitāsya-candraḥ \|*  
*uddāma-tāṇḍava-kalākulitāṅga-bhaṅgaḥ*  
*śrīmān atha svajana-madhyam alañcakāra \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With tears flowing over his golden body, with fresh drops of perspiration imbedded on his moon-like face, with his body stretched out by his intense dancing, he ornamented the assembly of devotees.**

### Text 9

*auttuṅgena nabha-sthalaṁ taralayan mārtaṇḍa-bimbaṁ muhuś*  
*cumban deva-sabhā-sabhājana-vidhiṁ sampādayan nirbharam \|*  
*brahmāṇḍāntara-saṁsthitasya nayanānandotsavotsāhakaḥ*  
*sāṭopaṁ mura-vairiṇo vijayate lakṣmī-mayaḥ syandanaḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Комментарий:**

Shaking the sky with his great height, kissing the disk of the sun constantly, making the assembly of devatās offer the greatest respect, producing the greatest enthusiasm and joy for the eyes for all persons within the universe, the chariot of Jagannātha, filled with the wealth and beauty of Lakṣmī, remained glorious with pride.

### Text 10

*kailāsaṁ namayann aśeṣa-vidhinā meruṁ hasan nirbharaṁ*  
*sotkaṇṭhaṁ kila vindhyakaṁ vikalayan gaurī-guruṁ glāpayan \|*  
*anyaḥ ko’py adhunāvanau śikhariṇāṁ rājeva kiṁ nirmito*  
*dhātrā syandana ity asau muraripu-śrī-mūrti-pīyūṣa-bhṛt \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Did the creator turn this chariot, carrying the nectar of Kṛṣṇa’s form, into the king of all mountains on earth—forcing Mount Kailāsa to bow down with all reverence, laughing loudly at Meru, agitating Vindhya with longing, and making Himālaya waste away?**

### Text 11

*upat-pakṣmākṣi-saroruhāñjali-puṭair nīlādri-cūḍāmaṇeḥ*  
*śrī-mūrti-cchuritāmṛtāni pibatām ullāsa-dhanyātmanām \|*  
*niṣpandaṁ pulakāvalī-vilasatām ānanda-mandākinī-*  
*kallolaiḥ kila tatra tatra bhavatām āsīn mahān utsavaḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This was a great festival for the people blessed with joy, who drank the nectar mixed with the Lord’s form with the cupped hands of their wide-open lotus eyes, whose hairs playfully moved about in the waves of the Mandākinī River of bliss.**

### Text 12

*bhūyo bhūyaḥ samantāt sarabhasa-manasām āgatānāṁ viśeṣaṁ*  
*tat-tat-sīmantinīnām alika-vikalitaiḥ kamra-sindūra-pūraiḥ \|*  
*saindūrīkartum āsīd ratha-parisara-bhūś cakra-niṣpīḍanena*  
*kṣubdhāpi prāyaśaḥ sā pramudita-manasātmānam utkaṇṭhiteva \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The earth around the chariot, agitated by the pounding of the wheels, developed longing for the Lord. Its mind became filled with great joy, turning its body (the ground) red because of the abundant sindhura in the part of the hair of women who had come there with eager minds.**

### Text 13

*nṛtyantaṁ gauracandraṁ caraṇa-sarasija-dvandva-vinyāsa-ramyaṁ*  
*dṛṣṭvā dṛṣṭvā prakāmaṁ sarabhasa-manaso bhrātaras te rathasthāḥ \|*  
*bhūyo’haṁpūrvikābhiḥ prasṛmara-gatayaḥ kautukenāgrato’mī*  
*jaṅghālās tatra tatra pramada-mada-bharān nartanaṁ kurvateva \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The deities on the chariots, with eager minds, constantly gazed at Gauracandra as he danced with attractive steps of his lotus feet. The expansive crowd, pushing themselves in front because of ego, running forwards, danced in joy.**

### Text 14

*ārundhan dik-karīndrotkara-kara-vivaraṁ sāmbu cakṣuḥ-sahasraṁ*  
*kurvann airāvaṇeśaṁ palita-vilasitā devatās tatra kurvan \|*  
*setu-bhrāntiṁ payodheḥ punar api racayann evam ujjṛmbhate’sau*  
*pronmīlac-cakra-cakroddalana-visṛmaroddhḥta-dhūli-pravāhaḥ \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The clouds of dust, which arose and spread from the turning of the shining wheels, blocked the nostrils of the elephants of the directions, made thousand eyed Indra, lord of Airāvata, shed tears, turned the hair of the devatās white, and caused the ocean to think there was a bridge over the ocean, as the dust spread everywhere.**

### Text 15

*kūrmo marma-vyatho’bhūt phaṇi-patir asakau śīrṣataḥ śīrṣa-madhyaṁ*  
*bhūyo bhūyo dharitrīṁ nayati nata-śirā jīrṇa-maṇḍaṁ babhūva \|*  
*velālolaiḥ payobhiś ciram iva jaladhiḥ kṣīṇa-maryāda āsīt*  
*tatrautsukyena nīla-kṣiti-dhara-tilake prasthite guṇḍicāyām \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As Jagannātha happily went to Guṇḍicā, Kūrma felt pain in his body, and Śeṣa, with bowed heads, placing the earth on one hood after the other, became fatigued. The ocean overflowed its shores with huge, agitating waves.**

### Text 16

*rājantāṁ tatra tās tāḥ surapati-pariṣat-kamra-lakṣmīr adho’dhaḥ*  
*kṛtvā kṛtvā murārer atha ratha-vijaye bhūtayo ratna-bhājaḥ \|*  
*tat tādṛg bhūṣaṇāḍhyaḥ svayam api bhagavān samyag ujjṛmbhatāṁ sa*  
*śrīmān kiṁ tv eṣa nṛtyann akhila-jana-mano-ruddha-gaurāṅga-candraḥ \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May the collection of gems manifested during the chariot procession of Jagannātha continually shine, defeating the beautiful wealth of Indra and his followers among all shining treasures. May the beautiful Lord, the ornament of ornaments, expand his fame unlimitedly. But moon-like Gaurāṅga danced, and paralyzed the minds of all people.**

### Text 17

*śacī-suta-kalā-nidhiḥ kim api sāṅga-bhaṅgaṁ muhur*  
*vilāsa-caraṇa-kramonmathita-ramya-paṅkeruhaḥ \|*  
*nirantara-dig-antara-cchuritam acchakānti-cchaṭā-*  
*mala-jjhala-malāyitaṁ manasi vaḥ samujjṛmbhatām \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of arts, the son of Śacī, was like an agitated lotus as he moved his feet with grace. May that pure dazzling effulgence which tinged all directions continuously increase within your minds.**

### Text 18

*amandatara-mandirāninada-saṅgi-saṅgītaka-*  
*dhvanir dhvanita-dig-vadhū-vadana-paṅkajaiḥ pūjitaḥ \|*  
*vibhidya muhur uccavac carama-khaṇḍa-khaṇḍāntaraṁ*  
*prayāti kati dūrataḥ sa khalu mīyatāṁ kaiḥ punaḥ \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sound of singing accompanied by loud clashes of cymbals was worshipped by the lotus faces of the directions as the sound spread out. That sound pierced the last shell of the universe. Who can measure how far that sound went?**

### Text 19

*muhur madhura-cakravad bhrami-vilolayāśleṣaṇaḥ*  
*parisphurita-dhārayā paridhi-bhūṣitaś candravat \|*  
*vilocana-payo-jharair valayitaiḥ samantād diśāṁ*  
*mukhāni parimārjayan jayati so’tra nṛtyodyame \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whirling about unsteadily like a sweet wheel, like the moon decorated with a halo of sparkling rays, purifying the faces of the directions completely with his tears, Mahāprabhu remained glorious while dancing.**

### Text 20

*jaya jaya jayatv ity uccair nināda-paraḥ śatair*  
*mukhara-mukharī-bhūtāḥ sarvā diśaḥ kim akurvata \|*  
*niravadhi dṛśau tāsu kṣiptvā yad eṣa vilohite*  
*naṭana-kalayā lola-śoṇī-cakāra jagat-talam \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All directions resounded incessantly when he shouted loudly “Jaya, jaya, jayatu.” What else could they do? Glancing with his red eyes in all directions constantly, he made the universe red with eagerness as he danced.**

### Text 21

*mukha-śaśi-samudgīrṇaiḥ phenair hasann iva śāradaṁ*  
*satata-vijitaṁ lakṣmyā lakṣmyākulaṁ hima-dīdhitim \|*  
*pulaka-paṭalair atyudbhinnaiḥ sumerum ivodgatā-*  
*ṅkura-śata-paricchedātītaḥ sa eṣa virājate \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He mocked the autumn moon, faulty with its spots, continually defeating it by the beauty of the foam emanating from his moon-like face. He surpassed Sumeru with a covering of new sprouts, by his body with hairs standing on end intensely.**

### Text 22

*unmīlya prathamaṁ pariplavayatā pakṣmāṇi bhūyaḥ kṣaṇāt*  
*śrīmad-gaṇḍa-taṭīṣu dīrgha-mayatā dhārābhir uccais tataḥ \|*  
*prāpyoraḥ-padavīṁ tridhā prasaratā bhūmau truṭan-mauktika-*  
*śreṇīvat kriyatāṁ sadaiva jagatāṁ harṣaḥ prabhor aśruṇā \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May the universe become joyful by the tears of the Lord, which, becoming visible, first flooded his eyelashes, and then quickly flowed in long, copious streams to the edges of his cheeks. They then reached his chest and in three streams spread to the earth, like pearls from a broken necklace.**

### Text 23

*viśvaṁ plāvayateva tatra luṭhatā bhūmāsu vakṣaḥ-sthale*  
*garbhodyat-kanakāśmaramya-taṭinī-śobhāṁ tiraskurvatā \|*  
*akṣṇor magna-saroja-sundara-saraḥ-śobhena gaura-prabhor*  
*ānandāśru-jhareṇa tena jagatām ānanda ādhīyatām \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May the universe experience bliss by Gaurāṅga’s tears of joy, endowed with the beauty of a lake holding the sunken lotuses of his eyes, which flooded the universe as they fell on the earth, and which derided the beauty of a river attractive with golden stones as the tears fell on his chest**

### Text 24

*gāyadbhir gāyanais taiḥ pramatha-valayite maṇḍale tad-bahiś ca*  
*śrī-kāśī-miśra-mukhyaiḥ parama-sumatibhis tat-padābja-prapannaiḥ \|*  
*hasta-grāhaṁ pramodāt satata-valayite tad-bahiś ca pratāpa-*  
*prāk-śrī-śrī-rudra-deve nibhṛtam ita ito veṣṭite bhāti nāthaḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord shone, surrounded by a first circle of singers, surrounded by a second continuous circle of great devotees surrendered to Mahāprabhu’s lotus feet, including Kāśī-miśra, who held hands in bliss. Outside this circle, King Pratāparudra stood alone.**

### Text 25

*indraḥ kiṁ kim atha vidhiḥ kim īśa-devo*  
*naiveṣāṁ bhavati tadā hy apekṣaṇīyaḥ \|*  
*śrī-gaure naṭana-vilāsa-veśa-ramye*  
*naivāsīt kṣaṇam api pakṣmaṇo nivṛttiḥ \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Indra, Brahmā and Śiva did not experience their bliss. That was to be expected. The eyelids did not close for a moment when Gaurāṅga, attractive in his clothing, danced with grace.**

### Text 26

*ānandena jaḍīkṛte bhuvi ciraṁ stabdhe tathā syandane*  
*śrī-nīlādri-pater upaiti ca sati vyagrībhavadbhir bhṛśam \|*  
*tairataiḥ kara-pallavair nija-nija-kroḍeṣu kṛtvā kiyad*  
*dūre svairam upārpito vijayate śrī-gauracandraḥ prabhuḥ \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When Mahāprabhu lay stunned on the ground in bliss for a long time and the chariot of Jagannātha approached, the affectionate, alarmed devotees put him in their laps with their lotus hands and quickly situated him at some distance. May that Gauracandra be glorious!**

### Text 27

*ānandena jaḍībhavann anupadaṁ huṅkāra-kolāhalair*  
*advaitārpita-pāṇi-pallava-rasa-snigdhoru-vakṣaḥ-sthalaḥ \|*  
*daṇḍākāram itas tato vinipatad-dor-daṇḍa-pāda-dvayor*  
*lāsyollāsa-manoharo vijayate śrī-gauracandraḥ prabhuḥ \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Stunned with bliss by shouting and roaring constantly, embracing to his broad chest with his affectionate budlike hands Advaitācārya, attractive with bliss as he danced, his two feet and rod-like arms falling here and there, may that Gauracandra who appeared like a rod (being stunned), remain glorious.**

### Text 28

*ānandotsāha-mūrcchā-gata iva bhavati spanda-niśvāsa-mande*  
*rohadromāñca-pūrair vikalita-vapuṣānanda-mandīkṛtena \|*  
*svandan-netrāravinda-dvaya-salila-juṣā rudra-devena bhūyaḥ*  
*sānandaṁ sevitāṅghri-dvaya-sarasiruho rājate gauracandraḥ \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Fainting because of great bliss, quivering and breathing faintly, with hairs standing on end because of bliss, with tears streaming from his lotus eyes, his lotus feet served by Pratāparuda in bliss, Gauracandra shone.**

### Text 29

*unmīlan-netra-padme pulaka-paṭalikālola-sarvāṅga-yaṣṭau*  
*niṣṭhīvat-pheṇa-pūrollasita-mukha-śaśi-dyota-nirdhūta-candre \|*  
*sāndrānandāli-mande madhurima-laharī-sindhu-saubhāgya-candre*
