**Перевод:**

**When Gaurāṅga, the moon of good fortune in the waves of the ocean of sweetness, languid with bliss, danced with open lotus eyes, hairs standing on end, and conquered the moon with his moon-like face, shining as it emitted saliva, who in the universe would not become stunned?**

### Text 30

*ānandaṁ netra-randhrair niravadhi paramānanda-sandoha-dhārā-*  
*dhauta-pratyaṅga-lakṣmī-madhurima-vibhavo rāmanīyotka-cittaḥ \|*  
*pītvā pītvā yadāyaṁ naṭana-rasa-dhunī-pūram ullāsa-lolo*  
*nispando bobhavīti prathayati paramānanda-purī saharṣam \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He was a wealth of sweetness in every limb, washed constantly by many streams of intense bliss flowing from his eyes in bliss. With his attractive heart full of desire, when he continually drank the full river of rasa from dancing, he became agitated with joy and frequently trembled in bliss. He was a fortress of the highest bliss.**

### Text 31

*dadhāra kaṭi-sūtarakaṁ prabhur itīha dāmodaraḥ*  
*svarūpa iva tasya kiṁ yati-varo’yam udghuṣyate \|*  
*ya eṣa naṭanotsave hṛdaya-kāya-vāg-vṛttibhiḥ*  
*śacī-sūta-kalā-nidhau praviśatīva sāndrotsukaḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In Puri, the best renunciate, was called Dāmodara because he wore a string around his waist (for his kaupiṇa). With intense desire, he seemed to enter into Mahāprabhu, the ocean of arts, with his body, mind and words as the Lord danced. Thus he was called Svarūpa (the Lord’s very form).**

### Text 32

*unmīlan-makaranda-sundara-pada-dvandvāravindollasad-*  
*vinyāsaḥ kṣitiṣu prakāma-manunā dāmodareṇa prabhuḥ \|*  
*āmugdhaiḥ kara-kuṭmalair ita ito harṣād adho’dho guru-*  
*snehārdreṇa dṛḍhopagūhita-pado nṛtyann asau dṛśyatām \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**See the Lord whose feet are embraced tightly here and there out of joy and intense affection by many bud-like, blissful hands placed low on the ground, as he dances with most intelligent Dāmodara, joyfully stepping on the ground with his beautiful lotus feet flowing with honey.**

### Text 33

*kāśīśvara-prabhṛtayo rabhasena kaśī-*  
*miśraś ca harṣa-bhara-viśramaṇaika-pātram \|*  
*govinda eṣa ca parasparam utka-cittā*  
*dṛbhis tadīya-naṭanāmṛtam ādhayanti \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kāśīsvara and other devotees as well as Kāśī-miśra, a vessel of great joy and repose, and Govinda, all with hearts full of longing, accepted the nectar of his dancing with their eyes.**

### Text 34

*nṛtyan kṣitau samupadiśya nijāṅghri-padmaṁ*  
*dorbhyāṁ sukhena parirabhya vilola-mauliḥ*  
*cumban janaṁ janam abhiprakaṭānurāgo*  
*mūrdhni kṣipan vijayate kanakādri-gauraḥ \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gracefully stepping with his lotus feet as he danced, happily embracing each devotee, shaking his head, kissing each one with great affection, placing his feet on their heads, the golden mountain remains glorious.**

### Text 35

*etad vinā jagati nānyad ihāsti ramyaṁ*  
*śrīmat-sugandhi-guru-kāruṇikaṁ durāpam \|*  
*ity ākalayya naṭane nija-pāda-padmaṁ*  
*hṛdy arpayan vijayate satataṁ ca cumban \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying “There is nothing in this universe as attractive as the Lord, full of beauty, fragrance and great mercy and difficult to achieve” while dancing, he placed his lotus feet on their hearts and constantly kissed them. May he remain glorious.**

### Text 36

*snihyann iva pratipadaṁ hṛdayāntareṣu*  
*kurvann ivākṣi-yugalena pibann ivāsau \|*  
*āsvādayann iva muhur nija-pāda-padmaṁ*  
*nṛtye jayaty avirataṁ kamanīya-gauraḥ \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Affectionate to the Lord’s lotus feet at every moment, holding those feet in his heart, drinking them with his eyes, tasting them constantly, beautiful Gaurāṅga remained glorious while dancing.**

### Text 37

*padāmbhoruha-dvandva-vinyāsane’bhi-*  
*sphuran mādhurī-dhauta-śonābja-śobhaḥ \|*  
*lalad-rāma-rambhā-vilāsāvalamba-*  
*sthaloru-nipīnollasat-śroṇi-bimbaḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He was as brilliant as a red lotus washed with sweetness, which manifested on moving his lotus feet. He had shining hips and a broad chest which supported the pastimes of playful Lakṣmī.**

### Text 38

*samudyaj-javājālakoddāma-raktāṁ-*  
*śukaṁ svaccha-śobhāruṇimnānuraktām \|*  
*trilokīṁ vidhāyodgatānanda-khelaḥ*  
*sphurat-tāṇḍavoddaṇḍa-dor-daṇḍa-līlaḥ \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Playing in bliss, he attracted the three worlds with the pure beauty of his red cloth, bright as a mass of fresh java flowers. He lifted his two arms gracefully as he danced with brilliance.**

### Text 39

*sphuran-muṣṭi-meyāvalagne nitānta-*  
*śrita-śrī-kaṭī-sūtra-kāntyātikāntaḥ \|*  
*guru-sveda-vāri-pravāhāplutora-*  
*sthalīkaḥ sad-uddāma-romāñca-pūraḥ \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He was most attractive with the beautiful cord tightly tied, stuck to his waist, which was as thin as a fist. His body was covered with extreme goose bumps and the plateau of his chest was flooded with streams of intense perspiration.**

### Text 40

*tad-ānanda-dhārāṁ vahan kṣīra-vārāṁ*  
*nidheḥ sānukārāṁ vikāri-pracārām \|*  
*vilolāli-khelā-vilāsākṣi-līlā-*  
*rasaiḥ sādhu kurvan janasyābja-garbham \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He flowed with rivers of bliss, imitating the Milk Ocean, which caused transformations in his body. By relishing playful glances which produced pastimes with the fickle gopīṣ, his eyes became centers of lotuses for his devotees’ vision.**

### Text 41

*alaṅkurvad-ānanda-mūrcchā-prakāśa-*  
*śrita-stambha-romāñca-kampa-prakāśaḥ \|*  
*anirvārya-bhāva-prakāśātireka-*  
*sphurad-deha-kānti-chaṭācchanna-lokaḥ \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He displayed as his ornaments trembling, hairs standing on end, and being stunned, which took shelter of consciousness and fainting (unconsciousness) caused by bliss. His beautiful bodily effulgence, manifesting to the extreme because of insuppressible emotions, covered all people.**

### Text 42

*trilokī-sphurat-kīrti-pīyūṣa-dhāraḥ*  
*prakāśī-kṛta-prema-bhakti-pracāraḥ \|*  
*lasat-tapta-kārtasvara-śrīmad-aṅga-*  
*cchaṭācchanna-lāvaṇya-tāruṇya-bhaṅgaḥ \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sweet streams of his fame spread through the three worlds. He revealed and spread prema-bhakti. He was a reservoir of youth and beauty covered by the glowing, molten golden complexion of his limbs.**

### Text 43

*nadan-mandirāvṛnda-riṅgan-mṛdaṅgaiḥ*  
*samudyan-mahollāsa-pāthodhi-bhaṅgaiḥ \|*  
*muhur gāyanair mugdha-saṅgīta-bhaṅgī-*  
*samutkaṇṭha-kaṇṭhaiḥ sadānanda-saṅgī \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He was constantly in bliss with the sounds of the cymbals and the surging mṛdaṅgas, with the waves in the ocean of increasing bliss, and with the singers expressing longing in the waves of attractive songs.**

### Text 44

*jagannātha-devaṁ vimugdhaṁ sva-lāsayair*  
*vilokyātiharṣāśru-gharmāmbu-hāsaiḥ \|*  
*rasotkarṣato niḥsaha-śrīmad-aṅgaḥ*  
*sadārajyad-ākuñcitāpāṅga-bhaṅgaiḥ \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On seeing beautiful Jagannātha, his limbs became intolerable, because of excellent rasa, with his playful, contracted, red glances, and with dancing, laughing, tears, and perspiration, expressing his joy.**

### Text 45

*purasthena nīlādri-maulīśvareṇa*  
*svālasyāvalokāsthirātyasthireṇa \|*  
*nimeṣaṁ dṛśoḥ kartum apy akṣameṇa*  
*pramattīkṛto bhūri-harṣodgamena \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He became mad, because of the intense arousal of joy, and was not able to tolerate the passing of one moment, just as Jagannātha situated in his house, became completely unsteady on seeing his dancing.**

### Text 46

*vilolānanāmbhoja-līlā-vilāsaḥ*  
*sphurac-chītkṛtodbhāsi-roma-prakāśaḥ \|*

*prado guṇḍicāyāṁ narīnarti nāthaḥ \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His lotus face swaying playfully, his hairs suddenly standing on end, loudly sighing, the Lord danced at the Guṇḍicā temple, as without precedence he distributed prema to the three worlds.**

### Text 47

*vilokyāsya lāsyaṁ lalan-mādhurīkaṁ*  
*kṣamo naiṣa kartuṁ nimeṣau dṛśoḥ kim \|*  
*yad-utphulla-pāthoruhākṣo’yam āsīt*  
*samastātmanā tatra magnaḥ prakāmam \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Mahāprabhu’s sweet, playful dancing, Jagannātha could not blink his eyes for a moment, for that lotus-eyed deity became absorbed fully in Gaurāṅga’s mood with his whole mind.**

### Text 48

*aṅguly-agraiḥ srajam anupamāṁ cakravad bhrāmayitvā*  
*harṣotkarṣāt kṣipati sa tathā maṇḍale tatra nṛtyan \|*  
*icchā-pūrvaṁ yam anu cakame cetasā tasya kaṇṭhe*  
*dūrasthasyāpi ca bata tathā rājate citram etat \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whirling an incomparable garland on the tip of his finger with great joy, he threw it into the crowd while he danced. The garland, which Jagannātha desired in his heart, then shone on the neck of Jagannātha by his desire, though he was situated far away. That was astonishing.**

### Text 49

*ity evaṁ bahudhā vidhāya naṭanaṁ ramyaṁ śacīnandanaḥ*  
*śrī-nīlācala-mauli-nīla-tilakasyāgre pathi premavān \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā tan-mukha-candra-sundara-ruciṁ pīyūṣavac chītalam*  
*ānandāmbunidhau mamajja subhṛśaṁ sārdhaṁ nijāṅghri-priyaiḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus the son of Śacī, full of prema, danced attractively in front of Jagannātha on the road. Seeing the attractive, cool beauty of Jagannātha’s moon-like face, like nectar, he became completely submerged in an ocean of bliss along with his devotees.**

## Chapter Seventeen

### Text 1

*naṭanāntare’tha ghanagharma-vāriṇā*  
*vilasat-tanur-vara-vilohitāṁśukaḥ \|*  
*purato’sitācala-pater muhur babhau*  
*kanakācalo rucira-dhātu-nirjharaḥ \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After dancing, with his body shining with perspiration and red cloth, he shone like a golden mountain gushing with an attractive red mineral waterfall in front of Jagannātha.**

### Text 2

*abhito’bhitaḥ pathi-rathāntarāntare*  
*pratimās tathāsya jagatāṁ pater muhuḥ \|*  
*avalokya tena kanakādri-kāntinā*  
*kim iveśitṛtvam iha tābhya ādadhe \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Constantly seeing the form of Jagannātha here and there on the road, did Gaurāṅga make each of those forms into the Lord?**

### Text 3

*suciraṁ vilasya purato rathasya*  
*sa praviveśa śītala-tala-drumāvaham \|*  
*asitādri-mauli-tilakasya vallabhaṁ*  
*śrama-śāntaye hy upavanaṁ manoramam \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After sporting in front of the chariot for a long time, Mahāprabhu entered an attractive grove, having trees with cool shade, dear to Jagannātha.**

### Text 4

*nava-jāti-kunda-karavīra-yūthikā-*  
*nava-mālikā-lalita-mādhavī-cayaiḥ \|*  
*bakulaiḥ rasāla-śiśubhiś ca campakaiḥ*  
*paritaḥ samāvṛtam amanda-vibhramam \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The intensely beautiful grove was crowded with fresh jasmine, kunda, karavīra, yūthika, nava-mālika, beautiful mādhavī, bakula, young mango and campaka flowers.**

### Text 5

*paritaḥ prasūna-bharam āśliṣaṁs*  
*tathā sarasāṁ vahan sarasa-śīkarotkaram \|*  
*tad-anusaṅgi-gharma-kaṇikāḥ samāharann*  
*abhajat prabhuṁ laghu laghu kṣaṇaṁ marut \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For a short time, a gentle breeze carrying pleasant drops of water, embracing all the flowers, worshipped Mahāprabhu, removing all the drops of perspiration from his body.**

### Text 6

*vanadevatābhir aniśaṁ manoramair*  
*nava-pallavair nava-śirīṣa-cāmaraiḥ \|*  
*laghu-vījyamāna-tanur utsukātmabhiḥ*  
*sadṛśaṁ babhau vihita-gaura-vigrahaḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That golden form shone nicely as the forest devatās with desire gently fanned his body with cāmaras made of fresh śirīṣa flowers and attractive new shoots at every moment.**

### Text 7

*madhurollasad-vadana-dīdhiti-*  
*cchaṭāmṛta-dhārayā snapayatīva kiṁ jagat \|*  
*trividhai\`c ca tāpa-tapanair durāsadair*  
*nahi bādhyatām iti sa gaura-candramāḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking, “The universe should not be afflicted with the three unconquerable miseries,” did the golden moon of Gaurāṅga flood the universe with rivers of sweet effulgence from his sweet, shining, moon-like face?**

### Text 8

*atha kecanāsya jagatāṁ pateḥ priyāḥ*  
*parama-prabhāva-bhara-bhūri-bhūṣitāḥ \|*  
*rasa-sāra-sindhava iva yayuḥ prabhoḥ*  
*pada-paṅkaja-dvayam avekṣituṁ tadā \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some of Jagannātha’s servants, ornamented with highest beauty, like an ocean of excellent rasa, came to see the lotus feet of Mahāprabhu.**

### Text 9

*sa-sanātanānupama-rūpa-rūpiṇaḥ*  
*sva-padābja-bhakti-rasa-sāgara-trayān \|*  
*pradadarśa visphurita-bhāva-vīcibhir*  
*jagad-āplutaṁ vidadhataḥ kṛpā-nidhiḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of mercy, flooding the universe with flashing waves of bhava, gazed intently at the three oceans of bhakti-rasa at his lotus feet-- Sanātana, Anupama and Rūpa.**

### Text 10

*atha te sametya nikaṭaṁ mahāprabhor*  
*anubhāva-sodaratamā iva trayaḥ \|*  
*priya-sodarā vihita-kāku-bhāṣitā*  
*bhṛśam astuvan jalaja-janmanaḥ stavaiḥ \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Approaching Mahāprabhu, like three brothers of anubhāva, the brothers, with humble words, began to praise him extensively with verses recited by Brahmā.**

### Text 11

*atha bhūyaśo galita-netra-vāribhiḥ*  
*pulakotkarair mṛdutayā ca cetasaḥ \|*  
*vivaśā mahāprabhu-samīpam āsthitāḥ*  
*stavanaṁ pracakrur atha vīta-sādhavasāḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Losing their fear, with tears flowing from their eyes, with hairs standing on end, spontaneously, with soft hearts, situated near Mahāprabhu, they recited prayers again.**

### Text 12

*sa niśamya tat-tad-avahitthayā prabhur*  
*nijagāda bhūyaśa idaṁ kṛpā-nidhiḥ \|*  
*ayam eṣa nīla-giri-mauli-candramāḥ*  
*purataḥ sametya kuruta stavaṁ na kim \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing their prayers, the Lord, the ocean of mercy, said, “Has Jagannātha personally come here in secret and recited prayers?”**

### Text 13

*niviḍānurāga-paṭalī-valattara-*  
*draḍhimāna eta iti yāntu vā katham \|*  
*ślathatāṁ tato’dhikam abhiprayatnataḥ*  
*stavanaṁ pracakrur api vīta-sādhvasāḥ \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With fear, they again recited prayers with more effort, for how can attraction which has increased greatly in strength become weakened?**

### Text 14

*vividha-prakāram apanīya sāhasaṁ*  
*na śaśāka vārayitum eṣa tān yadā \|*  
*atiharṣa-vāri-nidhi-pūra-sañcayair*  
*avagāhitā vidadhire tadaiva te \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though trying various means to stop their bold endeavors, he could not prevent their praises. At that time, he submerged them in great waves of intense bliss.**

### Text 15

*na me bhaktaś caturvedī mad-bhaktaḥ śvapaco guruḥ \|*  
*tasmai deyaṁ tato grāhyaṁ sa ca pūjyo yathā hy aham \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If a brāhmaṇa, well-versed in the four Vedas is not a devotee, he is not dear to me, but if a person, though born in a caṇḍāla family, is a devotee then he is very dear to me. Whatever offering that has to be made must be made to him with proper faith. His remnants must be accepted by all, knowing him to be worthy of everyone’s worship just as I am.**

### Text 16

*iti saṁnipaṭhya madhuraṁ mahāprabhuḥ*  
*praṇanāma bhūmiṣu nipatya daṇḍavat \|*  
*tad atipragalbha-manaso na te tato*  
*bhayam āyayuḥ prabala-bhakti-mattayā \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Reciting this verse in a sweet voice, Mahāprabhu offered respects, falling on the ground like a stick. The three brothers, though having very mature minds, became frightened by this display of madness arising from strong bhakti.**

### Text 17

*madhurollasad vada vadeti bhūyaśo*  
*vacanaṁ yad āvarabhavan mahāprabhoḥ \|*  
*dadṛśus tadābhimata-rūpam uttamaṁ*  
*śata-candra-sāndra-kiraṇa-prakāśavat \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When Mahāprabhu repeatedly uttered in sweet joy, “Speak, speak” they saw his pleasing excellent form, shining like the rays of a hundred bright moons.**

### Text 18

*sa tu gauracandra iti nirbharotsuko*  
*dviguṇa-prakāśa-madhu-mādhurī-mayaḥ \|*  
*avadan muhur vada vadeti nirbharaṁ*  
*smita-dīdhiti-snapita-bhūmi-maṇḍalaḥ \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra, filled with intense desire, showing twice as much sweetness, constantly uttered, “Speak, speak.” He bathed the whole earth in the moonlight of his smile.**

### Text 19

*atha te vihāya jalajodbhava-stavaṁ*  
*tṛṇa-sañcayaṁ ca parigṛhya dantakaiḥ \|*  
*adhikaṇṭham abhinibadhya vāsaso’*  
*ñcalam utsukā vidadhiretarāṁ stutim \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up recitation of Brahmā’s prayers, the three brothers took grass in their teeth, and, pulling their clothing up to their throats, eagerly began reciting other verses.**

### Text 20

*sva-mano’nukūlam abhivāñchita-pradaṁ*  
*vinipaṭhya gopa-ramaṇī-janoditam \|*  
*vidadhuḥ stavaṁ nayana-nīra-bhūṣitāḥ*  
*sukha-sāgare parimamajjur apy amī \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Decorated with tears, they recited verses spoken by the gopīs, which were sympathetic to their own mood and able to bestow what they desired. They drowned in an ocean of happiness.**

### Text 21

*iti nirbharaṁ parama-kāku-bhāṣitair*  
*madhuraṁ sudhāmayam ivākalayya saḥ \|*  
*bhṛśam ānayānaya vidhīyatāṁ drutaṁ*  
*sumahā-prasāda iti saspṛho’bhavat \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the sweet nectar of the verses recited in humble voices, Mahāprabhu, with desire, said repeatedly, “Quickly, bring the mahāprasādam.”**

### Text 22

*atha te padāmbuja-yugasya sannidhau*  
*kṣiti-mūlam adhyatiśaya-praveśitāḥ \|*  
*nipatanta eva nayanāmbu-nirjharaiḥ*  
*paridhauta-sarva-tanavaḥ samāsata \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With tears washing their bodies, in an extreme condition, they fell on the ground near the Lord’s lotus feet.**

### Text 23

*atha sa prasādita-mahā-prasādako*  
*lalitair ghasābhidha-ghaṭais tribhis tataḥ \|*  
*madhurollasad-vadana-candra-sundaro*  
*ruruce vibhur nija-jana-priyaṅkaraḥ \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord who is affectionate to his devotees, with his moon-like face shining sweetly, giving them mahāprasādam from three beautiful pots named ghasa, shone brightly.**

### Text 24

*atha te’pi nirvṛta-hṛdo manorathā-*  
*numata-prakāśa-ruci-darśanotsukāḥ \|*  
*vigalad-vilocana-jharāplutāṅgakāś*  
*calitā babhūvur atibhāgya-rāśayaḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With hearts joyful and restless on seeing the Lord’s beauty as they had desired, with tears and hairs standing on end, with great good fortune, the three brothers left.**

### Text 25

*upavanam adhi harṣa-vārāṁnidhir*  
*naṭana-rabhasa-lola-cittas tadā \|*  
*atha madhu-madhuraṁ cakārodbhaṭaṁ*  
*naṭanam abhirasaṁ samaṁ tad-vidhaiḥ \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of bliss, desiring to dance, then began dancing intensely and sweetly with the devotees.**

### Text 26

*sarabhasam api tatra vakreśvara-*  
*dvija-kula-śaśinā samaṁ premavān \|*  
*madhu-madhura-ruci-cchaṭā-sundaraḥ*  
*satatam iha tatāna līlāyitam \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Full of prema, the Lord, beautiful with his sweet effulgence, joyfully spread pastimes constantly with Vakreśvara, moon among the brāhmaṇas.**

### Text 27

*kṣaṇam api parirabhya vakreśvaraṁ*  
*sarabhasam anucumbati śrī-yutaḥ \|*  
*kṣaṇam api vinyasan rājate*  
*samadhu-rucira-pāda-padma-dvayam \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sometimes the beautiful Lord embraced Vakreśvara and joyfully kissed him. Sometimes stepping with sweet, attractive lotus feet, he shone.**

### Text 28

*kṣaṇam api parito muhur vibhramaṁ*  
*sa ca parirabhate’tha taṁ bhūyaśaḥ \|*  
*laghu laghu madhuraṁ kalaṁ gāyati*  
*smita-rucira-rucā kṣaṇaṁ dīpayan \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Spreading beauty all around, he embraced him many times, and sometimes sang sweet songs, illuminating all places instantly with his smiling beauty.**

### Text 29

*iti nibhṛtam anena vakreśvara-*  
*dvija-kula-śaśinātha sampādayan \|*  
*naṭanam abhirasaṁ rasāmbhonidhir*  
*nyadhita sa paritaḥ padāmbhoruham \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Dancing with rasa and determination along with Vakreśvara, the ocean of rasa placed his lotus feet everywhere.**

### Text 30

*tat tathaiva rabhasād upavanato*  
*vāsudeva iti nirbhara-madhuraḥ \|*  
*gāna-kautuka-rasair nija-dayitaṁ*  
*rañjayan kala-padaṁ rahasi jagau \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pleasing his Lord with the joyful rasa of songs, Vāsudeva, sweeter than the pleasant grove, sang a gentle song in solo.**

### Text 31

*ekakaḥ sumadhuraṁ kala-ninado*  
*gītam uttamatamaṁ madhu-madhuram \|*  
*yaj jagau katham ayaṁ tam atiraso*  
*no vikāram iha jātv ahaha kim u \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Singing alone with sweet voice a most excellent, sweet song, how could the Lord, full of rasa, not experience ecstasy?**

### Text 32

*gāyatīha madhuraṁ bhiṣag-ṛṣabhe*  
*vāsudeva iti nirabhara-madhure \|*  
*ānanarta rabhasād avaśa-tanur*  
*bhāva-bhāvita-tanu-dyuti-madhuraḥ \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While sweet Vāsudeva, the doctor, sang sweetly, the Lord, his body shining sweetly with bhāva, eagerly danced with uncontrolled limbs.**

### Text 33

*aśrubhiḥ subahalaiḥ pulaka-ghaṭā-*  
*pūritair avayavair atimadhuraiḥ \|*  
*stambha-gharma-hasitādibhir aniśaṁ*  
*tāṇḍavākulita-tanuḥ sa vijayate \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With profuse tears, hairs on end, with intensely sweet limbs, with paralysis, perspiration and laughing, the dancing Lord remained incessantly victorious.**

### Text 34

*candra-vartma-pihitaṁ vadana-rucā*  
*merur eṣa vijito’paghana-rucā \|*  
*ninditaṁ nu kamalaṁ pada-kamalair*  
*nṛtyato’sya madhuraṁ madhura-rucaḥ \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he danced, the Lord of sweet beauty covered the sky with the beauty of his face, conquered Meru with the beauty of his limbs and derided the lotus with his lotus feet.**

### Text 35

*yat tu gāyati mahā-rasa-valitaṁ*  
*tatra yad yad iha nāsty atilalitam \|*  
*bhāva-bhāvitam asau nija-dayite*  
*tat tato dviguṇitaṁ samakalayat \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though the song was filled with great rasa, some parts did not evoke intense bhāva in a graceful way. However, Mahāprabhu perceived it with double the bhāva since his dear devotee had sung it.**

### Text 36

*aṣṭa-bhāva-valitaṁ sa tu yugapat*  
*śrīmad-aṅga-talataḥ parikalayan \|*  
*ānanarta rabhasād avaśa-tanur*  
*gāyato’sya madhuraṁ bahu racayan \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Manifesting the eight sāttvika-bhāvas in his limbs simultaneously, with uncontrolled body, he danced with zeal, producing great sweetness for the singer.**

### Text 37

*tat tathopavanam adhy atimadhuraḥ*  
*śrī-śacī-jaṭhara-vāridhi-śaśabhṛt \|*  
*ramya-tāṇḍava-rasa-sphurita-tanuḥ*  
*sarvato’tanuta nirbhara-lalitam \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sweet moon which arose in the womb of Śacī, his body displaying the rasa of attractive dancing, spread intense beauty throughout the grove.**

### Text 38

*yo vilokayati tasya tu hṛdayaṁ*  
*tat-kṣaṇena culūkīkṛtam abhavat \|*  
*kintu tasya nayanaṁ gata-nimiṣaṁ*  
*tatra tatra subhṛśaṁ parimilati \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When anyone sees the Lord, his heart instantly becomes miniscule (unable to hold the experience), but his eyes stop blinking and become huge.**

### Text 39

*evam eṣa bhagavān atilalitaṁ*  
*vāsudeva-sahito naṭana-rasam \|*  
*āvidhāya parito laghu vilasaṁs*  
*tatra tatra sarasas taṭam agamat \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, experiencing the rasa of dancing with Vāsudeva in this way, sporting everywhere, came to the bank of the lake.**

### Text 40

*phulla-paṅkaja-rajaḥ-paṭalīkayā*  
*kurvatā sita-ruci-bhramara-kulam \|*  
*dīrghikā-rucira-śīkara-nikarair*  
*vāyunā paridhūtaṁ prabhum abhajat \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The lake worshipped the Lord by having the wind bath him with lotus pollen which turned bees pale and with drops of water from the lake.**

### Text 41

*tatra śītala-taṭe prasṛmarayā*  
*cchāyayā sumadhure madhura-mukhaḥ \|*  
*ādadhe sapadi viśramaṇa-vidhiṁ*  
*kaṁ na harṣati vastv atyatilalitam \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sweet-faced Lord relaxed on the cool, pleasant bank, endowed with extensive shade. Who does not experience joy from a beautiful object?**

### Text 42

*sūpaviṣṭavati kāruṇikatare*  
*saṅgatāḥ samabhavann atha katare \|*  
*bhāgya-sindhu-niviḍāpluta-tanavas*  
*tat-padābja-parilokana-kutukāt \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As the most merciful Lord sat comfortably there, some devotees, their bodies flooded in the ocean of good fortune, gathered there, anxious to see his lotus feet.**

### Text 43

*śrīman-nityānanda-padābja-pratipannas*  
*tat-tan-madhye ko’pi mahātmā bahu-bhāgyaḥ \|*  
*kṛṣṇād yo dāsaḥ sa dharitrīṣu ramyaḥ*  
*śrī-gaurāṅgaṁ taṁ tatra vilokyābhinanda \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Among them, one most fortunate devotee named Kṛṣṇa-dāsa, attractive to the whole world, surrendered to the lotus feet of Nityānanda, became blissful on seeing Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 44

*tam atha madhura-mukha-candram avekṣya*  
*kṣiti-sura-vara iha gaura-sudhāṁśoḥ \|*  
*naṭana-rabhasa-bhara-gharma-jalāktaṁ*  
*snapayitum atanuta cetasi ceṣṭām \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the Lord’s sweet, moon-like face, covered with perspiration from passionate dancing, the best of brāhmaṇas thought the Lord should take a bath.**

### Text 45

*sa kutaścid ātta-ghaṭa eva mahātmā*  
*laghu-dīrghikā-jala-cayena sa-tṛṣṇam \|*  
*prabhu-mūrdhni netra-salilāpluta-dehaḥ*  
*pulakāvalī-vilo’sito’tha siṣeca \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From somewhere, Kṛṣṇa-dāsa obtained pots and, with tears covering his body and hairs standing on end, and with thirst he sprinkled water from the small pond on the Lord’s head.**

### Text 46

*ity ānīya drutam atha salilaṁ*  
*cakre sekaṁ kalasa-śata-hṛtam \|*  
*advaito’yaṁ tad-avasara-gataḥ*  
*śrīmān reje prabhu-mukha-purataḥ \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With great speed, Kṛṣṇa-dāsa brought a hundred pots and bathed the Lord. Advaita came at that time and shone in front of the Lord’s face.**

### Text 47

*taṁ parilocya manorama-deho*  
*gaura-śaśī karam asya vidhṛtya \|*  
*pāṇi-dalena tad-ātma-samīpaṁ*  
*snāna-rasāya nināya kṛpāluḥ \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing him, the merciful golden moon with attractive body, took Advaita’s hand in his hand and brought him close for enjoying the bath.**

### Text 48

*advaito’yaṁ tat tathaivopaviṣṭaḥ*  
*snānārthaṁ śrī-gauracandrasya saṅge \|*  
*so’py evaṁ taṁ gauracandram ca bhūyaḥ*  
*svaccha-svacchair vāribhiḥ siñcati \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus Advaita sat with the Lord for bathing. He also sprinkled Gauracandra plentifully with pure water.**

### Text 49

*bhūyo bhūyas taiḥ payobhiḥ suśītair*  
*atyotkaṇṭhāt secayāmāsa vipraḥ \|*  
*netrāmbhobhiḥ so’pi tatrābhiṣiktaś*  
*citraṁ citraṁ gauracandrānubhāvaḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He sprinkled the Lord with huge amounts of water out of great longing. He also bathed himself with his own tears. How astonishing are the actions of Gauracandra!**

### Text 50

*tataḥ samāttodgamanīya-vastro*  
*govinda ānandamayo mahātmā \|*  
*samāyayau tat-puratas tato’sau*  
*jagrāha vāsaḥ sa-kaṭīra-sūtram \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great devotee Govinda, full of bliss, holding fresh clothing, came to the Lord. The Lord accepted the cloth along with his waist band.**

### Text 51

*evam ātta-vasanaḥ prabhus tadā*  
*tatra tatra ca mahā-prasādakam \|*  
*svair janaiḥ samam upāsya nirbharaṁ*  
*ramya-hāsa-parihāsavat tayā \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After dressing, the Lord, joking and smiling pleasantly, ate mahāprasādam with the devotees at that place.**

### Text 52

*tat tathopavana-vibhramekṣaṇe*  
*saspṛhaḥ pratilataṁ pratidrumam \|*  
*kautukāni manasā samāvahan*  
*nābabhau parama-ramya-ceṣṭitaḥ \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In seeing the beauty of the grove, the Lord, endowed with the most attractive activities, with great desire, became happy in his mind with each creeper and tree.**

### Text 53

*bhūyo’pi tatra ratha-sannikaṭaṁ sametya*  
*dṛṣṭvā jagatpatim amanda-vilāsa-ramyam \|*  
*harṣāt samaṁ nija-janaiḥ samupetya paścāt*  
*kṣipyan rathaṁ vijayate parama-prakāśaḥ \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Again he came near the chariot and seeing Jagannātha, attractive with unlimited pastimes, put the chariot behind him, met with the devotees, and stood victorious, radiating the highest effulgence.**

### Text 54

*kṣaṇam api kara-kamalaja-yuga-kalita-*  
*dhvani jaya jaya jaya jaya jaya jaya bhoḥ \|*  
*iti niravadhi ratha-parisara-pṛthivīm*  
*abhi kala-pada-mayam atirahasi jagau \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sometimes clapping his hands and continually saying “Jaya, jaya, jaya, jaya, jaya, jaya” he walked on the road of the chariot and sang confidential songs.**

### Text 55

*dhṛtvā dhṛtvā syandana-raśmīn*  
*śrī-gaurāṅgaḥ pāṇi-sarojaiḥ \|*  
*harṣotkarṣaiḥ sāṅga-vibhaṅgaṁ*  
*reje rājīvāyata-netraḥ \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Holding the chariot rope, Gaurāṅga, with his lotus hands, with blossoming lotus eyes, with intense joy, shone brightly while bending his body.**

### Text 56

*ullāsair harṣotkarṣai romāñcālī-rājad-deho*  
*gāyadbhis tais taiḥ svīyaiḥ svīyāṁ līlā-gāthām eva \|*  
*unmīlad-vidyun-mālā-kānti-prāya-śrīmat-kāntir*  
*babhrāja śrī-gaurāṅgo dhṛtvā dhṛtvā tat-tad-raśmīn \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gaurāṅga, his hairs standing on end, shining like multiple flashes of lightning, holding the chariot rope with great jubilation, while singers glorified the Lord’s pastimes, shone brightly.**

### Text 57

*uccair ucchrita-cūḍā-kumbha-grasta-patākā-*  
*cumbavad-bhāskara-bimbaḥ śrīmān syandana-mukhyaḥ \|*  
*so’yaṁ nīla-mahīdhra-śrīman-mauli-sudhāṁśor*  
*loke’smin nahi keṣām ānandaṁ tanute vā \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The chief chariot of the moon of Nīlācala, touching the sun with its flag attached to the top of the canopy, spread bliss to everyone in the world.**

### Text 58

*ity evaṁ pathi dṛṣṭvā dṛṣṭvā kautuka-ceṣṭā-*  
*mātra-vilāso lāsyoddāma-sumūrtiḥ \|*  
*śrīmat-syandana-yātāṁ trailokyādbhuta-rūpāṁ*  
*gaurāṅgo’tikṛpālur netrābhyām apibat saḥ \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Looking on the road, most merciful Gaurāṅga, whose every action was filled with joy, who danced intensely at all times, drank the movement of the beautiful chariot, most astonishing in the three worlds, with his two eyes.**

### Text 59

*astādri-stha-vanālīṁ viśrāmārtham upaiti*  
*trailokya-stha-tamiśraṁ bhūyo bhūya udasya \|*  
*arke syandana-mukhyaḥ śrī-nīlādri-sudhāṁśos*  
*tarke tatra niṣaṇṇo notsāho manujānām \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the sun, having eradicated darkness in the three worlds completely, reached the forests on Sunset Mountain to take rest, the chariot of Jagannātha stopped at that time. But it seemed that the enthusiasm of the people did not.**

### Text 60

*āgatyānaya kacche tatratyān sukha-sindhau*  
*kṣipyan sāyam akārṣīc chrī-nīlādri-sudhāṁśuḥ \|*  
*vartmany eva samantāt sañcāryaiḥ kaśipunā*  
*krāman pāda-vihārair ūrdhvāṁs tatra niveśam \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving at his destination, Jagannātha threw the devotees present there into the ocean of happiness as he made steps by walking on the path with his feet using pillows to his house, while he attracted the evening.**

### Text 61

*prāsādaṁ sa niveśya sva-sthāne kṛta-vāso*  
*nānā-vibhrama-ramyaś ceṣṭāmātra-vihāraḥ \|*  
*bhogān bhūri-rasāḍhyāṁs tatropāsya kṛpālur*  
*babhrājāsita-śaila-śīta-mayūkhaḥ \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Entering his palace, and living at that place, the merciful moon of Nīlācala, whose every action was a pastime, attractive with various types of beauty, eating food filled with many tastes, shone brightly.**

### Text 62

*atrāste sa niśāyā āgatyāmbuja-netro*  
*dṛṣṭvā tan-mukha-candraṁ niryal-locana-bāṣpaḥ \|*  
*bhūyo gaura-sudhāṁśur govindena sameto*  
*romāñcāñcita-deho babhrājāmita-ceṣṭaḥ \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lotus eyed Gaurāṅga arrived there at night and, with tears flowing, saw lotus faced Jagannātha. The golden moon of unlimited activities, standing with Govinda, shone, while his hairs stood on end.**

### Text 63

*ity evaṁ sa tu guṇḍicotsava-rasaṁ dṛṣṭvā samāsvādya ca*  
*prāyaḥ kīrtana-nartanena divasaṁ nītvā mahollāsavān \|*  
*harṣotkarṣa-manoharo’timadhuraḥ śrī-śrī-śacīnandanaḥ*  
*sarveṣāṁ hṛdayaṁ jahāra paramānandair vimugdhīkṛtam \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, having seen and tasted the rasa of the Guṇḍicā festival (rathayatra), he spent the day happily in singing and dancing. The sweet son of Śacī, attractive with excellent bliss, enchanted the hearts of all people with the highest bliss.**

### Text 64

*tat tādṛg vara-bhūṣaṇotkara-lasad-veśena sad-vibhramaṁ*  
*tat tādṛg vara-mālya-sañcaya-lasat-sarvāṅga-bhaṅgī-satam \|*  
*tat tādṛg vara-vaibhava-prasṛmarānandotsava-śrī-mayaṁ*  
*drāg dṛṣṭvaiva jagatpatiṁ jana-cayās tatraiva ceto dadhuḥ \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Jagannātha, beautiful with the best ornaments and shining dress, shining in all his limbs with the best garlands, endowed with a festival of bliss streaming forth with the greatest force, all the people gave their hearts to him.**

### Text 65

*śaktyā cen nayanaṁ nayaty atitarāṁ nīlādri-ratne janas*  
*tat-svāntaṁ punar atra citra-likhita-prāyaṁ śacī-nandane \|*  
*cet tatraiva dadāti locana-yugaṁ citraṁ caritraṁ tato’*  
*kasmād vā jaḍimā vimohana-karo’kasmān muhur jāyate \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the people looked at Jagannātha with their full strength, the son of Śacī appeared in their minds, as if he were painted in their hearts. If they looked at Gaurāṅga whose activities were amazing, suddenly, for some reason, they became stunned with bewilderment for a long time.**

### Text 66

*ity evaṁ rathayātrayā sarabhasaṁ svaiḥ svaiḥ svakīyair guṇaṁ*  
*saṅkīrtya svam avekṣya tatra muditaḥ pratyabdam ākrīḍati \|*  
*tat-tal-lāsya-vilāsa-kautuka-kathā kair vā samudgīyatāṁ*  
*brahmāder api nāsti nāsti nitarāṁ śaktis tathā tādṛśī \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At rathayatra in Puri, Mahāprabhu in joy enjoyed pastimes with his devotees every year, singing with enthusiasm the qualities of the Lord and with joy beholding Lord Jagannātha. Who can describe his joyful pastime of dancing? No one, not even Brahmā, has the power to do that.**

## Chapter Eighteen

### Text 1

*atha tatra rathotsave prabhuḥ svajanenaiva vilasya bhūyaśaḥ \|*  
*muhur aṣṭasu vāsareṣu ca pramumodopavane sa kautukī \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**During the ratha festival the blissful Lord with his devotees performed pastimes constantly for eight days in the grove and derived great joy.**

### Text 2

*iha ramya-saraḥsu sa-spṛhaṁ vihita-snāna-vidhir yathāyatham \|*  
*avalokya sitetarācala-dyumaṇiṁ rājati tatra tatra saḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He bathed in the pleasant lakes with longing at suitable times and, seeing Jagannātha, radiated beauty everywhere.**

### Text 3

*prati-bhūruha-mūlam ullasan prati-valli-prati-kuñjam añjasā \|*  
*prati-saikata-rañjita-sthalaṁ vilasan bhrājati tatra tatra saḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Spontaneously, he delighted in each tree base, each creeper, each bower, and sported on each sand bank, radiating beauty everywhere.**

### Text 4

*vilasat-kala-kaṇṭha-kākalīṁ*  
*kalayan komala-citta-vṛttikaḥ \|*  
*madhuraṁ madhupotkara-dhvaniṁ*  
*śravaṇenaiva piban virājate \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the playful chirping of birds and the sweet buzzing of bees, with softened heart, he radiated beauty.**

### Text 5

*iha tat-tad-adabhra-vibhramair*  
*bhramamāṇaḥ sa itas tato muhuḥ \|*  
*vijahau hṛdayasya karṣaṇaṁ*  
*cira-vṛndāvana-viprayogajam \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Wandering here and there constantly in the grove with unlimited types of beauty, he gave up painful feelings of separation from Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 6

*atha tasya bahir vihārato*  
*vijaye nīla-girau jagat-pateḥ \|*  
*sa tathaiva paricchadotkarair*  
*abhavat sarva-jana-pramoda-kṛt \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**While Jagannātha performed pastimes outside the grove with his chariot in Puri (Guṇḍicā), the Lord gave joy to all the devotees with a variety of paraphernalia.**

### Text 7

*nava-vāsara-madhyataḥ prabhuḥ*  
*sa narendākhya-sarovare tataḥ \|*  
*svajanaiḥ saha toya-khelanaṁ*  
*samam advaita-mahātmanākarot \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For nine days the Lord with Advaita and the devotees performed water pastimes at Narendra Sarovara.**

### Text 8

*upagamya narendra-saṁjñakāṁ*  
*sarasīṁ tāṁ sarasīruhekṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*kutukena nidāgha-śāntaye*  
*sa lalambe nija-bhakta-vatsalaḥ \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving at Narendra Sarovara, lotus-eyed Lord with his devotees relieved himself of the summer heat with amusements by going into the water.**

### Text 9

*aruṇāruṇa-pāda-paṅkajo*  
*druta-cāmīkara-gaura-vigrahaḥ \|*  
*karuṇāruṇa-locana-dvayas*  
*trividhottāpa-virāmakṛt sadā \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With his red lotus feet, his body of molten gold, and his merciful red eyes, he destroyed the three miseries.**

### Text 10

*avalambya sa ittham añjasā*  
*sarasīṁ sārasa-sālasekṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*kṣaṇavān jala-keli-kautuke*  
*saha tais tair amṛtāṁśuvad babhau \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With languid lotus eyes, with a desire for play, going in the lake, shining like the moon, he enjoyed water pastimes with the devotees.**

### Text 11

*katare dala-sañcayāḥ pare*  
*nava-kiñjalka-cayā iva sthitāḥ \|*  
*svayam eva varāṭakākṛtiḥ*  
*sa babhau gaura-śaśī ca padmavat \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some of the devotees were like petals, some were like masses of pollen, some were like the seed pods and Gauracandra was like the lotus.**

### Text 12

*kara-vāribhir asya ke ca te*  
*siṣicus tat-pada-paṅkajaṁ mṛdu \|*  
*katare nayanābja-randhrakair*  
*iha tad-rūpa-sudhāḥ samāpiban \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some sprinkled water on his lotus feet using their hands and some drank the nectar of his form with their lotus eyes.**

### Text 13

*sa tu bhūri-vilāsa-kautukaṁ*  
*racayann indu-mukhaḥ kṛpā-nidhiḥ \|*  
*śayitaṁ kutukena saṁśritaḥ*  
*sukham advaita-tanuṁ vyarocata \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Making joyous pastimes, the ocean of mercy with moon-like face, joyfully using the body of Advaita as his comfortable bed, shone.**

### Text 14

*sunipātya kṛpā-nidhis tadā*  
*prabhum advaitam adho jalāntare \|*  
*tad-upary api sālasaḥ svayaṁ*  
*parisuptaḥ sa yayau sanidratām \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of mercy, having Advaita lie down on the water, slept on him in a lazy mood.**

### Text 15

*iti bhūya ihaiva vibhramaṁ*  
*racayitvā taṭam udyayau prabhuḥ \|*  
*vigalaj-jala-bindu-sundaraḥ*  
*vasanaṁ bibhrad upātta-kautukaḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Performing many pastimes in the lake, the Lord, beautiful with flowing drops of water, climbed on the shore, full of joy, and put on his clothing.**

### Text 16

*atha nīla-girīndra-candramā*  
*vidadhe’ntar-vijayaṁ tathaiva saḥ \|*  
*sa tathaiva śacī-tanūbhavaḥ*  
*parilocya bhramadaṁ yayau muhuḥ \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then Jagannātha made his return journey. The son of Śacī, seeing him, became constantly bewildered.**

### Text 17

*prathamāvasaraṁ jagatpateḥ*  
*prayato draṣṭum asau śacī-sutaḥ \|*  
*śayanāt prahare samudyayau*  
*kṣaṇadāyāś carame kṛpānidhiḥ \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**To see Jagannātha at the first darśaṇa, the son of Śacī rose from bed in the last portion of the night.**

### Text 18

*śayanāt sa tathā śacīsutaḥ*  
*prabhur utthāya vibhor didṛkṣayā \|*  
*vividhaṁ vidadhe vidhānataḥ*  
*sa tu dainandina-karma nirmalam \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rising from bed, anxious to see Jagannātha, the Lord performed various pure, daily rituals according to the rules.**

### Text 19

*vimalaiḥ salilaiḥ pariṣkṛtair*  
*vihita-snāna-vidhir mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*kaṭi-sūtra-sametam añjasā*  
*vara-vāsaḥ sa dadhāra lohitam \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu bathed in clear, purified water and quickly put on his red cloth with his waist cloth.**

### Text 20

*mada-vāraṇa-rāja-vibhramo*  
*nija-nāma-grahaṇe kṛta-kṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*aruṇāmbara-saṁvṛtāṅgako*  
*bahir eṣo’tisukhena niryayau \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Attractive as an intoxicated elephant, constantly chanting the Lord’s name, covered in crimson cloth, he went outside in great happiness.**

### Text 21

*karakaṁ parigṛhya pāṇinā*  
*sa tu govinda-mahāmatis tataḥ \|*  
*satataṁ prabhu-saṅga-saṅgataḥ*  
*sa tu dāmodara ity asau yatiḥ \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Taking a water pot in his hand, he went with Govinda and the renunciate Dāmodara, who always accompanied him.**

### Text 22

*nija-nāma-sudhā-payonidheḥ*  
*satatāsvāda-lasan-manorathaḥ \|*  
*samupetya tataḥ prabhoḥ puraṁ*  
*praviveśa praṇatiṁ vidhāya saḥ \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With the strong desire to constantly relish the nectar ocean of the Lord’s name, Mahāprabhu arrived at the Gunḍicā temple and, entering, offering respects.**

### Text 23

*asitācala-mauli-candramo*  
*vadanenduḥ parilokya nirbharam \|*  
*vigalan-nayanāmbu-dhārayā*  
*paridhautāṅga-lato virājate \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gazing at moon-faced Jagannātha for a long time, he washed his body in tears.**

### Text 24

*nanu nīla-girīndra-candramāḥ*  
*parilokyainam adabhra-vibhramam \|*  
*abhiṣiñcati tad-vilocana-dvaya-*  
*nīrair atiharṣa-dharṣitaḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Did Jagannātha, seeing the person with infinite bewildering pastimes, bath him in his tears out of great joy?**

### Text 25

*nimiṣeṇa dunoti mānasaṁ*  
*bahudhety asya vilokane prabhuḥ \|*  
*asitācala-ratnam añjasā*  
*nayane nirnimiṣe cakāra kim \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking “In seeing him, blinking burns up the mind,” did Jagannātha immediately stop blinking his eyes?**

### Text 26

*sa śacī-tanūjo nijāṁ tanūm*  
*abhiṣicyākṣipayo jharair muhuḥ \|*  
*pulakair dviguṇībhavat tanur*  
*mumude harṣa-vaśas tathā tathā \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The son of Śacī bathed his own body in tears constantly and his hairs stood on end with double intensity. He rejoiced in great happiness.**

### Text 27

*prathamāvasaraḥ prabhṛty atho*  
*sa tu dhūpāvadhi tatra susthitaḥ \|*  
*bahudhā praṇati-pradakṣiṇāny*  
*api kṛtvā nijam alayaṁ yayau \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu stayed from the first darśaṇa until the incense offering. Offering respects many times, he then returned to his residence.**

### Text 28

*samupetya nijālayaṁ tato*  
*nija-nāmāni muhur muhur japan \|*  
*upaviśya rarāja candravat*  
*jagad-āhlāda-karaḥ prakāśavat \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving at his house while constantly chanting, sitting down, he shone brightly like the moon, bringing joy to the universe.**

### Text 29

*atha tatra sukhaṁ gṛhāntare*  
*sthitavantaṁ karuṇālayaṁ prabhum \|*  
*parolokitum añjasā muhuḥ*  
*paritaḥ svair muditāḥ samāyayuḥ \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Joyful devotees with their constant followers quickly arrived to see the Lord, the abode of mercy, sitting comfortably in his room.**

### Text 30

*prathamaṁ parigṛhya sādaraṁ*  
*prabhu-pūjārtham upāyanaṁ bahu \|*  
*pulakāśru-jharākulaḥ sukhaṁ*  
*prabhur advaita ihāgamat tadā \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Holding articles for worshipping Mahāprabhu, Advaita, with tears in his eyes, arrived first in joy.**

### Text 31

*padayor vinivedya bhaktitaḥ*  
*salilaṁ śuddhatamaṁ suvāsitam \|*  
*malayodbhava-paṅka-sañcayair*  
*atha bhāla-sthalam ālilepa saḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With devotion, Advaita offered pure, fragrant water to his feet, and then applied sandalwood to his forehead.**

### Text 32

*kusumāni manoharāṇy atho*  
*śuci-dūrvākṣata-sañcayaṁ tataḥ \|*  
*vililepa kṛpānidhis tadā*  
*prabhur advaita-vibhuṁ viśeṣataḥ \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then Mahāprabhu, the ocean of mercy, attentively sprinkled attractive flowers and white rice with dūrva on Advaita.**

### Text 33

*atha bhūsura-vaṁśa-candramāḥ*  
*prathito nārada ity asau bhuvi \|*  
*vihita-praṇipāta-saṁhatir*  
*nayanābjena tathā samarcayet \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then Śrīvāsa, the moon among brāhmaṇas, known as Nārada in this world, offering respects repeatedly, worshipped Mahāprabhu with his lotus eyes.**

### Text 34

*atha ye prabhupāda-pallava-*  
*priya-bhṛtyāḥ sunivāritāś ca te \|*  
*samayāt samupetya saspṛhaṁ*  
*nayanais tad-vadanaṁ papur muhuḥ \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Those who were dear servants at Mahāprabhu’s feet, who had renounced all else, came with desire and drank his face with their eyes constantly.**

### Text 35

*itare bahavo’pi sarvataḥ*  
*samupetāḥ prabhu-darśanotsukāḥ \|*  
*sabhaya-spṛha-kautuka-trayaṁ*  
*satato’dhika-ṣṭhita-citta-vṛttayaḥ \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Many others arrived from all around, eager to see Mahāprabhu, with hearts fixed in fear, desire and joy.**

### Text 36

*bahir eva ciraṁ sukhotkaraiḥ*  
*sthitavantaḥ sumahā-kṛpālayam \|*  
*dadṛśuḥ kramaśo’tisādhvasād*  
*api govinda-nivedanāntare \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Waiting outside for a long time with great happiness, eventually, with great trepidation, after Govinda gave permission, they saw the great abode of mercy.**

### Text 37

*iti te prahara-dvayāvadhi*  
*prathitā bhāva-śatena bhūyasā \|*  
*dadṛśuḥ prabhum ātta-kautukam*  
*vara-kalpa-drumavan manoramam \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For two praharas, the devotees, with various emotions, constantly saw the Lord, full of joy, as attractive as a desire tree.**

### Text 38

*hasitair api kāṁścid añjasā*  
*vacanenāpi tathetarān prabhuḥ \|*  
*kṛpayā ca kayā ca netarān*  
*akarod ātta-manorathotsukān \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord satisfied all their desires, making some happy with laughing, others by simply words, and others by inconceivable mercy.**

### Text 39

*hṛdayeṣu punar manorathān iha*  
*yo yo vidadhe yathā-vidhān \|*  
*sakalān svayam ātta-kautukaḥ*  
*saphalān eva cakāra tāṁs tathā \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The joyful Lord made whatever desires they possessed in their hearts come true.**

### Text 40

*na ca nirvavṛte vilokya taṁ*  
*na ca dṛṣṭī rahitāśru-vāhitā \|*  
*prapadānta-gam aśru nojjhitaṁ*  
*manujenāsya samīpatas tadā \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**They could not stop looking at him and could not stop the flow of tears from their eyes. The tears approached his lotus feet and could not give them up.**

### Text 41

*sa vidhāya samasta-dehināṁ*  
*sukham ālokana-bhāṣaṇādibhiḥ \|*  
*vidadhe madhurānanaḥ sukhād*  
*atha mādhyandina-karma śuddhimat \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Giving happiness to all beings by his glance and words, the pure, moon-faced Lord then performed his noon rituals with happiness.**

### Text 42

*śucibhiḥ salilaiḥ kṛtāplavo*  
*dhṛta-kaupīna-bahiḥ-sad-aṁśukaḥ \|*  
*malayodbhava-paṅka-bhūṣito*  
*nija-nāmāni gṛṇan babhau prabhuḥ \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bathing in pure water, donning a kaupīna and clean outer cloth, decorating himself with sandalwood, chanting the names of the Lord, he spread beauty.**

### Text 43

*upayujya ca śuddha-modanaṁ*  
*kṛta-śuddhācamanādika-kriyāḥ \|*  
*paridhāya ca bhinnam aṁśukaṁ*  
*śuci-kāntir vavṛdhe śriyā prabhuḥ \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Taking pure food and sipping pure water, the Lord of pure beauty put on fresh clothes and increased in beauty.**

### Text 44

*punar apy upagamya te ca te*  
*prabhupādāmbuja-sīdhu-lampaṭāḥ \|*  
*nayanāñjalibhir nirantaraṁ*  
*bahu tad-rūpa-sudhāṁ papus tadā \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Greedy for the nectar of his lotus feet, again the devotees came and drank the nectar of his form many times with their eyes, like cupped hands.**

### Text 45

*sa yathā-tatham ukti-mādhurī-*  
*madhura-smera-mukhendu-sundaraḥ \|*  
*muditān atha tān sa pūrvavat*  
*parisambhāṣya cakāra nirbharam \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As previously, beautiful with his moon-like face endowed with sweet smiles and sweet words, he gave joy to the devotees by talking to them.**

### Text 46

*nija-nāma-sudhāṁ muhuḥ pibann*  
*iti dainandina-karma bhūṣayan \|*  
*śaradi prati-yātram utsukaḥ*  
*sukha-sindhau parigāhate sma saḥ \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Constantly drinking the nectar of the name, performing his daily activities, longing for the festival in the autumn each year, he submerged himself in an ocean of happiness.**

### Text 47

*bahu-kautuka-vīkṣaṇa-kṣaṇān*  
*mudito dvādaśa-yātrakeṇa saḥ \|*  
*asitācala-mauli-maṇḍanaṁ*  
*nayanābhyām akarod ivātmani \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Joyful at seeing many interesting pastimes, the Lord observed twelve festivals, making Jagannātha his decoration by his eyes.**

### Text 48

*makarotsava-madhyataḥ prabhur*  
*vihitābhīra-rucir yathā-ruci \|*  
*ghṛta-dugdha-dadhīni bhārato*  
*nidadhat kaṇṭha-taṭe virājate \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**During the Makara month festival, the Lord, beautiful as a cowherd, carrying ghee, milk and yogurt on his shoulder, according to his taste, radiated great beauty.**

### Text 49

*kṣaṇam apy atisaukhya-cañcalo*  
*laguḍotkṣepaṇa-kautukī muhuḥ \|*  
*vara-gopa iveha harṣado*  
*jayati śrī-yuta-gaura-vigrahaḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Overflowing with joy, he sometimes had fun throwing a stick. Like the best cowherd, giving joy to all, the beautiful golden form remains victorious.**

### Text 50

*kṣaṇam utkṣipati kṣaṇaṁ padā*  
*kṣipati bhrāmayati kṣaṇaṁ tu tam \|*  
*bhuja-kakṣa-taṭoru-jānu-pat-*  
*kamalādho’dha itas tataḥ prabhuḥ \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sometimes he threw it up in the air, sometimes threw it with his foot, sometimes he whirled it around his arms, his side, his thigh, his knee, and lotus feet, lower and lower, here and there.**

### Text 51

*atikautuka-ceṣṭayā nṛṇāṁ*  
*nayanānandam atīva sāndrakam \|*  
*vidadhat sakalotsaveṣu sa*  
*prabhur ānandam amandam āyayau \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord in this way gave great, intense joy to the eyes of all people by his blissful activities during all the festivals and attained unlimited bliss.**

### Text 52

*atha dola itīrito hareḥ*  
*sumahān utsava eka uttamaḥ \|*  
*vividhaiḥ khalu kautukehitaiḥ*  
*purato nṛtyati gaura-vigrahaḥ \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There was a great festival called dola-yatra (holi). The blissful golden form danced in front with various blissful actions.**

### Text 53

*aruṇaiś ca sitaiś ca komalair*  
*atha hāridra-rajobhir uttamaiḥ \|*  
*malayodbhava-reṇubhiś ca tair*  
*bhagavāṁś citrita-vigraho babhau \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The colorful Lord Gaurāṅga (or Jagannātha) shone with excellent scarlet, white, soft yellow powders and sandalwood dust.**

### Text 54-55

*saphala-kramuka-drumoccayaiḥ*  
*phala-namraiḥ kadalī-drumair api \|*  
*sumano-bhara-niṣpatac-chikhais*  
*tarubhiś cādhika-maṇḍalī-kṛte \|\|54\|\|*

*vara-mañca-vibhūṣite lasad-*  
*vara-paryaṅka-taṭopari prabhau \|*  
*nija-bhakta-gaṇena dolite*  
*sati gaurāṅga-śaśī ca nṛtyati \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jagannātha was placed on a gleaming palanquin on an excellent stage surrounded by betel and banana trees as well as flowering trees and was swung by the devotees. At that time Gaurāṅga danced.**

### Text 56

*kanakācala-kānti-vigrahau*  
*muhur anyonya-vilokanotsukau \|*  
*abhidolana-nṛtya-cañcalāv*  
*atha govinda-śacīsutau prabhū \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jagannātha and the son of Śacī, two golden mountains, constantly gazing at each other with longing, were anxious to dance at the swing festival.**

### Text 57

*nija-ceṣṭita-vaibhava-śriyā*  
*janatānāṁ niviḍaṁ sukhotkaram \|*  
*avirāma-rasād akurvatām*  
*adhidolotsavam utsukātmanā \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the wealth of beauty of their actions, with eager hearts at the swing festival, they gave great happiness to the people from their continuous rasa.**

### Text 58

*itareṣu mahotsaveṣu sa*  
*prathito dola itīha yaḥ sadā \|*  
*sama eva rathasya kīrtito*  
*madhumāsa-prathame sa rājate \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Among the other festivals, the festival known as dola-yatra is glorified as equivalent to the rathayatra and takes place at the beginning of Caitra month.**

### Text 59

*nanu tat kim idaṁ jagatpater*  
*iha dolotsava-kautukaṁ janaiḥ \|*  
*kathanīyam amuṁ mahāprabhuḥ*  
*purataḥ paśyati nirbharaiḥ sukhaiḥ \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Who can describe the bliss of the dola-yatra of Jagannātha? Mahāprabhu saw it personally with great happiness.**

### Text 60

*punar apy atha taiḥ samāgatair*  
*atha yātrā-samaye mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*vilasaty aniśaṁ tathā tathā*  
*nija-saṅkīrtana-nartanādibhiḥ \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu constantly enjoyed pastimes at the time of the festival with his assembled devotees, with chanting and dancing.**

### Text 61

*iti viṁśati-hāyanaiḥ prabhur*  
*baladevasya rathāgrato muhuḥ \|*  
*naṭanāni vidhāya kīrtanair*  
*idam etad vyakiraj jagat-tale \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this way, for twenty years the Lord illuminated the universe with dancing and kīrtana in front of Baladeva’s chariot.**

### Text 62

*sa tu sarva-janāntara-sthito*  
*jagad-ādhāra iti prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*iti tasya puro muhur muhur*  
*naṭanaṁ kīrtanam ātatāna saḥ \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jagannātha is situated among the people and is thus famous as the support for the universe. Mahāprabhu (his other form) continually danced and sang in front of him.**

### Text 63

*itthaṁ śrī-puruṣottame viharaṇaṁ kṛtvā śacīnandano*  
*harṣād viṁśati-vatsareṇa vihita-krīḍo babhau nirbharam \|*  
*etan-madhyam adhiprāyaṇa-kutukād āgatya bhāgīrathī-*  
*tīre śrī-mathurām alaṅkṛti-matiṁ kartuṁ sa vikrīḍati \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this way, the son of Śacī performed pastimes in Puri for twenty years in joy and shone with his pastimes. During this time, with desire to travel, he went to the bank of the Gaṅgā and performed pastimes in Vṛndāvana.**

## Chapter Nineteen

### Text 1

*drūta-cāmīkarākāro mathurāṁ calituṁ tataḥ \|*  
*lasat-kari-karākāroddāma-dor-dvitayo babhau \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With complexion of molten gold, his two broad arms like radiant elephant trunks, Mahāprabhu shone as he was about to go to Mathurā.**

### Text 2

*prayātuṁ yamunā-tīraṁ gaṅgā-tīre mano dadhe \|*  
*yat taṭe so’vatīrṇo’sti tasyāṁ prītir mahīyasī \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**To go to the Yamunā, he decided to go to the bank of the Gaṅgā, since he was born there and had great affection for it.**

### Text 3

*dakṣiṇād āgato yāvat tāvat tatra mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*mathurāyāṁ calaty eva rāmānando’tra bādhate \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As he was about to go from the south to Mathurā, Rāmānanda stopped him.**

### Text 4

*cāturmāsyāntare nāthaṁ karhicid gamanodyatam \|*  
*uvā ca bahu-duḥkhena śrī-rāmānanda-rāyakaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At the end of cāturmāsya, Rāmānanda in great sorrow spoke the Lord, who was preparing to go.**

### Text 5

*daśamyāṁ vijayāyāṁ tu gamanaṁ bhavitā prabhoḥ \|*  
*daśamyāṁ vijayāyāṁ tu daśāyām aham agrataḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord will go on the vijaya-dāsamī. At that victorious time, I will die.”**

### Text 6

*govindo jagadānandaḥ śrī-dāmodara-paṇḍitaḥ \|*  
*paramānanda-purī ca tat-saṅge militā yayuḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Govinda, Jaganānanda, Dāmodara Paṇḍita and Paramānanda Purī went with him.**

### Text 7

*gaṅgā-tīre samāgatya vaiṣṇavebhyo visarjitum \|*  
*mahā-prasādān vividhān netuṁ tatrādiśat prabhuḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord ordered them to take various types of mahāprasādam to distribute to the devotees on coming to the bank of the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 8

*ete nātha-nideśena muditā bhūya ādaduḥ \|*  
*mahā-prasādān vipulān ḍora-candana-mukhyakān \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In joy, on the order of the Lord, they gathered great amounts of mahāprasādam and rope and sandalwood remnants.**

### Text 9

*mātre nirmālya-vasanam ātmecchābhir mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*paramānanda-pury-ūḍhāṁ paramāṁ yuktim ādadhe \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord by his desire entrusted Paramānanda Purī with some of Jagannātha cloth remnants for his mother.**

### Text 10

*idaṁ śrīmaj-jagannātha-nirmālyaṁ paramāṁśukam \|*  
*pratāparudreṇa ca me dattaṁ parama-durlabham \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Pratāparudra gave me this rare, excellent cloth of Jagannātha.**

### Text 11

*kasmai dāsyāmi tan nūnaṁ gadituṁ tvam ihārhasi \|*  
*mayā sandigdha-manasā sthīyate sāmprataṁ khalu \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“To whom shall I give this cloth? You should say. Right now, my mind is confused.”**

### Text 12

*ity ukto’sau purī svāmī babhāṣe’tha mahāprabhum \|*  
*jananyai deyam etat tu mamaitan matam uttamam \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When he said this, Paramānanda Purī said to Mahāprabhu, “It should be given to your mother. That is my good opinion.”**

### Text 13

*ūce pūrvedyur asakau rasa-kautuka-vibhramaḥ \|*  
*vibhrama-ccheda-kṛd dṛṣṭir hṛd-dṛṣṭi-sukhadaḥ prabhuḥ \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the morning, the Lord, whose glance could dispel illusion, enchanting with joyful rasa, giving joy to the heart, spoke.**

### Text 14

*gāyaṁ gāyaṁ gamiṣyāmi jagannāthaṁ vilokitum \|*  
*dāmodaro’sau mat-saṅge gāyan sthāsyati niścitam \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Singing and singing, I will go to see Jagannātha. Dāmodara, singing, should stay with me for certain.”**

### Text 15

*ity asau rajanī-śeṣe prathamāvasaraṁ vibhoḥ \|*  
*nija-kīrtana-saṁharṣair gacchan pathi babhau prabhuḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At the end of night the Lord walked on the path joyful with kīrtana to see the first darśana of Jagannātha.**

### Text 16

*daivād dāmodaraḥ so’yaṁ milito nābhavat tadā \|*  
*siṁha-dvāre kṣaṇaṁ tasthau tam apekṣya svayaṁ prabhuḥ \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By chance, Dāmodara could not go with him. Waiting for him, the Lord stood at the siṁha-dvāra for some time.**

### Text 17

*bhāvābhāvābhibhāvābhibhava-bhāve babhau bhavaḥ \|*  
*vibhāvevambhāva-bhāve babhūva bhuvi vaibhavam \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Dāmodara achieved success in his birth by experiencing great feelings in Mahāprabhu’s absence. This person attained glory on earth for such feelings for Mahāprabhu. (Two consonants only are used)**

### Text 18

*tataḥ kiñcid vilambena milito’bhūt sa bhūsuraḥ \|*  
*prabhur āviṣṭa-citto’sau taṁ dṛṣṭvā kupito’bhavat \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Dāmodara came and met him a little later. The Lord, overcome with emotion, on seeing him, became angry.**

### Text 19

*taṁ tu gītā-pustikayā pṛṣṭhe bhūyo jaghāna saḥ \|*  
*niṣpipeṣa padāghātaiḥ praṇayāt praṇayāmbudhiḥ \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of affection hit Dāmodara on his back with a song book, and out of affection kicked him with his foot.**

### Text 20

*itthaṁ praviśya prāsādaṁ dṛṣṭvā śrī-puruṣottamam \|*  
*natvā stutvā ca calituṁ manaś cakre kṛpānidhiḥ \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the ocean of mercy entered the temple and saw Jagannātha. Offering respects and prayers he decided to leave.**

### Text 21

*kīrtanaṁ cakrire ke ca samutsuka-mano-layāḥ \|*  
*nartanaṁ cakrire ke ca samutsuka-mano-layāḥ \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(gomūtrikā-bandhaḥ)**  
**Being very enthusiastic, some devotees performed kīrtana. Some enthusiastic devotees danced.**

### Text 22

*kāśī-miśra-mukhāḥ sarve paścāt paścāt samāyayuḥ \|*  
*samanuvrajatas tāṁs tān visasarja kṛpānidhiḥ \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the devotees headed by Kāśī-miśṛa came later. The ocean of mercy then left with the devotees, who followed after him.**

### Text 23

*niśāvasāne tair etaiḥ kīrtayadbhir muhur muhuḥ \|*  
*pratasthe gāna-kalayā lolaḥ śrī-gaurasundaraḥ \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With trembling mind, Gaurāṅga departed at the end of night, with devotees singing a sweet song.**

### Text 24

*govindo jagadānandaḥ śrī-dāmodara-paṇḍitaḥ \|*  
*yati-śreṣṭha-purī-svāmī kīrtayanatḥ samāyayuḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Govinda, Jagadānanda, Dāmodara Paṇḍita and Paramānada Purī did kīrtana as they went away.**

### Text 25

*lalal-līlo lalal-līlo lolo lolo lalal-lalaḥ \|*  
*līlālolo ’lilīlālīṁ līlālīṁ lolalāṁ laluḥ \|\|25\|\| (ekākṣaraḥ)*

**Перевод:**

**He who possessed the glorious pastimes of Vraja and gave up the pastimes in Puri, with agitated mind and great thirst to go to Vraja, was an inspiration to all people with his desire. He was greedy for pastimes. The devotees did many actions like the various movements of bees, which were filled with rashness, in order to catch the Lord. (One consonant is used.) used.)**

### Text 26

*tato’nu dolām āruhya śrī-rāmānanda-rāyakaḥ \|*  
*etadīyāś ca ye cānye sametās te ta āyayuḥ \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rāmānanda Rāya mounted a palanquin and the devotees gathered and followed him.**

### Text 27

*śrutvā sarve janās tatra strī-pumāṁsaḥ samantataḥ \|*  
*hariṁ vadeti sotkaṇṭhaṁ vadanto bhūya āyayuḥ \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the Lord’s words, men and women from all around gathered, saying with longing, “Chant the name of the Lord.”**

### Text 28

*tataḥ samudite bhānau bhānu-koṭi-sama-prabhaḥ \|*  
*prātaḥ-kṛtyaṁ cakārāsau tair etair nija-bhaktakaiḥ \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After the sun rose, the Lord, more brilliant than a million suns, performed morning duties along with the devotees.**

### Text 29

*sa tatra gamanārambhe nata-trātrā na nāvavau \|*  
*pavitrāṅghri-janānandaṁ bhavitrāgamanānanam \|\|29\|\| (muraja-bandhaḥ)*

**Перевод:**

**The protector of the protectors who offered respects, Mahāprabhu, when he prepared to leave, could not be stopped, for his face would give bliss to the devotees who served his feet on his auspicious return.**

### Text 30

*prabhāte pṛṣṭhato dṛṣṭvā prahārasya ca lakṣaṇam \|*  
*dāmodarasya pidadhe vastreṇaiva purī-prabhuḥ \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the marks of beating on Dāmodara’s back in the dawn, Paramānanda covered his back with a cloth.**

### Text 31

*kiyad dūraṁ tato gatvā virarāma mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*śrī-rāmānanda-rāyeṇa praṇaya-dvandvavān mithaḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At some distance, Mahāprabhu, desiring to quarrel with Rāmānanda in affection, stopped moving.**

### Text 32

*sa tyaktvā gacchatā tena prabhunānunayair bahu \|*  
*tarpito’pi na vai tṛptiṁ jagāma kṣaṇam apy uta \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up the debate, the Lord continued moving. Enlightened by Mahāprabhu’s various polite words, Rāmānanda could not be fully satisfied for a moment.**

### Text 33

*manojña-dṛṅ nāmanojña-vibhrama-bhramaṇākulaḥ \|*  
*manojña-dṛṅ nāma no jña-vibhrama-bhramaṇākulaḥ \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Attractive-eyed Rāmānanda became full of sorrow by the wanderings of the Lord, which were not without attractive beauty. Clearly having spiritual vision, he was not confused by the illusions created by the great errors of impersonal jñānīs. (The same syllables in two lines have different meanings.)**

### Text 34

*sa tu premāspadasyāsya rāmānando mahā-nidhiḥ \|*  
*tad-alokana-duḥkhena kathaṅkāraṁ bhaviṣyati \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Who can describe the unseen sorrow of the great ocean Rāmānanda for Mahāprabhu, the object of his prema?**

### Text 35

*tato mahā-prasādaughaḥ sadyas tatra caturvidhaḥ \|*  
*vāṇīnāthena prahito milito’bhūd anekaśaḥ \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Great quantities of mahāprasādam of four varieties sent by Vāṇīnātha suddenly arrived.**

### Text 36

*nīlācalāt samāyāntaṁ sadyaḥ śrīmad-anūttamam \|*  
*mahā-prasādaṁ dṛṣṭvāsau mumude parama-prabhuḥ \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the attractive, excellent mahāprasādam which had suddenly come from Puri, Gauracandra, the supreme Lord, was happy.**

### Text 37

*nānānā nuni nānenenān ānūn ananūn anu \|*  
*nānānūne nānanānnone no nānā na nun nanu \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Oh! A certain devotee with politeness considered a small amount of prasādam to be a great amount. There were plenty of varieties of food for the mouth without scarcity. No one said, “There is not enough.” (One consonant is used)**

### Text 38

*mahāprasādopayogaṁ kṛtvā tatra kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*viśramya ca kṣaṇaṁ harṣāt pratasthe taiḥ samaṁ punaḥ \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After eating the mahāprasādam, the ocean of mercy rested for a short time and then in joy departed with the devotees.**

### Text 39

*kañcid deśaṁ samāsādya sthitaṁ taṁ sarva eva hi \|*  
*draṣṭuṁ samantād autsukyād āyayau citram eva tat \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Coming to one place, the Lord stopped there. All people very eagerly came to see him. That was astonishing.**

### Text 40

*viramaty eva ye vāsmin kṛṣṭā āsan samantataḥ \|*  
*tatratyā vāyunā sārdhaṁ dhairya-sauhitya-saurabhaiḥ \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Whenever the Lord stopped, people were attracted by his calmness and friendly fragrance, as much as they were attracted by the wind.**

### Text 41

*līlā lolāli-lalanā lalan nalina-lālanaiḥ \|*  
*nalāla lalanālīnāṁ līlāṁ lān anilo lalan \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wind, desiring the playful, wandering female bees, flirting with those female bees by moving the lotuses, blew here and there in a happy mood. (Two consonants are used.)**

### Text 42

*pathi premāviṣkṛtibhiḥ kṛtibhis taiḥ samaṁ vrajan \|*  
*majjati smaiṣa paramām ānandāmṛta-dīrghikām \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Going with the intelligent devotees who were distributing prema. Gaurāṅga immersed himself in a great pond of sweet bliss.**

### Text 43

*atha vīkṣya drumaṁ śreṣṭhaṁ dhāvann ārād avāritaḥ \|*  
*skandham utplutya dhṛtvā ca lambamānaḥ śriyaṁ dadhe \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing an excellent tree, he ran freely and, jumping, held the tree. Supported by it, he appeared beautiful.**

### Text 44

*āliliṅga taruṁ bhūyo locanāmbubhir āplutaḥ \|*  
*kaṁ vā kena prakāreṇa noddadhāra mahāprabhuḥ \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His eyes filled with trees, he embraced the trees. Whom did Mahāprabhu not deliver by some method?**

### Text 45

*kākeneva vane kekā lāvakena na kevalā \|*  
*śuddhāsāra-rasāddhāśu nutir āsusurāti nu \|\|45\|\|*  
*(pratilomānuloma-pādaḥ)*

**Перевод:**

**In the forest, the cries of peacocks mixed with those of the partridge, similar to cries of crows. That sound of the peacock had the attractiveness of the monsoon season. Like loud recitation of verses of praise, it surpassed bliss-giving liquor.**

### Text 46

*vṛndāvana-drumān ittham āliṅgayati vihvalaḥ*  
*tathā liliṅga sa taruṁ yathā cūrṇāyate muhaḥ \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In joy, the Lord embraced the Vṛndāvana trees (seeing them as such) so strongly that it seemed they would be crushed.**

### Text 47

*adhaḥ kaṇṭaka-saṅkīrṇe nipatiṣyantam añjasā \|*  
*bhiyā puri-prabhṛtayo jagṛhur vara-bāhubhiḥ \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In fear the devotees headed by Paramānanda held him with their strong arms, as they thought he would fall into the thorny bushes.**

### Text 48

*ūce'tha paśyāyaṁ kṛṣṇa-candro'bhito'bhitaḥ \|*  
*pratidrumaṁ vilasati jagaty etan mayīkṣate \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He said, “Look! Kṛṣṇa is performing pastimes everywhere, at each tree. I see the whole world being made of Kṛṣṇa.”**

### Text 49

*avapayo'tivimalam anantam asakṛd babhau \|*  
*niṣpaṅkaṁ bhūtalaṁ cātha citra-citrā prabhor gatiḥ \|\|49\|\|*  
*(ślokāvṛttiḥ)*

**Перевод:**

**The unlimited, pure earth, filled with water, without mud constantly shone. At that time, the Lord travelled at an astonishing pace.**

### Text 50

*adhikaṁ śuśubhe tatra vijayena prabhor asau \|*  
*vikasat-kāśa-kusuma-susmitā surasā śarat \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the travelling of the Lord, the pleasant autumn season, smiling with blossoming kāśa flowers, became especially beautiful.**

### Text 51

*bhuvaneśvara āgatya dadarśa bhuvaneśvaram \|*  
*mahā-prasādaṁ propāsya tatraiva virarāma saḥ \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving at Bhuvaneśvara, the Lord saw the deity of Śiva and, after taking mahāprasādam, took rest.**

### Text 52

*anyedyuḥ rajanī-śeṣe pratasthe taiḥ samaṁ prabhuḥ \|*  
*haridāsaṁ puraḥ prāpyāviśad grāmaṁ mahāprabhuḥ \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The next day, at the end of night, Mahāprabhu departed with the devotees and putting Hari-dāsa in front, entered the town.**

### Text 53

*sā ra sā sa ra sā raṁ ra sā nū ta na nū ta nā \|*  
*nā ta nū na ta nū sā ra raṁ sā sā ra sa sā ra sā \|\|53\|\|*  
*(pratilomānuloma-ślokaḥ)*

**Перевод:**

**The lakes of autumn were the essence of beauty. With the rains, they became exceedingly fresh with water. There were many cranes at that time, and all creatures, without or without body (the directions), got strength. The tree and creepers blossomed. With a portion of the cool season, the time was ideal and the directions were pleased.**

### Text 54

*atra nūtana-gehādi kārayitvā nideśataḥ \|*  
*purā rāmānanda-rāyo nināya prabhum añjasā \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rāmānanda Rāya, with permission, had a house built previously and brought the Lord inside quickly.**

### Text 55

*lepitaṁ śuddham ālokya gṛhaṁ tatra kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*uvāsa parama-prītyā paramānanda-puriṇā \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the house pure and anointed, the ocean of mercy with great pleasure stayed there with Paramānanda Purī.**

### Text 56

*tato nīlācalād āśu samāyāto'bhavan muhuḥ \|*  
*mahāprasāda-nicayaḥ svanna-pānaka-piṣṭakaḥ \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From Puri, piles of mahāprasādam, with excellent rice, drinks and sweets soon arrived in a constant flow.**

### Text 57

*yad-ājñā brahma-rudrādyair ādhāya śirasīḍyate \|*  
*kiṁ tasya vibhavo lokair jñāyate vibhavo nu kaiḥ \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devatās like Brahmā and Śiva take his order on their heads and offer prayers. What person can know his powers? He has such inconceivable power.**

### Text 58

*taṁ dṛṣṭvā parama-prītaḥ premnopāsya ca taiḥ samam \|*  
*śrī-rāmānanda-rāyeṇa kathayā rajanīṁ yayau \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the mahāprasādam, the Lord was very pleased and ate with prema. He spent the night speaking with Rāmānanda and the devotees.**

### Text 59

*etenaiva samaṁ nānā-kathā-kathana-tatparaḥ \|*  
*nināya rajanīṁ nātho rajanī-nātha-sundaraḥ \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Absorbed in various topics of discussion with Rāmānanda, the Lord, beautiful as the moon, passed the night.**

### Text 60

*prabhuś ca paramānanda-purī cāpi puro yayau \|*  
*rāmānandas tu matimān paścāt paścāt samāyayau \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu and Paramānanda went in front and intelligent Rāmānanda followed behind.**

### Text 61

*evam evaṁ pathi calan madhurādhara-rociṣā \|*  
*jajāpa nija-nāmāni karuṇā-rasa-sāgaraḥ \|\|61\|\|*  
*(asaṁyogaḥ)*

**Перевод:**

**The ocean of mercy, going on the path, chanted the Lord’s names with his sweet, shining lips. (There are no conjunct consonants)**

### Text 62

*evaṁ vrajann upanadi vīkṣyāvāsaṁ manoramam \|*  
*ūce'nugāyan madhuraṁ madhurādhara-sundaraḥ \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Travelling in this way, he saw a pleasant place to stay on the bank of a river. Singing sweetly, beautiful with his sweet lips, he spoke.**

### Text 63

*agre gacchata yūyaṁ tu kaṭake tatra nīvṛti \|*  
*darśanaṁ mama gopīśa-prāsādeṣu bhaviṣyati \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Go ahead to Kaṭaka. I will go and see the deity of Gopīnātha later.**

### Text 64

*ity uktās te mahātmānaḥ purī-prabhṛtayas tadā \|*  
*prayayus tatra gaurāṅgo viśaśrāmātha kenacit \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When he said this, the devotees headed by Paramānanda departed and Gaurāṅga stayed there to take rest with one devotee.**

### Text 65

*āyāti karuṇā-sindhur iti śrutvā gajeśvaraḥ \|*  
*ājñayā sakalaṁ tīrthaṁ cakāra kara-lālitam \|\|65\|\|*  
*(niroṣṭhyaḥ)*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing that the ocean of mercy was coming, Pratāparudra had the bathing spots cleansed by hand. (This has no labial consonants.)**

### Text 66

*sarvāṅgīnair alaṅkārair mādhuryojaḥ-prasādavān \|*  
*gopīnātho rarājāsau vāg-vilāsaḥ kaver iva \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Decorated in all his limbs, bright with sweet effulgence. Gopīnātha shone like the poetic ornaments of a poet.**

### Text 67

*utkaṇṭhāṁ taruṇīṁ prāpya nirantara-navāṁ navām \|*  
*rarāja rājā madhuraḥ sa-śrīka iva caitrikaḥ \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The beautiful king at every moment became more youthful with fresh longing and shone like the beautiful spring.**

### Text 68

*tata ete mahātmāno gopīnātha-mahāprabhoḥ \|*  
*prāsādaṁ viviśur hṛṣṭāḥ prasādollasitānanāḥ \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees with pleased face joyfully entered the temple of Gopīnātha.**

### Text 69

*tatra tān parayā prītyā vetra-vellita-pāṇayaḥ \|*  
*anayann antaraṁ veśma vismṛtānya-manorathān \|\|69\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The servants of the deity, holding canes, with great affection brought the devotees who were devoid of other desires into the room.**

### Text 70

*te vilokyātha taṁ premṇā prītim āpur mahattarām \|*  
*atha kaścit samāgatya tatratyaḥ pṛthivī-suraḥ \|*  
*bhikṣārtham avṛṇot tatra paramānanda-pūriṇam \|\|70\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the deity with great prema, they were greatly pleased. One brāhmaṇa came and offered food to Paramānanda.**

### Text 71

*atrāntare gauracandraś candra-koṭi-samujjvalaḥ \|*  
*jvalat-kāñcana-śailābho lābhodaya ivāgamat \|\|71\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Just at that time, Gauracandra, shining like a million moons, dazzling like a golden mountain, arrived, as if rising over the horizon.**

### Text 72

*dṛṣṭvā ciraṁ kṛpā-sindhur gopīnāthaṁ manoramam \|*  
*manorathaṁ mūrtimantam iva tatra mudaṁ yayau \|\|72\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing attractive Gopīnātha, like the personification of Cupid, the ocean of mercy became joyful.**

### Text 73

*atha svapneśvaro nāma so'yaṁ dharaṇī-daivatam \|*  
*bhikṣārtham avṛṇot tatra gṛhe'pi ca samānayat \|\|73\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A brāhmaṇa named Svapneśvara offered Mahāprabhu food and brought him to his house.**

### Text 74

*anyāṁs tu jagadānanda-mukhyān sukha-parāyaṇān \|*  
*śrī-rāmānanda-rāyo'sau nināya nija-mandiram \|\|74\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rāmānanda Rāya brought the devotees headed by Jagadānanda, all in bliss, to his house.**

### Text 75

*āgārāhityasukhade manorāme sa tān atha \|*  
*āgārāhityasukhade sadārāme tadānayat \|\|75\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He brought the devotees to an attractive garden, which gave benefit to the house, bliss and happiness. It was an excellent garden near the house, giving the continual joy of a forest.**

### Text 76

*ārāmārāma-lalitān kṛtvā tān atha satvaram \|*  
*rāmānando janānandollāsakṛd bhūpam āsadat \|\|76\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Making the devotees happy in the garden, Rāmānanda quickly went to the king, who gives happiness to the people.**

### Text 77

*te tatra randhanodyogaṁ cakrur viśraṇāntaram \|*  
*kṛta-bhikṣaḥ purī-svāmī prabhuṇā tatra cāgamat \|\|77\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After resting, the devotees began cooking. Paramānanda, after begging, came with the Lord.**

### Text 78

*tatropavana-madhye'sti sūcchrito bakula-drumaḥ \|*  
*visārī niviḍa-cchāyaḥ kulānāṁ bakula-drumaḥ \|\|78\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In that garden there was a very tall bakula tree, giving extensive, dense shade. It was the best among bakula trees.**

### Text 79

*paramānena lalitā paramānena sarvataḥ \|*  
*rājī-vanasya sājīvarājīva-yug athābhavat \|\|79\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The tree was huge, bigger than all other trees, and was surrounded by lotuses, giving life to the forest.**

### Text 80

*bakula-druma-mūle'sau vasan bhāti sma susmitaḥ \|*  
*anena hema-rūpeṇa jambu-vṛkṣaṁ jigāya saḥ \|\|80\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sitting under the bakula tree, the Lord shone with his smiling face. He defeated the jambu tree with his golden form.**

### Text 81

*atrāntare guru-śrīko bhūpa-cakra-śiromaṇiḥ \|*  
*vijayaṁ gauracandrāṅghri-dṛṣṭyai tatra cakāra saḥ \|\|81\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At that time, the crest jewel of kings, most glorious Pratāparudra, came to see the lotus feet of Gauracandra.**

### Text 82

*rāmānanda-sahāyaḥ sa savasanta iva smaraḥ \|*  
*caturaṅga-balair yuktaḥ samayāt samayāt tataḥ \|\|82\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Accompanied by Rāmānanda, he was like Cupid with spring. He eventually arrived with the four parts of his army (elephants, horses, chariots and foot soldiers).**

### Text 83

*avatīrya gaja-skandhāt gaja-skandhātisundaraḥ \|*  
*tad-ārāmaṁ prati prītyā bhūmau gacchan babhau bhṛśam \|\|83\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Alighting from the elephant’s shoulders, Pratāparudra endowed with elephant shoulders, went to the garden with affection, shining radiantly.**

### Text 84

*sadā sadānair gurubhir nāgair nāgair hayair vṛtaḥ \|*  
*patti-sampatti-sañcāyair bhūyo bhūyo rarāja saḥ \|\|84\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Surrounded by a wealth of soldiers, horses, and huge elephants exuding mada, he appeared splendid.**

### Text 85

*nāsty evāsya samo rājā kiṁ svarge kiṁ mahī-tale \|*  
*itīyaṁ tac ca tac coccaiḥ kṣurair akṣobhi ghoṭakaiḥ \|\|85\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The horses shook the earth with their hooves, proclaiming loudly, “There is not king equal to him in Svarga, or on earth.”**

### Text 86

*rāmānanda-bhujaṁ dhṛtvā niyojyāmātya-sañcayam \|*  
*abhito'bhiyayau rājā pūrṇa-candro'rka-yug yathā \|\|86\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Like the moon with the sun, the king, holding the arm of Rāmānanda, went here and there, engaging his ministers.**

### Text 87

*amātyair amara-prāyair antarbala-niveśibhiḥ \|*  
*prathamaṁ valayī-bhūto bhū-pradeśo rarāja saḥ \|\|87\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Like the earth surrounded by a ring (of islands), he shone radiantly, being surrounded by ministers, like devaṭās, who first entered the garden.**

### Text 88

*tad-bahiḥ pattayo'tiṣṭhaṁs tad-bahir haya-sañcayaḥ \|*  
*tad-bahiś ca gajāḥ sarve vyūha evābhavat tadā \|\|88\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On the outside were foot soldiers, beyond that, the horses and beyond that, the elephants. This was the arrangement of the whole array.**

### Text 89

*pādāravinda-yugalaṁ vīkṣya tatra dravan-manāḥ \|*  
*bhūpatir bhūtalaṁ bhūyaḥ prāpa harṣāśruṇā saha \|\|89\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the Lord’s lotus feet, the king’s heart melted. He fell to the earth with tears of joy in his eyes.**

### Text 90

*praṇamya bahudhā dṛgbhyām apibad vadanāmbujam \|*  
*na ca tṛptim agād bhūpaś citraṁ gaurāṅga-ceṣṭitam \|\|90\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Offering respects many times, the king drank with his eyes the lotus face of the Lord. But he could not be satisfied. This is the astonishing nature of Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 91

*bahudhā gauracandro'pi premṇābhāṣya vaco'mṛtaiḥ \|*  
*siṣeca tasya sarvāṅgaṁ sarvāṅgīnam ivāśliṣan \|\|91\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra profusely sprinkled him with sweet words in affection, while embracing him in every limb.**

### Text 92

*ājñāyājñāṁ prasādaṁ ca kṛta-kṛtyaṁ sa niryayau \|*  
*amātya-nicayāḥ sarve tato draṣṭuṁ yayur drutam \|\|92\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Finishing his duties, and by the order of the Lord, he gave orders to his followers and pleased them. All the ministers then entered to see the Lord.**

### Text 93

*pāre citrotpalaṁ so'kūpāre citrotpalaṁ yathā \|*  
*yiyāsoḥ svamataṁ jñātvā bhūpaḥ sat-pātram abravīt \|\|93\|\|*  
*(padma-bhedaḥ)*

**Перевод:**

**The king ordered his good servant, knowing the mind of Gaurāṅga. “The Lord, beautiful as a lotus, desires to go across the Citropala River to the ocean.**

### Text 94

*maṅga-rāja bhavān eva hari-candana-saṅgataḥ \|*  
*pāre-mahānadi mahāprabhum anvetu satvaram \|\|94\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O Maṅgarāja, quickly go with Haricandana across the great river, following Mahāprabhu.”**

### Text 95

*tad-ājñajātha te so’pi śrī-rāmānanda-rāyakaḥ \|*  
*naukāḥ sumahatīś cakre prabhuṁ cātha samānayan \|\|95\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On his order, Maṅgarāja, Haricandana and Rāmānanda Rāya, taking Mahāprabhu, got in a large boat.**

### Text 96

*udiyāya tadā pūrṇo bhagavān mṛga-lāñchanaḥ \|*  
*karaiḥ sammārjayāmāsa panthānam akhilaṁ tataḥ \|\|96\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**At that time the full moon rose up, cleaning the whole path with its rays.**

### Text 97

*tato gacchati gaurāṅge rājakīyas tadāgataḥ \|*  
*tatratyāṁs tatra nirṇīya tad-ājñāṁ nijagāda saḥ \|\|97\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After Gaurāṅga left, the king’s servant came there. Understanding king’s order, he spoke to the local people.**

### Text 98

*ājñāpayati devo yac chrūyatāṁ tan-mahottamāḥ \|*  
*āropyo’tra stambha eko yena tīrthaṁ bhaved idam \|\|98\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“O great people! Hear what the king has ordered. Erect a pillar here so that this place becomes a place of pilgrimage.”**

### Text 99

*iti śrutvā nṛpājñāṁ te stambham āropya tatra ca \|*  
*naukām āropya muditāḥ prabhuṁ harṣād upāsata \|\|99\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the king’s order, the people erected a pillar there. Having placed the Lord in a boat, the people worshipped him with joy.**

### Text 100

*itthaṁ pārenadi sa tu caturdvāram āgatya tais tai*
