*rātrau candrātapa-madhurima-vyāvṛtāyāṁ samantāt \|*  
*svāpaṁ cakre prabhur atha jagannātha-san-maṇḍapāntar*  
*lokair lakṣāvadhibhir api tu sthānam evātra nāpe \|\|100\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Crossing the river, the Lord came to Caturdvāra. At the night he slept in the deity’s outer hall covered with the attractive canopy. There, even hundreds of thousands of people could not enter.**

## Chapter Twenty

### Text 1

*rātrir yātā nātha talpaṁ jahīhīty*  
*ākarṇāyaṁ pakṣiṇāṁ kūjitāni \|*  
*netre nidrā-mudrite jāgṛhīti*  
*drāg ākṣipyan pāṇināthodiyāya \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the birds chirping, “O Lord! Night has ended. Rise from bed,” with eyes closed from sleep, he woke up, and waving his hand to silence them, rose quickly.**

### Text 2

*nirmālyānnaṁ tatra sadyaḥ sametaṁ*  
*dṛṣṭvā harṣād āhnikāny ārabheta \|*  
*annaṁ pānaṁ piṣṭakādi prakāmaṁ*  
*tais tair bhuktvā prītimāṁś ca pratasthe \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing with joy the garland and food remnants, which had suddenly arrived, he performed his daily rites. Eating rice, drinks and sweets and being satisfied, he departed.**

### Text 3

*tatrāmātyau tena samyag visṛṣṭau*  
*tābhyāṁ bhūyo netra-pāthoja-pāthaḥ \|*  
*tene kṣāme tat tanū hanta tābhyām*  
*utsāho’yaṁ kaḥ prakāro vidhātuḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He dismissed the two officers (Maṅgarāja and Haricandana). They shed tears and their bodies became thin. What is the power of the creator?**

### Text 4

*deśaṁ deśaṁ pratyupeyuḥ samantād*  
*ājñā rājño lekha-pūrvāḥ samastāḥ \|*  
*sthāne sthāne navya-navyaṁ niśāntaṁ*  
*sāmagrībhiḥ kartum agre pavitram \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The king sent orders through letters before the Lord arrived to the various places, in order make new, pure houses, with furnishings.**

### Text 5

*udyann āsīd yatra tair eṣa nātho*  
*harṣotkarṣāl lakṣa-saṅkhyair manuṣyaiḥ \|*  
*niṣpratyūhaṁ tatra tatrekṣaṇābjaiḥ*  
*kāku-proktaiḥ pūjitaḥ saṁstuteś ca \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Wherever the Lord went, hundreds of thousands of people worshipped him with their lotus eyes and humble words in joy, without creating any obstacles.**

### Text 6

*adya śvo vā nūnam atraiṣyatīti*  
*proccair āsīd agrato harṣa-nādaḥ \|*  
*paścād āyātīti tasmād upeto*  
*bho bhoḥ paścād eva sarvatra bhūyaḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“He will come today or tomorrow.” “He is coming.” “He has arrived.” There were loud shouts of joy in this manner before his arrival.**

### Text 7

*kecit kecit tatra papracchur āryāḥ*  
*kvāsau kvāsau kṛṣṇa-caitanya-candraḥ \|*  
*itthaṁ nāthaṁ pūriṇaṁ taṁ prabhuṁ taṁ*  
*tāv anyonyaṁ darśayāmāsatus tān \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some of the elders said, “Where is Kṛṣṇa Caitanya?” Mahāprabhu and Paramānanda then showed each other to the people.**

### Text 8

*vāsaṁ vāsaṁ pratyupete prabhāte*  
*rājñām ājñā-yantritāḥ sarva eva \|*  
*deśe deśe śuddha-gehāni kṛtvā*  
*sāmagrīṁ ca pronmadā ānayanti \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When dawn came, acting according to the king’s order, all the people in all houses, in all places, cleansed their houses and, in bliss, brought auspicious items.**

### Text 9

*rāmānando bhadra-paryantam etya*  
*pratyāvṛttas tena samyag visṛṣṭaḥ \|*  
*vicchedārtaḥ kṣetram eva pratasthe*  
*gaurāṅgo’yaṁ so’py upeyād udīcīm \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rāmānanda went as far as Badreśvara, the Lord dismissed him and he went back to Puri, feeling pain in separation. Gaurāṅga proceeded north.**

### Text 10

*oḍraṁ yāvad bhūpater lekha-yuktā*  
*āsaṁs tāvad tāvad evaṁ dhurīṇaiḥ \|*  
*prātar dhūpāntargataṁ rāja-yogyaṁ*  
*nirmālyaṁ cānītam eva prakāmam \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Through all of Orissa, the people acted according to the king’s letter, and in the morning brought abundant items including incense worthy of a king and deity remnants.**

### Text 11

*śrīmān gauḍaṁ deśam āsādya gaṅgā*  
*draṣṭavyeti prema-vaihvalya-nunnaḥ \|*  
*tat-saṁsṛṣṭi-snigdha-mugdhāntarātmā*  
*tat-tat-sthānāpyāyitāṅgaḥ sa reje \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Realizing that the Lord had reached Bengal and would come to the Gaṅgā, the people were overcome with prema. Their hearts joyful affectionate because of impending contact with him, they increased in beauty, being pleased, even in their houses.**

### Text 12

*āgatya śrī-rāghavasyāśramāntaḥ*  
*śrī-gaurāṅgaś candravat pūrva-śailam \|*  
*gandhair mālyaiḥ puṣpa-dhūpopahāraiḥ*  
*premāviṣṭaḥ kautukī saṁmamāda \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Like the moon rising on the eastern horizon, he came to Rāghava’s house (in Pānihaṭi). Joyful and full of prema, he was satisfied with the fragrances, garlands, incense and gifts.**

### Text 13

*tatra sthitvā rāghavasyāśrame’sau*  
*nītvā nāthaḥ pañcaṣān vāsarān saḥ \|*  
*jyeṣṭhaṁ tāvac chrī-navadvīpa-bhūmāv*  
*agre prītyā preṣayāmāsa hṛṣṭaḥ \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He spend five or six days in his place, and with great joy, affectionately sent Paramānanda to Navadvīpa ahead of himself.**

### Text 14

*tasmin yāte gauracandraḥ sametaḥ*  
*śrīvāsasya prema-pātrasya geham \|*  
*sthitvā tatra prāṇimātre dayāluḥ*  
*sarvatrāsau saṁvyadhattānukampām \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After Paramānanda left, Gauracandra went to Śrīvāsa’s house (in Kumāra-haṭta) and stayed there. Merciful all living beings, he showed compassion to them all.**

### Text 15

*dvitrair asmin vāsarair lakṣa-saṅkhyā*  
*bhūyo bhūyo harṣa-pāthodhi-magnāḥ \|*  
*yātāyātaṁ sarvataś cakrur atra*  
*cchidraṁ nāsīc caivam asyānubhāvaḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Staying several days there, hundreds of thousands of people came, absorbed in great bliss. There was no fault in Mahāprabhu’s glory.**

### Text 16

*rathyāsvoka-dvāri kecid drumeṣu*  
*prācīreṣu prāyaśo’nye manuṣyāḥ \|*  
*āsan līlā-bhitti-citra-pratīkā*  
*notkaṇṭhānāṁ pāram īyuḥ kadācit \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some people stood on the road, some in their doors, some in trees, and some on walls, like painted pictures on a wall. Their longing had no end.**

### Text 17

*rātrāv eko’pahnuto naukayāsau*  
*tat-tad-grāmasyottareṇānya-deśam \|*  
*āyātaḥ śrī-vāsudevasya gehaṁ*  
*gatvāpāyāt śrī-śivānanda-geham \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the night, alone and without being seen, he went by boat to another place, north of the town. He went to Vāsudeva’s house and then to Śīvānanda’s house.**

### Text 18

*asmin gehe rātrim ekāṁ tu nītvā*  
*bhikṣāṁ cakre deśa evottare saḥ \|*  
*tat-tal-lokair lakṣa-saṅkhyaiḥ sameto*  
*naukārūḍhaḥ śāntipuryāṁ jagāma \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Staying there one day, he begged to the north of the village, and then went to Śāntipura with thousands of people by boat.**

### Text 19

*śrīvāsādyais tair athālokya nainaṁ*  
*pratyudvignaiḥ sarvato’nviṣya bhūyaḥ \|*  
*yāvan naiṣo’darśi tāvat suduḥkhair*  
*gāḍhaṁ gāḍham ardyamānair abhāṣi \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa, not seeing him, looked everywhere with a disturbed mind. He felt deep grief as long as he did not see him.**

### Text 20

*nāvā gacchan svardhunī-madhya-bhūmau*  
*nāmnāṁ gāthāṁ lola-cittaḥ prakāśya \|*  
*advaitasya grāmam āsādya nāthaḥ*  
*premnottasthau gantum atyantam utkaḥ \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Going by boat on the Gaṅgā, with fickle heart, the Lord chanted the name. Coming to Advaita’s village, the Lord, most anxious to meet Advaita, felt great prema.**

### Text 21

*madhye-dvāraṁ tena sārdhaṁ mahārhaḥ*  
*saṅgas tasyāśleṣa-kolāhalena \|*  
*āsīn naiṣāṁ prāṇināṁ bhāgya-bhājāṁ*  
*cakṣuḥ-śrotra-dvandva-tṛptyai babhūva \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Advaita met him at the door and embraced him with bliss. The fortunate living entities could not be satisfied with seeing and hearing about this.**

### Text 22

*bhūyo bhūyo gāḍham āśleṣa-pīḍau*  
*premāviṣṭau stas tathādvaita-gaurau \|*  
*tatrānte’sau taṁ tathā yogam enaṁ*  
*pūjācaryāvāg-vilāsair upāsīt \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Again and again they embraced, overwhelmed by prema. Then Advaita worshipped him with worthy items and gracious words.**

### Text 23

*āgatyātho śrī-śacī nāma devī*  
*trailokyānām eva mātā tam enam \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā mene harṣa-pāthodhi-magnaṁ*  
*tatrātmānaṁ sa-pramodārti-lajjam \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śacī devī, mother of the three worlds, came and, seeing him, felt herself drown in an ocean of bliss, while experiencing joy, sorrow and shyness.**

### Text 24

*tatraivāsīt ṣaḍ dināni krameṇa*  
*śrī-gaurāṅgo mātṛ-dattānutṛptaḥ \|*  
*ācāryeṇa prīty-upānīta-caryo*  
*netrānandaṁ prāṇinām eva kurvan \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Accepting services rendered with affection by Advaita, Gauracandra, giving bliss to all eyes, spent six days in Śāntipura and ate with satisfaction food given by his mother.**

### Text 25

*teṣāṁ teṣāṁ vāsarāṇāṁ samūhe*  
*yāmo lokā lakṣa-koṭyaḥ samīyuḥ \|*  
*ācāryo’sau pratyahaṁ tās tathaiva*  
*dravyair bhūyaḥ prīṇayāmāsa harṣāt \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On all those days, at every hour, thousands of people came. Advaita in joy pleased all of them each day with various articles.**

### Text 26

*anyedyuḥ sa śrī-navadvīpa-bhūmeḥ*  
*pāre-gaṅgaṁ paścime kvāpi deśe \|*  
*śrīmān sarva-prāṇināṁ tat-tad-aṅgair*  
*netrānandaṁ samyag āgatya tene \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One day Gauracandra went across the Gaṅgā from Navadvīpa and in a place to the west spread bliss to all beings by his limbs.**

### Text 27

*kiṁ vā mūkaḥ kiṁ nu paṅguḥ kim andhaḥ*  
*kiṁ vā vṛddhaḥ kiṁ śiśuḥ kiṁ striyo vā \|*  
*ye ye sarve śrī-navadvīpa-bhūsthāḥ*  
*prīty-udrekāt te ta evātha jagmuḥ \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mute, lame, blind, old, young and woman, all persons living in Navadvīpa came with great affection.**

### Text 28

*yāvat tasthau tatra gaurāṅga-candras*  
*tāvat sarve sarvato lakṣa-koṭyaḥ \|*  
*gāḍhotkaṇṭhā-nirbharārtāḥ samīyur*  
*draṣṭuṁ taṁ te kiṁ striyaḥ kiṁ pumāṁsaḥ \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**As long as Gauracandra remained there, thousands and thousands of people, both men and women, filled with intense longing, came from all places to see him.**

### Text 29

*madhye madhye tatra loka-pracāyair*  
*atyudvigno bhūyaso’ntardadhāti \|*  
*kintūtkaṇṭhā vardhate gāḍha-gāḍhaṁ*  
*teṣāṁ teṣāṁ krandatāṁ mukta-kaṇṭham \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**After some time Gauracandra became disturbed by all the people and disappeared. But their longing increased and they cried loudly.**

### Text 30

*evaṁ nītvā tatra nātho dināni*  
*prīty-udrekāt pañcaṣāṇi krameṇa \|*  
*netrānandaṁ sarva-lokasya tadvaṁs*  
*tais tair divyaṁ deśam eva pratasthe \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Out of affection he stayed in the village five or six days, giving happiness to their eyes. They then returned to their own places.**

### Text 31

*kañcid gopīnātha-śīti-prasiddhaṁ*  
*gopīnāthe śeta ity anvayena \|*  
*tasmin deśe kvāpi gauracandraḥ*  
*premāviṣṭo vīkṣya śaśvan nananda \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing a place called Gopīnātha-śi, the place where Gopīnātha sleeps, Gauracandra became overwhelmed with prema, and rejoiced constantly.**

### Text 32

*kālindīye tīra eva prayātuṁ*  
*gāḍhotkaṇṭhaḥ paścime kvāpi gatvā \|*  
*pratyāvṛtto bhūya eṣa sva-citte*  
*kiṁ vālokya svardhunī-tīram āyāt \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Desiring to go to the Yamunā, he went west, but then turned around. Considering in his mind, he went to the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 33

*tat-tad-deśe bhūya eva prakāmaṁ*  
*sthitvā kṛtvā dīrgha-dīrghānukampām \|*  
*śrī-nīlādrau bhūya eva pratasthe*  
*citraṁ citraṁ tasya tat tac caritram \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Staying at that place for a good amount time, he gave great mercy and then returned to Puri. His character was most amazing.**

### Text 34

*tat-tad-vyājāt svardhunī-tīram āyāt*  
*yatra śrīmāṁś citram evāvatīrṇaḥ \|*  
*netrānandaṁ sarva-lokasya kṛtvā*  
*nīlādri-stha-prītaye bhūya āsīt \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On this pretext (mercy), he went to the Gaṅgā, where he was born, and having given bliss to all people’s eyes, again went to please the people of Puri.**

### Text 35

*sthitvā tatra śrīmayo gauracandraḥ*  
*kañcit kālaṁ bhūyo’dhvanaiva \|*  
*kālindīyaṁ tīram eva pratasthe*  
*vicchedārtāṁs tatra tāṁs tān vidhāya \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gauracandra stayed for some time in Puri and then departed for the bank of the Yamunā, giving pain of separation to the people of Puri.**

### Text 36

*rāmānandas tad-viyogādhi-pīḍā-*  
*kṣīṇa-kṣīṇas tatyaje’sūn mahātmā \|*  
*vicchede syād yogyam etac caritraṁ*  
*premnas tāvat tādṛśasyāsya nūnam \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Pained by the separation, Rāmānanda Rāya became very thin and gave up his life airs. This is the nature of prema in separation.**

### Text 37

*sthitvā tatra dināni hanta katicid bhūyo’sitādrau prabhuḥ*  
*śrīmān etya nananda nandayati ca smaitān ajasraṁ janān \|*  
*evaṁ viṁśati-hāyanāntara-bhavāṁ yātrāṁ vilokyākhilāṁ*  
*svaṁ dhāmātha jagāma kaiścid api taiḥ sārdhaṁ kṛpā-sāgaraḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Staying in Vṛndāvana for some days, the Lord again returned to Puri and gave unlimited bliss to the people there, as well as experiencing bliss himself. In this way for twenty years he observed all the festivals and then, along with his devotees, the ocean of mercy went to his abode.**

### Text 38

*premāmbhodhau jagad atiśaye majjayitvā sa bhūyo*  
*vicchedāgnāv api ca vidadhe magnam atyanta-durge \|*  
*citraṁ citraṁ tad api satataṁ prema-sindhur balīyān*  
*āsīt ko’yaṁ śiva śiva mahān gauracandrānubhāvaḥ \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord submerged the universe in the ocean of prema, and then drowned the universe in the insurmountable fire of suffering in separation. What is most astonishing is that the inconceivable ocean of prema became constantly strong. This is the great power of Gauracandra.**

### Text 39

*nānā-deśān nija-nija-janān evam ekatra kṛtvā*  
*tān anyonyaṁ praṇaya-niviḍān kārayitvā prakāmam \|*  
*tais taiḥ sārdhaṁ bata vilasito hanta gauḍotkaleṣu*  
*svaṁ dhāmāsmin gatavati gatā bhūr viyogāgni-sindhau \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bringing people of various places together as his loyal devotees and making them full of concentrated love, he performed pastimes with them in Bengal and Orissa and, after going to his abode, put the world in an ocean of fire caused by separation.**

### Text 40

*caturviṁśe tāvat prakaṭita-nija-prema-vivaśaḥ*  
*prakāmaṁ sannyāsaṁ samakṛta navadvīpa-talataḥ \|*  
*trivarṣaṁ ca kṣetrād api tata ito yann agamayat*  
*tathā dṛṣṭvā yātrā vyanayad akhilā viṁśati-samāḥ \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu manifested his prema for twenty-four years. He accepted sannyāsa, left Navadvīpa and wandered here and there outside of Puri for three years, and then spent twenty years observing the festivals of Jagannātha.**

### Text 41

*itthaṁ catvāriṁśatā sapta-bhājā*  
*śrī-gaurāṅgo hāyamānāṁ krameṇa \|*  
*nānā-līlā-lāsyam āsādya bhūmau*  
*krīḍan dhāma svaṁ tato’sau jagāma \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this way for forty-seven years Gaurāṅga performed his various pastimes, playing on earth, and then went to his abode.**

### Text 42

*āśaiśavaṁ prabhu-caritra-vilāsa-vijñaiḥ*  
*kecin murārir iti maṅgala-nāma-dheyaiḥ \|*  
*yad yad vilāsa-lalitaṁ samalekhi taj-jñais*  
*tat tad vilokya vililekha śiśuḥ sa eṣaḥ \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee with the auspicious name of Murāri wrote about his activities from his childhood, knowing his pastimes. Consulting this work, this child has written this work.**

### Text 43

*baddhāñjaliḥ śirasi nirabhara-kāku-vādair*  
*bhūyo namāmy aham asau sa murāri-saṁjñam \|*  
*taṁ mugdha-komala-dhiyaṁ nanu yat-prasādāc*  
*caitanya-candra-caritāmṛtam akṣi-pītam \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Putting my hands on my head, with humble words I offer respects again and again to Murāri who was endowed with a blissful and tender mind. By his mercy, this work describing the sweet pastimes of Caitanya-candra has become visible.**

### Text 44

*caitanya-candra-caritāmṛtam atyudāraṁ*  
*sarve dṛśā ca manasā mudā vahantu \|*  
*yad dṛṣṭa-mātram apahanti durāpa-pāraṁ*  
*saṁsāra-sāgaram ajasram udagra-hiṁsram \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May all people joyfully hold the magnanimous work Caitanya-candra-caritāmṛta in their eyes and mind. Simply by seeing the work, people can destroy the violent, insurmountable ocean of saṁsāra.**

### Text 45

*nāhaṁ stutau bata natau vinatau na śakto*  
*yat taiś ca tair jana-cayaṁ sva-vaśe kariṣye \|*  
*āśritya kintu nija-kāruṇikatvam eva*  
*yad yogyam atra tad aho racayantu dhīrāḥ \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am not capable of praising, offering respects or acting politely. Let people do these things by their power. But may the discerning people, having recourse to their mercy, please give me that mercy, as they see fit.**

### Text 46

*iha parama-kṛpālor gauracandrasya ko’pi*  
*praṇaya-rasa-śarīraḥ śrī-śivānanda-senaḥ \|*  
*bhuvi vilasati tasyāpatyam ekaṁ kanīyas*  
*tv akṛta parama-maugdhyāc citram etaṁ prabandham \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śivānanda Sen, a devotee full of affection for the most merciful Gauracandra, appeared on earth. His youngest son wrote this astonishing work out of great foolishness.**

### Text 47

*dhīrodātta-mahattamo guṇa-nidhir yasminn asau nāyako*  
*yatrāmūr lipayo nirantara-valat-prema-prakāśākṣarāḥ \|*  
*yatrāneka-mahā-mahottama-dhiyāṁ cāritram antargataṁ*  
*tac caitanya-caritra-varṇanam idaṁ jīyād ajasraṁ bhuvi \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May this work, which describes the ocean of good qualities, the greatest dhīrodātta , which constantly manifests by its words increasing prema, and which describes the pastimes of many great, wise devotees, remain eternally glorious on earth.**

### Text 48

*etat tāpa-traya-nirasanaṁ prema-mātraika-bījaṁ*  
*śrī-gaurāṅga-praṇaya-valitotkīrti-mātra-svarūpam \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā svāntaḥ-karaṇa-padavīṁ mām anālocya dhīrāḥ*  
*śaśvat kaṇṭhe dadhatu muditā ramyam enaṁ prabandham \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May the wise joyfully wear this enjoyable work, which destroys the three miseries, whose very seed is prema, whose very essence is great glorification of Gaurāṅga’s prema, around their necks at all times, seeing the situation in their own hearts, not seeing me.**

### Text 49

*vedāḥ rasāḥ śrutaya indur iti prasiddhe*  
*śāke tathā khalu śucau śubhage ca māsi \|*  
*vāre sudhākiraṇa-nāmny asita-dvitīyā-*  
*tithy-antare parismāptir abhūd amuṣya \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This work was completed on the second tithi of the waning moon, on Monday, in Āsāḍha month, in the year 1464 śākābda.**
